----------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\agents\agent_manifest.py

"""
Agent Manifest Definition.

The AgentManifest describes the identity and basic metadata
of an EasyChatBot AI Agent.

The manifest does not execute the agent.
It only defines:
- who the agent is
- what the agent represents
- its version information

Execution is handled by the AI Runtime.
"""

from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class AgentManifest:
    """
    Immutable definition of an AI Agent.

    Agents are identified through their manifest.
    The Runtime uses this information for controlled execution.

    Attributes:
        agent_id:
            Unique identifier of the agent.

        name:
            Human-readable agent name.

        description:
            Purpose and responsibility of the agent.

        version:
            Version of the agent definition.
    """

    agent_id: str
    name: str
    description: str
    version: str = "1.0.0"

----------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\agents\agent_result.py
----------------------------------------------------------------------

"""
Agent Result Contract.

Defines the standard output returned by AI Agents.

All agents should return the same structure
so the Runtime can process results consistently.
"""

from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class AgentResult:
    """
    Immutable result returned after agent execution.

    Attributes:
        success:
            Indicates whether execution completed successfully.

        agent_id:
            Identifier of the agent that produced the result.

        output:
            Agent response payload.

        error:
            Error message when execution fails.
    """

    success: bool
    agent_id: str
    output: Any = None
    error: str | None = None

----------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\agents\base_agent.py
----------------------------------------------------------------------

"""
Base AI Agent Contract.

All EasyChatBot AI Agents inherit this contract.

The BaseAgent defines:
- agent identity
- execution interface
- runtime interaction

Agents do not:
- call providers directly
- manage memory
- modify external systems

The AI Runtime controls execution.
"""

from abc import ABC, abstractmethod

from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import ExecutionContext
from backend.agents.agent_manifest import AgentManifest
from backend.agents.agent_result import AgentResult


class BaseAgent(ABC):
    """
    Abstract base class for EasyChatBot agents.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        manifest: AgentManifest,
    ):
        """
        Initialise an agent.

        Args:
            manifest:
                Agent identity definition.
        """

        self.manifest = manifest

    @abstractmethod
    async def execute(
        self,
        context: ExecutionContext,
    ) -> AgentResult:
        """
        Execute the agent.

        Args:
            context:
                Runtime execution context.

        Returns:
            AgentResult.
        """

        pass

----------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\agents\conversation_agent.py
----------------------------------------------------------------------

"""
Conversation Agent.

Provides character-aware conversational responses
for the EasyChatBot hackathon runtime.

The agent remains provider-independent.

Architecture:

ExecutionContext
|
v
ConversationAgent
|
+--> CharacterProfile
|
+--> Optional IntelligenceContext
|
v
ConversationRequest
|
v
AgentResult

The actual LLM execution remains outside this
hackathon agent and can later consume ConversationRequest
through the appropriate EasyChatBot LLM service.

The ConversationRequest remains part of the internal
agent result contract. Public API responses must decide
separately which fields are exposed externally.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from backend.agents.agent_manifest import (
    AgentManifest,
)

from backend.agents.agent_result import (
    AgentResult,
)

from backend.agents.base_agent import (
    BaseAgent,
)

from backend.characters.character_profile import (
    CharacterProfile,
)

from backend.conversation.conversation_request import (
    ConversationRequest,
)

from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import (
    ExecutionContext,
)


class ConversationAgent(BaseAgent):
    """
    Character-aware conversation agent.

    Responsibilities:

    - apply character identity
    - construct a provider-independent conversation request
    - include optional live intelligence
    - return a standard AgentResult

    Does not:

    - call an LLM provider
    - call Parallel directly
    - manage voice
    - manage memory
    - execute external workflows
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        character: CharacterProfile,
    ) -> None:

        super().__init__(
            AgentManifest(
                agent_id="conversation_agent",
                name="Conversation Agent",
                description=(
                    "Provides character-aware "
                    "conversation responses."
                ),
            )
        )

        self.character = character

    def build_request(
        self,
        context: ExecutionContext,
    ) -> ConversationRequest:
        """
        Build a provider-independent conversation request.

        This method translates runtime state and character
        identity into the application-level conversation
        contract.

        No provider-specific terminology is introduced.
        """

        system_instruction = (
            f"You are {self.character.name}, "
            f"a character in EasyChatBot. "
            f"Your personality is: "
            f"{self.character.personality} "
            f"Your learning style is: "
            f"{self.character.learning_style} "
            f"Communicate in a way that is consistent "
            f"with this character."
        )

        conversation_context = None

        if context.has_intelligence_context:
            conversation_context = (
                context.intelligence_context.to_context_text()
            )

        return ConversationRequest(
            system_instruction=system_instruction,
            user_prompt=context.request,
            conversation_context=conversation_context,
            character_id=self.character.character_id,
            character_name=self.character.name,
        )

    async def execute(
        self,
        context: ExecutionContext,
    ) -> AgentResult:
        """
        Execute the conversation agent.

        The current hackathon implementation prepares a
        provider-independent ConversationRequest.

        Actual LLM generation remains outside this agent.

        The ConversationRequest is retained in the
        AgentResult as an internal runtime contract.
        Public API serialization should not expose this
        internal request directly.
        """

        conversation_request = self.build_request(
            context
        )

        output = {
            "character": self.character.name,

            "character_profile": {
                "character_id": (
                    self.character.character_id
                ),
                "name": self.character.name,
                "description": (
                    self.character.description
                ),
                "personality": (
                    self.character.personality
                ),
                "learning_style": (
                    self.character.learning_style
                ),
            },

            "personality": (
                self.character.personality
            ),

            "learning_style": (
                self.character.learning_style
            ),

            "message": (
                f"{self.character.name}: "
                f"Let's explore this together. "
                f"You asked: {context.request}"
            ),

            "intelligence_context": (
                conversation_request.conversation_context
            ),

            "conversation_request": (
                conversation_request
            ),
        }

        return AgentResult(
            success=True,
            agent_id=self.manifest.agent_id,
            output=output,
        )

"""
Quality Agent.

Internal AI operations agent.

Evaluates AI outputs.

It does not:
- modify responses
- control agents
- interact with users

It provides quality insights.
"""


from backend.agents.base_agent import BaseAgent
from backend.agents.agent_manifest import AgentManifest
from backend.agents.agent_result import AgentResult

from backend.evaluation.quality_result import (
    QualityResult,
)

from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import (
    ExecutionContext,
)


class QualityAgent(BaseAgent):
    """
    Evaluates AI response quality.
    """


    def __init__(self):

        super().__init__(
            AgentManifest(
                agent_id="quality_agent",
                name="Quality Agent",
                description=(
                    "Evaluates AI outputs "
                    "for quality and governance."
                ),
            )
        )


    async def execute(
        self,
        context: ExecutionContext,
    ) -> AgentResult:

        quality = QualityResult(
            score=0.95,
            passed=True,
            findings=[],
            recommendations=[
                "Continue monitoring response quality"
            ],
        )

        return AgentResult(
            success=True,
            agent_id=self.manifest.agent_id,
            output={
                "quality_score": quality.score,
                "passed": quality.passed,
                "findings": quality.findings,
                "recommendations": (
                    quality.recommendations
                ),
            },
        )

----------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\agents\research_agent.py
----------------------------------------------------------------------

"""
Research Agent.

Retrieves external information through approved
knowledge providers and converts the result into
provider-independent IntelligenceContext data.

Architecture:

Research Agent
        |
        v
ParallelSearchProvider
        |
        v
ParallelClientWrapper
        |
        v
IntelligenceContext
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from backend.agents.agent_manifest import AgentManifest
from backend.agents.agent_result import AgentResult
from backend.agents.base_agent import BaseAgent

from backend.intelligence.intelligence_context import (
    IntelligenceContext,
    IntelligenceSource,
)

from backend.providers.parallel.parallel_provider import (
    ParallelSearchProvider,
)

from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import (
    ExecutionContext,
)


class ResearchAgent(BaseAgent):
    """
    Agent responsible for world information retrieval.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        provider=None,
    ):
        super().__init__(
            AgentManifest(
                agent_id="research_agent",
                name="Research Agent",
                description=(
                    "Retrieves live world information "
                    "for AI learning experiences."
                ),
            )
        )

        self.provider = (
            provider
            or ParallelSearchProvider()
        )

    async def execute(
        self,
        context: ExecutionContext,
    ) -> AgentResult:
        """
        Execute research and produce a normalized
        IntelligenceContext.
        """

        result = await self.provider.execute(
            context
        )

        if not result.success:
            return AgentResult(
                success=False,
                agent_id=self.manifest.agent_id,
                output=None,
                error=result.error,
            )

        intelligence = (
            self._build_intelligence_context(
                query=context.request,
                provider_output=result.output,
            )
        )

        return AgentResult(
            success=True,
            agent_id=self.manifest.agent_id,
            output=intelligence,
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _build_intelligence_context(
        query: str,
        provider_output,
    ) -> IntelligenceContext:
        """
        Convert provider output into the
        provider-independent IntelligenceContext.
        """

        if not isinstance(
            provider_output,
            dict,
        ):
            raise TypeError(
                "Research provider output must be a dictionary."
            )

        raw_results = provider_output.get(
            "results",
            {},
        )

        if isinstance(
            raw_results,
            dict,
        ):
            raw_results = raw_results.get(
                "results",
                [],
            )

        if not isinstance(
            raw_results,
            list,
        ):
            raw_results = []

        sources: list[IntelligenceSource] = []

        for raw_result in raw_results:

            if not isinstance(
                raw_result,
                dict,
            ):
                continue

            title = str(
                raw_result.get(
                    "title",
                    "",
                )
            ).strip()

            url = str(
                raw_result.get(
                    "url",
                    "",
                )
            ).strip()

            if not title or not url:
                continue

            content = ResearchAgent._extract_content(
                raw_result
            )

            sources.append(
                IntelligenceSource(
                    title=title,
                    url=url,
                    source="parallel",
                    content=content,
                )
            )

        return IntelligenceContext(
            query=query,
            sources=tuple(sources),
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _extract_content(
        raw_result: dict,
    ) -> str:
        """
        Extract available content from a normalized
        Parallel result.
        """

        excerpts = raw_result.get(
            "excerpts",
        )

        if excerpts is None:
            excerpts = raw_result.get(
                "snippet",
                raw_result.get(
                    "content",
                    "",
                ),
            )

        if isinstance(
            excerpts,
            list,
        ):
            return "\n\n".join(
                str(item).strip()
                for item in excerpts
                if str(item).strip()
            )

        return str(
            excerpts or ""
        ).strip()

FOLDER: .\backend\conversation

----------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\conversation\conversation_request.py
----------------------------------------------------------------------

"""
Provider-independent conversation request.

Represents the information required to generate a
character-aware conversational response.

This contract belongs to the application/runtime boundary.

It deliberately does not know about:

- Gemini
- OpenAI
- Ollama
- Parallel
- voice
- HTTP
- persistence
- provider SDKs

Architecture:

CharacterProfile
        +
ExecutionContext
        +
Optional IntelligenceContext
        |
        v
ConversationRequest
        |
        v
Future / existing EasyChatBot LLM service

The production EasyChatBot conversation and voice
architecture remains outside this hackathon runtime.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class ConversationRequest:
    """
    Immutable provider-independent conversation request.

    Attributes
    ----------
    system_instruction:
        Stable instruction defining how the character
        should behave.

    user_prompt:
        User-facing request to be answered.

    conversation_context:
        Optional contextual information, such as live
        intelligence retrieved by the Research Agent.

    character_id:
        Identifier of the character producing the response.

    character_name:
        Display name of the character.
    """

    system_instruction: str

    user_prompt: str

    conversation_context: str | None = None

    character_id: str = ""

    character_name: str = ""

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        """
        Validate request invariants.
        """

        if not isinstance(
            self.system_instruction,
            str,
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "system_instruction must be a string."
            )

        if not isinstance(
            self.user_prompt,
            str,
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "user_prompt must be a string."
            )

        system_instruction = (
            self.system_instruction.strip()
        )

        user_prompt = (
            self.user_prompt.strip()
        )

        if not system_instruction:
            raise ValueError(
                "system_instruction cannot be empty."
            )

        if not user_prompt:
            raise ValueError(
                "user_prompt cannot be empty."
            )

        object.__setattr__(
            self,
            "system_instruction",
            system_instruction,
        )

        object.__setattr__(
            self,
            "user_prompt",
            user_prompt,
        )

        if self.conversation_context is not None:
            context = (
                self.conversation_context.strip()
            )

            if not context:
                raise ValueError(
                    "conversation_context cannot be blank."
                )

            object.__setattr__(
                self,
                "conversation_context",
                context,
            )

        if not isinstance(
            self.character_id,
            str,
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "character_id must be a string."
            )

        if not isinstance(
            self.character_name,
            str,
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "character_name must be a string."
            )

        object.__setattr__(
            self,
            "character_id",
            self.character_id.strip(),
        )

        object.__setattr__(
            self,
            "character_name",
            self.character_name.strip(),
        )

    @property
    def has_conversation_context(self) -> bool:
        """
        Return whether contextual information exists.
        """

        return bool(
            self.conversation_context
        )

    @property
    def context_length(self) -> int:
        """
        Return the length of the conversation context.

        Useful for diagnostics and future token estimation.
        """

        if not self.conversation_context:
            return 0

        return len(
            self.conversation_context
        )

    def summary(self) -> dict[str, str]:
        """
        Return a safe diagnostic summary.

        Prompt and context content are deliberately
        excluded from the summary.
        """

        return {
            "character_id": self.character_id,
            "character_name": self.character_name,
            "has_context": str(
                self.has_conversation_context
            ),
            "system_instruction_length": str(
                len(self.system_instruction)
            ),
            "user_prompt_length": str(
                len(self.user_prompt)
            ),
            "context_length": str(
                self.context_length
            ),
        }

"""
Intelligence capabilities for EasyChatBot.
"""

----------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\intelligence\intelligence_context.py
----------------------------------------------------------------------

"""
Intelligence Context.

Provider-independent representation of external
information prepared for AI execution.

Architecture:

Research Agent
        |
        v
IntelligenceContext
        |
        v
AI Runtime integration
        |
        v
LLMRequest.context

The context must not expose provider-specific
implementation details to the application layer.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class IntelligenceSource:
    """
    Normalised external information source.
    """

    title: str
    url: str
    source: str
    content: str = ""

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if not self.title.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Intelligence source title cannot be empty."
            )

        if not self.url.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Intelligence source URL cannot be empty."
            )

        if not self.source.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Intelligence source name cannot be empty."
            )


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class IntelligenceContext:
    """
    Provider-independent intelligence context.

    Represents external information that may be supplied
    to an existing AI conversation.

    This object deliberately does not contain:
    - provider SDK objects
    - API credentials
    - model information
    - runtime state
    - persistence objects
    """

    query: str

    sources: tuple[IntelligenceSource, ...] = field(
        default_factory=tuple,
    )

    summary: str = ""

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if not self.query.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Intelligence query cannot be empty."
            )

        if not isinstance(
            self.sources,
            tuple,
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "Intelligence sources must be a tuple."
            )

        if not isinstance(
            self.summary,
            str,
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "Intelligence summary must be a string."
            )

    @property
    def has_sources(self) -> bool:
        """
        Return whether external sources are available.
        """

        return bool(self.sources)

    @property
    def source_count(self) -> int:
        """
        Return the number of external sources.
        """

        return len(self.sources)

    @property
    def has_summary(self) -> bool:
        """
        Return whether a summary is available.
        """

        return bool(
            self.summary.strip()
        )

    def to_context_text(self) -> str:
        """
        Convert intelligence into a safe textual context.

        The result is suitable for eventual inclusion
        in an existing LLMRequest.context field.
        """

        sections: list[str] = [
            "LIVE INTELLIGENCE",
            f"Research query: {self.query.strip()}",
        ]

        if self.summary.strip():
            sections.extend(
                (
                    "",
                    "Summary:",
                    self.summary.strip(),
                )
            )

        if self.sources:
            sections.extend(
                (
                    "",
                    "Sources:",
                )
            )

            for index, source in enumerate(
                self.sources,
                start=1,
            ):
                sections.append(
                    f"{index}. {source.title}"
                )
                sections.append(
                    f"   URL: {source.url}"
                )
                sections.append(
                    f"   Source: {source.source}"
                )

                if source.content.strip():
                    sections.append(
                        f"   Content: {source.content.strip()}"
                    )

        return "\n".join(sections)

----------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\intelligence\research_service.py
----------------------------------------------------------------------

"""
Research Service.

Coordinates the Research Agent and exposes a
provider-independent intelligence capability.

Architecture:

Application / Runtime
        |
        v
ResearchService
        |
        v
ResearchAgent
        |
        v
Search Provider
        |
        v
IntelligenceContext

The service does not know about Parallel SDK details.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from backend.agents.research_agent import (
    ResearchAgent,
)

from backend.intelligence.intelligence_context import (
    IntelligenceContext,
)

from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import (
    ExecutionContext,
)


class ResearchService:
    """
    Application-facing service for live research.

    The service coordinates the Research Agent but does
    not implement provider-specific search behaviour.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        agent: ResearchAgent | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self.agent = (
            agent
            or ResearchAgent()
        )

    async def research(
        self,
        user_id: str,
        query: str,
    ) -> IntelligenceContext:
        """
        Execute research and return normalized intelligence.

        Args:
            user_id:
                Identifier for the current user/session.

            query:
                Research question.

        Returns:
            Provider-independent IntelligenceContext.

        Raises:
            ValueError:
                If the query is empty.

            RuntimeError:
                If the Research Agent fails.
        """

        if not query.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Research query cannot be empty."
            )

        context = ExecutionContext(
            user_id=user_id,
            request=query,
        )

        result = await self.agent.execute(
            context
        )

        if not result.success:
            raise RuntimeError(
                result.error
                or "Research execution failed."
            )

        if not isinstance(
            result.output,
            IntelligenceContext,
        ):
            raise TypeError(
                "Research Agent did not return "
                "an IntelligenceContext."
            )

        return result.output

----------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\providers\base_provider.py
----------------------------------------------------------------------

"""
Base Provider Contract.

Defines the interface for external AI and knowledge providers.

Examples:
- Gemini
- Parallel Search
- Future LLM providers

The Runtime communicates with providers through this contract.

Providers are replaceable implementations.
The Agent layer must remain independent from provider details.
"""

from abc import ABC, abstractmethod

from backend.providers.provider_result import ProviderResult
from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import ExecutionContext


class BaseProvider(ABC):
    """
    Abstract base class for EasyChatBot providers.

    Providers are responsible for communicating
    with external services.

    They are NOT responsible for:
    - agent decisions
    - workflow orchestration
    - memory management
    - user interaction
    """

    provider_id: str

    def __init__(
        self,
        provider_id: str,
    ):
        """
        Initialise provider.

        Args:
            provider_id:
                Unique provider identifier.
        """

        self.provider_id = provider_id

    @abstractmethod
    async def execute(
        self,
        context: ExecutionContext,
    ) -> ProviderResult:
        """
        Execute provider operation.

        Args:
            context:
                Runtime execution context.

        Returns:
            ProviderResult containing provider output.
        """

        pass

----------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\providers\parallel\parallel_client.py
----------------------------------------------------------------------

"""
Parallel Search API Client Wrapper.

This module isolates the official Parallel SDK.

The rest of EasyChatBot does not directly depend on
the Parallel SDK implementation.

Architecture:

Research Agent
        |
        v
Parallel Provider
        |
        v
Parallel Client Wrapper
        |
        v
Parallel API
"""

import asyncio
import os
from typing import Any

from dotenv import load_dotenv


load_dotenv()


class ParallelClientWrapper:
    """
    Wrapper around the official Parallel Search SDK.

    Responsibilities:
    - authenticate with Parallel
    - execute searches
    - normalize SDK responses

    Does not:
    - create learning content
    - manage agents
    - decide workflows
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        api_key: str | None = None,
    ):
        """
        Initialise the Parallel SDK client.
        """

        self.api_key = (
            api_key
            or os.getenv("PARALLEL_API_KEY")
        )

        if not self.api_key:
            raise ValueError(
                "PARALLEL_API_KEY environment variable is required"
            )

        self._client = self._create_client()


    def _create_client(self):
        """
        Create the official Parallel SDK client.

        SDK:
            parallel-web 1.1.0
        """

        from parallel import Parallel

        return Parallel(
            api_key=self.api_key
        )


    @staticmethod
    def _normalize_response(
        response: Any,
    ) -> Any:
        """
        Convert the Parallel SDK response into
        application-safe data.

        Pydantic SDK models are converted to dictionaries.
        Other JSON-compatible values are returned unchanged.
        """

        if hasattr(response, "model_dump"):
            return response.model_dump()

        if hasattr(response, "dict"):
            return response.dict()

        return response


    async def search(
        self,
        query: str,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Execute a Parallel web search.

        Args:
            query:
                Natural-language research objective.

        Returns:
            Normalized application-level search result.
        """

        search_query = self._build_search_query(
            query
        )

        response = await asyncio.to_thread(
            self._client.search,
            search_queries=[
                search_query
            ],
            mode="advanced",
            objective=query,
        )

        return {
            "query": query,
            "search_queries": [
                search_query
            ],
            "results": self._normalize_response(
                response
            ),
        }


    @staticmethod
    def _build_search_query(
        query: str,
    ) -> str:
        """
        Build a concise search query from the
        user's research request.

        The full request remains the Parallel
        objective while the search query is kept
        concise.
        """

        words = query.strip().split()

        if len(words) <= 6:
            return query.strip()

        return " ".join(words[:6])

----------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\providers\parallel\parallel_provider.py
----------------------------------------------------------------------

"""
Parallel Search Provider.

Provides live world information
through the Parallel Search API.
"""

from backend.providers.base_provider import (
    BaseProvider,
)

from backend.providers.provider_result import (
    ProviderResult,
)

from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import (
    ExecutionContext,
)


class ParallelSearchProvider(BaseProvider):
    """
    Provider implementation using Parallel Search.

    The SDK client is created only when required.
    This allows tests to run without API credentials.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        client=None,
    ):
        super().__init__(
            provider_id="parallel_search"
        )

        self.client = client


    def _get_client(self):
        """
        Lazy-load the real Parallel client.

        Avoids requiring API keys during unit tests.
        """

        if self.client is None:

            from backend.providers.parallel.parallel_client import (
                ParallelClientWrapper,
            )

            self.client = ParallelClientWrapper()

        return self.client


    async def execute(
        self,
        context: ExecutionContext,
    ) -> ProviderResult:
        """
        Execute a live Parallel search.
        """

        try:

            client = self._get_client()

            result = await client.search(
                query=context.request
            )

            return ProviderResult(
                success=True,
                provider_id=self.provider_id,
                output=result,
            )

        except Exception as exc:

            return ProviderResult(
                success=False,
                provider_id=self.provider_id,
                output=None,
                error=str(exc),
            )

"""
Provider Result Contract.

Defines the standard response returned
by external providers.

All providers return this structure so
the Runtime can process results consistently.
"""

from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ProviderResult:
    """
    Immutable provider execution result.

    Attributes:

        success:
            Indicates whether provider execution succeeded.

        provider_id:
            Identifier of the provider.

        output:
            Provider response payload.

        error:
            Error details if execution failed.
    """

    success: bool
    provider_id: str
    output: Any = None
    error: str | None = None

"""
Runtime Execution Context.

Provides immutable runtime context shared with agents.

Responsibilities:
- identify an execution
- carry user request information
- provide runtime metadata
- optionally carry live intelligence context

Does not:
- execute providers
- manage memory
- modify systems
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Any
from uuid import uuid4


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ExecutionContext:
    """
    Runtime execution context shared with agents.

    Attributes
    ----------
    user_id:
        Identifier of the user initiating the execution.

    request:
        User request being processed.

    execution_id:
        Unique identifier for this execution.

    created_at:
        UTC timestamp when the context was created.

    metadata:
        Runtime metadata.

    intelligence_context:
        Optional provider-independent live intelligence
        generated by the Research Agent.

    Responsibilities:
    - identify an execution
    - carry user request information
    - provide runtime metadata
    - optionally carry live intelligence context

    Does not:
    - execute providers
    - manage memory
    - modify systems
    """

    user_id: str

    request: str

    execution_id: str = field(
        default_factory=lambda: str(uuid4())
    )

    created_at: datetime = field(
        default_factory=lambda: datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    )

    metadata: dict[str, Any] = field(
        default_factory=dict
    )

    intelligence_context: Any | None = None

    @property
    def has_intelligence_context(self) -> bool:
        """
        Return whether live intelligence is attached
        to this execution.
        """

        return self.intelligence_context is not None

"""
EasyChatBot Runtime Engine.

The Runtime is responsible for orchestrating
agent execution.

Responsibilities:
- receive execution requests
- provide execution context
- call approved agents
- return structured results

The Runtime does not:
- implement business logic
- call providers directly
- manage user interfaces
"""

from backend.agents.base_agent import BaseAgent
from backend.agents.agent_result import AgentResult
from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import ExecutionContext


class RuntimeEngine:
    """
    Central execution orchestrator.

    Agents execute through the Runtime.
    """

    async def execute(
        self,
        agent: BaseAgent,
        context: ExecutionContext,
    ) -> AgentResult:
        """
        Execute an agent using runtime control.

        Args:
            agent:
                Agent implementation.

            context:
                Runtime execution context.

        Returns:
            AgentResult.
        """

        return await agent.execute(context)

----------------------------------------------------------------------

----------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\services\companion_service.py
----------------------------------------------------------------------

"""
Companion Service.

Coordinates:

Character
Conversation Agent
Research Agent
Quality Agent
Memory Service

Application orchestration layer.

Live Intelligence flow:

CompanionService
        |
        +--> ResearchAgent
        |        |
        |        v
        |   IntelligenceContext
        |
        +--> ConversationAgent
                 ^
                 |
          enriched ExecutionContext
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import replace

from backend.agents.conversation_agent import (
    ConversationAgent,
)

from backend.agents.research_agent import (
    ResearchAgent,
)

from backend.agents.quality_agent import (
    QualityAgent,
)

from backend.characters.character_profile import (
    CharacterProfile,
)

from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import (
    ExecutionContext,
)

from backend.memory.memory_service import (
    MemoryService,
)


class CompanionService:
    """
    Coordinates AI companion experiences.

    The service orchestrates agents but does not contain
    provider-specific logic.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        character: CharacterProfile,
        conversation_agent=None,
        research_agent=None,
        quality_agent=None,
        memory_service=None,
    ):

        self.character = character

        self.conversation_agent = (
            conversation_agent
            or ConversationAgent(character)
        )

        self.research_agent = (
            research_agent
            or ResearchAgent()
        )

        self.quality_agent = (
            quality_agent
            or QualityAgent()
        )

        self.memory_service = (
            memory_service
            or MemoryService()
        )

    async def interact(
        self,
        context: ExecutionContext,
        use_research: bool = False,
    ):
        """
        Execute a complete companion interaction.

        When research is requested, the Research Agent
        produces an IntelligenceContext which is attached
        to a new immutable ExecutionContext before the
        Conversation Agent executes.

        The original ExecutionContext is never mutated.
        """

        memories = (
            self.memory_service.recall(
                context.user_id
            )
        )

        research_result = None

        conversation_context = context

        if use_research:

            research_result = (
                await self.research_agent.execute(
                    context
                )
            )

            if (
                research_result.success
                and research_result.output is not None
            ):
                conversation_context = replace(
                    context,
                    intelligence_context=(
                        research_result.output
                    ),
                )

        conversation_result = (
            await self.conversation_agent.execute(
                conversation_context
            )
        )

        quality_result = (
            await self.quality_agent.execute(
                conversation_context
            )
        )

        self.memory_service.remember(
            user_id=context.user_id,
            category="conversation",
            content=context.request,
        )

        return {
            "character": self.character.name,

            "conversation":
                conversation_result.output,

            "research":
                (
                    research_result.output
                    if research_result
                    else None
                ),

            "quality":
                quality_result.output,

            "memory_count":
                len(memories),
        }

"""
EasyChatBot Character Registry.

The registry provides the approved characters available
to the application.

Characters define identity and behaviour.

Agents remain responsible for capabilities and execution.
"""

from backend.characters.character_profile import (
    CharacterProfile,
)


class CharacterRegistry:
    """
    Registry of approved EasyChatBot AI companions.
    """

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self._characters = {
            "yeti": CharacterProfile(
                character_id="yeti",
                name="Yeti",
                description=(
                    "A friendly AI companion who helps "
                    "you discover knowledge and practice languages."
                ),
                personality=(
                    "Curious, encouraging and supportive."
                ),
                learning_style=(
                    "Conversation based learning."
                ),
            ),
            "clara": CharacterProfile(
                character_id="clara",
                name="Clara",
                description=(
                    "A professional learning companion "
                    "focused on technology and career topics."
                ),
                personality=(
                    "Analytical, structured and supportive."
                ),
                learning_style=(
                    "Structured and practical learning."
                ),
            ),
            "luminate": CharacterProfile(
                character_id="luminate",
                name="Luminate",
                description=(
                    "A creative companion for culture, "
                    "ideas and exploration."
                ),
                personality=(
                    "Creative, inspiring and curious."
                ),
                learning_style=(
                    "Exploration and discussion."
                ),
            ),
        }

    def get(
        self,
        character_id: str,
    ) -> CharacterProfile:
        """
        Return an approved character.

        Raises:
            ValueError:
                If the character does not exist.
        """

        normalized_id = character_id.strip().lower()

        try:
            return self._characters[normalized_id]
        except KeyError as exc:
            raise ValueError(
                f"Unknown character: {character_id}"
            ) from exc

    def list_characters(
        self,
    ) -> list[CharacterProfile]:
        """
        Return all approved characters.
        """

        return list(self._characters.values())

"""
EasyChatBot API Routes.

The API layer exposes the application.

Routes do not contain agent logic.
They delegate execution to application services.
"""

from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException

from backend.characters.character_registry import (
    CharacterRegistry,
)

from backend.services.companion_service import (
    CompanionService,
)

from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import (
    ExecutionContext,
)


router = APIRouter()

character_registry = CharacterRegistry()


def create_companion_service(
    character,
) -> CompanionService:
    """
    Create the application-level CompanionService.

    Kept as a small dependency seam so API tests can
    replace the service without invoking external providers.

    Production behaviour remains the same.
    """

    return CompanionService(
        character=character
    )


@router.get("/characters")
def get_characters():
    """
    Return available AI companions.
    """

    characters = (
        character_registry.list_characters()
    )

    return {
        "characters": [
            {
                "id": character.character_id,
                "name": character.name,
                "description": character.description,
                "personality": character.personality,
                "learning_style": character.learning_style,
            }
            for character in characters
        ]
    }


@router.post("/chat")
async def chat(
    payload: dict,
):
    """
    Execute an EasyChatBot companion interaction.

    Expected payload:

    {
        "user_id": "demo-user",
        "character": "yeti",
        "message": "Explain AI",
        "research": false
    }
    """

    message = payload.get("message")

    if not message:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=400,
            detail="message is required",
        )

    character_id = payload.get(
        "character",
        "yeti",
    )

    try:
        character = character_registry.get(
            character_id
        )

    except ValueError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=400,
            detail=str(exc),
        ) from exc

    companion_service = (
        create_companion_service(
            character
        )
    )

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id=payload.get(
            "user_id",
            "demo-user",
        ),
        request=message,
    )

    result = await companion_service.interact(
        context,
        use_research=payload.get(
            "research",
            False,
        ),
    )

    return result

----------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\src\ai_runtime\capabilities\errors.py
----------------------------------------------------------------------

"""
AI Runtime capability errors.

These exceptions provide explicit Runtime-level failure semantics.

The Runtime must distinguish between:

- invalid requests
- unknown capabilities
- duplicate capability registration
- governance rejection
- capability execution failure

Provider-specific exceptions must not leak through the Runtime
boundary.
"""

from __future__ import annotations


class RuntimeCapabilityError(Exception):
    """
    Base exception for Runtime capability failures.
    """


class InvalidCapabilityRequestError(
    RuntimeCapabilityError
):
    """
    Raised when a capability request is invalid.
    """


class CapabilityNotRegisteredError(
    RuntimeCapabilityError
):
    """
    Raised when no executor is registered for a capability.
    """


class CapabilityAlreadyRegisteredError(
    RuntimeCapabilityError
):
    """
    Raised when an executor is registered more than once.
    """


class CapabilityExecutionError(
    RuntimeCapabilityError
):
    """
    Raised when a capability executor fails during execution.

    The original provider/implementation exception is retained
    as the exception cause, but callers receive a Runtime-level
    error type.
    """


class CapabilityGovernanceError(
    RuntimeCapabilityError
):
    """
    Raised when governance evaluation itself fails.

    This is deliberately different from a governance rejection.

    Rejection:
        governance worked and said no.

    Governance error:
        governance could not produce a valid decision.
    """

----------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\src\ai_runtime\capabilities\executor.py
----------------------------------------------------------------------

"""
AI Runtime capability execution contract.

This module defines the interface between the AI Runtime
and concrete capability executors.

Architecture:

AI Runtime
    |
    v
Capability Executor Interface
    |
    +---- Live Intelligence Executor
    +---- Learning Executor
    +---- Evaluation Executor
    +---- Future Executors

The Runtime depends on this contract.

It does not depend directly on:
- agents
- providers
- SDKs
- external APIs

Concrete executors are responsible for translating the
provider-independent CapabilityRequest into the appropriate
capability implementation.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from abc import ABC, abstractmethod

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.models import (
    CapabilityRequest,
    CapabilityResult,
    CapabilityType,
)


class CapabilityExecutor(ABC):
    """
    Abstract executor for one Runtime capability.

    Each concrete executor must declare which capability
    it handles and implement asynchronous execution.
    """

    @property
    @abstractmethod
    def capability(self) -> CapabilityType:
        """
        Return the capability handled by this executor.
        """
        raise NotImplementedError

    @abstractmethod
    async def execute(
        self,
        request: CapabilityRequest,
    ) -> CapabilityResult:
        """
        Execute a capability request.

        Implementations must return a provider-independent
        CapabilityResult.

        They must not bypass the Runtime governance boundary.
        """
        raise NotImplementedError

----------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\src\ai_runtime\capabilities\gateway.py
----------------------------------------------------------------------

"""
AI Runtime Capability Gateway.

Controlled execution boundary between applications and
concrete AI capabilities.

Flow:

Application
    â†“
CapabilityRequest
    â†“
CapabilityGateway
    â†“
Governance
    â†“
CapabilityRegistry
    â†“
CapabilityExecutor
    â†“
CapabilityResult
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Protocol

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.errors import (
    CapabilityExecutionError,
    CapabilityGovernanceError,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.models import (
    CapabilityExecutionStatus,
    CapabilityRequest,
    CapabilityResult,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.registry import (
    CapabilityRegistry,
)


class GovernanceDecisionResult(Protocol):
    """
    Minimal governance result required by the Runtime.
    """

    @property
    def allowed(self) -> bool:
        """
        Whether execution is permitted.
        """
        ...


class CapabilityGovernance(Protocol):
    """
    Governance boundary required by the Runtime gateway.
    """

    def evaluate(
        self,
        request: CapabilityRequest,
    ) -> GovernanceDecisionResult:
        """
        Evaluate whether a capability may execute.
        """
        ...


class CapabilityGateway:
    """
    Controlled Runtime entry point for capability execution.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        registry: CapabilityRegistry,
        governance: CapabilityGovernance,
    ) -> None:
        self._registry = registry
        self._governance = governance

    async def execute(
        self,
        request: CapabilityRequest,
    ) -> CapabilityResult:
        """
        Execute an approved capability.

        Governance is evaluated before resolving and executing
        the capability.
        """

        try:
            governance_result = (
                self._governance.evaluate(request)
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            raise CapabilityGovernanceError(
                "Governance evaluation failed."
            ) from exc

        if not governance_result.allowed:
            return CapabilityResult(
                request_id=request.request_id,
                capability=request.capability,
                status=CapabilityExecutionStatus.REJECTED,
                error=(
                    "Capability execution was rejected "
                    "by governance."
                ),
                metadata={
                    "governance_decision": "rejected",
                },
            )

        executor = self._registry.resolve(
            request.capability
        )

        try:
            return await executor.execute(request)
        except Exception as exc:
            raise CapabilityExecutionError(
                "Capability executor failed: "
                f"{request.capability.value}"
            ) from exc

----------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\src\ai_runtime\capabilities\live_intelligence_executor.py
----------------------------------------------------------------------

"""
AI Runtime executor for Live Intelligence.

This adapter bridges the provider-independent AI Runtime
capability contract with the existing LiveIntelligenceAgent.

Architecture:

AI Runtime
    |
    v
CapabilityRequest
    |
    v
LiveIntelligenceExecutor
    |
    v
LiveIntelligenceAgent
    |
    v
LiveIntelligenceResponse

The adapter:

- validates the Runtime capability
- translates Runtime payloads into LiveIntelligenceRequest
- invokes the existing Live Intelligence Agent
- translates the agent response into CapabilityResult

It does not:

- implement intelligence logic
- call Parallel directly
- modify the LiveIntelligenceAgent
- perform governance decisions
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from src.ai_agents.live_intelligence_agent.handler import (
    LiveIntelligenceAgent,
    LiveIntelligenceRequest,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.executor import (
    CapabilityExecutor,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.models import (
    CapabilityExecutionStatus,
    CapabilityRequest,
    CapabilityResult,
    CapabilityType,
)


class LiveIntelligenceExecutor(
    CapabilityExecutor,
):
    """
    Runtime adapter for the existing LiveIntelligenceAgent.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        agent: LiveIntelligenceAgent,
    ) -> None:
        self._agent = agent

    @property
    def capability(self) -> CapabilityType:
        """
        Return the capability handled by this executor.
        """

        return CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE

    async def execute(
        self,
        request: CapabilityRequest,
    ) -> CapabilityResult:
        """
        Translate and execute a Live Intelligence request.
        """

        if (
            request.capability
            != CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "LiveIntelligenceExecutor received "
                "an unsupported capability: "
                f"{request.capability.value}"
            )

        agent_request = (
            self._build_agent_request(request)
        )

        response = await self._agent.execute(
            agent_request
        )

        return CapabilityResult(
            request_id=request.request_id,
            capability=request.capability,
            status=CapabilityExecutionStatus.COMPLETED,
            output=response,
            metadata={
                "executor": (
                    "live_intelligence_executor"
                ),
            },
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _build_agent_request(
        request: CapabilityRequest,
    ) -> LiveIntelligenceRequest:
        """
        Translate Runtime payload into the existing
        LiveIntelligenceRequest contract.
        """

        payload = request.payload

        topic = payload.get("topic")

        if not isinstance(topic, str):
            raise ValueError(
                "Live Intelligence capability requires "
                "a string 'topic'."
            )

        language = payload.get(
            "language",
            "French",
        )

        learner_level = payload.get(
            "learner_level",
            "B2",
        )

        if not isinstance(language, str):
            raise ValueError(
                "Live Intelligence 'language' "
                "must be a string."
            )

        if not isinstance(
            learner_level,
            str,
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "Live Intelligence 'learner_level' "
                "must be a string."
            )

        return LiveIntelligenceRequest(
            topic=topic,
            language=language,
            learner_level=learner_level,
        )

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\src\ai_runtime\capabilities\models.py
----------------------------------------------------------------------

"""
AI Runtime capability contracts.

This module defines the provider-independent contract used by
the AI Runtime to request execution of an approved capability.

Architecture:

Application
    |
    v
AI Runtime
    |
    v
Capability Request
    |
    v
Governance
    |
    v
Capability Execution

This module contains contracts only.

It does not:
- execute agents
- call providers
- evaluate governance policies
- perform external side effects
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import StrEnum
from typing import Any, Mapping


class CapabilityType(StrEnum):
    """
    Supported runtime capability categories.

    The list is intentionally small for the initial framework
    implementation. New capabilities should be added explicitly.
    """

    LIVE_INTELLIGENCE = "live_intelligence"
    LEARNING_GENERATION = "learning_generation"
    EVALUATION = "evaluation"


class CapabilityExecutionStatus(StrEnum):
    """
    Runtime-level execution status.
    """

    ACCEPTED = "accepted"
    COMPLETED = "completed"
    REJECTED = "rejected"
    FAILED = "failed"


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class CapabilityRequest:
    """
    Provider-independent request submitted to the AI Runtime.

    Attributes:
        capability:
            Logical capability requested by the application.

        request_id:
            Correlation identifier for the execution.

        payload:
            Explicit capability input.

        metadata:
            Non-business execution metadata.

        requested_by:
            Component or application requesting execution.
    """

    capability: CapabilityType
    request_id: str
    payload: Mapping[str, Any]
    metadata: Mapping[str, Any] = field(
        default_factory=dict
    )
    requested_by: str = "application"

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if not self.request_id.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Capability request_id cannot be empty."
            )

        if not self.requested_by.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Capability requested_by cannot be empty."
            )


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class CapabilityResult:
    """
    Provider-independent result returned by the AI Runtime.

    The result contains execution information but does not expose
    provider-specific response objects.
    """

    request_id: str
    capability: CapabilityType
    status: CapabilityExecutionStatus
    output: Any = None
    error: str | None = None
    metadata: Mapping[str, Any] = field(
        default_factory=dict
    )

    @property
    def succeeded(self) -> bool:
        """
        Return True when execution completed successfully.
        """

        return (
            self.status
            == CapabilityExecutionStatus.COMPLETED
        )

    @property
    def failed(self) -> bool:
        """
        Return True when execution failed.
        """

        return (
            self.status
            == CapabilityExecutionStatus.FAILED
        )

    @property
    def rejected(self) -> bool:
        """
        Return True when execution was rejected before execution.
        """

        return (
            self.status
            == CapabilityExecutionStatus.REJECTED
        )

----------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\src\ai_runtime\capabilities\registry.py
----------------------------------------------------------------------

"""
AI Runtime capability registry.

The registry maps logical Runtime capabilities to their
concrete executors.

The registry:
- resolves executors by capability
- prevents duplicate registrations
- provides explicit dependency injection
- does not execute capabilities
- does not perform governance decisions
- does not know about providers
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.errors import (
    CapabilityAlreadyRegisteredError,
    CapabilityNotRegisteredError,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.executor import (
    CapabilityExecutor,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.models import (
    CapabilityType,
)


class CapabilityRegistry:
    """
    Dependency-injection registry for Runtime capabilities.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        executors: list[CapabilityExecutor] | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self._executors: dict[
            CapabilityType,
            CapabilityExecutor,
        ] = {}

        for executor in executors or []:
            self.register(executor)

    def register(
        self,
        executor: CapabilityExecutor,
    ) -> None:
        """
        Register an executor for its declared capability.
        """

        capability = executor.capability

        if capability in self._executors:
            raise CapabilityAlreadyRegisteredError(
                "Capability executor already registered: "
                f"{capability.value}"
            )

        self._executors[capability] = executor

    def resolve(
        self,
        capability: CapabilityType,
    ) -> CapabilityExecutor:
        """
        Resolve the executor responsible for a capability.
        """

        try:
            return self._executors[capability]
        except KeyError as exc:
            raise CapabilityNotRegisteredError(
                "No executor registered for capability: "
                f"{capability.value}"
            ) from exc

    def contains(
        self,
        capability: CapabilityType,
    ) -> bool:
        """
        Return whether an executor is registered.
        """

        return capability in self._executors

    def capabilities(
        self,
    ) -> tuple[CapabilityType, ...]:
        """
        Return all registered capabilities.
        """

        return tuple(self._executors.keys())

"""
Flash Deal commerce integration boundary.

Converts a user-facing FlashDealCard into explicit commerce
contracts.

Architecture:

FlashDealCard
      |
      v
FlashDealCommerce
      |
      +----> CommerceAction
      |
      +----> PaymentPass
                    |
                    v
             CommerceService
                    |
                    v
             Vendor / Checkout

This module does not:
- process payments
- store payment information
- call vendor APIs
- execute autonomous purchases
- decide whether a product should be recommended

The discovery layer remains responsible for discovery.
The commerce layer remains responsible for commerce.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.models import (
    CommerceAction,
    CommerceActionType,
)

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.payment_pass import (
    PaymentPass,
)

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.flash_deal_card import (
    FlashDealCard,
)


class FlashDealCommerce:
    """
    Application boundary between a FlashDealCard and
    the commerce subsystem.
    """

    def create_action(
        self,
        *,
        user_id: str,
        card: FlashDealCard,
    ) -> CommerceAction:
        """
        Create an explicit purchase action from a Flash Deal.

        No purchase occurs here.
        """

        if not user_id.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Commerce user_id cannot be empty."
            )

        return CommerceAction(
            user_id=user_id,
            deal_id=card.deal_id,
            action_type=(
                CommerceActionType.INITIATE_PURCHASE
            ),
            destination_url=card.get_deal_url,
            metadata={
                "source": "flash_deal_card",
                "character_id": card.character_id,
                "vendor": card.vendor_name,
            },
        )

    def create_payment_pass(
        self,
        *,
        user_id: str,
        card: FlashDealCard,
        payment_pass_id: str | None = None,
    ) -> PaymentPass:
        """
        Create a demo-only payment handoff from a Flash Deal.

        No payment occurs here.

        The resulting PaymentPass is always:
        - READY
        - DEMO
        - not executed
        """

        if not user_id.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Commerce user_id cannot be empty."
            )

        return PaymentPass(
            payment_pass_id=(
                payment_pass_id
                if payment_pass_id is not None
                else f"payment-pass-{card.deal_id}"
            ),
            deal_id=card.deal_id,
            user_id=user_id,
            product_name=card.product_name,
            vendor_name=card.vendor_name,
            amount=card.current_price,
            currency=card.currency,
            checkout_url=card.get_deal_url,
        )

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\src\ai_runtime\commerce\models.py

"""
Hackathon commerce contracts.

These contracts define the boundary between EasyChatBot's
discovery experience and an eventual commerce/payment
integration.

EasyChatBot does not implement a marketplace here.

The external commerce provider is responsible for the
actual payment and vendor transaction.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum
from uuid import uuid4


class CommerceActionType(str, Enum):
    """
    Supported commerce actions.
    """

    VIEW_PRODUCT = "view_product"

    INITIATE_PURCHASE = "initiate_purchase"


class CommerceActionStatus(str, Enum):
    """
    Result status of a commerce action.
    """

    READY = "ready"

    REDIRECT = "redirect"

    REJECTED = "rejected"


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CommerceAction:
    """
    Explicit request to perform a commerce action.

    This is an application-level contract.

    It is intentionally independent from Stripe, Amazon,
    OLX, or any other vendor.
    """

    user_id: str

    deal_id: str

    action_type: CommerceActionType

    destination_url: str

    action_id: str = field(
        default_factory=lambda: str(uuid4())
    )

    metadata: dict[str, str] = field(
        default_factory=dict
    )

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if not self.user_id.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Commerce action user_id cannot be empty."
            )

        if not self.deal_id.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Commerce action deal_id cannot be empty."
            )

        if not self.destination_url.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Commerce action destination URL "
                "cannot be empty."
            )

        if not (
            self.destination_url.startswith(
                "https://"
            )
            or self.destination_url.startswith(
                "http://"
            )
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "Commerce action destination URL "
                "must use HTTP or HTTPS."
            )


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CommerceActionResult:
    """
    Provider-independent result of a commerce action.
    """

    action_id: str

    deal_id: str

    status: CommerceActionStatus

    next_url: str | None = None

    message: str | None = None

    metadata: dict[str, str] = field(
        default_factory=dict
    )

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\src\ai_runtime\commerce\payment_pass.py
----------------------------------------------------------------------

"""
Hackathon Payment Pass.

This module defines the minimal payment handoff contract required
for the EasyChatBot hackathon.

Architecture:

FlashDealCard
    |
    v
CommerceService
    |
    v
PaymentPass
    |
    v
Vendor / Checkout URL

The PaymentPass is a DEMO-ONLY checkout handoff.

It does not:
- execute payments
- call payment providers
- store payment credentials
- store card numbers
- store bank information
- create payment transactions
- confirm a purchase
- perform autonomous commerce actions

Important invariant:

PaymentPass != PaymentTransaction

The hackathon can demonstrate that a checkout/payment journey is
ready without actually charging the user.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from enum import Enum


class PaymentPassStatus(str, Enum):
    """Status of the demo payment handoff."""

    READY = "READY"


class PaymentExecutionMode(str, Enum):
    """Execution mode for the hackathon payment boundary."""

    DEMO = "DEMO"


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PaymentPass:
    """
    Immutable payment handoff contract for the hackathon.

    This object represents a checkout journey that is ready to be
    handed to the vendor. It does not represent an executed payment.
    """

    payment_pass_id: str
    deal_id: str
    user_id: str

    product_name: str
    vendor_name: str

    amount: float
    currency: str

    checkout_url: str

    status: PaymentPassStatus = PaymentPassStatus.READY
    execution_mode: PaymentExecutionMode = (
        PaymentExecutionMode.DEMO
    )

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        """Validate the payment handoff contract."""

        required_text_fields = {
            "payment_pass_id": self.payment_pass_id,
            "deal_id": self.deal_id,
            "user_id": self.user_id,
            "product_name": self.product_name,
            "vendor_name": self.vendor_name,
            "currency": self.currency,
            "checkout_url": self.checkout_url,
        }

        for field_name, value in required_text_fields.items():
            if not value.strip():
                raise ValueError(
                    f"PaymentPass {field_name} cannot be empty."
                )

        if self.amount < 0:
            raise ValueError(
                "PaymentPass amount cannot be negative."
            )

        if not (
            self.checkout_url.startswith("https://")
            or self.checkout_url.startswith("http://")
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "PaymentPass checkout_url must use "
                "HTTP or HTTPS."
            )

        if self.status is not PaymentPassStatus.READY:
            raise ValueError(
                "Hackathon PaymentPass status must be READY."
            )

        if self.execution_mode is not PaymentExecutionMode.DEMO:
            raise ValueError(
                "Hackathon PaymentPass execution_mode must be DEMO."
            )

    @property
    def is_ready(self) -> bool:
        """Return whether the checkout handoff is ready."""

        return self.status is PaymentPassStatus.READY

    @property
    def is_demo_only(self) -> bool:
        """Return whether the pass is restricted to demo execution."""

        return (
            self.execution_mode
            is PaymentExecutionMode.DEMO
        )

    @property
    def payment_executed(self) -> bool:
        """
        Explicitly indicate whether payment was executed.

        The hackathon PaymentPass can never represent an executed
        payment. It is a checkout handoff only.
        """

        return False

    def display_amount(self) -> str:
        """Return a UI-friendly amount."""

        return (
            f"{self.currency} "
            f"{self.amount:.2f}"
        )

    def render_demo(self) -> str:
        """
        Render the payment handoff for the hackathon demonstration.

        The output deliberately makes the non-execution boundary
        explicit to judges and users.
        """

        return "\n".join(
            [
                "PAYMENT PASS",
                "-" * 72,
                f"Product: {self.product_name}",
                f"Vendor: {self.vendor_name}",
                f"Amount: {self.display_amount()}",
                "",
                f"Payment status: {self.status.value}",
                (
                    "Execution mode: "
                    f"{self.execution_mode.value}"
                ),
                "",
                "[ PROCEED TO CHECKOUT ]",
                "",
                "No payment has been executed.",
                (
                    "Vendor checkout remains outside "
                    "the hackathon demo."
                ),
            ]
        )

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\src\ai_runtime\commerce\service.py
----------------------------------------------------------------------

"""
Hackathon Commerce Service.

Provides the controlled application boundary for commerce
actions and demo-only payment handoffs.

The service does not process money itself.

It prepares commerce actions and payment handoffs for an
approved integration.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.models import (
    CommerceAction,
    CommerceActionResult,
    CommerceActionStatus,
)

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.payment_pass import (
    PaymentPass,
)


class CommerceService:
    """
    Controlled commerce application service.

    For the hackathon, the service supports:
    - safe external-vendor handoff
    - demo-only payment preparation

    A future payment/partner adapter can be injected without
    changing DealCard or FlashDeal.
    """

    def initiate(
        self,
        action: CommerceAction,
    ) -> CommerceActionResult:
        """
        Prepare a commerce action.

        This establishes the external-vendor boundary rather
        than executing a payment.
        """

        return CommerceActionResult(
            action_id=action.action_id,
            deal_id=action.deal_id,
            status=CommerceActionStatus.REDIRECT,
            next_url=action.destination_url,
            message=(
                "Continue to the approved vendor "
                "to complete the purchase."
            ),
        )

    def prepare_payment(
        self,
        payment_pass: PaymentPass,
    ) -> PaymentPass:
        """
        Prepare a demo-only payment handoff.

        No payment provider is called and no transaction is
        executed. The PaymentPass contract itself enforces
        READY + DEMO semantics.
        """

        if not payment_pass.is_ready:
            raise ValueError(
                "PaymentPass must be READY."
            )

        if not payment_pass.is_demo_only:
            raise ValueError(
                "Hackathon payment must use DEMO execution mode."
            )

        return payment_pass
