FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\src\ai_runtime\discovery\flash_deal_card.py
----------------------------------------------------------------------

"""
Hackathon Flash Deal Card presentation contract.

This module defines the user-facing data contract for the
EasyChatBot Flash Deal Card shown by a character.

Architecture:

Parallel / Discovery
        |
        v
     DealCard
        |
        +---- Memory Context
        |
        +---- Character
        |
        v
  FlashDealCard
        |
        +---- GET DEAL
        |
        +---- MORE INFORMATION
        |
        v
 External Vendor / Future Commerce Flow

The model is presentation-oriented.

It does not:
- call providers
- access SQLite
- perform payments
- execute agents
- scrape vendor pages
- invent product information
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.models import DealCard


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class FlashDealCard:
    """
    User-facing Flash Deal Card.

    This contract maps directly to the hackathon UX:

    - character discovery
    - product information
    - pricing
    - discount
    - personal relevance
    - vendor
    - GET DEAL action
    - MORE INFORMATION action

    The card deliberately contains no payment implementation.
    """

    deal_id: str
    character_id: str
    character_name: str
    character_message: str

    product_name: str
    product_image_url: str
    tagline: str
    highlights: tuple[str, ...]

    current_price: float
    currency: str
    previous_price: float | None
    discount_percentage: float | None
    savings: float | None

    relevance_reason: str

    vendor_name: str
    more_information_url: str
    get_deal_url: str

    badge: str = "NEW FIND!"
    card_id: str | None = None
    metadata: dict[str, str] = field(
        default_factory=dict
    )

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        """
        Validate the presentation contract.
        """

        required_text_fields = {
            "deal_id": self.deal_id,
            "character_id": self.character_id,
            "character_name": self.character_name,
            "character_message": self.character_message,
            "product_name": self.product_name,
            "product_image_url": self.product_image_url,
            "tagline": self.tagline,
            "currency": self.currency,
            "relevance_reason": self.relevance_reason,
            "vendor_name": self.vendor_name,
            "more_information_url": self.more_information_url,
            "get_deal_url": self.get_deal_url,
        }

        for field_name, value in required_text_fields.items():
            if not value.strip():
                raise ValueError(
                    f"FlashDealCard {field_name} cannot be empty."
                )

        if not self.highlights:
            raise ValueError(
                "FlashDealCard must contain at least one highlight."
            )

        if any(
            not highlight.strip()
            for highlight in self.highlights
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "FlashDealCard highlights cannot contain empty values."
            )

        if self.current_price < 0:
            raise ValueError(
                "FlashDealCard current_price cannot be negative."
            )

        if (
            self.previous_price is not None
            and self.previous_price < 0
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "FlashDealCard previous_price cannot be negative."
            )

        if (
            self.discount_percentage is not None
            and not 0 <= self.discount_percentage <= 100
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "FlashDealCard discount_percentage must be "
                "between 0 and 100."
            )

        if self.savings is not None and self.savings < 0:
            raise ValueError(
                "FlashDealCard savings cannot be negative."
            )

        self._validate_url(
            self.product_image_url,
            "product_image_url",
        )
        self._validate_url(
            self.more_information_url,
            "more_information_url",
        )
        self._validate_url(
            self.get_deal_url,
            "get_deal_url",
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _validate_url(
        url: str,
        field_name: str,
    ) -> None:
        """
        Validate a user-facing URL.
        """

        if not (
            url.startswith("https://")
            or url.startswith("http://")
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                f"FlashDealCard {field_name} must use "
                "HTTP or HTTPS."
            )

    @classmethod
    def from_deal(
        cls,
        *,
        deal: DealCard,
        character_id: str,
        character_name: str,
        character_message: str,
        relevance_reason: str,
        product_image_url: str,
        tagline: str,
        highlights: list[str] | tuple[str, ...],
        badge: str = "NEW FIND!",
        metadata: dict[str, str] | None = None,
    ) -> "FlashDealCard":
        """
        Build a presentation card from a domain DealCard.

        Product, pricing and vendor information comes from the
        DealCard. Character and personalisation information is
        supplied explicitly by the application layer.

        No product or pricing information is invented here.
        """

        savings: float | None = None

        if (
            deal.previous_price is not None
            and deal.previous_price > deal.price
        ):
            savings = round(
                deal.previous_price - deal.price,
                2,
            )

        return cls(
            deal_id=deal.deal_id,
            character_id=character_id,
            character_name=character_name,
            character_message=character_message,
            product_name=deal.product_name,
            product_image_url=product_image_url,
            tagline=tagline,
            highlights=tuple(highlights),
            current_price=deal.price,
            currency=deal.currency,
            previous_price=deal.previous_price,
            discount_percentage=deal.discount_percentage,
            savings=savings,
            relevance_reason=relevance_reason,
            vendor_name=deal.vendor,
            more_information_url=(
                deal.commerce_link.destination_url
            ),
            get_deal_url=(
                deal.commerce_link.click_url
            ),
            badge=badge,
            metadata=(
                dict(metadata)
                if metadata is not None
                else {}
            ),
        )

    @property
    def has_discount(self) -> bool:
        """Return whether a verified discount exists."""

        return (
            self.discount_percentage is not None
            and self.discount_percentage > 0
        )

    @property
    def has_previous_price(self) -> bool:
        """Return whether a previous price is available."""

        return self.previous_price is not None

    @property
    def display_discount(self) -> str | None:
        """Return a UI-friendly discount label."""

        if self.discount_percentage is None:
            return None

        return f"{self.discount_percentage:.0f}% OFF"

    @property
    def display_savings(self) -> str | None:
        """Return a UI-friendly savings label."""

        if self.savings is None:
            return None

        return f"Save {self.currency} {self.savings:.2f}"

"""
Hackathon Flash Deal Card demo builder.

This module provides a deterministic demonstration flow for the
user-facing Flash Deal Card.

Flow:

Memory
    |
    v
DealCard
    |
    v
Personal relevance
    |
    v
Character
    |
    v
FlashDealCard

The module is intentionally deterministic for the hackathon.

It does not:
- call external APIs
- call Parallel
- process payments
- access SQLite directly
- execute agents
- create real orders
- pretend that demo pricing is live vendor data

Production discovery should provide the real DealCard and
verified source information.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.flash_deal_card import (
    FlashDealCard,
)

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.models import (
    CommerceLink,
    DealCard,
)

from src.ai_runtime.memory.models import (
    MemoryEntry,
    MemoryType,
)


DEMO_PRODUCT_IMAGE_URL = (
    "https://example.com/demo/iphone17-pro.jpg"
)

DEMO_VENDOR_PRODUCT_URL = (
    "https://example.com/vendor/iphone17-pro"
)


def build_demo_deal() -> DealCard:
    """
    Create deterministic demo discovery data.

    This is presentation/demo data only. It must not be
    interpreted as a live product offer.
    """

    commerce_link = CommerceLink(
        vendor="Demo Vendor",
        destination_url=DEMO_VENDOR_PRODUCT_URL,
    )

    return DealCard(
        product_name="iPhone 17 Pro",
        vendor="Demo Vendor",
        price=999.99,
        currency="EUR",
        previous_price=1199.00,
        discount_percentage=17.0,
        commerce_link=commerce_link,
        source="hackathon_demo",
        description=(
            "Demo product used to demonstrate the "
            "EasyChatBot discovery experience."
        ),
    )


def build_demo_memory() -> MemoryEntry:
    """
    Create deterministic demo user memory.

    The memory represents an explicit user preference that
    can be used to explain why the discovery is relevant.
    """

    return MemoryEntry(
        user_id="demo-user",
        memory_type=MemoryType.PREFERENCE,
        content=(
            "prefers powerful mobile photography "
            "and high-performance devices"
        ),
    )


def build_demo_flash_deal_card(
    deal: DealCard | None = None,
) -> FlashDealCard:
    """
    Build the complete deterministic hackathon Flash Deal Card.

    This function demonstrates the intended vertical slice:

        Memory
          +
        DealCard
          +
        Character
          =
        Personalised FlashDealCard
    """

    if deal is None:
        deal = build_demo_deal()

    memory = build_demo_memory()

    relevance_reason = (
        "You often look for powerful mobile photography "
        "and high-performance devices."
    )

    return FlashDealCard.from_deal(
        deal=deal,
        character_id="yeti",
        character_name="Yeti",
        character_message=(
            "Look what I found! "
            "This could be perfect for you."
        ),
        relevance_reason=relevance_reason,
        product_image_url=DEMO_PRODUCT_IMAGE_URL,
        tagline="Pro. Beautiful. Powerful.",
        highlights=[
            "Pro camera system",
            "High-performance chip",
            "All-day battery life",
            "Premium display",
        ],
        badge="NEW FIND!",
        metadata={
            "demo": "true",
            "memory_type": MemoryType.PREFERENCE.value,
            "memory_id": memory.memory_id,
            "source": "hackathon_demo",
        },
    )


def render_demo_flash_deal_card(
    card: FlashDealCard | None = None,
) -> str:
    """
    Render a terminal-friendly representation of the Flash
    Deal Card.

    This is useful for the hackathon demonstration before a
    graphical frontend is connected.
    """

    if card is None:
        card = build_demo_flash_deal_card()

    lines = [
        "",
        "=" * 72,
        "EASYCHATBOT â€” FLASH DEAL",
        "=" * 72,
        "",
        f"[ {card.badge} ]",
        "",
        f"{card.character_name}",
        f'"{card.character_message}"',
        "",
        f"{card.product_name}",
        card.tagline,
        "",
        "HIGHLIGHTS",
        "-" * 72,
    ]

    for highlight in card.highlights:
        lines.append(f"  â€¢ {highlight}")

    lines.extend(
        [
            "",
            "PRICE",
            "-" * 72,
            f"  Previous price: "
            f"{_format_price(card.previous_price, card.currency)}",
            f"  Discount: "
            f"{card.display_discount or 'No verified discount'}",
            f"  Current price: "
            f"{_format_price(card.current_price, card.currency)}",
            f"  Savings: "
            f"{card.display_savings or 'Not available'}",
            "",
            "WHY THIS IS RELEVANT TO YOU",
            "-" * 72,
            f"  {card.relevance_reason}",
            "",
            f"AVAILABLE FROM: {card.vendor_name}",
            "",
            "[ GET DEAL ]",
            f"  {card.get_deal_url}",
            "",
            "[ MORE INFORMATION ]",
            f"  {card.more_information_url}",
            "",
            "NOTE: This is deterministic hackathon demo data.",
            "=" * 72,
        ]
    )

    return "\n".join(lines)


def _format_price(
    value: float | None,
    currency: str,
) -> str:
    """
    Format an optional price for terminal output.
    """

    if value is None:
        return "N/A"

    return f"{currency} {value:,.2f}"


if __name__ == "__main__":
    print(
        render_demo_flash_deal_card()
    )

"""
Flash Deal Card presenter.

Pure presentation layer for the EasyChatBot hackathon.

Architecture:

FlashDealCard
      |
      v
FlashDealPresenter
      |
      v
Markdown / terminal output

Responsibilities:
- render an existing FlashDealCard
- present verified product and pricing information
- present character context
- present personal relevance
- expose the existing vendor actions

Non-responsibilities:
- discovery
- memory access
- provider calls
- database access
- commerce execution
- payment processing
- URL generation
- product data generation

The presenter must never mutate the FlashDealCard.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.flash_deal_card import (
    FlashDealCard,
)


class FlashDealPresenter:
    """
    Pure renderer for the user-facing Flash Deal Card.

    The presenter consumes the existing FlashDealCard contract
    and produces deterministic Markdown suitable for the
    hackathon terminal demonstration or a future chat UI.
    """

    def render(
        self,
        card: FlashDealCard,
    ) -> str:
        """
        Render a FlashDealCard as user-facing Markdown.

        No external calls or business decisions are performed.
        """

        sections: list[str] = []

        sections.append(
            f"## {card.character_name}"
        )

        sections.append(
            f"> {card.character_message}"
        )

        sections.append(
            f"### {card.badge}"
        )

        sections.append(
            f"**{card.product_name}**"
        )

        sections.append(
            f"*{card.tagline}*"
        )

        sections.append(
            self._render_highlights(card)
        )

        sections.append(
            self._render_price(card)
        )

        sections.append(
            "### WHY THIS IS RELEVANT TO YOU"
        )

        sections.append(
            card.relevance_reason
        )

        sections.append(
            "### AVAILABLE FROM"
        )

        sections.append(
            card.vendor_name
        )

        sections.append(
            self._render_actions(card)
        )

        return "\n\n".join(sections)

    @staticmethod
    def _render_highlights(
        card: FlashDealCard,
    ) -> str:
        """Render the product highlights."""

        lines = ["### HIGHLIGHTS"]

        lines.extend(
            f"- {highlight}"
            for highlight in card.highlights
        )

        return "\n".join(lines)

    @staticmethod
    def _render_price(
        card: FlashDealCard,
    ) -> str:
        """Render verified pricing information."""

        lines = ["### PRICE"]

        if card.previous_price is not None:
            lines.append(
                "Previous price: "
                f"{card.currency} "
                f"{card.previous_price:.2f}"
            )

        lines.append(
            "Current price: "
            f"{card.currency} "
            f"{card.current_price:.2f}"
        )

        if card.display_discount is not None:
            lines.append(
                f"Discount: {card.display_discount}"
            )

        if card.display_savings is not None:
            lines.append(
                f"Savings: {card.display_savings}"
            )

        return "\n".join(lines)

    @staticmethod
    def _render_actions(
        card: FlashDealCard,
    ) -> str:
        """
        Render vendor action destinations.

        The presenter only exposes URLs already present on the
        FlashDealCard. It does not create or transform them.
        """

        return "\n".join(
            [
                "### ACTIONS",
                "",
                f"[GET DEAL]({card.get_deal_url})",
                "",
                (
                    "[MORE INFORMATION]"
                    f"({card.more_information_url})"
                ),
            ]
        )

----------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\src\ai_runtime\discovery\flash_idea.py
----------------------------------------------------------------------

"""
Hackathon personalised Flash Idea builder.

Combines:
- a discovered DealCard
- user memory
- character identity

into a character-led discovery message.

This module does not:
- process payments
- call vendors
- call external APIs
- write memory
- execute agents
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.models import (
    DealCard,
    FlashIdea,
)

from src.ai_runtime.memory.models import (
    MemoryEntry,
    MemoryType,
)


class FlashIdeaBuilder:
    """
    Builds a personalised FlashIdea from a deal
    and explicit user memories.
    """

    def build(
        self,
        *,
        character_id: str,
        deal: DealCard,
        memories: list[MemoryEntry],
    ) -> FlashIdea:
        """
        Build a personalised flash idea.

        Preference memories are used to explain why
        the discovery may be relevant to the user.
        """

        preferences = [
            memory
            for memory in memories
            if memory.memory_type
            == MemoryType.PREFERENCE
        ]

        if preferences:
            preference = preferences[0].content

            message = (
                f"I remembered that {preference.lower()} "
                f"I found this {deal.product_name} "
                f"at {deal.price:.2f} {deal.currency} "
                f"from {deal.vendor}."
            )
        else:
            message = (
                f"I found something you might like: "
                f"{deal.product_name} "
                f"at {deal.price:.2f} {deal.currency} "
                f"from {deal.vendor}."
            )

        if deal.discount_percentage is not None:
            message += (
                f" It's currently "
                f"{deal.discount_percentage:.0f}% off."
            )

        return FlashIdea(
            character_id=character_id,
            deal_id=deal.deal_id,
            title="Flash idea",
            message=message,
            metadata={
                "vendor": deal.vendor,
                "commerce_url": (
                    deal.commerce_link.click_url
                ),
            },
        )

----------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\src\ai_runtime\discovery\models.py
----------------------------------------------------------------------

"""
Hackathon Discovery domain models.

These models represent the small discovery layer required
for the hackathon experience.

The hackathon is NOT a marketplace.

EasyChatBot:
    discovers products
    evaluates opportunities
    presents character-led flash ideas
    provides an outbound vendor link

The external vendor:
    owns the product page
    owns checkout
    owns payment
    fulfils the purchase

Architecture:

Discovery Intelligence
        |
        v
     DealCard
        |
        +--> CommerceLink
        |
        v
    FlashIdea
        |
        v
     Character
        |
        v
       User
        |
        v
 External Vendor
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from enum import Enum
from uuid import uuid4


class CommerceLinkType(str, Enum):
    """
    Type of outbound commerce link.
    """

    DIRECT = "direct"

    AFFILIATE = "affiliate"


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CommerceLink:
    """
    Outbound link to the external vendor.

    EasyChatBot does not process the transaction.

    The destination remains the vendor's website.
    """

    vendor: str

    destination_url: str

    link_type: CommerceLinkType = (
        CommerceLinkType.DIRECT
    )

    tracking_url: str | None = None

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        """
        Validate the commerce link.
        """

        if not self.vendor.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Commerce vendor cannot be empty."
            )

        if not self.destination_url.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Commerce destination URL "
                "cannot be empty."
            )

        if not (
            self.destination_url.startswith(
                "https://"
            )
            or self.destination_url.startswith(
                "http://"
            )
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "Commerce destination URL must "
                "use HTTP or HTTPS."
            )

        if self.tracking_url is not None:
            if not self.tracking_url.strip():
                raise ValueError(
                    "Commerce tracking URL cannot "
                    "be empty when provided."
                )

            if not (
                self.tracking_url.startswith(
                    "https://"
                )
                or self.tracking_url.startswith(
                    "http://"
                )
            ):
                raise ValueError(
                    "Commerce tracking URL must "
                    "use HTTP or HTTPS."
                )

    @property
    def click_url(self) -> str:
        """
        Return the URL that should be opened by the user.

        A tracking URL takes precedence when one is
        available.
        """

        return (
            self.tracking_url
            if self.tracking_url is not None
            else self.destination_url
        )


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DealCard:
    """
    Compact representation of a discovered product opportunity.

    This is intentionally not a product catalogue entity.

    It represents a single discovery opportunity surfaced
    to a user.
    """

    product_name: str

    vendor: str

    price: float

    currency: str

    commerce_link: CommerceLink

    deal_id: str = field(
        default_factory=lambda: str(uuid4())
    )

    previous_price: float | None = None

    discount_percentage: float | None = None

    source: str | None = None

    description: str | None = None

    discovered_at: datetime = field(
        default_factory=lambda: datetime.now(
            timezone.utc
        )
    )

    metadata: dict[str, str] = field(
        default_factory=dict
    )

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        """
        Validate the DealCard.
        """

        if not self.product_name.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Deal product name cannot be empty."
            )

        if not self.vendor.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Deal vendor cannot be empty."
            )

        if self.price < 0:
            raise ValueError(
                "Deal price cannot be negative."
            )

        if not self.currency.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Deal currency cannot be empty."
            )

        if (
            self.previous_price is not None
            and self.previous_price < 0
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "Deal previous price cannot "
                "be negative."
            )

        if (
            self.discount_percentage is not None
            and not 0 <= self.discount_percentage <= 100
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "Deal discount percentage must "
                "be between 0 and 100."
            )

        if (
            self.commerce_link.vendor
            != self.vendor
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "Deal vendor must match the "
                "commerce link vendor."
            )


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class FlashIdea:
    """
    Character-led presentation of a discovered deal.

    A FlashIdea is the user-facing discovery message.

    It is not a transaction.
    """

    character_id: str

    deal_id: str

    message: str

    title: str = "Flash idea"

    idea_id: str = field(
        default_factory=lambda: str(uuid4())
    )

    created_at: datetime = field(
        default_factory=lambda: datetime.now(
            timezone.utc
        )
    )

    metadata: dict[str, str] = field(
        default_factory=dict
    )

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        """
        Validate the FlashIdea.
        """

        if not self.character_id.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "FlashIdea character_id "
                "cannot be empty."
            )

        if not self.deal_id.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "FlashIdea deal_id "
                "cannot be empty."
            )

        if not self.message.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "FlashIdea message "
                "cannot be empty."
            )

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\src\ai_runtime\discovery\notification.py

"""
Hackathon Discovery Notification.

Defines the lightweight user notification produced from a
personalised FlashDealCard.

Architecture:

DiscoveryService
      |
      v
FlashDealCard
      |
      v
DiscoveryNotification
      |
      +--> Telegram
      +--> Web UI
      +--> Future mobile UI

The notification layer is presentation/delivery oriented.

It does not:
- search for products
- access providers
- decide recommendations
- access memory directly
- process payments
- execute purchases
- build a shopping dashboard

The FlashDealCard remains the source of truth for the
discovered offer.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.flash_deal_card import (
    FlashDealCard,
)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DiscoveryNotification:
    """
    Lightweight character-driven notification.

    Designed for a single discovery event rather than a
    persistent shopping dashboard.
    """

    notification_id: str
    user_id: str
    character_id: str
    character_name: str
    title: str
    message: str
    deal: FlashDealCard

    @classmethod
    def from_flash_deal(
        cls,
        *,
        notification_id: str,
        user_id: str,
        deal: FlashDealCard,
    ) -> "DiscoveryNotification":
        """
        Create a notification from an existing FlashDealCard.

        No discovery or commerce logic occurs here.
        """

        if not notification_id.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Notification notification_id cannot be empty."
            )

        if not user_id.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Notification user_id cannot be empty."
            )

        return cls(
            notification_id=notification_id,
            user_id=user_id,
            character_id=deal.character_id,
            character_name=deal.character_name,
            title=(
                f"{deal.character_name} found something for you"
            ),
            message=deal.character_message,
            deal=deal,
        )

    @property
    def product_name(self) -> str:
        """Return the discovered product name."""

        return self.deal.product_name

    @property
    def relevance_reason(self) -> str:
        """Return the personalisation explanation."""

        return self.deal.relevance_reason

    @property
    def get_deal_url(self) -> str:
        """Return the controlled commerce destination."""

        return self.deal.get_deal_url

    @property
    def more_information_url(self) -> str:
        """Return the vendor information destination."""

        return self.deal.more_information_url

    def render(self) -> str:
        """
        Render a compact notification suitable for a chat UI.

        The full FlashDealCard remains available for the richer
        product presentation.
        """

        discount = (
            self.deal.display_discount
            or "NEW FIND"
        )

        return "\n".join(
            [
                f"{self.character_name}:",
                self.message,
                "",
                f"{discount} â€” {self.product_name}",
                f"{self.deal.currency} "
                f"{self.deal.current_price:.2f}",
                "",
                "Why this may interest you:",
                self.relevance_reason,
            ]
        )

----------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\src\ai_runtime\discovery\service.py
----------------------------------------------------------------------

"""
Hackathon Discovery Service.

This module provides the application-level orchestration boundary
for personalised product discovery.

Architecture:

Discovery inputs
    |
    +--> DealCard
    |
    +--> Memory context
    |
    +--> Character context
    |
    v
DiscoveryService
    |
    v
FlashDealCard

Responsibilities:
- orchestrate existing discovery components
- apply explicit memory context
- apply explicit character context
- create the user-facing FlashDealCard
- preserve domain data and identifiers

Non-responsibilities:
- provider SDK calls
- web searching
- memory persistence
- payment processing
- vendor APIs
- autonomous purchasing
- character business logic

The service deliberately receives already-discovered DealCard data.
Provider integration remains in the provider/agent layers.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.flash_deal_card import (
    FlashDealCard,
)

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.models import (
    DealCard,
)

from src.ai_runtime.memory.models import (
    MemoryEntry,
    MemoryType,
)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CharacterContext:
    """
    Explicit character presentation context.

    The character is a presentation/personality concern.
    It does not perform discovery or commerce operations.
    """

    character_id: str
    character_name: str
    message: str

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if not self.character_id.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Character context character_id cannot be empty."
            )

        if not self.character_name.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Character context character_name cannot be empty."
            )

        if not self.message.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Character context message cannot be empty."
            )


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DiscoveryRequest:
    """
    Application-level request for personalised discovery.

    The request contains already-discovered product information
    and explicit user context.

    No external search is performed by DiscoveryService.
    """

    user_id: str
    deal: DealCard
    character: CharacterContext
    product_image_url: str
    tagline: str
    highlights: tuple[str, ...]

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if not self.user_id.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Discovery request user_id cannot be empty."
            )

        if not self.product_image_url.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Discovery request product_image_url "
                "cannot be empty."
            )

        if not self.tagline.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Discovery request tagline cannot be empty."
            )

        if not self.highlights:
            raise ValueError(
                "Discovery request must contain at least "
                "one highlight."
            )


class DiscoveryService:
    """
    Orchestrates personalised discovery.

    This service intentionally contains no provider logic and no
    persistence logic. It composes the existing DealCard,
    MemoryEntry and FlashDealCard contracts.
    """

    def create_flash_deal(
        self,
        request: DiscoveryRequest,
        memories: list[MemoryEntry] | None = None,
    ) -> FlashDealCard:
        """
        Create a personalised FlashDealCard.

        Memory is read-only input to this operation.

        Preference memory is used to generate the personal
        relevance explanation. Other memory types are not
        automatically treated as preferences.
        """

        memory_context = memories or []

        relevance_reason = self._build_relevance_reason(
            memory_context
        )

        return FlashDealCard.from_deal(
            deal=request.deal,
            character_id=request.character.character_id,
            character_name=request.character.character_name,
            character_message=request.character.message,
            relevance_reason=relevance_reason,
            product_image_url=request.product_image_url,
            tagline=request.tagline,
            highlights=request.highlights,
            metadata={
                "user_id": request.user_id,
                "discovery_service": "hackathon",
            },
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _build_relevance_reason(
        memories: list[MemoryEntry],
    ) -> str:
        """
        Build a deterministic relevance explanation.

        Only explicit preference memories are used.

        This is deliberately deterministic for the hackathon;
        an LLM-based personalisation layer can be introduced later
        behind an explicit interface.
        """

        preferences = [
            memory
            for memory in memories
            if memory.memory_type
            == MemoryType.PREFERENCE
        ]

        if not preferences:
            return (
                "This discovery may be relevant to your "
                "interests."
            )

        preference = preferences[0].content.strip()

        return (
            "This may be relevant because you "
            f"{preference.rstrip('.') }."
        )

----------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\src\ai_runtime\memory\models.py
----------------------------------------------------------------------

"""
Hackathon Memory contracts.

This module defines the minimal persistent memory model
required by the hackathon discovery flow.

This is intentionally lightweight.

It is NOT the production EasyChatBot memory architecture.

Hackathon responsibilities:

- represent user memories
- distinguish memory categories
- provide stable identifiers
- preserve timestamps
- support future persistence

Out of scope:

- vector embeddings
- semantic retrieval
- LLM-generated memory extraction
- autonomous memory writes
- purchase transactions
- production learner-memory architecture
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from enum import Enum
from uuid import uuid4


class MemoryType(str, Enum):
    """
    Supported hackathon memory categories.
    """

    PREFERENCE = "preference"

    DISCOVERY_CONTEXT = "discovery_context"

    SAVED_ITEM = "saved_item"


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class MemoryEntry:
    """
    Persistent user memory entry.

    Memory is explicit application state.

    Agents must not directly mutate this object or
    the underlying persistence layer.
    """

    user_id: str

    memory_type: MemoryType

    content: str

    memory_id: str = field(
        default_factory=lambda: str(uuid4())
    )

    created_at: datetime = field(
        default_factory=lambda: datetime.now(
            timezone.utc
        )
    )

    updated_at: datetime = field(
        default_factory=lambda: datetime.now(
            timezone.utc
        )
    )

    metadata: dict[str, str] = field(
        default_factory=dict
    )

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        """
        Validate the memory contract.
        """

        if not self.user_id.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Memory user_id cannot be empty."
            )

        if not self.content.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Memory content cannot be empty."
            )

    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, object]:
        """
        Convert the memory entry into a persistence-safe
        representation.
        """

        return {
            "memory_id": self.memory_id,
            "user_id": self.user_id,
            "memory_type": self.memory_type.value,
            "content": self.content,
            "created_at": self.created_at.isoformat(),
            "updated_at": self.updated_at.isoformat(),
            "metadata": dict(self.metadata),
        }

----------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\src\ai_runtime\memory\service.py
----------------------------------------------------------------------

"""
Hackathon Memory Service.

Application-facing service for the lightweight hackathon
memory capability.

Architecture:

Character / Discovery
        |
        v
MemoryService
        |
        v
MemoryStore
        |
        v
SQLite

The service owns memory application behaviour.

It does not:
- access SQLite directly
- call providers
- call agents
- generate memories autonomously
- implement shopping logic
- implement recommendation logic
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from src.ai_runtime.memory.models import (
    MemoryEntry,
    MemoryType,
)

from src.ai_runtime.memory.store import (
    MemoryStore,
)


class MemoryService:
    """
    Application service for hackathon memory.

    Memory writes are explicit.

    This is intentional: an agent should not silently
    create persistent user state merely because it generated
    a response.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        store: MemoryStore,
    ) -> None:
        self._store = store

    def remember(
        self,
        *,
        user_id: str,
        memory_type: MemoryType,
        content: str,
        metadata: dict[str, str] | None = None,
    ) -> MemoryEntry:
        """
        Create and persist a new memory entry.
        """

        memory = MemoryEntry(
            user_id=user_id,
            memory_type=memory_type,
            content=content,
            metadata=(
                dict(metadata)
                if metadata is not None
                else {}
            ),
        )

        return self._store.save(
            memory
        )

    def get(
        self,
        memory_id: str,
    ) -> MemoryEntry | None:
        """
        Retrieve a memory by identifier.
        """

        return self._store.get(
            memory_id
        )

    def list_for_user(
        self,
        *,
        user_id: str,
        memory_type: MemoryType | None = None,
    ) -> list[MemoryEntry]:
        """
        Retrieve memories for a user.

        Optionally restrict results to one memory type.
        """

        return self._store.list_for_user(
            user_id=user_id,
            memory_type=memory_type,
        )

    def preferences(
        self,
        *,
        user_id: str,
    ) -> list[MemoryEntry]:
        """
        Retrieve user preference memories.
        """

        return self.list_for_user(
            user_id=user_id,
            memory_type=MemoryType.PREFERENCE,
        )

    def discovery_context(
        self,
        *,
        user_id: str,
    ) -> list[MemoryEntry]:
        """
        Retrieve current discovery context.
        """

        return self.list_for_user(
            user_id=user_id,
            memory_type=MemoryType.DISCOVERY_CONTEXT,
        )

    def saved_items(
        self,
        *,
        user_id: str,
    ) -> list[MemoryEntry]:
        """
        Retrieve saved discovery items.
        """

        return self.list_for_user(
            user_id=user_id,
            memory_type=MemoryType.SAVED_ITEM,
        )

    def delete(
        self,
        memory_id: str,
    ) -> bool:
        """
        Delete a memory.
        """

        return self._store.delete(
            memory_id
        )

----------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\src\ai_runtime\memory\store.py
----------------------------------------------------------------------

"""
Hackathon Memory persistence.

This module provides the minimal persistent Memory Store
required by the hackathon.

Architecture:

MemoryService
      |
      v
MemoryStore
      |
      v
SQLite

The store is deliberately small.

It is responsible only for persistence and retrieval of
MemoryEntry objects.

It does not:
- decide what should be remembered
- call an LLM
- perform semantic retrieval
- execute agents
- perform governance decisions
- manage purchases
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sqlite3
from pathlib import Path

from src.ai_runtime.memory.models import (
    MemoryEntry,
    MemoryType,
)


class MemoryStore:
    """
    SQLite-backed persistence for hackathon memories.

    The database schema is created automatically when the
    store is initialised.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        database_path: str | Path,
    ) -> None:
        self._database_path = str(database_path)

        self._initialise_database()

    def _connect(self) -> sqlite3.Connection:
        """
        Create a SQLite connection.
        """

        connection = sqlite3.connect(
            self._database_path
        )

        connection.row_factory = sqlite3.Row

        return connection

    def _initialise_database(self) -> None:
        """
        Create the Memory table if it does not exist.
        """

        with self._connect() as connection:
            connection.execute(
                """
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS memories (
                    memory_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                    user_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                    memory_type TEXT NOT NULL,
                    content TEXT NOT NULL,
                    created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
                    updated_at TEXT NOT NULL,
                    metadata_json TEXT NOT NULL
                )
                """
            )

            connection.execute(
                """
                CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS
                idx_memories_user_id
                ON memories(user_id)
                """
            )

            connection.execute(
                """
                CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS
                idx_memories_user_type
                ON memories(user_id, memory_type)
                """
            )

    def save(
        self,
        memory: MemoryEntry,
    ) -> MemoryEntry:
        """
        Persist a MemoryEntry.

        Existing entries with the same memory_id are
        replaced.
        """

        with self._connect() as connection:
            connection.execute(
                """
                INSERT OR REPLACE INTO memories (
                    memory_id,
                    user_id,
                    memory_type,
                    content,
                    created_at,
                    updated_at,
                    metadata_json
                )
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
                """,
                (
                    memory.memory_id,
                    memory.user_id,
                    memory.memory_type.value,
                    memory.content,
                    memory.created_at.isoformat(),
                    memory.updated_at.isoformat(),
                    json.dumps(
                        memory.metadata,
                        ensure_ascii=False,
                    ),
                ),
            )

        return memory

    def get(
        self,
        memory_id: str,
    ) -> MemoryEntry | None:
        """
        Retrieve a single memory by identifier.
        """

        with self._connect() as connection:
            row = connection.execute(
                """
                SELECT
                    memory_id,
                    user_id,
                    memory_type,
                    content,
                    created_at,
                    updated_at,
                    metadata_json
                FROM memories
                WHERE memory_id = ?
                """,
                (memory_id,),
            ).fetchone()

        if row is None:
            return None

        return self._row_to_memory(row)

    def list_for_user(
        self,
        user_id: str,
        memory_type: MemoryType | None = None,
    ) -> list[MemoryEntry]:
        """
        Retrieve memories belonging to a user.

        Results are returned newest first.
        """

        if not user_id.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Memory user_id cannot be empty."
            )

        with self._connect() as connection:

            if memory_type is None:
                rows = connection.execute(
                    """
                    SELECT
                        memory_id,
                        user_id,
                        memory_type,
                        content,
                        created_at,
                        updated_at,
                        metadata_json
                    FROM memories
                    WHERE user_id = ?
                    ORDER BY updated_at DESC
                    """,
                    (user_id,),
                ).fetchall()

            else:
                rows = connection.execute(
                    """
                    SELECT
                        memory_id,
                        user_id,
                        memory_type,
                        content,
                        created_at,
                        updated_at,
                        metadata_json
                    FROM memories
                    WHERE user_id = ?
                      AND memory_type = ?
                    ORDER BY updated_at DESC
                    """,
                    (
                        user_id,
                        memory_type.value,
                    ),
                ).fetchall()

        return [
            self._row_to_memory(row)
            for row in rows
        ]

    def delete(
        self,
        memory_id: str,
    ) -> bool:
        """
        Delete a memory.

        Returns True when a memory was deleted.
        """

        with self._connect() as connection:
            cursor = connection.execute(
                """
                DELETE FROM memories
                WHERE memory_id = ?
                """,
                (memory_id,),
            )

        return cursor.rowcount > 0

    @staticmethod
    def _row_to_memory(
        row: sqlite3.Row,
    ) -> MemoryEntry:
        """
        Convert a SQLite row into a MemoryEntry.
        """

        return MemoryEntry(
            memory_id=row["memory_id"],
            user_id=row["user_id"],
            memory_type=MemoryType(
                row["memory_type"]
            ),
            content=row["content"],
            created_at=(
                __import__("datetime")
                .datetime.fromisoformat(
                    row["created_at"]
                )
            ),
            updated_at=(
                __import__("datetime")
                .datetime.fromisoformat(
                    row["updated_at"]
                )
            ),
            metadata=json.loads(
                row["metadata_json"]
            ),
        )

"""
Live Intelligence Agent Handler.

Responsible for executing the Live Intelligence Agent workflow.

Architecture:

AI Runtime
    |
    v
Live Intelligence Agent Handler
    |
    +--> Search Provider Interface
    |
    +--> Knowledge Processing
    |
    v
Structured Intelligence Output

Responsibilities:
- Validate agent input
- Call approved provider interfaces
- Normalise research output
- Return explicit structured results

Non-responsibilities:
- Provider SDK management
- Direct external API handling
- Autonomous actions
- Configuration changes
"""

from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import List, Protocol

from src.providers.search_provider.interface import (
    SearchProvider,
    SearchResult,
)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class LiveIntelligenceRequest:
    """
    Input contract for Live Intelligence Agent.
    """

    topic: str
    language: str = "French"
    learner_level: str = "B2"


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class LiveIntelligenceResponse:
    """
    Output contract from Live Intelligence Agent.
    """

    topic: str
    summary: str
    sources: List[SearchResult]
    key_information: List[str]


class IntelligenceProcessor(Protocol):
    """
    Contract for future LLM-based processing.

    Keeps the agent independent from
    any specific model/provider.
    """

    async def summarise(
        self,
        results: List[SearchResult],
        language: str,
        learner_level: str,
    ) -> tuple[str, List[str]]:
        ...


class LiveIntelligenceAgent:
    """
    Live Intelligence Agent implementation.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        search_provider: SearchProvider,
        processor: IntelligenceProcessor,
    ) -> None:
        self._search_provider = search_provider
        self._processor = processor

    async def execute(
        self,
        request: LiveIntelligenceRequest,
    ) -> LiveIntelligenceResponse:
        """
        Execute research workflow.

        Flow:

        Request
          |
          v
        Search Provider
          |
          v
        Intelligence Processor
          |
          v
        Structured Response
        """

        self._validate_request(request)

        search_results = await self._search_provider.search(
            query=request.topic,
            max_results=5,
        )

        summary, key_information = await self._processor.summarise(
            results=search_results,
            language=request.language,
            learner_level=request.learner_level,
        )

        return LiveIntelligenceResponse(
            topic=request.topic,
            summary=summary,
            sources=search_results,
            key_information=key_information,
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _validate_request(
        request: LiveIntelligenceRequest,
    ) -> None:
        """
        Validate required input.
        """

        if not request.topic.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Topic cannot be empty"
            )

"""
Search Provider Interface

Defines the provider-independent contract for external search capabilities.

Architecture:
Agent -> Provider Interface -> Provider Adapter

The agent must never depend directly on a vendor SDK.
"""

from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import List


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SearchResult:
    """
    Normalised search result returned by any provider.
    """

    title: str
    url: str
    content: str
    source: str


class SearchProvider(ABC):
    """
    Abstract search provider contract.

    Implementations:
    - ParallelSearchProvider
    - Future providers
    """

    @abstractmethod
    async def search(
        self,
        query: str,
        max_results: int = 5,
    ) -> List[SearchResult]:
        """
        Execute a search query.

        Args:
            query:
                Search query.

            max_results:
                Maximum number of results.

        Returns:
            Normalised search results.
        """
        raise NotImplementedError

----------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\src\providers\search_provider\parallel_adapter.py
----------------------------------------------------------------------

"""
Parallel Search Provider Adapter.

Bridges the provider-independent SearchProvider contract
to the existing EasyChatBot ParallelClientWrapper.

Architecture:

Live Intelligence Agent
        |
        v
SearchProvider Interface
        |
        v
ParallelSearchProvider
        |
        v
Existing ParallelClientWrapper
        |
        v
Parallel Search API

The official Parallel SDK remains isolated inside:

    backend/providers/parallel/parallel_client.py

This adapter must not import or instantiate the Parallel SDK
directly.
"""

from typing import Any

from backend.providers.parallel.parallel_client import (
    ParallelClientWrapper,
)

from src.providers.search_provider.interface import (
    SearchProvider,
    SearchResult,
)


class ParallelSearchProvider(SearchProvider):
    """
    SearchProvider implementation backed by the existing
    ParallelClientWrapper.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        api_key: str | None = None,
        client: ParallelClientWrapper | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        """
        Initialise the provider.

        Args:
            api_key:
                Optional Parallel API key.

                If omitted, the existing ParallelClientWrapper
                loads PARALLEL_API_KEY from the environment/.env.

            client:
                Optional injected client for testing.
        """

        self._client = client or ParallelClientWrapper(
            api_key=api_key
        )

    async def search(
        self,
        query: str,
        max_results: int = 5,
    ) -> list[SearchResult]:
        """
        Execute a Parallel search and normalise its results.

        Args:
            query:
                Natural-language research objective.

            max_results:
                Maximum number of results returned.

        Returns:
            Provider-independent SearchResult objects.
        """

        if not query.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Search query cannot be empty"
            )

        if max_results <= 0:
            raise ValueError(
                "max_results must be greater than zero"
            )

        response = await self._client.search(
            query=query
        )

        raw_results = self._extract_results(
            response
        )

        normalised_results: list[SearchResult] = []

        for raw_result in raw_results[:max_results]:
            result = self._normalise_result(
                raw_result
            )

            if result is not None:
                normalised_results.append(result)

        return normalised_results

    @staticmethod
    def _extract_results(
        response: Any,
    ) -> list[Any]:
        """
        Extract the actual result collection from the
        ParallelClientWrapper response.

        The existing wrapper returns:

            {
                "query": "...",
                "search_queries": [...],
                "results": <normalised SDK response>
            }

        The SDK response may itself contain:

            {
                "results": [...]
            }

        Therefore this method handles both levels of wrapping.
        """

        if not isinstance(response, dict):
            raise TypeError(
                "Parallel client response must be a dictionary"
            )

        raw_results = response.get(
            "results",
            []
        )

        # The SDK response itself may contain a nested
        # "results" collection.
        if isinstance(raw_results, dict):
            nested_results = raw_results.get(
                "results"
            )

            if isinstance(nested_results, list):
                return nested_results

            # Some SDK versions may expose the result
            # collection under another common field.
            nested_data = raw_results.get(
                "data"
            )

            if isinstance(nested_data, list):
                return nested_data

        if isinstance(raw_results, list):
            return raw_results

        # If the SDK returned an object that was not converted
        # to a dictionary, try to inspect its result attribute.
        nested_results = getattr(
            raw_results,
            "results",
            None,
        )

        if isinstance(nested_results, list):
            return nested_results

        raise TypeError(
            "Parallel search response does not contain "
            "a supported results collection"
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _normalise_result(
        raw_result: Any,
    ) -> SearchResult | None:
        """
        Convert one Parallel result into SearchResult.

        Parallel results may arrive either as dictionaries
        or SDK/Pydantic objects.
        """

        if isinstance(raw_result, dict):
            title = str(
                raw_result.get(
                    "title",
                    ""
                )
            ).strip()

            url = str(
                raw_result.get(
                    "url",
                    ""
                )
            ).strip()

            excerpts = raw_result.get(
                "excerpts",
                []
            )

            # Support providers/SDK versions that use
            # "snippet" or "content" instead.
            if not excerpts:
                excerpts = raw_result.get(
                    "snippet",
                    raw_result.get(
                        "content",
                        []
                    ),
                )

        else:
            title = str(
                getattr(
                    raw_result,
                    "title",
                    ""
                )
            ).strip()

            url = str(
                getattr(
                    raw_result,
                    "url",
                    ""
                )
            ).strip()

            excerpts = getattr(
                raw_result,
                "excerpts",
                None,
            )

            if not excerpts:
                excerpts = getattr(
                    raw_result,
                    "snippet",
                    getattr(
                        raw_result,
                        "content",
                        [],
                    ),
                )

        if not title or not url:
            return None

        content = ParallelSearchProvider._normalise_excerpts(
            excerpts
        )

        return SearchResult(
            title=title,
            url=url,
            content=content,
            source="parallel",
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _normalise_excerpts(
        excerpts: Any,
    ) -> str:
        """
        Convert Parallel excerpts/content into a single
        application-level content string.
        """

        if excerpts is None:
            return ""

        if isinstance(excerpts, str):
            return excerpts.strip()

        if isinstance(excerpts, list):
            return "\n\n".join(
                str(excerpt).strip()
                for excerpt in excerpts
                if str(excerpt).strip()
            )

        return str(excerpts).strip()

import pytest

from backend.agents.conversation_agent import (
    ConversationAgent,
)

from backend.characters.character_registry import (
    CharacterRegistry,
)

from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import (
    ExecutionContext,
)


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_clara_uses_clara_profile():

    registry = CharacterRegistry()

    clara = registry.get(
        "clara"
    )

    agent = ConversationAgent(
        clara
    )

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request=(
            "Explain artificial intelligence"
        ),
    )

    result = await agent.execute(
        context
    )

    assert result.success is True

    assert result.output["character"] == (
        "Clara"
    )

    profile = result.output[
        "character_profile"
    ]

    assert profile["character_id"] == (
        "clara"
    )

    assert profile["name"] == (
        "Clara"
    )

    assert profile["description"] == (
        "A professional learning companion "
        "focused on technology and career topics."
    )

    assert profile["personality"] == (
        "Analytical, structured and supportive."
    )

    assert profile["learning_style"] == (
        "Structured and practical learning."
    )

from backend.characters.character_registry import (
    CharacterRegistry,

def test_character_registry_returns_yeti():

    registry = CharacterRegistry()

    character = registry.get("yeti")

    assert character.character_id == "yeti"

    assert character.name == "Yeti"


def test_character_registry_returns_clara():

    registry = CharacterRegistry()

    character = registry.get("clara")

    assert character.character_id == "clara"

    assert character.name == "Clara"

    character = registry.get("CLARA")

    assert character.character_id == "clara"

import pytest

from backend.agents.agent_result import (
    AgentResult,
)

from backend.characters.character_profile import (
    CharacterProfile,
)

from backend.intelligence.intelligence_context import (
    IntelligenceContext,
    IntelligenceSource,
)

from backend.memory.memory_service import (
    MemoryService,
)

from backend.memory.memory_store import (
    MemoryStore,
)

from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import (
    ExecutionContext,
)

from backend.services.companion_service import (
    CompanionService,
)


class FakeConversationAgent:

    def __init__(self):
        self.received_context = None

    async def execute(
        self,
        context,
    ):
        self.received_context = context

        return AgentResult(
            success=True,
            agent_id="conversation_agent",
            output={
                "character": "Yeti",
                "personality": (
                    "Curious and encouraging"
                ),
                "learning_style": (
                    "Conversation based learning"
                ),
                "intelligence_context": (
                    context.intelligence_context
                ),
            },
        )


class FakeResearchAgent:

    async def execute(
        self,
        context,
    ):
        return AgentResult(
            success=True,
            agent_id="research_agent",
            output=IntelligenceContext(
                query=context.request,
                sources=(
                    IntelligenceSource(
                        title=(
                            "European AI Regulation"
                        ),
                        url=(
                            "https://example.com/ai"
                        ),
                        source="test",
                        content=(
                            "Current regulatory "
                            "information."
                        ),
                    ),
                ),
                summary=(
                    "Recent European AI "
                    "regulatory developments."
                ),
            ),
        )


class FakeQualityAgent:

    async def execute(
        self,
        context,
    ):
        return AgentResult(
            success=True,
            agent_id="quality_agent",
            output={
                "quality_score": 1.0,
                "passed": True,
                "findings": [],
                "recommendations": [],
            },
        )


def create_character():
    return CharacterProfile(
        character_id="yeti",
        name="Yeti",
        description=(
            "Friendly AI learning companion"
        ),
        personality=(
            "Curious and encouraging"
        ),
        learning_style=(
            "Conversation based learning"
        ),
    )


def create_memory_service(
    tmp_path,
):
    return MemoryService(
        store=MemoryStore(
            database_path=(
                tmp_path / "memory.db"
            )
        )
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_companion_passes_research_to_conversation(
    tmp_path,
):

    conversation_agent = (
        FakeConversationAgent()
    )

    service = CompanionService(
        character=create_character(),
        conversation_agent=(
            conversation_agent
        ),
        research_agent=(
            FakeResearchAgent()
        ),
        quality_agent=(
            FakeQualityAgent()
        ),
        memory_service=(
            create_memory_service(
                tmp_path
            )
        ),
    )

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request=(
            "Explain the latest "
            "European AI regulation"
        ),
    )

    result = await service.interact(
        context,
        use_research=True,
    )

    assert result["character"] == (
        "Yeti"
    )

    assert (
        result["research"]
        is not None
    )

    assert isinstance(
        result["research"],
        IntelligenceContext,
    )

    received_context = (
        conversation_agent.received_context
    )

    assert (
        received_context is not None
    )

    assert (
        received_context
        is not context
    )

    assert (
        received_context.intelligence_context
        is not None
    )

    assert (
        received_context.intelligence_context.query
        == context.request
    )

    assert (
        received_context.intelligence_context.source_count
        == 1
    )

    assert (
        result["conversation"][
            "intelligence_context"
        ]
        is received_context.intelligence_context
    )

    assert (
        context.intelligence_context
        is None
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_companion_without_research_preserves_normal_conversation(
    tmp_path,
):

    conversation_agent = (
        FakeConversationAgent()
    )

    service = CompanionService(
        character=create_character(),
        conversation_agent=(
            conversation_agent
        ),
        research_agent=(
            FakeResearchAgent()
        ),
        quality_agent=(
            FakeQualityAgent()
        ),
        memory_service=(
            create_memory_service(
                tmp_path
            )
        ),
    )

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request=(
            "Explain artificial intelligence"
        ),
    )

    result = await service.interact(
        context,
        use_research=False,
    )

    assert result["research"] is None

    assert (
        conversation_agent.received_context
        is context
    )

    assert (
        conversation_agent.received_context
        .intelligence_context
        is None
    )

    assert (
        result["conversation"][
            "intelligence_context"
        ]
        is None
    )

import pytest

from backend.characters.character_profile import (
    CharacterProfile,
)

from backend.memory.memory_service import (
    MemoryService,
)

from backend.memory.memory_store import (
    MemoryStore,
)

from backend.services.companion_service import (
    CompanionService,
)

from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import (
    ExecutionContext,
)


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_companion_stores_memory(
    tmp_path,
):

    character = CharacterProfile(
        character_id="yeti",
        name="Yeti",
        description="Learning companion",
        personality="Friendly",
        learning_style="Conversation",
    )

    memory_service = MemoryService(
        store=MemoryStore(
            database_path=(
                tmp_path / "memory.db"
            )
        )
    )

    service = CompanionService(
        character=character,
        memory_service=memory_service,
    )

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request=(
            "I want to improve French speaking"
        ),
    )

    result = await service.interact(
        context
    )

    assert result["character"] == (
        "Yeti"
    )

    memories = (
        service.memory_service.recall(
            "demo-user"
        )
    )

    assert len(memories) == 1

    assert memories[0].content == (
        "I want to improve French speaking"
    )

    assert memories[0].category == (
        "conversation"
    )

import pytest

from backend.agents.conversation_agent import (
    ConversationAgent,
)

from backend.characters.character_profile import (
    CharacterProfile,
)

from backend.conversation.conversation_request import (
    ConversationRequest,
)

from backend.intelligence.intelligence_context import (
    IntelligenceContext,
    IntelligenceSource,
)

from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import (
    ExecutionContext,
)


def create_character():
    return CharacterProfile(
        character_id="yeti",
        name="Yeti",
        description=(
            "Friendly AI learning companion"
        ),
        personality=(
            "Curious and encouraging"
        ),
        learning_style=(
            "Conversation based learning"
        ),
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_conversation_agent():

    character = create_character()

    agent = ConversationAgent(
        character
    )

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request=(
            "Explain artificial intelligence"
        ),
    )

    result = await agent.execute(
        context
    )

    assert result.success is True

    assert result.agent_id == (
        "conversation_agent"
    )

    assert result.output["character"] == (
        "Yeti"
    )

    assert result.output["personality"] == (
        "Curious and encouraging"
    )

    assert result.output["learning_style"] == (
        "Conversation based learning"
    )

    assert isinstance(
        result.output["conversation_request"],
        ConversationRequest,
    )

    request = (
        result.output["conversation_request"]
    )

    assert request.character_id == "yeti"

    assert request.character_name == "Yeti"

    assert request.user_prompt == (
        "Explain artificial intelligence"
    )

    assert (
        request.has_conversation_context
        is False
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_conversation_agent_includes_live_intelligence():

    character = create_character()

    agent = ConversationAgent(
        character
    )

    intelligence = IntelligenceContext(
        query=(
            "Latest European AI regulation"
        ),
        sources=(
            IntelligenceSource(
                title="European AI Regulation",
                url="https://example.com/ai",
                source="parallel",
                content=(
                    "Current regulatory information."
                ),
            ),
        ),
        summary=(
            "Recent regulatory developments."
        ),
    )

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request=(
            "Explain the latest European "
            "AI regulation"
        ),
        intelligence_context=intelligence,
    )

    result = await agent.execute(
        context
    )

    assert result.success is True

    request = (
        result.output["conversation_request"]
    )

    assert isinstance(
        request,
        ConversationRequest,
    )

    assert (
        request.has_conversation_context
        is True
    )

    assert (
        "LIVE INTELLIGENCE"
        in request.conversation_context
    )

    assert (
        "European AI Regulation"
        in request.conversation_context
    )

    assert (
        "Current regulatory information."
        in request.conversation_context
    )

    assert (
        result.output["intelligence_context"]
        == request.conversation_context
    )


def test_conversation_agent_build_request_without_intelligence():

    character = create_character()

    agent = ConversationAgent(
        character
    )

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request="Explain AI",
    )

    request = agent.build_request(
        context
    )

    assert isinstance(
        request,
        ConversationRequest,
    )

    assert request.character_id == "yeti"

    assert request.character_name == "Yeti"

    assert request.user_prompt == (
        "Explain AI"
    )

    assert (
        request.conversation_context
        is None
    )


def test_conversation_agent_build_request_with_intelligence():

    character = create_character()

    agent = ConversationAgent(
        character
    )

    intelligence = IntelligenceContext(
        query="Latest AI regulation",
        sources=(
            IntelligenceSource(
                title="AI Act",
                url="https://example.com/ai-act",
                source="parallel",
                content="AI regulation information.",
            ),
        ),
    )

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request="Explain the AI Act",
        intelligence_context=intelligence,
    )

    request = agent.build_request(
        context
    )

    assert request.has_conversation_context

    assert (
        "LIVE INTELLIGENCE"
        in request.conversation_context
    )

    assert (
        request.character_name
        == "Yeti"
    )

import pytest

from backend.intelligence.intelligence_context import (
    IntelligenceContext,
)

from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import (
    ExecutionContext,
)


def test_execution_context_creation():

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request="Hello",
    )

    assert context.user_id == (
        "demo-user"
    )

    assert context.request == (
        "Hello"
    )

    assert context.execution_id

    assert context.created_at

    assert context.metadata == {}

    assert context.intelligence_context is None

    assert context.has_intelligence_context is False


def test_execution_context_is_immutable():

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request="Hello",
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        AttributeError
    ):
        context.request = "Changed"


def test_execution_context_can_carry_intelligence():

    intelligence = IntelligenceContext(
        query="Latest European AI regulation",
    )

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request="Explain this to me",
        intelligence_context=intelligence,
    )

    assert context.intelligence_context is intelligence

    assert context.has_intelligence_context is True

    assert (
        context.intelligence_context.query
        == "Latest European AI regulation"
    )

from backend.memory.memory_service import (
    MemoryService,
)

from backend.memory.memory_store import (
    MemoryStore,
)


def test_memory_save_and_recall(
    tmp_path,
):

    database_path = (
        tmp_path / "memory.db"
    )

    service = MemoryService(
        store=MemoryStore(
            database_path=database_path
        )
    )

    service.remember(
        user_id="demo-user",
        category="learning_goal",
        content=(
            "Improve French conversation skills"
        ),
    )

    memories = service.recall(
        "demo-user"
    )

    assert len(memories) == 1

    assert memories[0].user_id == (
        "demo-user"
    )

    assert memories[0].category == (
        "learning_goal"
    )

    assert memories[0].content == (
        "Improve French conversation skills"
    )

from backend.runtime.models.model_id import ModelId
from backend.runtime.models.model_capabilities import ModelCapabilities
from backend.runtime.models.model_descriptor import ModelDescriptor


def test_model_id():

    model = ModelId(
        provider="google",
        name="gemini-2.5-flash",
    )

    assert model.full_name() == (
        "google:gemini-2.5-flash"
    )


def test_model_descriptor():

    descriptor = ModelDescriptor(
        model_id=ModelId(
            provider="google",
            name="gemini-2.5-flash",
        ),
        capabilities=ModelCapabilities(
            reasoning=True,
            tool_usage=True,
        ),
        description="Fast reasoning model",
    )

    assert descriptor.model_id.provider == "google"

    assert descriptor.capabilities.reasoning is True

    assert descriptor.capabilities.tool_usage is True

import pytest

from backend.providers.base_provider import BaseProvider
from backend.providers.provider_result import ProviderResult
from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import ExecutionContext


class DemoProvider(BaseProvider):

    async def execute(
        self,
        context: ExecutionContext,
    ) -> ProviderResult:

        return ProviderResult(
            success=True,
            provider_id=self.provider_id,
            output="Provider response",
        )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_provider_execution():

    provider = DemoProvider(
        provider_id="demo_provider"
    )

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="user1",
        request="Generate response"
    )

    result = await provider.execute(context)

    assert result.success is True
    assert result.provider_id == "demo_provider"
    assert result.output == "Provider response"

import pytest

@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_quality_is_returned(
    tmp_path,
):

        character_id="yeti",
        name="Yeti",
        description="Companion",
