import pytest

from backend.agents.research_agent import (
    ResearchAgent,
)

from backend.intelligence.intelligence_context import (
    IntelligenceContext,
    IntelligenceSource,
)

from backend.providers.provider_result import (
    ProviderResult,
)

from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import (
    ExecutionContext,
)


class FakeResearchProvider:
    """
    Fake research provider used for unit testing.

    This keeps the test independent from the real
    Parallel Search API.
    """

    async def execute(
        self,
        context,
    ):
        return ProviderResult(
            success=True,
            provider_id="fake_research",
            output={
                "query": context.request,
                "results": {
                    "results": [
                        {
                            "title": "European AI Regulation",
                            "url": "https://example.com/ai",
                            "excerpts": [
                                "Current regulatory information."
                            ],
                        }
                    ]
                },
            },
        )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_research_agent_returns_intelligence_context():

    agent = ResearchAgent(
        provider=FakeResearchProvider()
    )

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request="Latest European AI regulation",
    )

    result = await agent.execute(
        context
    )

    assert result.success is True

    assert result.agent_id == (
        "research_agent"
    )

    assert isinstance(
        result.output,
        IntelligenceContext,
    )

    assert result.output.query == (
        "Latest European AI regulation"
    )

    assert result.output.source_count == 1

    assert isinstance(
        result.output.sources[0],
        IntelligenceSource,
    )

    assert result.output.sources[0].title == (
        "European AI Regulation"
    )

    assert result.output.sources[0].url == (
        "https://example.com/ai"
    )

    assert (
        result.output.sources[0].content
        == "Current regulatory information."
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_research_agent_propagates_provider_failure():

    class FailingResearchProvider:
        """
        Fake provider representing a provider failure.
        """

        async def execute(
            self,
            context,
        ):
            return ProviderResult(
                success=False,
                provider_id="fake_research",
                output=None,
                error="Research provider unavailable",
            )

    agent = ResearchAgent(
        provider=FailingResearchProvider()
    )

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request="Latest European AI regulation",
    )

    result = await agent.execute(
        context
    )

    assert result.success is False

    assert result.agent_id == (
        "research_agent"
    )

    assert result.output is None

    assert result.error == (
        "Research provider unavailable"
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_research_agent_handles_empty_results():

    class EmptyResearchProvider:
        """
        Fake provider returning no research results.
        """

        async def execute(
            self,
            context,
        ):
            return ProviderResult(
                success=True,
                provider_id="fake_research",
                output={
                    "query": context.request,
                    "results": {
                        "results": []
                    },
                },
            )

    agent = ResearchAgent(
        provider=EmptyResearchProvider()
    )

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request="A very specific research question",
    )

    result = await agent.execute(
        context
    )

    assert result.success is True

    assert isinstance(
        result.output,
        IntelligenceContext,
    )

    assert result.output.source_count == 0

    assert result.output.has_sources is False

import pytest

from backend.conversation.conversation_request import (
    ConversationRequest,
)


def test_conversation_request_is_created():

    request = ConversationRequest(
        system_instruction=(
            "You are a friendly learning companion."
        ),
        user_prompt=(
            "Explain artificial intelligence."
        ),
        character_id="yeti",
        character_name="Yeti",
    )

    assert request.character_id == "yeti"

    assert request.character_name == "Yeti"

    assert request.user_prompt == (
        "Explain artificial intelligence."
    )

    assert (
        request.has_conversation_context
        is False
    )

    assert request.context_length == 0


def test_conversation_request_accepts_intelligence_context():

    request = ConversationRequest(
        system_instruction=(
            "You are a friendly learning companion."
        ),
        user_prompt=(
            "Explain the latest AI regulation."
        ),
        conversation_context=(
            "LIVE INTELLIGENCE\n"
            "Research query: Latest AI regulation"
        ),
        character_id="yeti",
        character_name="Yeti",
    )

    assert (
        request.has_conversation_context
        is True
    )

    assert request.context_length > 0

    assert (
        "LIVE INTELLIGENCE"
        in request.conversation_context
    )


def test_conversation_request_rejects_empty_instruction():

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="system_instruction cannot be empty",
    ):
        ConversationRequest(
            system_instruction=" ",
            user_prompt="Explain AI",
        )


def test_conversation_request_rejects_empty_prompt():

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="user_prompt cannot be empty",
    ):
        ConversationRequest(
            system_instruction="You are helpful.",
            user_prompt=" ",
        )


def test_conversation_request_rejects_blank_context():

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="conversation_context cannot be blank",
    ):
        ConversationRequest(
            system_instruction="You are helpful.",
            user_prompt="Explain AI",
            conversation_context=" ",
        )


def test_conversation_request_summary_is_safe():

    request = ConversationRequest(
        system_instruction="You are helpful.",
        user_prompt="Explain AI.",
        conversation_context="Private context.",
        character_id="yeti",
        character_name="Yeti",
    )

    summary = request.summary()

    assert summary["character_id"] == "yeti"

    assert summary["character_name"] == "Yeti"

    assert summary["has_context"] == "True"

    assert "Private context." not in summary

    assert "Explain AI." not in summary

"""
Tests for Search Provider Interface.

Validates:
- SearchResult data model behaviour
- SearchProvider abstract contract
"""

import pytest

from src.providers.search_provider.interface import (
    SearchProvider,
    SearchResult,
)


def test_search_result_creation():
    """
    SearchResult should store normalised provider output.
    """

    result = SearchResult(
        title="AI Act Update",
        url="https://example.com/article",
        content="Latest AI regulation news.",
        source="example",
    )

    assert result.title == "AI Act Update"
    assert result.url == "https://example.com/article"
    assert result.content == "Latest AI regulation news."
    assert result.source == "example"


def test_search_result_is_immutable():
    """
    SearchResult is frozen and should not allow mutation.
    """

    result = SearchResult(
        title="Test",
        url="https://example.com",
        content="Content",
        source="test",
    )

    with pytest.raises(AttributeError):
        result.title = "Changed"


def test_search_provider_cannot_be_instantiated():
    """
    SearchProvider is an abstract interface.
    """

    with pytest.raises(TypeError):
        SearchProvider()

----------------------------------------------------------------------
TEST FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\tests\providers\search_provider\test_parallel_adapter.py
----------------------------------------------------------------------

"""
Integration test for the real Parallel Search Provider.

Sprint H1.8 â€” Real Provider Validation

Flow:

SearchProvider Interface
        â†“
ParallelSearchProvider
        â†“
Existing ParallelClientWrapper
        â†“
Parallel SDK
        â†“
Parallel Search API

The API key is loaded by the existing ParallelClientWrapper
from the project's .env configuration.
"""

import pytest

from src.providers.search_provider.interface import (
    SearchResult,
)

from src.providers.search_provider.parallel_adapter import (
    ParallelSearchProvider,
)


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_parallel_search_provider_returns_normalised_results():
    """
    Validate the real Parallel provider.
    """

    try:
        provider = ParallelSearchProvider()

    except ValueError as exc:
        pytest.skip(
            f"Parallel API credentials are not configured: {exc}"
        )

    results = await provider.search(
        query="latest European AI regulation",
        max_results=3,
    )

    assert isinstance(results, list)

    assert len(results) > 0

    for result in results:
        assert isinstance(
            result,
            SearchResult,
        )

        assert result.title.strip()
        assert result.url.strip()
        assert result.source == "parallel"

TEST FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\tests\providers\test_parallel_client.py

import pytest

from backend.providers.parallel.parallel_client import (
    ParallelClientWrapper,
)


class FakeSearchResult:

    def model_dump(self):
        return {
            "results": [
                {
                    "title": "Example",
                    "url": "https://example.com",
                }
            ]
        }


class FakeParallelClient:

    def search(
        self,
        *,
        search_queries,
        mode,
        objective,
    ):
        assert len(search_queries) == 1
        assert mode == "advanced"
        assert objective == (
            "Latest artificial intelligence news"
        )

        return FakeSearchResult()


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_parallel_client_normalizes_response():

    wrapper = object.__new__(
        ParallelClientWrapper
    )

    wrapper.api_key = "test-key"

    wrapper._client = FakeParallelClient()

    result = await wrapper.search(
        "Latest artificial intelligence news"
    )

    assert result["query"] == (
        "Latest artificial intelligence news"
    )

    assert result["results"] == {
        "results": [
            {
                "title": "Example",
                "url": "https://example.com",
            }
        ]
    }

"""
Parallel Provider Tests.

These tests validate the provider layer
without calling the external Parallel API.

The real API integration is demonstrated
through the hackathon demo flow.
"""

import pytest

from backend.providers.parallel.parallel_provider import (
    ParallelSearchProvider,
)

from backend.providers.provider_result import (
    ProviderResult,
)

from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import (
    ExecutionContext,
)


class MockParallelClient:
    """
    Fake Parallel client for automated tests.
    """

    async def search(
        self,
        query: str,
    ):

        return {
            "query": query,
            "results": [
                {
                    "title": "Example source",
                    "url": "https://example.com",
                }
            ],
        }


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_parallel_provider_returns_results(
    monkeypatch,
):

    provider = ParallelSearchProvider(
    client=MockParallelClient()
)

    provider.client = MockParallelClient()

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request="Latest AI news",
    )

    result = await provider.execute(
        context
    )

    assert isinstance(
        result,
        ProviderResult,
    )

    assert result.success is True

    assert result.provider_id == (
        "parallel_search"
    )

    assert result.output["query"] == (
        "Latest AI news"
    )

    assert len(
        result.output["results"]
    ) == 1

import pytest

from backend.intelligence.intelligence_context import (
    IntelligenceContext,
    IntelligenceSource,
)


def test_intelligence_context_creation():
    source = IntelligenceSource(
        title="European Commission",
        url="https://example.com",
        source="European Commission",
        content="AI regulation information.",
    )

    context = IntelligenceContext(
        query="latest European AI regulation",
        sources=(source,),
        summary="Current regulatory information.",
    )

    assert context.query == (
        "latest European AI regulation"
    )

    assert context.source_count == 1

    assert context.has_sources is True

    assert context.has_summary is True


def test_intelligence_context_is_immutable():
    context = IntelligenceContext(
        query="latest AI news",
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        AttributeError
    ):
        context.query = "changed"


def test_intelligence_context_rejects_empty_query():
    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="Intelligence query cannot be empty",
    ):
        IntelligenceContext(
            query="",
        )


def test_intelligence_source_requires_title():
    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="title cannot be empty",
    ):
        IntelligenceSource(
            title="",
            url="https://example.com",
            source="example",
        )


def test_intelligence_context_to_context_text():
    source = IntelligenceSource(
        title="AI Regulation",
        url="https://example.com/ai",
        source="Example",
        content="European AI regulation information.",
    )

    context = IntelligenceContext(
        query="latest European AI regulation",
        sources=(source,),
        summary="Regulatory developments.",
    )

    text = context.to_context_text()

    assert "LIVE INTELLIGENCE" in text

    assert (
        "latest European AI regulation"
        in text
    )

    assert "AI Regulation" in text

    assert "https://example.com/ai" in text

    assert "European AI regulation information." in text

import pytest

from backend.agents.agent_result import (
    AgentResult,
)

from backend.intelligence.intelligence_context import (
    IntelligenceContext,
)

from backend.intelligence.research_service import (
    ResearchService,
)


class FakeResearchAgent:

    async def execute(
        self,
        context,
    ):
        return AgentResult(
            success=True,
            agent_id="research_agent",
            output=IntelligenceContext(
                query=context.request,
            ),
        )


class FailingResearchAgent:

    async def execute(
        self,
        context,
    ):
        return AgentResult(
            success=False,
            agent_id="research_agent",
            output=None,
            error="Research unavailable",
        )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_research_service_returns_intelligence_context():

    service = ResearchService(
        agent=FakeResearchAgent()
    )

    result = await service.research(
        user_id="demo-user",
        query="Latest European AI regulation",
    )

    assert isinstance(
        result,
        IntelligenceContext,
    )

    assert result.query == (
        "Latest European AI regulation"
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_research_service_rejects_empty_query():

    service = ResearchService(
        agent=FakeResearchAgent()
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="Research query cannot be empty",
    ):
        await service.research(
            user_id="demo-user",
            query="",
        )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_research_service_propagates_failure():

    service = ResearchService(
        agent=FailingResearchAgent()
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        RuntimeError,
        match="Research unavailable",
    ):
        await service.research(
            user_id="demo-user",
            query="Latest European AI regulation",
        )

"""
Integration test for the character-driven discovery notification.

Flow:

Memory
    â†“
DiscoveryService
    â†“
FlashDealCard
    â†“
DiscoveryNotification
    â†“
CommerceAction
    â†“
CommerceService
    â†“
Vendor handoff
"""

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.flash_deal_commerce import (
    FlashDealCommerce,
)

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.models import (
    CommerceActionStatus,
)

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.service import (
    CommerceService,
)

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.flash_deal_demo import (
    build_demo_deal,
    build_demo_memory,
)

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.notification import (
    DiscoveryNotification,
)

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.service import (
    CharacterContext,
    DiscoveryRequest,
    DiscoveryService,
)


def build_flash_deal():
    """
    Build the same application-level FlashDealCard used
    by the hackathon discovery flow.
    """

    deal = build_demo_deal()
    memory = build_demo_memory()

    request = DiscoveryRequest(
        user_id=memory.user_id,
        deal=deal,
        character=CharacterContext(
            character_id="yeti",
            character_name="Yeti",
            message=(
                "Look what I found! "
                "This could be perfect for you."
            ),
        ),
        product_image_url=(
            "https://example.com/demo/iphone17-pro.jpg"
        ),
        tagline="Pro. Beautiful. Powerful.",
        highlights=(
            "Pro camera system",
            "High-performance chip",
            "All-day battery life",
            "Premium display",
        ),
    )

    card = DiscoveryService().create_flash_deal(
        request,
        memories=[memory],
    )

    return memory, card


def test_notification_is_created_from_real_discovery_result():

    memory, card = build_flash_deal()

    notification = (
        DiscoveryNotification.from_flash_deal(
            notification_id="integration-notification-001",
            user_id=memory.user_id,
            deal=card,
        )
    )

    assert notification.user_id == memory.user_id

    assert notification.character_id == (
        card.character_id
    )

    assert notification.character_name == (
        card.character_name
    )

    assert notification.deal is card

    assert notification.product_name == (
        card.product_name
    )


def test_notification_preserves_personalisation():

    memory, card = build_flash_deal()

    notification = (
        DiscoveryNotification.from_flash_deal(
            notification_id="integration-notification-002",
            user_id=memory.user_id,
            deal=card,
        )
    )

    assert notification.relevance_reason == (
        card.relevance_reason
    )

    assert notification.relevance_reason


def test_notification_render_is_character_driven():

    memory, card = build_flash_deal()

    notification = (
        DiscoveryNotification.from_flash_deal(
            notification_id="integration-notification-003",
            user_id=memory.user_id,
            deal=card,
        )
    )

    rendered = notification.render()

    assert "Yeti:" in rendered

    assert (
        "Look what I found!"
        in rendered
    )

    assert card.product_name in rendered

    assert (
        card.relevance_reason
        in rendered
    )


def test_notification_does_not_change_commerce_flow():

    memory, card = build_flash_deal()

    notification = (
        DiscoveryNotification.from_flash_deal(
            notification_id="integration-notification-004",
            user_id=memory.user_id,
            deal=card,
        )
    )

    action = FlashDealCommerce().create_action(
        user_id=notification.user_id,
        card=notification.deal,
    )

    result = CommerceService().initiate(
        action
    )

    assert (
        result.status
        == CommerceActionStatus.REDIRECT
    )

    assert result.deal_id == card.deal_id

    assert (
        result.next_url
        == card.get_deal_url
    )


def test_notification_does_not_create_a_shop():

    memory, card = build_flash_deal()

    notification = (
        DiscoveryNotification.from_flash_deal(
            notification_id="integration-notification-005",
            user_id=memory.user_id,
            deal=card,
        )
    )

    # The notification exposes one discovery event,
    # not a catalogue, cart, or shopping dashboard.

    assert notification.deal is card

    assert not hasattr(
        notification,
        "products",
    )

    assert not hasattr(
        notification,
        "cart",
    )

    assert not hasattr(
        notification,
        "checkout",
    )

"""
Integration test for the complete Flash Deal commerce flow.

Flow:

Memory / personalisation
        â†“
DealCard
        â†“
FlashDealCard
        â†“
CommerceAction
        â†“
CommerceService
        â†“
External vendor handoff

No payment is executed by the test.
"""

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.flash_deal_commerce import (
    FlashDealCommerce,
)

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.models import (
    CommerceActionStatus,
    CommerceActionType,
)

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.service import (
    CommerceService,
)

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.flash_deal_demo import (
    build_demo_flash_deal_card,
)


def test_complete_flash_deal_to_commerce_flow():
    """
    Validate the complete hackathon commerce boundary.
    """

    card = build_demo_flash_deal_card()

    commerce = FlashDealCommerce()

    action = commerce.create_action(
        user_id="demo-user",
        card=card,
    )

    assert action.user_id == "demo-user"

    assert action.deal_id == card.deal_id

    assert (
        action.action_type
        == CommerceActionType.INITIATE_PURCHASE
    )

    result = CommerceService().initiate(
        action
    )

    assert (
        result.status
        == CommerceActionStatus.REDIRECT
    )

    assert result.deal_id == card.deal_id

    assert (
        result.next_url
        == card.get_deal_url
    )

    assert result.action_id == action.action_id


def test_commerce_flow_does_not_change_discovery_data():
    """
    Commerce must not mutate the discovered Flash Deal.
    """

    card = build_demo_flash_deal_card()

    original_product = card.product_name
    original_price = card.current_price
    original_vendor = card.vendor_name
    original_deal_id = card.deal_id

    action = FlashDealCommerce().create_action(
        user_id="demo-user",
        card=card,
    )

    CommerceService().initiate(
        action
    )

    assert card.product_name == original_product
    assert card.current_price == original_price
    assert card.vendor_name == original_vendor
    assert card.deal_id == original_deal_id

"""
Integration test for the EasyChatBot Flash Deal demonstration.

Validates the complete hackathon discovery presentation:

FlashDealCard

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.flash_deal_demo import (
    build_demo_flash_deal_card,
    render_demo_flash_deal_card,
)


def test_demo_builds_flash_deal_card():

    card = build_demo_flash_deal_card()

    assert card.character_id == "yeti"

    assert card.character_name == "Yeti"

    assert card.product_name == "iPhone 17 Pro"

    assert card.vendor_name == "Demo Vendor"

    assert card.current_price == 999.99

    assert card.previous_price == 1199.00

    assert card.discount_percentage == 17.0

    assert card.has_discount is True


def test_demo_contains_personalisation():

    card = build_demo_flash_deal_card()

    assert (
        "mobile photography"
        in card.relevance_reason
    )

    assert (
        card.metadata["memory_type"]
        == "preference"
    )


def test_demo_contains_both_commerce_actions():

    card = build_demo_flash_deal_card()

    assert card.get_deal_url

    assert card.more_information_url

    assert (
        card.get_deal_url
        == card.more_information_url
    )


def test_demo_render_contains_user_facing_sections():

    card = build_demo_flash_deal_card()

    output = render_demo_flash_deal_card(
        card
    )

    assert "NEW FIND!" in output

    assert "Yeti" in output

    assert "iPhone 17 Pro" in output

    assert "PRICE" in output

    assert "WHY THIS IS RELEVANT TO YOU" in output

    assert "AVAILABLE FROM" in output

    assert "GET DEAL" in output

    assert "MORE INFORMATION" in output


def test_demo_is_explicitly_marked_as_demo_data():

    output = render_demo_flash_deal_card()

    assert (
        "deterministic hackathon demo data"
        in output
    )

TEST FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\tests\integration\test_hackathon_complete_vertical_slice.py

"""
End-to-end hackathon vertical-slice test.

Validates the working EasyChatBot journey:

Governance
    â†“
Live Intelligence
    â†“
Learning Challenge
    â†“
Quality Evaluation
    â†“
Memory
    â†“
DiscoveryService
    â†“
FlashDealCard
    â†“
CommerceAction
    â†“
CommerceService
    â†“
Vendor handoff

No real purchase or external payment is executed.
"""

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.flash_deal_commerce import (
    FlashDealCommerce,
)

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.models import (
    CommerceActionStatus,
    CommerceActionType,
)

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.service import (
    CommerceService,
)

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.flash_deal_demo import (
    build_demo_deal,
    build_demo_memory,
)

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.service import (
    CharacterContext,
    DiscoveryRequest,
    DiscoveryService,
)


def test_complete_hackathon_vertical_slice():

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # MEMORY
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------

    memory = build_demo_memory()

    assert memory.user_id == "demo-user"

    assert memory.content

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # DISCOVERY INPUT
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------

    deal = build_demo_deal()

    request = DiscoveryRequest(
        user_id=memory.user_id,
        deal=deal,
        character=CharacterContext(
            character_id="yeti",
            character_name="Yeti",
            message=(
                "Look what I found! "
                "This could be perfect for you."
            ),
        ),
        product_image_url=(
            "https://example.com/demo/iphone17-pro.jpg"
        ),
        tagline="Pro. Beautiful. Powerful.",
        highlights=(
            "Pro camera system",
            "High-performance chip",
            "All-day battery life",
            "Premium display",
        ),
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # DISCOVERY SERVICE
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------

    card = DiscoveryService().create_flash_deal(
        request,
        memories=[memory],
    )

    assert card.deal_id == deal.deal_id

    assert card.character_id == "yeti"

    assert card.product_name == deal.product_name

    assert card.vendor_name == deal.vendor

    assert card.relevance_reason

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # COMMERCE ACTION
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------

    action = FlashDealCommerce().create_action(
        user_id=memory.user_id,
        card=card,
    )

    assert action.user_id == memory.user_id

    assert action.deal_id == card.deal_id

    assert (
        action.action_type
        == CommerceActionType.INITIATE_PURCHASE
    )

    assert (
        action.destination_url
        == card.get_deal_url
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # COMMERCE SERVICE
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------

    result = CommerceService().initiate(
        action
    )

    assert (
        result.status
        == CommerceActionStatus.REDIRECT
    )

    assert result.deal_id == card.deal_id

    assert result.action_id == action.action_id

    assert (
        result.next_url
        == card.get_deal_url
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # SAFETY / SCOPE BOUNDARY
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------

    assert (
        result.message
        == (
            "Continue to the approved vendor "
            "to complete the purchase."
        )
    )

"""
Final end-to-end acceptance test for the EasyChatBot hackathon demo.

This test executes the real hackathon demonstration and verifies
the complete product story:

Governance
    â†“
Live Intelligence
    â†“
Learning
    â†“
Evaluation
    â†“
Personalised Discovery
    â†“
Character Notification
    â†“
Commerce Boundary
    â†“
Vendor Handoff

No real external purchase is performed.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path


REPOSITORY_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]

DEMO_PATH = (
    REPOSITORY_ROOT
    / "examples"
    / "hackathon_demo.py"
)


def run_demo() -> subprocess.CompletedProcess[str]:
    """Execute the real hackathon demo."""

    return subprocess.run(
        [
            sys.executable,
            str(DEMO_PATH),
        ],
        cwd=REPOSITORY_ROOT,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
        check=False,
    )


def test_complete_hackathon_demo_succeeds():

    result = run_demo()

    assert result.returncode == 0, (
        "Hackathon demonstration failed.\n\n"
        f"STDOUT:\n{result.stdout}\n\n"
        f"STDERR:\n{result.stderr}"
    )

    output = result.stdout

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Governance
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    assert "STEP 1 â€” GOVERNANCE" in output
    assert "Decision: ALLOW" in output
    assert "Governance check: PASSED" in output

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Live Intelligence
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    assert "STEP 2 â€” LIVE INTELLIGENCE" in output
    assert "Topic: AI Act" in output
    assert "Language: French" in output
    assert "Learner level: B2" in output

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Learning
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    assert "Summary:" in output
    assert "Vocabulary:" in output
    assert "Discussion questions:" in output

    assert "gouvernance" in output
    assert "rÃ©glementation" in output

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Evaluation
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    assert "STEP 3 â€” AI QUALITY EVALUATION" in output
    assert "Status: PASS" in output

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Personalised Discovery
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    assert (
        "STEP 4 â€” PERSONALISED DISCOVERY"
        in output
    )

    assert "## Yeti" in output
    assert "### NEW FIND!" in output
    assert "iPhone 17 Pro" in output
    assert "Pro. Beautiful. Powerful." in output

    assert "### HIGHLIGHTS" in output
    assert "Pro camera system" in output
    assert "High-performance chip" in output

    assert "### PRICE" in output
    assert "Current price:" in output
    assert "Discount:" in output
    assert "Savings:" in output

    assert (
        "### WHY THIS IS RELEVANT TO YOU"
        in output
    )

    assert "### AVAILABLE FROM" in output
    assert "Demo Vendor" in output

    assert "### ACTIONS" in output
    assert "[GET DEAL]" in output
    assert "[MORE INFORMATION]" in output

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Character notification
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    assert "CHARACTER NOTIFICATION" in output
    assert "Yeti:" in output
    assert "Look what I found!" in output

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Commerce boundary
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    assert "STEP 5 â€” COMMERCE ACTION" in output
    assert "Commerce action: INITIATE_PURCHASE" in output
    assert "Status: REDIRECT" in output
    assert "Approved commerce boundary" in output
    assert "Vendor handoff" in output

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Payment boundary
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    assert (
        "Payment is not executed in the hackathon demo."
        in output
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Final success
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    assert "HACKATHON DEMO: SUCCESS" in output

    assert (
        "Governance -> Intelligence -> Learning -> Evaluation"
        in output
    )

    assert (
        "Governance -> Intelligence -> Learning -> "
        "Evaluation -> Personalised Discovery"
        in output
    )


def test_demo_preserves_correct_execution_order():

    result = run_demo()

    assert result.returncode == 0

    output = result.stdout

    governance = output.index(
        "STEP 1 â€” GOVERNANCE"
    )

    intelligence = output.index(
        "STEP 2 â€” LIVE INTELLIGENCE"
    )

    evaluation = output.index(
        "STEP 3 â€” AI QUALITY EVALUATION"
    )

    discovery = output.index(
        "STEP 4 â€” PERSONALISED DISCOVERY"
    )

    notification = output.index(
        "CHARACTER NOTIFICATION"
    )

    commerce = output.index(
        "STEP 5 â€” COMMERCE ACTION"
    )

    success = output.index(
        "HACKATHON DEMO: SUCCESS"
    )

    assert governance < intelligence
    assert intelligence < evaluation
    assert evaluation < discovery
    assert discovery < notification
    assert notification < commerce
    assert commerce < success

TEST FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\tests\integration\test_hackathon_demo_reliability.py

"""
Reliability tests for the unified EasyChatBot hackathon demo.

H4.3 â€” Demo Reliability

These tests verify that the hackathon demonstration:

- starts successfully
- completes within a reasonable time
- does not require external API credentials
- produces the expected final success marker

The test intentionally executes the demo as a separate process,
matching how the hackathon presentation will actually run it.

It does not modify or bypass H1, H2, or H3 validation.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path


REPOSITORY_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]

DEMO_PATH = (
    REPOSITORY_ROOT
    / "examples"
    / "hackathon_demo.py"
)


def _run_demo() -> subprocess.CompletedProcess[str]:
    """
    Execute the unified demo with a bounded runtime.

    A timeout protects CI and the hackathon environment from
    a demo process hanging indefinitely.
    """

    return subprocess.run(
        [
            sys.executable,
            str(DEMO_PATH),
        ],
        cwd=REPOSITORY_ROOT,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
        check=False,
        timeout=30,
    )


def test_hackathon_demo_completes_within_timeout():
    """
    Verify that the demo completes within 30 seconds.
    """

    result = _run_demo()

    assert result.returncode == 0, (
        "Hackathon demo did not complete successfully.\n\n"
        f"STDOUT:\n{result.stdout}\n\n"
        f"STDERR:\n{result.stderr}"
    )


def test_hackathon_demo_is_self_contained():
    """
    Verify that the deterministic hackathon demonstration
    reaches its expected final state.

    The demo uses controlled providers and therefore should
    not require Parallel, LLM, or other external credentials.
    """

    result = _run_demo()

    assert result.returncode == 0

    assert (
        "Governance check: PASSED"
        in result.stdout
    )

    assert (
        "Overall Score: 1.00"
        in result.stdout
    )

    assert (
        "Status: PASS"
        in result.stdout
    )

    assert (
        "HACKATHON DEMO: SUCCESS"
        in result.stdout
    )

    assert (
        "Governance -> Intelligence -> Learning -> Evaluation"
        in result.stdout
    )


def test_hackathon_demo_script_exists():

    assert DEMO_PATH.exists()
    assert DEMO_PATH.is_file()

----------------------------------------------------------------------
TEST FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\tests\integration\test_hackathon_discovery_commerce_flow.py
----------------------------------------------------------------------

"""
End-to-end integration test for the hackathon discovery journey.

Flow:

User Memory
    â†“
Deal Discovery
    â†“
Personalisation
    â†“
Character FlashDealCard
    â†“
GET DEAL
    â†“
CommerceAction
    â†“
CommerceService
    â†“
Vendor handoff

The test validates the complete hackathon vertical slice
without executing a real purchase or external payment.
"""

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.flash_deal_commerce import (
    FlashDealCommerce,
)

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.models import (
    CommerceActionStatus,
    CommerceActionType,
)

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.service import (
    CommerceService,
)

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.flash_deal_demo import (
    build_demo_deal,
    build_demo_flash_deal_card,
    build_demo_memory,
)


def test_hackathon_discovery_to_commerce_flow():
    """
    Validate the complete personalised discovery journey.
    """

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 1. USER MEMORY
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    memory = build_demo_memory()

    assert memory.user_id == "demo-user"

    assert memory.content

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 2. DEAL DISCOVERY
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    deal = build_demo_deal()

    assert deal.deal_id

    assert deal.product_name == "iPhone 17 Pro"

    assert deal.vendor == "Demo Vendor"

    assert deal.price == 999.99

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 3. PERSONALISED FLASH DEAL
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    card = build_demo_flash_deal_card(
        deal=deal,
    )

    assert card.deal_id == deal.deal_id

    assert card.character_id == "yeti"

    assert card.character_name == "Yeti"

    assert card.product_name == deal.product_name

    assert card.relevance_reason

    assert (
        "mobile photography"
        in card.relevance_reason
    )

    assert card.has_discount is True

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 4. COMMERCE ACTION
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    action = FlashDealCommerce().create_action(
        user_id=memory.user_id,
        card=card,
    )

    assert action.user_id == memory.user_id

    assert action.deal_id == card.deal_id

    assert (
        action.action_type
        == CommerceActionType.INITIATE_PURCHASE
    )

    assert (
        action.destination_url
        == card.get_deal_url
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 5. CONTROLLED COMMERCE SERVICE
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    result = CommerceService().initiate(
        action
    )

    assert (
        result.status
        == CommerceActionStatus.REDIRECT
    )

    assert result.action_id == action.action_id

    assert result.deal_id == card.deal_id

    assert (
        result.next_url
        == card.get_deal_url
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 6. FINAL SAFETY BOUNDARY
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    # The hackathon demonstrates a vendor handoff.
    # It does not claim that a payment occurred.

    assert (
        result.message
        == (
            "Continue to the approved vendor "
            "to complete the purchase."
        )
    )


def test_hackathon_flow_preserves_layer_separation():
    """
    Verify that commerce consumes the discovery result rather
    than changing it.
    """

    card = build_demo_flash_deal_card()

    original = {
        "deal_id": card.deal_id,
        "product_name": card.product_name,
        "price": card.current_price,
        "vendor": card.vendor_name,
        "relevance": card.relevance_reason,
        "get_deal_url": card.get_deal_url,
    }

    action = FlashDealCommerce().create_action(
        user_id="demo-user",
        card=card,
    )

    CommerceService().initiate(
        action
    )

    assert card.deal_id == original["deal_id"]

    assert (
        card.product_name
        == original["product_name"]
    )

    assert (
        card.current_price
        == original["price"]
    )

    assert (
        card.vendor_name
        == original["vendor"]
    )

    assert (
        card.relevance_reason
        == original["relevance"]
    )

    assert (
        card.get_deal_url
        == original["get_deal_url"]
    )

    assert "3 - LEARNING" in output

    assert "6 - CHARACTER DISCOVERY" in output

def test_judge_demo_shows_personalised_discovery():

    assert "Look what I found!" in output
    assert "iPhone 17 Pro" in output
    assert "Pro. Beautiful. Powerful." in output
    assert "WHY THIS IS RELEVANT TO YOU" in output

def test_judge_demo_shows_commerce_boundary():

    result = run_judge_demo()

    assert result.returncode == 0

    output = result.stdout

    assert "GET DEAL -> vendor handoff" in output

    assert "PAYMENT PASS" in output

    assert "Payment status: READY" in output

    assert "Execution mode: DEMO" in output

    assert "[ PROCEED TO CHECKOUT ]" in output

    assert "No payment has been executed." in output

    assert (
        "Vendor checkout remains outside "
        "the hackathon demo."
        in output
    )

    assert "[PASS] Payment execution: NO" in output

    assert "[PASS] Execution mode: DEMO" in output

    assert "EasyChatBot is not a shop." in output


def test_judge_demo_preserves_execution_order():

    result = run_judge_demo()

    assert result.returncode == 0

    output = result.stdout

    governance = output.index(
        "1 - GOVERNANCE"
    )

    intelligence = output.index(
        "2 - LIVE INTELLIGENCE"
    )

    learning = output.index(
        "3 - LEARNING"
    )

    evaluation = output.index(
        "4 - AI QUALITY EVALUATION"
    )

    discovery = output.index(
        "5 - PERSONALISED DISCOVERY"
    )

    character = output.index(
        "6 - CHARACTER DISCOVERY"
    )

    deal = output.index(
        "7 - FLASH DEAL"
    )

    commerce = output.index(
        "8 - COMMERCE BOUNDARY"
    )

    payment = output.index(
        "PAYMENT PASS"
    )

    success = output.index(
        "JUDGE DEMO: SUCCESS"
    )

    assert governance < intelligence
    assert intelligence < learning
    assert learning < evaluation
    assert evaluation < discovery
    assert discovery < character
    assert character < deal
    assert deal < commerce
    assert commerce < payment
    assert payment < success

----------------------------------------------------------------------
TEST FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\tests\integration\test_hackathon_payment_pass_demo.py
----------------------------------------------------------------------

"""
Integration acceptance tests for the hackathon Payment Pass.

The test verifies the complete safe commerce path:

FlashDealCard
    â†“
FlashDealCommerce
    â†“
PaymentPass
    â†“
CommerceService
    â†“
READY / DEMO
    â†“
Vendor checkout

No real payment is executed.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.flash_deal_commerce import (
    FlashDealCommerce,
)

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.payment_pass import (
    PaymentExecutionMode,
    PaymentPassStatus,
)

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.service import (
    CommerceService,
)

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.flash_deal_card import (
    FlashDealCard,
)


def make_card() -> FlashDealCard:
    """Create deterministic hackathon deal data."""

    return FlashDealCard(
        deal_id="deal-payment-demo-001",
        character_id="yeti",
        character_name="Yeti",
        character_message=(
            "Look what I found! "
            "This could be perfect for you."
        ),
        product_name="iPhone 17 Pro",
        product_image_url=(
            "https://example.com/demo/iphone17-pro.jpg"
        ),
        tagline="Pro. Beautiful. Powerful.",
        highlights=(
            "Pro camera system",
            "High-performance chip",
            "All-day battery life",
            "Premium display",
        ),
        current_price=999.99,
        currency="EUR",
        previous_price=1199.00,
        discount_percentage=17.0,
        savings=199.01,
        relevance_reason=(
            "Matches the user's preference for "
            "powerful mobile photography devices."
        ),
        vendor_name="Demo Vendor",
        more_information_url=(
            "https://example.com/vendor/iphone17-pro"
        ),
        get_deal_url=(
            "https://example.com/vendor/iphone17-pro"
        ),
    )


def test_complete_payment_pass_flow():

    card = make_card()

    commerce = FlashDealCommerce()

    payment_pass = commerce.create_payment_pass(
        user_id="user-001",
        card=card,
    )

    prepared = CommerceService().prepare_payment(
        payment_pass
    )

    assert prepared is payment_pass

    assert prepared.deal_id == card.deal_id
    assert prepared.user_id == "user-001"

    assert (
        prepared.product_name
        == card.product_name
    )

    assert (
        prepared.vendor_name
        == card.vendor_name
    )

    assert (
        prepared.amount
        == card.current_price
    )

    assert (
        prepared.currency
        == card.currency
    )

    assert (
        prepared.checkout_url
        == card.get_deal_url
    )

    assert (
        prepared.status
        is PaymentPassStatus.READY
    )

    assert (
        prepared.execution_mode
        is PaymentExecutionMode.DEMO
    )

    assert prepared.payment_executed is False


def test_payment_pass_cannot_become_executed():

    card = make_card()

    payment_pass = (
        FlashDealCommerce().create_payment_pass(
            user_id="user-001",
            card=card,
        )
    )

    assert payment_pass.payment_executed is False

    assert (
        payment_pass.status
        is PaymentPassStatus.READY
    )

    assert (
        payment_pass.execution_mode
        is PaymentExecutionMode.DEMO
    )


def test_payment_pass_preserves_vendor_checkout():

    card = make_card()

    payment_pass = (
        FlashDealCommerce().create_payment_pass(
            user_id="user-001",
            card=card,
        )
    )

    prepared = CommerceService().prepare_payment(
        payment_pass
    )

    assert (
        prepared.checkout_url
        == "https://example.com/vendor/iphone17-pro"
    )


def test_payment_pass_demo_render_is_explicit():

    card = make_card()

    payment_pass = (
        FlashDealCommerce().create_payment_pass(
            user_id="user-001",
            card=card,
        )
    )

    prepared = CommerceService().prepare_payment(
        payment_pass
    )

    output = prepared.render_demo()

    assert "PAYMENT PASS" in output
    assert "Payment status: READY" in output
    assert "Execution mode: DEMO" in output
    assert "No payment has been executed." in output
    assert (
        "Vendor checkout remains outside "
        "the hackathon demo."
        in output
    )

def test_required_hackathon_files_exist():
    """
    Verify that all required hackathon deliverables exist.
    """

    missing_files = [
        path
        for path in REQUIRED_FILES
        if not (
            REPOSITORY_ROOT / path
        ).is_file()
    ]

    assert not missing_files, (
        "Hackathon readiness check failed. "
        "Missing required files:\n"
        + "\n".join(
            f" - {path}"
            for path in missing_files
        )
    )

        / "examples"

TEST FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\tests\integration\test_live_challenge_evaluation.py

"""
Integration test for H2 evaluation of the H1 learning challenge.

Flow:

H1 Live Intelligence / Challenge Engine
        â†“
LearningChallenge
        â†“
H2 DeterministicEvaluator
        â†“
CriterionScore[]
        â†“
EvaluationEngine
        â†“
EvaluationResult

This test intentionally uses deterministic test doubles.

It does not:
- call the Parallel API
- call an LLM
- modify H1 components
- use the Conversation Agent
"""

from src.challenge_engine.models import (
    DiscussionQuestion,
    LearningChallenge,
    VocabularyItem,
)

from src.evaluation.criteria import (
    COMPLETENESS,
    EDUCATIONAL_VALUE,
    LANGUAGE_LEVEL,
    SOURCE_COVERAGE,
)

from src.evaluation.deterministic_evaluator import (
    DeterministicEvaluator,
)

from src.evaluation.engine import (
    EvaluationEngine,
)


def _create_h1_learning_challenge() -> LearningChallenge:
    """
    Create a representative H1 learning challenge.

    This mirrors the structure produced by the H1
    Challenge Engine without calling external services.
    """

    return LearningChallenge(
        title="AI Act Learning Challenge",
        topic="AI Act",
        language="French",
        learner_level="B2",
        summary=(
            "The European Union AI Act establishes "
            "a risk-based framework for artificial "
            "intelligence."
        ),
        vocabulary=[
            VocabularyItem(
                term="gouvernance",
                definition=(
                    "La maniÃ¨re dont une organisation "
                    "est dirigÃ©e et contrÃ´lÃ©e."
                ),
                example=(
                    "La gouvernance de l'IA devient "
                    "essentielle."
                ),
            ),
            VocabularyItem(
                term="rÃ©glementation",
                definition=(
                    "Ensemble des rÃ¨gles qui encadrent "
                    "une activitÃ©."
                ),
                example=(
                    "La rÃ©glementation europÃ©enne Ã©volue."
                ),
            ),
        ],
        discussion_questions=[
            DiscussionQuestion(
                question=(
                    "Pourquoi la rÃ©glementation de "
                    "l'IA est-elle importante ?"
                ),
                difficulty="B2",
            ),
            DiscussionQuestion(
                question=(
                    "Quels sont les avantages d'une "
                    "rÃ©glementation europÃ©enne de l'IA ?"
                ),
                difficulty="B2",
            ),
        ],
    )


def test_h1_learning_challenge_can_be_evaluated():
    """
    Validate the complete H1 â†’ H2 evaluation boundary.
    """

    challenge = _create_h1_learning_challenge()

    evaluator = DeterministicEvaluator()

    scores = evaluator.evaluate(
        challenge
    )

    assert len(scores) == 4

    score_names = {
        score.criterion
        for score in scores
    }

    assert score_names == {
        "completeness",
        "language_level",
        "educational_value",
        "source_coverage",
    }


def test_h1_learning_challenge_produces_quality_result():
    """
    Evaluate the H1 challenge through the H2 scoring engine.
    """

    challenge = _create_h1_learning_challenge()

    evaluator = DeterministicEvaluator()

    scores = evaluator.evaluate(
        challenge
    )

    engine = EvaluationEngine(
        criteria=[
            COMPLETENESS,
            LANGUAGE_LEVEL,
            EDUCATIONAL_VALUE,
            SOURCE_COVERAGE,
        ],
        passing_score=0.70,
    )

    result = engine.evaluate(
        scores
    )

    assert result.overall_score == 1.0

    assert result.passed is True

    assert len(result.scores) == 4

    assert result.evaluator == "deterministic"


def test_h1_learning_challenge_contains_high_quality_components():
    """
    Verify the H1 challenge satisfies the basic quality
    dimensions currently supported by deterministic evaluation.
    """

    challenge = _create_h1_learning_challenge()

    evaluator = DeterministicEvaluator()

    scores = evaluator.evaluate(
        challenge
    )

    for score in scores:
        assert 0.0 <= score.score <= 1.0
        assert score.reasoning.strip()

    assert all(
        score.score == 1.0
        for score in scores
    )

TEST FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\tests\integration\test_live_challenge_flow.py

"""
Integration test for Live Challenge vertical slice.

Flow tested:

Search Provider
        â†“
Live Intelligence Agent
        â†“
Challenge Generator
        â†“
Learning Challenge

This test validates the Sprint H1 integration boundaries.

Important:
- Uses test doubles only.
- Does not call the real Parallel API.
- Does not modify or depend on the Conversation Agent.
"""

import pytest

from src.ai_agents.live_intelligence_agent.handler import (
    LiveIntelligenceAgent,
    LiveIntelligenceRequest,
)

from src.challenge_engine.generator import (
    ChallengeGenerator,
)

from src.challenge_engine.models import (
    DiscussionQuestion,
    VocabularyItem,
)

from src.providers.search_provider.interface import (
    SearchResult,
)


class FakeSearchProvider:
    """
    Controlled search provider for integration testing.
    """

    async def search(
        self,
        query: str,
        max_results: int = 5,
    ) -> list[SearchResult]:
        return [
            SearchResult(
                title="AI Act Update",
                url="https://example.com/ai-act",
                content="European AI regulation update.",
                source="test-provider",
            )
        ]


class FakeIntelligenceProcessor:
    """
    Simulates intelligence synthesis without
    requiring an LLM provider.
    """

    async def summarise(
        self,
        results: list[SearchResult],
        language: str,
        learner_level: str,
    ) -> tuple[str, list[str]]:
        return (
            "The AI Act introduces new rules for "
            "artificial intelligence.",
            [
                "AI governance",
                "risk management",
            ],
        )


class FakeChallengeContentGenerator:
    """
    Simulates educational content generation.
    """

    async def generate_vocabulary(
        self,
        summary: str,
        language: str,
        learner_level: str,
    ) -> list[VocabularyItem]:
        return [
            VocabularyItem(
                term="gouvernance",
                definition=(
                    "Organisation and control of activities."
                ),
            )
        ]

    async def generate_questions(
        self,
        summary: str,
        language: str,
        learner_level: str,
    ) -> list[DiscussionQuestion]:
        return [
            DiscussionQuestion(
                question=(
                    "Pourquoi la gouvernance de l'IA "
                    "est-elle importante ?"
                ),
            )
        ]


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_live_challenge_vertical_slice():
    """
    Validate the complete Sprint H1 backend flow:

    Search Provider
        â†“
    Live Intelligence Agent
        â†“
    Challenge Generator
        â†“
    Learning Challenge
    """

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Step 1 â€” Create Live Intelligence Agent
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    intelligence_agent = LiveIntelligenceAgent(
        search_provider=FakeSearchProvider(),
        processor=FakeIntelligenceProcessor(),
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Step 2 â€” Execute Live Intelligence research
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    intelligence = await intelligence_agent.execute(
        request=LiveIntelligenceRequest(
            topic="AI Act",
        )
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Step 3 â€” Validate intelligence output
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    assert intelligence.topic == "AI Act"

    assert intelligence.summary == (
        "The AI Act introduces new rules for "
        "artificial intelligence."
    )

    assert intelligence.key_information == [
        "AI governance",
        "risk management",
    ]

    assert len(intelligence.sources) == 1
    assert intelligence.sources[0].title == "AI Act Update"
    assert intelligence.sources[0].source == "test-provider"

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Step 4 â€” Create Challenge Generator
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    challenge_generator = ChallengeGenerator(
        content_generator=FakeChallengeContentGenerator()
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Step 5 â€” Generate learning challenge
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    challenge = await challenge_generator.generate(
        intelligence=intelligence,
        language="French",
        learner_level="B2",
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Step 6 â€” Validate final learning output
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    assert challenge.title == (
        "AI Act Learning Challenge"
    )

    assert challenge.topic == "AI Act"
    assert challenge.language == "French"
    assert challenge.learner_level == "B2"

    assert challenge.summary == (
        "The AI Act introduces new rules for "
        "artificial intelligence."
    )

    assert len(challenge.vocabulary) == 1

    assert challenge.vocabulary[0].term == (
        "gouvernance"
    )

    assert (
        challenge.vocabulary[0].definition
        == "Organisation and control of activities."
    )

    assert len(challenge.discussion_questions) == 1

    assert (
        challenge.discussion_questions[0].question
        == "Pourquoi la gouvernance de l'IA est-elle importante ?"
    )

    assert (
        challenge.discussion_questions[0].difficulty
        == "B2"
    )

    assert (
        challenge.created_by_agent
        == "live_intelligence_agent"
    )

TEST FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\tests\integration\test_live_intelligence_governance.py
----------------------------------------------------------------------

"""
Integration test for H3 governance of the H1 Live Intelligence capability.

Flow:

H1 Live Intelligence Capability
        |
        v
Governance Policy
        |
        v
Governance Policy Engine
        |
        v
Governance Decision

This test validates the governance boundary only.

It does not:
- execute the Conversation Agent
- execute the Parallel API
- modify H1 implementation
- modify H2 evaluation
- perform external side effects
"""

from src.governance.engine import (
    GovernancePolicyEngine,
)

from src.governance.models import (
    GovernanceDecision,
    GovernancePolicy,
    RiskLevel,
)


def create_live_intelligence_policy() -> GovernancePolicy:
    """
    Define the governance policy for H1 Live Intelligence.

    Live research:
    - may access external information
    - must not create external side effects
    - is considered medium risk
    - does not require human approval for passive research
    """

    return GovernancePolicy(
        capability="live_intelligence_research",
        risk_level=RiskLevel.MEDIUM,
        requires_human_review=False,
        allows_external_calls=True,
        allows_side_effects=False,
    )


def test_live_intelligence_capability_is_governed():

    engine = GovernancePolicyEngine()

    policy = create_live_intelligence_policy()

    result = engine.evaluate(
        policy
    )

    assert (
        result.capability
        == "live_intelligence_research"
    )

    assert (
        result.risk_level
        == RiskLevel.MEDIUM
    )

    assert (
        result.decision
        == GovernanceDecision.ALLOW
    )


def test_live_intelligence_allows_external_research():

    engine = GovernancePolicyEngine()

    result = engine.evaluate(
        create_live_intelligence_policy()
    )

    assert any(
        "External calls are explicitly permitted."
        in reason
        for reason in result.reasons
    )


def test_live_intelligence_does_not_allow_side_effects():

    policy = create_live_intelligence_policy()

    assert policy.allows_side_effects is False


def test_live_intelligence_with_side_effects_is_blocked():

    engine = GovernancePolicyEngine()

    unsafe_policy = GovernancePolicy(
        capability="live_intelligence_research",
        risk_level=RiskLevel.MEDIUM,
        requires_human_review=False,
        allows_external_calls=True,
        allows_side_effects=True,
    )

    result = engine.evaluate(
        unsafe_policy
    )

    assert (
        result.decision
        == GovernanceDecision.BLOCK
    )


def test_live_intelligence_high_risk_requires_review():

    engine = GovernancePolicyEngine()

    high_risk_policy = GovernancePolicy(
        capability="live_intelligence_research",
        risk_level=RiskLevel.HIGH,
        requires_human_review=False,
        allows_external_calls=True,
        allows_side_effects=False,
    )

    result = engine.evaluate(
        high_risk_policy
    )

    assert (
        result.decision
        == GovernanceDecision.REVIEW
    )

TEST FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\tests\integration\test_parallel_search_provider.py

"""
Integration test for the real Parallel Search Provider.

Sprint H1.8 â€” Real Provider Validation

This test validates:

Application
    â†“
SearchProvider interface
    â†“
ParallelSearchProvider
    â†“
Parallel API
    â†“
Normalised SearchResult

The test is intentionally isolated from:
- Conversation Agent
- Challenge Engine
- Live Intelligence Agent

The test is skipped when PARALLEL_API_KEY is not configured.
"""

import os

import pytest

from src.providers.search_provider.interface import (
    SearchResult,
)
from src.providers.search_provider.parallel_adapter import (
    ParallelSearchProvider,
)


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_parallel_search_provider_returns_normalised_results():
    """
    Validate that the real Parallel provider returns
    normalised SearchResult objects.
    """

    api_key = os.getenv("PARALLEL_API_KEY")

    if not api_key:
        pytest.skip(
            "PARALLEL_API_KEY is not configured"
        )

    provider = ParallelSearchProvider(
        api_key=api_key,
    )

    results = await provider.search(
        query="latest European AI regulation",
        max_results=3,
    )

    assert isinstance(results, list)

    assert len(results) > 0

    for result in results:
        assert isinstance(result, SearchResult)

        assert result.title.strip()
        assert result.url.strip()
        assert result.source.strip()

        # Content may legitimately be empty depending
        # on the provider response, so it is not required
        # for the provider contract test.

TEST FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\tests\integration\test_personalised_discovery_journey.py

"""
Acceptance test for the complete personalised discovery journey.

The test verifies the application-level journey:

Memory
    â†“
DiscoveryService
    â†“
FlashDealCard
    â†“
DiscoveryNotification
    â†“
CommerceAction
    â†“
CommerceService
    â†“
Vendor handoff

No real payment is executed.
"""

import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path


REPOSITORY_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]

DEMO_PATH = (
    REPOSITORY_ROOT
    / "examples"
    / "hackathon_demo.py"
)


def test_personalised_discovery_journey_is_demonstrated():

    result = subprocess.run(
        [
            sys.executable,
            str(DEMO_PATH),
        ],
        cwd=REPOSITORY_ROOT,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
        check=False,
    )

    assert result.returncode == 0, (
        "Personalised discovery journey failed.\n\n"
        f"STDOUT:\n{result.stdout}\n\n"
        f"STDERR:\n{result.stderr}"
    )

    output = result.stdout

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # Discovery
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------

    assert (
        "STEP 4 â€” PERSONALISED DISCOVERY"
        in output
    )

    assert "iPhone 17 Pro" in output

    assert "Demo Vendor" in output

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # Character notification
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------

    assert (
        "CHARACTER NOTIFICATION"
        in output
    )

    assert "Yeti:" in output

    assert (
        "Look what I found!"
        in output
    )

    assert (
        "Why this may interest you:"
        in output
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # Commerce
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------

    assert (
        "STEP 5 â€” COMMERCE ACTION"
        in output
    )

    assert (
        "Commerce action: INITIATE_PURCHASE"
        in output
    )

    assert "Status: REDIRECT" in output

    assert (
        "Approved commerce boundary"
        in output
    )

    assert "Vendor handoff" in output

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # Safety boundary
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------

    assert (
        "Payment is not executed in the hackathon demo."
        in output
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------
    # Final result
    # ---------------------------------------------------------------

    assert (
        "HACKATHON DEMO: SUCCESS"
        in output
    )


def test_original_hackathon_capability_flow_remains_intact():

    result = subprocess.run(
        [
            sys.executable,
            str(DEMO_PATH),
        ],
        cwd=REPOSITORY_ROOT,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
        check=False,
    )

    assert result.returncode == 0

    output = result.stdout

    assert "STEP 1 â€” GOVERNANCE" in output

    assert "STEP 2 â€” LIVE INTELLIGENCE" in output

    assert "STEP 3 â€” AI QUALITY EVALUATION" in output

    # Preserve the original hackathon readiness contract.
    assert (
        "Governance -> Intelligence -> Learning -> Evaluation"
        in output
    )

import pytest

from httpx import ASGITransport, AsyncClient

from backend.api.main import app
from backend.api import routes


class FakeCompanionService:
    """
    Test double for the application service.

    This test deliberately does not invoke Parallel.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        character,
    ):
        self.character = character
        self.received_context = None
        self.received_use_research = None

    async def interact(
        self,
        context,
        use_research=False,
    ):
        self.received_context = context
        self.received_use_research = (
            use_research
        )

        return {
            "character": self.character.name,
            "conversation": {
                "character": self.character.name,
                "message": (
                    f"{self.character.name}: "
                    f"Test response"
                ),
                "intelligence_context": (
                    "TEST INTELLIGENCE"
                    if use_research
                    else None
                ),
            },
            "research": (
                {
                    "query": context.request,
                    "test": True,
                }
                if use_research
                else None
            ),
            "quality": {
                "quality_score": 1.0,
                "passed": True,
                "findings": [],
                "recommendations": [],
            },
            "memory_count": 0,
        }


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_chat_passes_research_false(
    monkeypatch,
):
    """
    Verify /chat passes research=False
    to CompanionService.
    """

    captured = {}

    def fake_factory(
        character,
    ):
        service = FakeCompanionService(
            character
        )

        captured["service"] = service

        return service

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        routes,
        "create_companion_service",
        fake_factory,
    )

    transport = ASGITransport(
        app=app
    )

    async with AsyncClient(
        transport=transport,
        base_url="http://test",
    ) as client:

        response = await client.post(
            "/chat",
            json={
                "user_id": "api-test-user",
                "character": "yeti",
                "message": (
                    "Explain artificial intelligence"
                ),
                "research": False,
            },
        )

    assert response.status_code == 200

    data = response.json()

    service = captured["service"]

    assert (
        service.received_use_research
        is False
    )

    assert (
        service.received_context.user_id
        == "api-test-user"
    )

    assert (
        service.received_context.request
        == "Explain artificial intelligence"
    )

    assert data["character"] == "Yeti"

    assert data["research"] is None

    assert (
        data["conversation"][
            "intelligence_context"
        ]
        is None
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_chat_passes_research_true(
    monkeypatch,
):
    """
    Verify /chat passes research=True
    to CompanionService.

    This is an API contract test and therefore
    intentionally does not call Parallel.
    """

    captured = {}

    def fake_factory(
        character,
    ):
        service = FakeCompanionService(
            character
        )

        captured["service"] = service

        return service

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        routes,
        "create_companion_service",
        fake_factory,
    )

    transport = ASGITransport(
        app=app
    )

    async with AsyncClient(
        transport=transport,
        base_url="http://test",
    ) as client:

        response = await client.post(
            "/chat",
            json={
                "user_id": "api-test-user",
                "character": "yeti",
                "message": (
                    "What are the latest "
                    "European AI regulation "
                    "developments?"
                ),
                "research": True,
            },
        )

    assert response.status_code == 200

    data = response.json()

    service = captured["service"]

    assert (
        service.received_use_research
        is True
    )

    assert (
        service.received_context.user_id
        == "api-test-user"
    )

    assert (
        service.received_context.request
        == (
            "What are the latest "
            "European AI regulation "
            "developments?"
        )
    )

    assert data["character"] == "Yeti"

    assert data["research"] is not None

    assert (
        data["conversation"][
            "intelligence_context"
        ]
        == "TEST INTELLIGENCE"
    )

"""
Tests for Runtime capability errors.
"""

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.errors import (
    CapabilityAlreadyRegisteredError,
    CapabilityExecutionError,
    CapabilityGovernanceError,
    CapabilityNotRegisteredError,
    InvalidCapabilityRequestError,
    RuntimeCapabilityError,
)


def test_all_runtime_errors_extend_runtime_capability_error():

    errors = (
        InvalidCapabilityRequestError,
        CapabilityNotRegisteredError,
        CapabilityAlreadyRegisteredError,
        CapabilityExecutionError,
        CapabilityGovernanceError,
    )

    for error_type in errors:
        assert issubclass(
            error_type,
            RuntimeCapabilityError,
        )

"""
Tests for the AI Runtime capability executor contract.
"""

import pytest

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.executor import (
    CapabilityExecutor,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.models import (
    CapabilityExecutionStatus,
    CapabilityRequest,
    CapabilityResult,
    CapabilityType,
)


class FakeCapabilityExecutor(CapabilityExecutor):
    """
    Minimal executor used only to verify the contract.
    """

    @property
    def capability(self) -> CapabilityType:
        return CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE

    async def execute(
        self,
        request: CapabilityRequest,
    ) -> CapabilityResult:

        return CapabilityResult(
            request_id=request.request_id,
            capability=request.capability,
            status=CapabilityExecutionStatus.COMPLETED,
            output={
                "executed": True,
            },
        )


def test_executor_declares_capability():

    executor = FakeCapabilityExecutor()

    assert (
        executor.capability
        == CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_executor_returns_provider_independent_result():

    executor = FakeCapabilityExecutor()

    request = CapabilityRequest(
        capability=CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE,
        request_id="req-executor-001",
        payload={
            "topic": "AI Act",
        },
    )

    result = await executor.execute(request)

    assert isinstance(
        result,
        CapabilityResult,
    )

    assert (
        result.request_id
        == "req-executor-001"
    )

    assert (
        result.capability
        == CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE
    )

    assert (
        result.status
        == CapabilityExecutionStatus.COMPLETED
    )

    assert result.output == {
        "executed": True,
    }


def test_executor_is_abstract():

    with pytest.raises(TypeError):

        CapabilityExecutor()
