"""
Tests for the AI Runtime Capability Gateway.
"""

import pytest

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.errors import (
    CapabilityExecutionError,
    CapabilityGovernanceError,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.executor import (
    CapabilityExecutor,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.gateway import (
    CapabilityGateway,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.models import (
    CapabilityExecutionStatus,
    CapabilityRequest,
    CapabilityResult,
    CapabilityType,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.registry import (
    CapabilityRegistry,
)


class FakeGovernanceResult:
    """
    Minimal governance result for gateway tests.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        allowed: bool,
    ) -> None:
        self.allowed = allowed


class FakeGovernance:
    """
    Deterministic governance implementation for tests.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        allowed: bool,
    ) -> None:
        self.allowed = allowed
        self.calls = 0

    def evaluate(
        self,
        request: CapabilityRequest,
    ) -> FakeGovernanceResult:

        self.calls += 1

        return FakeGovernanceResult(
            allowed=self.allowed
        )


class FailingGovernance:
    """
    Governance implementation that fails unexpectedly.
    """

    def evaluate(
        self,
        request: CapabilityRequest,
    ) -> FakeGovernanceResult:

        raise RuntimeError(
            "governance infrastructure failure"
        )


class FakeExecutor(CapabilityExecutor):
    """
    Fake executor used to verify gateway orchestration.
    """

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.calls = 0
        self.last_request: CapabilityRequest | None = None

    @property
    def capability(self) -> CapabilityType:
        return CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE

    async def execute(
        self,
        request: CapabilityRequest,
    ) -> CapabilityResult:

        self.calls += 1
        self.last_request = request

        return CapabilityResult(
            request_id=request.request_id,
            capability=request.capability,
            status=CapabilityExecutionStatus.COMPLETED,
            output={
                "executed": True,
            },
        )


class FailingExecutor(CapabilityExecutor):
    """
    Executor that fails unexpectedly.
    """

    @property
    def capability(self) -> CapabilityType:
        return CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE

    async def execute(
        self,
        request: CapabilityRequest,
    ) -> CapabilityResult:

        raise RuntimeError(
            "provider failure"
        )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_gateway_executes_allowed_capability():

    executor = FakeExecutor()

    registry = CapabilityRegistry(
        executors=[executor]
    )

    governance = FakeGovernance(
        allowed=True
    )

    gateway = CapabilityGateway(
        registry=registry,
        governance=governance,
    )

    request = CapabilityRequest(
        capability=CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE,
        request_id="gateway-001",
        payload={
            "topic": "AI Act",
        },
    )

    result = await gateway.execute(
        request
    )

    assert (
        result.status
        == CapabilityExecutionStatus.COMPLETED
    )

    assert result.succeeded is True

    assert governance.calls == 1

    assert executor.calls == 1

    assert executor.last_request is request


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_gateway_rejects_blocked_capability():

    executor = FakeExecutor()

    registry = CapabilityRegistry(
        executors=[executor]
    )

    governance = FakeGovernance(
        allowed=False
    )

    gateway = CapabilityGateway(
        registry=registry,
        governance=governance,
    )

    request = CapabilityRequest(
        capability=CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE,
        request_id="gateway-002",
        payload={
            "topic": "AI Act",
        },
    )

    result = await gateway.execute(
        request
    )

    assert (
        result.status
        == CapabilityExecutionStatus.REJECTED
    )

    assert result.rejected is True

    assert executor.calls == 0

    assert governance.calls == 1

    assert (
        result.metadata["governance_decision"]
        == "rejected"
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_gateway_passes_original_request_to_executor():

    executor = FakeExecutor()

    registry = CapabilityRegistry(
        executors=[executor]
    )

    governance = FakeGovernance(
        allowed=True
    )

    gateway = CapabilityGateway(
        registry=registry,
        governance=governance,
    )

    request = CapabilityRequest(
        capability=CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE,
        request_id="gateway-003",
        payload={
            "topic": "European AI regulation",
            "language": "French",
        },
        metadata={
            "trace_id": "trace-003",
        },
        requested_by="learning_application",
    )

    await gateway.execute(
        request
    )

    assert executor.last_request is request

    assert (
        executor.last_request.payload["language"]
        == "French"
    )

    assert (
        executor.last_request.metadata["trace_id"]
        == "trace-003"
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_governance_is_evaluated_before_execution():

    executor = FakeExecutor()

    registry = CapabilityRegistry(
        executors=[executor]
    )

    governance = FakeGovernance(
        allowed=False
    )

    gateway = CapabilityGateway(
        registry=registry,
        governance=governance,
    )

    request = CapabilityRequest(
        capability=CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE,
        request_id="gateway-004",
        payload={},
    )

    result = await gateway.execute(
        request
    )

    assert result.rejected is True

    assert governance.calls == 1

    assert executor.calls == 0


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_gateway_wraps_governance_failure():

    executor = FakeExecutor()

    registry = CapabilityRegistry(
        executors=[executor]
    )

    gateway = CapabilityGateway(
        registry=registry,
        governance=FailingGovernance(),
    )

    request = CapabilityRequest(
        capability=CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE,
        request_id="gateway-error-001",
        payload={
            "topic": "AI Act",
        },
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        CapabilityGovernanceError,
        match="Governance evaluation failed",
    ):
        await gateway.execute(request)

    assert executor.calls == 0


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_gateway_wraps_executor_failure():

    executor = FailingExecutor()

    registry = CapabilityRegistry(
        executors=[executor]
    )

    gateway = CapabilityGateway(
        registry=registry,
        governance=FakeGovernance(
            allowed=True
        ),
    )

    request = CapabilityRequest(
        capability=CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE,
        request_id="gateway-error-002",
        payload={
            "topic": "AI Act",
        },
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        CapabilityExecutionError,
        match="Capability executor failed",
    ):
        await gateway.execute(request)

"""
Tests for the Runtime Live Intelligence executor adapter.
"""

import pytest

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.live_intelligence_executor import (
    LiveIntelligenceExecutor,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.models import (
    CapabilityExecutionStatus,
    CapabilityRequest,
    CapabilityType,
)


class FakeLiveIntelligenceAgent:
    """
    Fake implementation of the existing agent contract.
    """

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.calls = 0
        self.last_request = None

    async def execute(
        self,
        request,
    ):
        self.calls += 1
        self.last_request = request

        return {
            "topic": request.topic,
            "language": request.language,
            "learner_level": request.learner_level,
            "summary": "AI Act learning content",
        }


def test_executor_declares_live_intelligence():

    executor = LiveIntelligenceExecutor(
        agent=FakeLiveIntelligenceAgent()
    )

    assert (
        executor.capability
        == CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_executor_translates_runtime_request():

    agent = FakeLiveIntelligenceAgent()

    executor = LiveIntelligenceExecutor(
        agent=agent
    )

    request = CapabilityRequest(
        capability=CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE,
        request_id="live-runtime-001",
        payload={
            "topic": "AI Act",
            "language": "French",
            "learner_level": "B2",
        },
    )

    result = await executor.execute(
        request
    )

    assert (
        result.status
        == CapabilityExecutionStatus.COMPLETED
    )

    assert result.succeeded is True

    assert agent.calls == 1

    assert agent.last_request.topic == "AI Act"

    assert (
        agent.last_request.language
        == "French"
    )

    assert (
        agent.last_request.learner_level
        == "B2"
    )

async def test_executor_applies_default_language_and_level():

    agent = FakeLiveIntelligenceAgent()

    executor = LiveIntelligenceExecutor(

    )

        request_id="live-runtime-002",

            "topic": "AI Act",
        },

    )

    )

        == "B2"
    )

    )

    request = CapabilityRequest(

    agent = FakeLiveIntelligenceAgent()

    executor = LiveIntelligenceExecutor(

            request

"""
Tests for AI Runtime capability contracts.
"""

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.models import (
    CapabilityExecutionStatus,
    CapabilityRequest,
    CapabilityResult,
    CapabilityType,
)


def test_capability_request_accepts_valid_request():

    request = CapabilityRequest(
        capability=CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE,
        request_id="req-001",
        payload={
            "topic": "AI Act",
        },
    )

    assert (
        request.capability
        == CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE
    )

    assert request.request_id == "req-001"

    assert request.payload == {
        "topic": "AI Act",
    }

    assert request.requested_by == "application"


def test_capability_request_supports_metadata():

    request = CapabilityRequest(
        capability=CapabilityType.EVALUATION,
        request_id="req-002",
        payload={
            "challenge_id": "challenge-001",
        },
        metadata={
            "trace_id": "trace-001",
        },
        requested_by="learning_application",
    )

    assert request.metadata["trace_id"] == "trace-001"

    assert (
        request.requested_by
        == "learning_application"
    )


def test_empty_request_id_is_rejected():

    try:
        CapabilityRequest(
            capability=CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE,
            request_id="",
            payload={},
        )
    except ValueError as exc:
        assert "request_id" in str(exc)
    else:
        raise AssertionError(
            "Expected empty request_id to be rejected."
        )


def test_empty_requester_is_rejected():

    try:
        CapabilityRequest(
            capability=CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE,
            request_id="req-003",
            payload={},
            requested_by="",
        )
    except ValueError as exc:
        assert "requested_by" in str(exc)
    else:
        raise AssertionError(
            "Expected empty requested_by to be rejected."
        )


def test_capability_result_reports_success():

    result = CapabilityResult(
        request_id="req-004",
        capability=CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE,
        status=CapabilityExecutionStatus.COMPLETED,
        output={
            "summary": "AI Act research",
        },
    )

    assert result.succeeded is True
    assert result.failed is False
    assert result.rejected is False


def test_capability_result_reports_failure():

    result = CapabilityResult(
        request_id="req-005",
        capability=CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE,
        status=CapabilityExecutionStatus.FAILED,
        error="Provider unavailable.",
    )

    assert result.succeeded is False
    assert result.failed is True
    assert result.rejected is False


def test_capability_result_reports_rejection():

    result = CapabilityResult(
        request_id="req-006",
        capability=CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE,
        status=CapabilityExecutionStatus.REJECTED,
        error="Governance policy blocked execution.",
    )

    assert result.succeeded is False
    assert result.failed is False
    assert result.rejected is True


def test_capability_result_preserves_metadata():

    result = CapabilityResult(
        request_id="req-007",
        capability=CapabilityType.EVALUATION,
        status=CapabilityExecutionStatus.ACCEPTED,
        metadata={
            "governance_decision": "allow",
        },
    )

    assert (
        result.metadata["governance_decision"]
        == "allow"
    )

"""
Tests for the AI Runtime capability registry.
"""

import pytest

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.errors import (
    CapabilityAlreadyRegisteredError,
    CapabilityNotRegisteredError,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.executor import (
    CapabilityExecutor,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.models import (
    CapabilityExecutionStatus,
    CapabilityRequest,
    CapabilityResult,
    CapabilityType,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.registry import (
    CapabilityRegistry,
)


class FakeLiveIntelligenceExecutor(
    CapabilityExecutor,
):
    """
    Fake executor used to test dependency injection.
    """

    @property
    def capability(self) -> CapabilityType:
        return CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE

    async def execute(
        self,
        request: CapabilityRequest,
    ) -> CapabilityResult:

        return CapabilityResult(
            request_id=request.request_id,
            capability=request.capability,
            status=CapabilityExecutionStatus.COMPLETED,
            output={"ok": True},
        )


class FakeEvaluationExecutor(
    CapabilityExecutor,
):
    """
    Second fake executor used to verify multiple
    capability registrations.
    """

    @property
    def capability(self) -> CapabilityType:
        return CapabilityType.EVALUATION

    async def execute(
        self,
        request: CapabilityRequest,
    ) -> CapabilityResult:

        return CapabilityResult(
            request_id=request.request_id,
            capability=request.capability,
            status=CapabilityExecutionStatus.COMPLETED,
            output={"evaluated": True},
        )


def test_registry_registers_executor():

    executor = FakeLiveIntelligenceExecutor()

    registry = CapabilityRegistry()

    registry.register(executor)

    assert registry.contains(
        CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE
    )


def test_registry_resolves_registered_executor():

    executor = FakeLiveIntelligenceExecutor()

    registry = CapabilityRegistry(
        executors=[executor]
    )

    resolved = registry.resolve(
        CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE
    )

    assert resolved is executor


def test_registry_supports_multiple_capabilities():

    live_executor = FakeLiveIntelligenceExecutor()
    evaluation_executor = FakeEvaluationExecutor()

    registry = CapabilityRegistry(
        executors=[
            live_executor,
            evaluation_executor,
        ]
    )

    assert registry.resolve(
        CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE
    ) is live_executor

    assert registry.resolve(
        CapabilityType.EVALUATION
    ) is evaluation_executor


def test_duplicate_capability_registration_is_rejected():

    registry = CapabilityRegistry()

    registry.register(
        FakeLiveIntelligenceExecutor()
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        CapabilityAlreadyRegisteredError,
        match="already registered",
    ):
        registry.register(
            FakeLiveIntelligenceExecutor()
        )


def test_unknown_capability_resolution_is_rejected():

    registry = CapabilityRegistry()

    with pytest.raises(
        CapabilityNotRegisteredError,
        match="No executor registered",
    ):
        registry.resolve(
            CapabilityType.LEARNING_GENERATION
        )


def test_contains_returns_false_for_unknown_capability():

    registry = CapabilityRegistry()

    assert (
        registry.contains(
            CapabilityType.EVALUATION
        )
        is False
    )


def test_capabilities_returns_registered_capabilities():

    registry = CapabilityRegistry(
        executors=[
            FakeLiveIntelligenceExecutor(),
            FakeEvaluationExecutor(),
        ]
    )

    capabilities = registry.capabilities()

    assert (
        CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE
        in capabilities
    )

    assert (
        CapabilityType.EVALUATION
        in capabilities
    )

    assert (
        CapabilityType.LEARNING_GENERATION
        not in capabilities
    )

"""
Regression tests for the CommerceService payment boundary.
"""

import pytest

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.payment_pass import (
    PaymentPass,
)

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.service import (
    CommerceService,
)


def make_payment_pass() -> PaymentPass:
    """Create a valid demo payment pass."""

    return PaymentPass(
        payment_pass_id="payment-pass-001",
        deal_id="deal-001",
        user_id="user-001",
        product_name="iPhone 17 Pro",
        vendor_name="Demo Vendor",
        amount=999.99,
        currency="EUR",
        checkout_url=(
            "https://example.com/vendor/iphone17-pro"
        ),
    )


def test_commerce_service_prepares_demo_payment():

    payment_pass = make_payment_pass()

    result = CommerceService().prepare_payment(
        payment_pass
    )

    assert result is payment_pass
    assert result.is_ready is True
    assert result.is_demo_only is True
    assert result.payment_executed is False


def test_commerce_service_does_not_change_payment_pass():

    payment_pass = make_payment_pass()

    result = CommerceService().prepare_payment(
        payment_pass
    )

    assert result.payment_pass_id == payment_pass.payment_pass_id
    assert result.deal_id == payment_pass.deal_id
    assert result.user_id == payment_pass.user_id
    assert result.amount == payment_pass.amount
    assert result.currency == payment_pass.currency
    assert result.checkout_url == payment_pass.checkout_url


def test_commerce_service_rejects_non_ready_payment_pass():

    class InvalidPaymentPass:
        is_ready = False
        is_demo_only = True

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="PaymentPass must be READY",
    ):
        CommerceService().prepare_payment(
            InvalidPaymentPass()
        )


def test_commerce_service_rejects_non_demo_payment():

    class InvalidPaymentPass:
        is_ready = True
        is_demo_only = False

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="DEMO execution mode",
    ):
        CommerceService().prepare_payment(
            InvalidPaymentPass()
        )

"""
Integration test for:

DealCard
    â†“
CommerceAction
    â†“
CommerceService
"""

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.models import (
    CommerceAction,
    CommerceActionStatus,
    CommerceActionType,
)

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.service import (
    CommerceService,
)

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.models import (
    CommerceLink,
    DealCard,
)


def test_deal_can_become_commerce_action():

    link = CommerceLink(
        vendor="Example Vendor",
        destination_url=(
            "https://example.com/product/001"
        ),
    )

    deal = DealCard(
        product_name="Travel Backpack",
        vendor="Example Vendor",
        price=89.0,
        currency="EUR",
        commerce_link=link,
    )

    action = CommerceAction(
        user_id="user-001",
        deal_id=deal.deal_id,
        action_type=(
            CommerceActionType.INITIATE_PURCHASE
        ),
        destination_url=(
            deal.commerce_link.click_url
        ),
    )

    result = CommerceService().initiate(
        action
    )

    assert (
        result.status
        == CommerceActionStatus.REDIRECT
    )

    assert (
        result.deal_id
        == deal.deal_id
    )

    assert (
        result.next_url
        == deal.commerce_link.click_url
    )

"""

"""

import pytest

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.flash_deal_commerce import (
    FlashDealCommerce,

    CommerceActionType,
)

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.flash_deal_demo import (
    build_demo_flash_deal_card,

def test_flash_deal_creates_purchase_action():

    card = build_demo_flash_deal_card()

    action = FlashDealCommerce().create_action(
        user_id="demo-user",
        card=card,
    )

    assert action.user_id == "demo-user"

    assert action.deal_id == card.deal_id

    assert (
        action.action_type
        == CommerceActionType.INITIATE_PURCHASE
    )

        action.destination_url
        == card.get_deal_url
    )

    card = build_demo_flash_deal_card()

    action = FlashDealCommerce().create_action(
        user_id="demo-user",
        card=card,
    )

    assert (
        action.metadata["character_id"]
        == "yeti"
    )

    assert (
        action.metadata["vendor"]
        == card.vendor_name
    )


def test_flash_deal_commerce_rejects_empty_user():

    card = build_demo_flash_deal_card()

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="user_id cannot be empty",
    ):
        FlashDealCommerce().create_action(
            user_id="",
            card=card,
        )

TEST FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\tests\ai_runtime\commerce\test_flash_deal_payment_integration.py

"""
Tests for Flash Deal commerce integration.

These tests verify that a FlashDealCard can produce both:
- the existing CommerceAction
- the new demo-only PaymentPass

No payment is executed.
"""

import pytest

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.flash_deal_commerce import (
    FlashDealCommerce,
)

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.models import (
    CommerceActionType,
)

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.payment_pass import (
    PaymentExecutionMode,
    PaymentPassStatus,
)

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.flash_deal_card import (
    FlashDealCard,
)


def make_card() -> FlashDealCard:
    """Create a deterministic FlashDealCard for tests."""

    return FlashDealCard(
        deal_id="deal-001",
        character_id="yeti",
        character_name="Yeti",
        character_message=(
            "Look what I found!"
        ),
        product_name="iPhone 17 Pro",
        product_image_url=(
            "https://example.com/demo/iphone17-pro.jpg"
        ),
        tagline="Pro. Beautiful. Powerful.",
        highlights=(
            "Pro camera system",
            "High-performance chip",
        ),
        current_price=999.99,
        currency="EUR",
        previous_price=1199.00,
        discount_percentage=17.0,
        savings=199.01,
        relevance_reason=(
            "Matches the user's preference for "
            "powerful mobile photography devices."
        ),
        vendor_name="Demo Vendor",
        more_information_url=(
            "https://example.com/vendor/iphone17-pro"
        ),
        get_deal_url=(
            "https://example.com/vendor/iphone17-pro"
        ),
    )


def test_flash_deal_commerce_still_creates_existing_action():

    card = make_card()

    action = FlashDealCommerce().create_action(
        user_id="user-001",
        card=card,
    )

    assert action.user_id == "user-001"
    assert action.deal_id == "deal-001"
    assert (
        action.action_type
        is CommerceActionType.INITIATE_PURCHASE
    )
    assert (
        action.destination_url
        == card.get_deal_url
    )


def test_flash_deal_creates_demo_payment_pass():

    card = make_card()

    payment_pass = (
        FlashDealCommerce().create_payment_pass(
            user_id="user-001",
            card=card,
            payment_pass_id="payment-pass-001",
        )
    )

    assert (
        payment_pass.payment_pass_id
        == "payment-pass-001"
    )
    assert payment_pass.user_id == "user-001"
    assert payment_pass.deal_id == card.deal_id
    assert payment_pass.product_name == card.product_name
    assert payment_pass.vendor_name == card.vendor_name
    assert payment_pass.amount == card.current_price
    assert payment_pass.currency == card.currency
    assert (
        payment_pass.checkout_url
        == card.get_deal_url
    )

    assert (
        payment_pass.status
        is PaymentPassStatus.READY
    )

    assert (
        payment_pass.execution_mode
        is PaymentExecutionMode.DEMO
    )

    assert payment_pass.payment_executed is False


def test_flash_deal_payment_pass_preserves_checkout_boundary():

    card = make_card()

    payment_pass = (
        FlashDealCommerce().create_payment_pass(
            user_id="user-001",
            card=card,
        )
    )

    assert (
        payment_pass.checkout_url
        == card.get_deal_url
    )

    assert payment_pass.payment_executed is False


def test_flash_deal_payment_pass_rejects_empty_user():

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="Commerce user_id cannot be empty",
    ):
        FlashDealCommerce().create_payment_pass(
            user_id="",
            card=make_card(),
        )

"""
Tests for the hackathon PaymentPass.

The PaymentPass is a demo-only checkout handoff.

These tests verify:
- valid construction
- immutable/read-only behaviour
- READY status
- DEMO execution mode
- no executed payment
- validation
- safe checkout URL contract
- deterministic demo rendering
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.payment_pass import (
    PaymentExecutionMode,
    PaymentPass,
    PaymentPassStatus,
)


def make_payment_pass() -> PaymentPass:
    """Create a valid PaymentPass for tests."""

    return PaymentPass(
        payment_pass_id="payment-pass-001",
        deal_id="deal-001",
        user_id="user-001",
        product_name="iPhone 17 Pro",
        vendor_name="Demo Vendor",
        amount=999.99,
        currency="EUR",
        checkout_url=(
            "https://example.com/vendor/iphone17-pro"
        ),
    )


def test_payment_pass_creates_valid_demo_handoff():

    payment_pass = make_payment_pass()

    assert (
        payment_pass.payment_pass_id
        == "payment-pass-001"
    )
    assert payment_pass.deal_id == "deal-001"
    assert payment_pass.user_id == "user-001"
    assert payment_pass.product_name == "iPhone 17 Pro"
    assert payment_pass.vendor_name == "Demo Vendor"
    assert payment_pass.amount == 999.99
    assert payment_pass.currency == "EUR"
    assert (
        payment_pass.checkout_url
        == "https://example.com/vendor/iphone17-pro"
    )


def test_payment_pass_is_ready():

    payment_pass = make_payment_pass()

    assert payment_pass.status is PaymentPassStatus.READY
    assert payment_pass.is_ready is True


def test_payment_pass_is_demo_only():

    payment_pass = make_payment_pass()

    assert (
        payment_pass.execution_mode
        is PaymentExecutionMode.DEMO
    )
    assert payment_pass.is_demo_only is True


def test_payment_pass_never_reports_payment_as_executed():

    payment_pass = make_payment_pass()

    assert payment_pass.payment_executed is False


def test_payment_pass_formats_amount():

    payment_pass = make_payment_pass()

    assert (
        payment_pass.display_amount()
        == "EUR 999.99"
    )


def test_payment_pass_renders_demo_handoff():

    payment_pass = make_payment_pass()

    output = payment_pass.render_demo()

    assert "PAYMENT PASS" in output
    assert "Product: iPhone 17 Pro" in output
    assert "Vendor: Demo Vendor" in output
    assert "Amount: EUR 999.99" in output
    assert "Payment status: READY" in output
    assert "Execution mode: DEMO" in output
    assert "[ PROCEED TO CHECKOUT ]" in output
    assert "No payment has been executed." in output
    assert (
        "Vendor checkout remains outside "
        "the hackathon demo."
        in output
    )


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "field,value",
    [
        ("payment_pass_id", ""),
        ("deal_id", ""),
        ("user_id", ""),
        ("product_name", ""),
        ("vendor_name", ""),
        ("currency", ""),
        ("checkout_url", ""),
    ],
)
def test_payment_pass_rejects_empty_required_text(
    field: str,
    value: str,
):

    values = {
        "payment_pass_id": "payment-pass-001",
        "deal_id": "deal-001",
        "user_id": "user-001",
        "product_name": "iPhone 17 Pro",
        "vendor_name": "Demo Vendor",
        "amount": 999.99,
        "currency": "EUR",
        "checkout_url": (
            "https://example.com/vendor/iphone17-pro"
        ),
    }

    values[field] = value

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match=f"PaymentPass {field} cannot be empty",
    ):
        PaymentPass(**values)


def test_payment_pass_rejects_negative_amount():

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="amount cannot be negative",
    ):
        PaymentPass(
            payment_pass_id="payment-pass-001",
            deal_id="deal-001",
            user_id="user-001",
            product_name="iPhone 17 Pro",
            vendor_name="Demo Vendor",
            amount=-1.0,
            currency="EUR",
            checkout_url=(
                "https://example.com/vendor/iphone17-pro"
            ),
        )


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "checkout_url",
    [
        "ftp://example.com/checkout",
        "vendor.example.com/checkout",
    ],
)
def test_payment_pass_rejects_non_http_checkout_url(
    checkout_url: str,
):

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="checkout_url must use HTTP or HTTPS",
    ):
        PaymentPass(
            payment_pass_id="payment-pass-001",
            deal_id="deal-001",
            user_id="user-001",
            product_name="iPhone 17 Pro",
            vendor_name="Demo Vendor",
            amount=999.99,
            currency="EUR",
            checkout_url=checkout_url,
        )


def test_payment_pass_rejects_non_demo_execution_mode():

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="execution_mode must be DEMO",
    ):
        PaymentPass(
            payment_pass_id="payment-pass-001",
            deal_id="deal-001",
            user_id="user-001",
            product_name="iPhone 17 Pro",
            vendor_name="Demo Vendor",
            amount=999.99,
            currency="EUR",
            checkout_url=(
                "https://example.com/vendor/iphone17-pro"
            ),
            execution_mode="LIVE",
        )


def test_payment_pass_rejects_non_ready_status():

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="status must be READY",
    ):
        PaymentPass(
            payment_pass_id="payment-pass-001",
            deal_id="deal-001",
            user_id="user-001",
            product_name="iPhone 17 Pro",
            vendor_name="Demo Vendor",
            amount=999.99,
            currency="EUR",
            checkout_url=(
                "https://example.com/vendor/iphone17-pro"
            ),
            status="EXECUTED",
        )


def test_payment_pass_is_immutable():

    payment_pass = make_payment_pass()

    with pytest.raises(AttributeError):
        payment_pass.amount = 100.0

"""
Tests for the hackathon Commerce Service.
"""

import pytest

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.models import (
    CommerceAction,
    CommerceActionResult,
    CommerceActionStatus,
    CommerceActionType,
)

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.service import (
    CommerceService,
)


def make_action() -> CommerceAction:
    return CommerceAction(
        user_id="user-001",
        deal_id="deal-001",
        action_type=(
            CommerceActionType.INITIATE_PURCHASE
        ),
        destination_url=(
            "https://example.com/product/001"
        ),
    )


def test_commerce_action_is_created():

    action = make_action()

    assert action.user_id == "user-001"

    assert action.deal_id == "deal-001"

    assert (
        action.action_type
        == CommerceActionType.INITIATE_PURCHASE
    )

    assert (
        action.destination_url
        == "https://example.com/product/001"
    )

    assert action.action_id


def test_commerce_service_returns_vendor_handoff():

    action = make_action()

    result = CommerceService().initiate(
        action
    )

    assert isinstance(
        result,
        CommerceActionResult,
    )

    assert (
        result.status
        == CommerceActionStatus.REDIRECT
    )

    assert (
        result.next_url
        == action.destination_url
    )

    assert result.deal_id == action.deal_id


def test_commerce_action_rejects_empty_user():

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="user_id cannot be empty",
    ):
        CommerceAction(
            user_id="",
            deal_id="deal-001",
            action_type=(
                CommerceActionType.INITIATE_PURCHASE
            ),
            destination_url=(
                "https://example.com/product/001"
            ),
        )


def test_commerce_action_rejects_invalid_url():

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="HTTP or HTTPS",
    ):
        CommerceAction(
            user_id="user-001",
            deal_id="deal-001",
            action_type=(
                CommerceActionType.INITIATE_PURCHASE
            ),
            destination_url="invalid-url",
        )

"""
Tests for the hackathon Discovery domain models.
"""

import pytest

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.models import (
    CommerceLink,
    CommerceLinkType,
    DealCard,
    FlashIdea,
)


def test_commerce_link_uses_vendor_destination():

    link = CommerceLink(
        vendor="Example Vendor",
        destination_url=(
            "https://example.com/product/123"
        ),
    )

    assert link.vendor == "Example Vendor"

    assert (
        link.destination_url
        == "https://example.com/product/123"
    )

    assert (
        link.link_type
        == CommerceLinkType.DIRECT
    )

    assert (
        link.click_url
        == link.destination_url
    )


def test_commerce_link_supports_affiliate_url():

    link = CommerceLink(
        vendor="Example Vendor",
        destination_url=(
            "https://example.com/product/123"
        ),
        link_type=CommerceLinkType.AFFILIATE,
        tracking_url=(
            "https://example.com/track/123"
        ),
    )

    assert (
        link.link_type
        == CommerceLinkType.AFFILIATE
    )

    assert (
        link.click_url
        == "https://example.com/track/123"
    )


def test_commerce_link_rejects_invalid_url():

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="HTTP or HTTPS",
    ):
        CommerceLink(
            vendor="Example Vendor",
            destination_url="not-a-url",
        )


def test_deal_card_contains_external_vendor_link():

    link = CommerceLink(
        vendor="Example Vendor",
        destination_url=(
            "https://example.com/product/123"
        ),
    )

    deal = DealCard(
        product_name="Travel Backpack",
        vendor="Example Vendor",
        price=89.0,
        currency="EUR",
        commerce_link=link,
        previous_price=129.0,
        discount_percentage=31.0,
        source="parallel",
    )

    assert deal.product_name == "Travel Backpack"

    assert deal.vendor == "Example Vendor"

    assert deal.price == 89.0

    assert deal.currency == "EUR"

    assert deal.commerce_link.click_url == (
        "https://example.com/product/123"
    )

    assert deal.deal_id


def test_deal_card_rejects_negative_price():

    link = CommerceLink(
        vendor="Example Vendor",
        destination_url=(
            "https://example.com/product/123"
        ),
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="price cannot be negative",
    ):
        DealCard(
            product_name="Travel Backpack",
            vendor="Example Vendor",
            price=-1,
            currency="EUR",
            commerce_link=link,
        )


def test_deal_card_rejects_vendor_mismatch():

    link = CommerceLink(
        vendor="Vendor A",
        destination_url=(
            "https://example.com/product/123"
        ),
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="vendor must match",
    ):
        DealCard(
            product_name="Travel Backpack",
            vendor="Vendor B",
            price=89.0,
            currency="EUR",
            commerce_link=link,
        )


def test_flash_idea_connects_character_to_deal():

    idea = FlashIdea(
        character_id="yeti",
        deal_id="deal-001",
        title="Yeti found this",
        message=(
            "I found a travel backpack "
            "that matches your style."
        ),
    )

    assert idea.character_id == "yeti"

    assert idea.deal_id == "deal-001"

    assert (
        idea.title
        == "Yeti found this"
    )

    assert (
        "travel backpack"
        in idea.message
    )

    assert idea.idea_id

"""
Tests for DiscoveryNotification.
"""

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.flash_deal_demo import (
    build_demo_flash_deal_card,
)

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.notification import (
    DiscoveryNotification,
)


def test_notification_is_created_from_flash_deal():

    deal = build_demo_flash_deal_card()

    notification = (
        DiscoveryNotification.from_flash_deal(
            notification_id="notification-001",
            user_id="demo-user",
            deal=deal,
        )
    )

    assert notification.notification_id == (
        "notification-001"
    )

    assert notification.user_id == "demo-user"

    assert notification.character_id == "yeti"

    assert notification.character_name == "Yeti"

    assert notification.product_name == (
        deal.product_name
    )


def test_notification_preserves_flash_deal_identity():

    deal = build_demo_flash_deal_card()

    notification = (
        DiscoveryNotification.from_flash_deal(
            notification_id="notification-002",
            user_id="demo-user",
            deal=deal,
        )
    )

    assert notification.deal is deal

    assert notification.get_deal_url == (
        deal.get_deal_url
    )

    assert notification.more_information_url == (
        deal.more_information_url
    )


def test_notification_contains_personalisation():

    deal = build_demo_flash_deal_card()

    notification = (
        DiscoveryNotification.from_flash_deal(
            notification_id="notification-003",
            user_id="demo-user",
            deal=deal,
        )
    )

    assert notification.relevance_reason == (
        deal.relevance_reason
    )


def test_notification_renders_compact_message():

    deal = build_demo_flash_deal_card()

    notification = (
        DiscoveryNotification.from_flash_deal(
            notification_id="notification-004",
            user_id="demo-user",
            deal=deal,
        )
    )

    output = notification.render()

    assert "Yeti" in output

    assert "iPhone 17 Pro" in output

    assert "Why this may interest you:" in output

    assert deal.relevance_reason in output


def test_notification_rejects_empty_notification_id():

    deal = build_demo_flash_deal_card()

    try:
        DiscoveryNotification.from_flash_deal(
            notification_id="",
            user_id="demo-user",
            deal=deal,
        )
    except ValueError as exc:
        assert "notification_id cannot be empty" in str(exc)
    else:
        raise AssertionError(
            "Expected ValueError"
        )


def test_notification_rejects_empty_user_id():

    deal = build_demo_flash_deal_card()

    try:
        DiscoveryNotification.from_flash_deal(
            notification_id="notification-005",
            user_id="",
            deal=deal,
        )
    except ValueError as exc:
        assert "user_id cannot be empty" in str(exc)
    else:
        raise AssertionError(
            "Expected ValueError"
        )

"""
Tests for the hackathon DiscoveryService.

Flow:

DealCard
    +
Memory
    +
CharacterContext
    â†“
DiscoveryService
    â†“
FlashDealCard
"""

import pytest

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.models import (
    CommerceLink,
    DealCard,
)

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.service import (
    CharacterContext,
    DiscoveryRequest,
    DiscoveryService,
)

from src.ai_runtime.memory.models import (
    MemoryEntry,
    MemoryType,
)


def make_deal() -> DealCard:
    """
    Create deterministic discovery data for tests.
    """

    return DealCard(
        product_name="iPhone 17 Pro",
        vendor="Demo Vendor",
        price=999.99,
        currency="EUR",
        previous_price=1199.00,
        discount_percentage=17.0,
        commerce_link=CommerceLink(
            vendor="Demo Vendor",
            destination_url=(
                "https://example.com/product"
            ),
        ),
        source="test",
        description="Test discovery product.",
    )


def make_request() -> DiscoveryRequest:
    """
    Create a deterministic discovery request.
    """

    return DiscoveryRequest(
        user_id="user-001",
        deal=make_deal(),
        character=CharacterContext(
            character_id="yeti",
            character_name="Yeti",
            message=(
                "Look what I found! "
                "This could be perfect for you."
            ),
        ),
        product_image_url=(
            "https://example.com/product.jpg"
        ),
        tagline="Pro. Beautiful. Powerful.",
        highlights=(
            "Pro camera system",
            "High-performance chip",
            "All-day battery life",
        ),
    )


def make_preference_memory() -> MemoryEntry:
    """
    Create explicit user preference memory.
    """

    return MemoryEntry(
        user_id="user-001",
        memory_type=MemoryType.PREFERENCE,
        content=(
            "prefer powerful mobile photography "
            "devices"
        ),
    )


def test_discovery_service_creates_flash_deal():

    request = make_request()

    result = DiscoveryService().create_flash_deal(
        request
    )

    assert result.deal_id == request.deal.deal_id

    assert result.product_name == "iPhone 17 Pro"

    assert result.vendor_name == "Demo Vendor"

    assert result.character_id == "yeti"

    assert result.character_name == "Yeti"

    assert result.current_price == 999.99

    assert result.discount_percentage == 17.0


def test_discovery_service_applies_preference_memory():

    request = make_request()

    memory = make_preference_memory()

    result = DiscoveryService().create_flash_deal(
        request,
        memories=[memory],
    )

    assert (
        "powerful mobile photography devices"
        in result.relevance_reason
    )


def test_discovery_service_does_not_require_memory():

    request = make_request()

    result = DiscoveryService().create_flash_deal(
        request,
        memories=[],
    )

    assert result.relevance_reason == (
        "This discovery may be relevant to your interests."
    )


def test_discovery_service_ignores_non_preference_memory():

    request = make_request()

    non_preference_type = next(
        memory_type
        for memory_type in MemoryType
        if memory_type != MemoryType.PREFERENCE
    )

    memory = MemoryEntry(
        user_id="user-001",
        memory_type=non_preference_type,
        content=(
            "Discussed photography during the "
            "previous conversation."
        ),
    )

    result = DiscoveryService().create_flash_deal(
        request,
        memories=[memory],
    )

    assert result.relevance_reason == (
        "This discovery may be relevant to your interests."
    )


def test_discovery_service_preserves_deal_identity():

    deal = make_deal()

    request = DiscoveryRequest(
        user_id="user-001",
        deal=deal,
        character=CharacterContext(
            character_id="yeti",
            character_name="Yeti",
            message="I found something for you.",
        ),
        product_image_url=(
            "https://example.com/product.jpg"
        ),
        tagline="A useful discovery.",
        highlights=(
            "Useful",
            "Practical",
        ),
    )

    result = DiscoveryService().create_flash_deal(
        request
    )

    assert result.deal_id == deal.deal_id


def test_discovery_service_preserves_character_context():

    request = make_request()

    result = DiscoveryService().create_flash_deal(
        request
    )

    assert result.character_id == "yeti"

    assert result.character_name == "Yeti"

    assert result.character_message == (
        "Look what I found! "
        "This could be perfect for you."
    )


def test_discovery_service_rejects_empty_user_id():

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="user_id cannot be empty",
    ):
        DiscoveryRequest(
            user_id="",
            deal=make_deal(),
            character=CharacterContext(
                character_id="yeti",
                character_name="Yeti",
                message="I found something.",
            ),
            product_image_url=(
                "https://example.com/product.jpg"
            ),
            tagline="A useful discovery.",
            highlights=("Useful",),
        )


def test_character_context_rejects_empty_character_id():

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="character_id cannot be empty",
    ):
        CharacterContext(
            character_id="",
            character_name="Yeti",
            message="I found something.",
        )


def test_discovery_request_rejects_empty_highlights():

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="at least one highlight",
    ):
        DiscoveryRequest(
            user_id="user-001",
            deal=make_deal(),
            character=CharacterContext(
                character_id="yeti",
                character_name="Yeti",
                message="I found something.",
            ),
            product_image_url=(
                "https://example.com/product.jpg"
            ),
            tagline="A useful discovery.",
            highlights=(),
        )

"""
Tests for the hackathon Flash Deal Card presentation contract.

The tests validate the user-facing card described by the
hackathon UX:

Character
    â†“
Product
    â†“
Pricing / Deal
    â†“
Why relevant to you
    â†“
Vendor
    â†“
GET DEAL / MORE INFORMATION
"""

import pytest

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.flash_deal_card import (
    FlashDealCard,
)

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.models import (
    CommerceLink,
    CommerceLinkType,
    DealCard,
)


def make_deal(
    *,
    affiliate: bool = False,
) -> DealCard:
    link = CommerceLink(
        vendor="eBay",
        destination_url=(
            "https://www.ebay.com/itm/example"
        ),
        link_type=(
            CommerceLinkType.AFFILIATE
            if affiliate
            else CommerceLinkType.DIRECT
        ),
        tracking_url=(
            "https://tracking.example.com/deal/001"
            if affiliate
            else None
        ),
    )

    return DealCard(
        product_name="iPhone 17 Pro",
        vendor="eBay",
        price=999.99,
        currency="EUR",
        previous_price=1199.00,
        discount_percentage=17.0,
        commerce_link=link,
        source="parallel",
        description="Pro smartphone with advanced camera system.",
    )


def test_flash_deal_card_is_created_from_deal():

    deal = make_deal()

    card = FlashDealCard.from_deal(
        deal=deal,
        character_id="yeti",
        character_name="Yeti",
        character_message="Look what I found!",
        relevance_reason=(
            "This matches your interest in powerful "
            "mobile photography."
        ),
        product_image_url=(
            "https://example.com/images/iphone17.jpg"
        ),
        tagline="Pro. Beautiful. Powerful.",
        highlights=[
            "Pro camera system",
            "A19 Bionic chip",
            "All-day battery life",
            "6.3-inch Super Retina XDR display",
        ],
    )

    assert card.deal_id == deal.deal_id
    assert card.character_id == "yeti"
    assert card.character_name == "Yeti"

    assert card.product_name == "iPhone 17 Pro"
    assert card.tagline == "Pro. Beautiful. Powerful."

    assert len(card.highlights) == 4

    assert card.current_price == 999.99
    assert card.previous_price == 1199.00
    assert card.discount_percentage == 17.0

    assert card.savings == 199.01

    assert card.vendor_name == "eBay"

    assert (
        card.more_information_url
        == "https://www.ebay.com/itm/example"
    )

    assert (
        card.get_deal_url
        == "https://www.ebay.com/itm/example"
    )


def test_flash_deal_card_contains_personal_relevance():

    deal = make_deal()

    card = FlashDealCard.from_deal(
        deal=deal,
        character_id="yeti",
        character_name="Yeti",
        character_message="Look what I found!",
        relevance_reason=(
            "You often look for strong mobile "

        ),
        product_image_url=(
            "https://example.com/images/iphone17.jpg"

        highlights=[
            "Pro camera system",
        ],
    )

    assert (
        card.relevance_reason
        == "You often look for strong mobile "
        "photography performance."
    )


def test_flash_deal_card_exposes_discount():

    card = FlashDealCard.from_deal(
        deal=deal,
        character_id="yeti",
        character_name="Yeti",
        character_message="Look what I found!",

        product_image_url=(
            "https://example.com/images/iphone17.jpg"
        ),
        tagline="Pro. Beautiful. Powerful.",
        highlights=["Pro camera system"],
    )

    assert card.has_discount is True
    assert card.has_previous_price is True

    assert card.display_discount == "17% OFF"
    assert card.display_savings == "Save EUR 199.01"

def test_flash_deal_card_allows_no_discount():

    deal = DealCard(
        product_name="Travel Backpack",
        vendor="Example Vendor",
        price=89.00,
        currency="EUR",
        commerce_link=CommerceLink(
            vendor="Example Vendor",
            destination_url=(
                "https://example.com/backpack"
            ),
        ),
    )

    card = FlashDealCard.from_deal(
        deal=deal,
        character_id="yeti",
        character_name="Yeti",
        character_message="I found something for you.",
        relevance_reason="This matches your travel interests.",
        product_image_url=(
            "https://example.com/images/backpack.jpg"
        ),
        tagline="Lightweight and practical.",
        highlights=[
            "Lightweight",
            "Water resistant",
        ],
    )

    assert card.has_discount is False
    assert card.has_previous_price is False
    assert card.display_discount is None
    assert card.display_savings is None
    assert card.savings is None

"""
Tests for the FlashDealPresenter.

The presenter must remain a pure presentation layer over
FlashDealCard.
"""

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.flash_deal_demo import (
    build_demo_flash_deal_card,
)

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.flash_deal_presenter import (
    FlashDealPresenter,
)


def test_presenter_renders_character():

    card = build_demo_flash_deal_card()

    output = FlashDealPresenter().render(card)

    assert "## Yeti" in output

    assert (
        "Look what I found!"
        in output
    )


def test_presenter_renders_product():

    card = build_demo_flash_deal_card()

    output = FlashDealPresenter().render(card)

    assert "iPhone 17 Pro" in output

    assert (
        "Pro. Beautiful. Powerful."
        in output
    )


def test_presenter_renders_highlights():

    card = build_demo_flash_deal_card()

    output = FlashDealPresenter().render(card)

    assert "### HIGHLIGHTS" in output

    for highlight in card.highlights:
        assert highlight in output


def test_presenter_renders_verified_pricing():

    card = build_demo_flash_deal_card()

    output = FlashDealPresenter().render(card)

    assert "### PRICE" in output

    assert (
        f"{card.currency} "
        f"{card.current_price:.2f}"
        in output
    )

    assert (
        f"{card.currency} "
        f"{card.previous_price:.2f}"
        in output
    )

    assert (
        card.display_discount
        in output
    )

    assert (
        card.display_savings
        in output
    )


def test_presenter_renders_personalisation():

    card = build_demo_flash_deal_card()

    output = FlashDealPresenter().render(card)

    assert (
        "### WHY THIS IS RELEVANT TO YOU"
        in output
    )

    assert (
        card.relevance_reason
        in output
    )


def test_presenter_renders_vendor():

    card = build_demo_flash_deal_card()

    output = FlashDealPresenter().render(card)

    assert "### AVAILABLE FROM" in output

    assert card.vendor_name in output


def test_presenter_renders_commerce_actions():

    card = build_demo_flash_deal_card()

    output = FlashDealPresenter().render(card)

    assert "### ACTIONS" in output

    assert (
        f"[GET DEAL]({card.get_deal_url})"
        in output
    )

    assert (
        "[MORE INFORMATION]"
        f"({card.more_information_url})"
        in output
    )


def test_presenter_does_not_mutate_card():

    card = build_demo_flash_deal_card()

    original = {
        "deal_id": card.deal_id,
        "product_name": card.product_name,
        "price": card.current_price,
        "vendor": card.vendor_name,
        "relevance": card.relevance_reason,
        "get_deal_url": card.get_deal_url,
        "more_information_url": (
            card.more_information_url
        ),
    }

    FlashDealPresenter().render(card)

    assert card.deal_id == original["deal_id"]

    assert (
        card.product_name
        == original["product_name"]
    )

    assert (
        card.current_price
        == original["price"]
    )

    assert (
        card.vendor_name
        == original["vendor"]
    )

    assert (
        card.relevance_reason
        == original["relevance"]
    )

    assert (
        card.get_deal_url
        == original["get_deal_url"]
    )

    assert (
        card.more_information_url
        == original["more_information_url"]
    )

"""
Tests for personalised Flash Ideas.
"""

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.flash_idea import (
    FlashIdeaBuilder,
)

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.models import (
    CommerceLink,
    DealCard,
)

from src.ai_runtime.memory.models import (
    MemoryEntry,
    MemoryType,
)


def make_deal() -> DealCard:
    link = CommerceLink(
        vendor="Example Vendor",
        destination_url=(
            "https://example.com/product/backpack"
        ),
    )

    return DealCard(
        product_name="Minimalist Travel Backpack",
        vendor="Example Vendor",
        price=89.0,
        currency="EUR",
        previous_price=129.0,
        discount_percentage=31.0,
        commerce_link=link,
        source="parallel",
    )


def test_flash_idea_uses_user_preference():

    memory = MemoryEntry(
        user_id="user-001",
        memory_type=MemoryType.PREFERENCE,
        content="prefers minimalist travel products",
    )

    idea = FlashIdeaBuilder().build(
        character_id="yeti",
        deal=make_deal(),
        memories=[memory],
    )

    assert idea.character_id == "yeti"

    assert (
        idea.deal_id
        == make_deal().deal_id
        or idea.deal_id
    )

    assert (
        "minimalist travel products"
        in idea.message
    )

    assert (
        "Minimalist Travel Backpack"
        in idea.message
    )

    assert (
        "31% off"
        in idea.message
    )


def test_flash_idea_works_without_memory():

    idea = FlashIdeaBuilder().build(
        character_id="yeti",
        deal=make_deal(),
        memories=[],
    )

    assert (
        "I found something you might like"
        in idea.message
    )

    assert (
        "Minimalist Travel Backpack"
        in idea.message
    )


def test_flash_idea_contains_commerce_url():

    idea = FlashIdeaBuilder().build(
        character_id="yeti",
        deal=make_deal(),
        memories=[],
    )

    assert (
        idea.metadata["commerce_url"]
        == "https://example.com/product/backpack"
    )


def test_non_preference_memory_does_not_become_preference():

    memory = MemoryEntry(
        user_id="user-001",
        memory_type=MemoryType.DISCOVERY_CONTEXT,
        content="Looking for travel gear",
    )

    idea = FlashIdeaBuilder().build(
        character_id="yeti",
        deal=make_deal(),
        memories=[memory],
    )

    assert (
        "I found something you might like"
        in idea.message
    )

"""
Tests for the hackathon Memory contracts.
"""

from datetime import datetime

import pytest

from src.ai_runtime.memory.models import (
    MemoryEntry,
    MemoryType,
)


def test_memory_entry_creates_valid_preference():

    memory = MemoryEntry(
        user_id="user-001",
        memory_type=MemoryType.PREFERENCE,
        content="Prefers lightweight laptops.",
    )

    assert memory.user_id == "user-001"

    assert (
        memory.memory_type
        == MemoryType.PREFERENCE
    )

    assert (
        memory.content
        == "Prefers lightweight laptops."
    )

    assert memory.memory_id

    assert isinstance(
        memory.created_at,
        datetime,
    )


def test_memory_entry_supports_discovery_context():

    memory = MemoryEntry(
        user_id="user-001",
        memory_type=MemoryType.DISCOVERY_CONTEXT,
        content="Looking for a laptop under EUR 1000.",
    )

    assert (
        memory.memory_type
        == MemoryType.DISCOVERY_CONTEXT
    )


def test_memory_entry_supports_saved_items():

    memory = MemoryEntry(
        user_id="user-001",
        memory_type=MemoryType.SAVED_ITEM,
        content="MacBook Air M4",
        metadata={
            "category": "laptop",
        },
    )

    assert (
        memory.memory_type
        == MemoryType.SAVED_ITEM
    )

    assert (
        memory.metadata["category"]
        == "laptop"
    )


def test_memory_requires_user_id():

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="user_id cannot be empty",
    ):
        MemoryEntry(
            user_id="",
            memory_type=MemoryType.PREFERENCE,
            content="Prefers lightweight products.",
        )


def test_memory_requires_content():

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="content cannot be empty",
    ):
        MemoryEntry(
            user_id="user-001",
            memory_type=MemoryType.PREFERENCE,
            content="",
        )


def test_memory_to_dict_is_persistence_safe():

    memory = MemoryEntry(
        user_id="user-001",
        memory_type=MemoryType.PREFERENCE,
        content="Prefers lightweight laptops.",
    )

    data = memory.to_dict()

    assert data["memory_id"] == memory.memory_id

    assert data["user_id"] == "user-001"

    assert (
        data["memory_type"]
        == "preference"
    )

    assert (
        data["content"]
        == "Prefers lightweight laptops."
    )

    assert isinstance(
        data["created_at"],
        str,
    )

    assert isinstance(
        data["updated_at"],
        str,
    )

"""
Tests for the hackathon Memory Service.
"""

from src.ai_runtime.memory.models import (
    MemoryType,
)

from src.ai_runtime.memory.service import (
    MemoryService,
)

from src.ai_runtime.memory.store import (
    MemoryStore,
)


def test_memory_service_remembers_preference(
    tmp_path,
):

    store = MemoryStore(
        tmp_path / "memory.db"
    )

    service = MemoryService(
        store=store
    )

    memory = service.remember(
        user_id="user-001",
        memory_type=MemoryType.PREFERENCE,
        content="Prefers minimalist products.",
    )

    assert memory.user_id == "user-001"

    assert (
        memory.memory_type
        == MemoryType.PREFERENCE
    )

    assert (
        memory.content
        == "Prefers minimalist products."
    )


def test_memory_service_retrieves_preferences(
    tmp_path,
):

    service = MemoryService(
        store=MemoryStore(
            tmp_path / "memory.db"
        )
    )

    service.remember(
        user_id="user-001",
        memory_type=MemoryType.PREFERENCE,
        content="Likes minimalist design.",
    )

    service.remember(
        user_id="user-001",
        memory_type=MemoryType.DISCOVERY_CONTEXT,
        content="Looking for travel gear.",
    )

    preferences = service.preferences(
        user_id="user-001"
    )

    assert len(preferences) == 1

    assert (
        preferences[0].content
        == "Likes minimalist design."
    )


def test_memory_service_retrieves_discovery_context(
    tmp_path,
):

    service = MemoryService(
        store=MemoryStore(
            tmp_path / "memory.db"
        )
    )

    service.remember(
        user_id="user-001",
        memory_type=MemoryType.DISCOVERY_CONTEXT,
        content="Looking for lightweight travel gear.",
    )

    contexts = service.discovery_context(
        user_id="user-001"
    )

    assert len(contexts) == 1

    assert (
        contexts[0].content
        == "Looking for lightweight travel gear."
    )


def test_memory_service_retrieves_saved_items(
    tmp_path,
):

    service = MemoryService(
        store=MemoryStore(
            tmp_path / "memory.db"
        )
    )

    service.remember(
        user_id="user-001",
        memory_type=MemoryType.SAVED_ITEM,
        content="Minimalist travel backpack",
        metadata={
            "price": "89",
            "currency": "EUR",
        },
    )

    saved_items = service.saved_items(
        user_id="user-001"
    )

    assert len(saved_items) == 1

    assert (
        saved_items[0].content
        == "Minimalist travel backpack"
    )

    assert (
        saved_items[0].metadata["price"]
        == "89"
    )


def test_memory_service_does_not_mix_users(
    tmp_path,
):

    service = MemoryService(
        store=MemoryStore(
            tmp_path / "memory.db"
        )
    )

    service.remember(
        user_id="user-001",
        memory_type=MemoryType.PREFERENCE,
        content="Likes hiking products.",
    )

    service.remember(
        user_id="user-002",
        memory_type=MemoryType.PREFERENCE,
        content="Likes photography products.",
    )

    memories = service.list_for_user(
        user_id="user-001"
    )

    assert len(memories) == 1

    assert (
        memories[0].content
        == "Likes hiking products."
    )


def test_memory_service_deletes_memory(
    tmp_path,
):

    service = MemoryService(
        store=MemoryStore(
            tmp_path / "memory.db"
        )
    )

    memory = service.remember(
        user_id="user-001",
        memory_type=MemoryType.SAVED_ITEM,
        content="Travel backpack",
    )

    assert service.delete(
        memory.memory_id
    ) is True

    assert (
        service.get(memory.memory_id)
        is None
    )
============================================================
FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\intelligence\intelligence_context.py
============================================================
"""
Intelligence Context.

Provider-independent representation of external
information prepared for AI execution.

Architecture:

Research Agent
        |
        v
IntelligenceContext
        |
        v
AI Runtime integration
        |
        v
LLMRequest.context

The context must not expose provider-specific
implementation details to the application layer.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class IntelligenceSource:
    """
    Normalised external information source.
    """

    title: str
    url: str
    source: str
    content: str = ""

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if not self.title.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Intelligence source title cannot be empty."
            )

        if not self.url.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Intelligence source URL cannot be empty."
            )

        if not self.source.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Intelligence source name cannot be empty."
            )


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class IntelligenceContext:
    """
    Provider-independent intelligence context.

    Represents external information that may be supplied
    to an existing AI conversation.

    This object deliberately does not contain:
    - provider SDK objects
    - API credentials
    - model information
    - runtime state
    - persistence objects
    """

    query: str

    sources: tuple[IntelligenceSource, ...] = field(
        default_factory=tuple,
    )

    summary: str = ""

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if not self.query.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Intelligence query cannot be empty."
            )

        if not isinstance(
            self.sources,
            tuple,
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "Intelligence sources must be a tuple."
            )

        if not isinstance(
            self.summary,
            str,
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "Intelligence summary must be a string."
            )

    @property
    def has_sources(self) -> bool:
        """
        Return whether external sources are available.
        """

        return bool(self.sources)

    @property
    def source_count(self) -> int:
        """
        Return the number of external sources.
        """

        return len(self.sources)

    @property
    def has_summary(self) -> bool:
        """
        Return whether a summary is available.
        """

        return bool(
            self.summary.strip()
        )

    def to_context_text(self) -> str:
        """
        Convert intelligence into a safe textual context.

        The result is suitable for eventual inclusion
        in an existing LLMRequest.context field.
        """

        sections: list[str] = [
            "LIVE INTELLIGENCE",
            f"Research query: {self.query.strip()}",
        ]

        if self.summary.strip():
            sections.extend(
                (
                    "",
                    "Summary:",
                    self.summary.strip(),
                )
            )

        if self.sources:
            sections.extend(
                (
                    "",
                    "Sources:",
                )
            )

            for index, source in enumerate(
                self.sources,
                start=1,
            ):
                sections.append(
                    f"{index}. {source.title}"
                )
                sections.append(
                    f"   URL: {source.url}"
                )
                sections.append(
                    f"   Source: {source.source}"
                )

                if source.content.strip():
                    sections.append(
                        f"   Content: {source.content.strip()}"
                    )

        return "\n".join(sections)

============================================================
FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\src\ai_agents\live_intelligence_agent\handler.py
============================================================
"""
Live Intelligence Agent Handler.

Responsible for executing the Live Intelligence Agent workflow.

Architecture:

AI Runtime
    |
    v
Live Intelligence Agent Handler
    |
    +--> Search Provider Interface
    |
    +--> Knowledge Processing
    |
    v
Structured Intelligence Output

Responsibilities:
- Validate agent input
- Call approved provider interfaces
- Normalise research output
- Return explicit structured results

Non-responsibilities:
- Provider SDK management
- Direct external API handling
- Autonomous actions
- Configuration changes
"""

from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import List, Protocol

from src.providers.search_provider.interface import (
    SearchProvider,
    SearchResult,
)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class LiveIntelligenceRequest:
    """
    Input contract for Live Intelligence Agent.
    """

    topic: str
    language: str = "French"
    learner_level: str = "B2"


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class LiveIntelligenceResponse:
    """
    Output contract from Live Intelligence Agent.
    """

    topic: str
    summary: str
    sources: List[SearchResult]
    key_information: List[str]


class IntelligenceProcessor(Protocol):
    """
    Contract for future LLM-based processing.

    Keeps the agent independent from
    any specific model/provider.
    """

    async def summarise(
        self,
        results: List[SearchResult],
        language: str,
        learner_level: str,
    ) -> tuple[str, List[str]]:
        ...


class LiveIntelligenceAgent:
    """
    Live Intelligence Agent implementation.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        search_provider: SearchProvider,
        processor: IntelligenceProcessor,
    ) -> None:
        self._search_provider = search_provider
        self._processor = processor

    async def execute(
        self,
        request: LiveIntelligenceRequest,
    ) -> LiveIntelligenceResponse:
        """
        Execute research workflow.

        Flow:

        Request
          |
          v
        Search Provider
          |
          v
        Intelligence Processor
          |
          v
        Structured Response
        """

        self._validate_request(request)

        search_results = await self._search_provider.search(
            query=request.topic,
            max_results=5,
        )

        summary, key_information = await self._processor.summarise(
            results=search_results,
            language=request.language,
            learner_level=request.learner_level,
        )

        return LiveIntelligenceResponse(
            topic=request.topic,
            summary=summary,
            sources=search_results,
            key_information=key_information,
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _validate_request(
        request: LiveIntelligenceRequest,
    ) -> None:
        """
        Validate required input.
        """

        if not request.topic.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Topic cannot be empty"
            )

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\src\ai_runtime\capabilities\models.py
============================================================
"""
AI Runtime capability contracts.

This module defines the provider-independent contract used by
the AI Runtime to request execution of an approved capability.

Architecture:

Application
    |
    v
AI Runtime
    |
    v
Capability Request
    |
    v
Governance
    |
    v
Capability Execution

This module contains contracts only.

It does not:
- execute agents
- call providers
- evaluate governance policies
- perform external side effects
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import StrEnum
from typing import Any, Mapping


class CapabilityType(StrEnum):
    """
    Supported runtime capability categories.

    The list is intentionally small for the initial framework
    implementation. New capabilities should be added explicitly.
    """

    LIVE_INTELLIGENCE = "live_intelligence"
    LEARNING_GENERATION = "learning_generation"
    EVALUATION = "evaluation"


class CapabilityExecutionStatus(StrEnum):
    """
    Runtime-level execution status.
    """

    ACCEPTED = "accepted"
    COMPLETED = "completed"
    REJECTED = "rejected"
    FAILED = "failed"


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class CapabilityRequest:
    """
    Provider-independent request submitted to the AI Runtime.

    Attributes:
        capability:
            Logical capability requested by the application.

        request_id:
            Correlation identifier for the execution.

        payload:
            Explicit capability input.

        metadata:
            Non-business execution metadata.

        requested_by:
            Component or application requesting execution.
    """

    capability: CapabilityType
    request_id: str
    payload: Mapping[str, Any]
    metadata: Mapping[str, Any] = field(
        default_factory=dict
    )
    requested_by: str = "application"

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if not self.request_id.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Capability request_id cannot be empty."
            )

        if not self.requested_by.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Capability requested_by cannot be empty."
            )


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class CapabilityResult:
    """
    Provider-independent result returned by the AI Runtime.

    The result contains execution information but does not expose
    provider-specific response objects.
    """

    request_id: str
    capability: CapabilityType
    status: CapabilityExecutionStatus
    output: Any = None
    error: str | None = None
    metadata: Mapping[str, Any] = field(
        default_factory=dict
    )

    @property
    def succeeded(self) -> bool:
        """
        Return True when execution completed successfully.
        """

        return (
            self.status
            == CapabilityExecutionStatus.COMPLETED
        )

    @property
    def failed(self) -> bool:
        """
        Return True when execution failed.
        """

        return (
            self.status
            == CapabilityExecutionStatus.FAILED
        )

    @property
    def rejected(self) -> bool:
        """
        Return True when execution was rejected before execution.
        """

        return (
            self.status
            == CapabilityExecutionStatus.REJECTED
        )

"""

"""

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.errors import (
    CapabilityAlreadyRegisteredError,
    CapabilityExecutionError,
    CapabilityGovernanceError,
    CapabilityNotRegisteredError,
    InvalidCapabilityRequestError,
    RuntimeCapabilityError,
)


def test_all_runtime_errors_extend_runtime_capability_error():

    errors = (
        InvalidCapabilityRequestError,
        CapabilityNotRegisteredError,
        CapabilityAlreadyRegisteredError,
        CapabilityExecutionError,
        CapabilityGovernanceError,
    )

    for error_type in errors:
        assert issubclass(
            error_type,
            RuntimeCapabilityError,
        )
