"""
Tests for the AI Runtime Capability Gateway.
"""

import pytest

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.errors import (
    CapabilityExecutionError,
    CapabilityGovernanceError,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.executor import (
    CapabilityExecutor,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.gateway import (
    CapabilityGateway,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.models import (
    CapabilityExecutionStatus,
    CapabilityRequest,
    CapabilityResult,
    CapabilityType,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.registry import (
    CapabilityRegistry,
)


class FakeGovernanceResult:
    """
    Minimal governance result for gateway tests.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        allowed: bool,
    ) -> None:
        self.allowed = allowed


class FakeGovernance:
    """
    Deterministic governance implementation for tests.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        allowed: bool,
    ) -> None:
        self.allowed = allowed
        self.calls = 0

    def evaluate(
        self,
        request: CapabilityRequest,
    ) -> FakeGovernanceResult:

        self.calls += 1

        return FakeGovernanceResult(
            allowed=self.allowed
        )


class FailingGovernance:
    """
    Governance implementation that fails unexpectedly.
    """

    def evaluate(
        self,
        request: CapabilityRequest,
    ) -> FakeGovernanceResult:

        raise RuntimeError(
            "governance infrastructure failure"
        )


class FakeExecutor(CapabilityExecutor):
    """
    Fake executor used to verify gateway orchestration.
    """

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.calls = 0
        self.last_request: CapabilityRequest | None = None

    @property
    def capability(self) -> CapabilityType:
        return CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE

    async def execute(
        self,
        request: CapabilityRequest,
    ) -> CapabilityResult:

        self.calls += 1
        self.last_request = request

        return CapabilityResult(
            request_id=request.request_id,
            capability=request.capability,
            status=CapabilityExecutionStatus.COMPLETED,
            output={
                "executed": True,
            },
        )


class FailingExecutor(CapabilityExecutor):
    """
    Executor that fails unexpectedly.
    """

    @property
    def capability(self) -> CapabilityType:
        return CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE

    async def execute(
        self,
        request: CapabilityRequest,
    ) -> CapabilityResult:

        raise RuntimeError(
            "provider failure"
        )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_gateway_executes_allowed_capability():

    executor = FakeExecutor()

    registry = CapabilityRegistry(
        executors=[executor]
    )

    governance = FakeGovernance(
        allowed=True
    )

    gateway = CapabilityGateway(
        registry=registry,
        governance=governance,
    )

    request = CapabilityRequest(
        capability=CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE,
        request_id="gateway-001",
        payload={
            "topic": "AI Act",
        },
    )

    result = await gateway.execute(
        request
    )

    assert (
        result.status
        == CapabilityExecutionStatus.COMPLETED
    )

    assert result.succeeded is True

    assert governance.calls == 1

    assert executor.calls == 1

    assert executor.last_request is request


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_gateway_rejects_blocked_capability():

    executor = FakeExecutor()

    registry = CapabilityRegistry(
        executors=[executor]
    )

    governance = FakeGovernance(
        allowed=False
    )

    gateway = CapabilityGateway(
        registry=registry,
        governance=governance,
    )

    request = CapabilityRequest(
        capability=CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE,
        request_id="gateway-002",
        payload={
            "topic": "AI Act",
        },
    )

    result = await gateway.execute(
        request
    )

    assert (
        result.status
        == CapabilityExecutionStatus.REJECTED
    )

    assert result.rejected is True

    assert executor.calls == 0

    assert governance.calls == 1

    assert (
        result.metadata["governance_decision"]
        == "rejected"
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_gateway_passes_original_request_to_executor():

    executor = FakeExecutor()

    registry = CapabilityRegistry(
        executors=[executor]
    )

    governance = FakeGovernance(
        allowed=True
    )

    gateway = CapabilityGateway(
        registry=registry,
        governance=governance,
    )

    request = CapabilityRequest(
        capability=CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE,
        request_id="gateway-003",
        payload={
            "topic": "European AI regulation",
            "language": "French",
        },
        metadata={
            "trace_id": "trace-003",
        },
        requested_by="learning_application",
    )

    await gateway.execute(
        request
    )

    assert executor.last_request is request

    assert (
        executor.last_request.payload["language"]
        == "French"
    )

    assert (
        executor.last_request.metadata["trace_id"]
        == "trace-003"
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_governance_is_evaluated_before_execution():

    executor = FakeExecutor()

    registry = CapabilityRegistry(
        executors=[executor]
    )

    governance = FakeGovernance(
        allowed=False
    )

    gateway = CapabilityGateway(
        registry=registry,
        governance=governance,
    )

    request = CapabilityRequest(
        capability=CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE,
        request_id="gateway-004",
        payload={},
    )

    result = await gateway.execute(
        request
    )

    assert result.rejected is True

    assert governance.calls == 1

    assert executor.calls == 0


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_gateway_wraps_governance_failure():

    executor = FakeExecutor()

    registry = CapabilityRegistry(
        executors=[executor]
    )

    gateway = CapabilityGateway(
        registry=registry,
        governance=FailingGovernance(),
    )

    request = CapabilityRequest(
        capability=CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE,
        request_id="gateway-error-001",
        payload={
            "topic": "AI Act",
        },
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        CapabilityGovernanceError,
        match="Governance evaluation failed",
    ):
        await gateway.execute(request)

    assert executor.calls == 0


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_gateway_wraps_executor_failure():

    executor = FailingExecutor()

    registry = CapabilityRegistry(
        executors=[executor]
    )

    gateway = CapabilityGateway(
        registry=registry,
        governance=FakeGovernance(
            allowed=True
        ),
    )

    request = CapabilityRequest(
        capability=CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE,
        request_id="gateway-error-002",
        payload={
            "topic": "AI Act",
        },
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        CapabilityExecutionError,
        match="Capability executor failed",
    ):
        await gateway.execute(request)

"""
Tests for AI Runtime capability contracts.
"""

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.models import (
    CapabilityExecutionStatus,
    CapabilityRequest,
    CapabilityResult,
    CapabilityType,
)


def test_capability_request_accepts_valid_request():

    request = CapabilityRequest(
        capability=CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE,
        request_id="req-001",
        payload={
            "topic": "AI Act",
        },
    )

    assert (
        request.capability
        == CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE
    )

    assert request.request_id == "req-001"

    assert request.payload == {
        "topic": "AI Act",
    }

    assert request.requested_by == "application"


def test_capability_request_supports_metadata():

    request = CapabilityRequest(
        capability=CapabilityType.EVALUATION,
        request_id="req-002",
        payload={
            "challenge_id": "challenge-001",
        },
        metadata={
            "trace_id": "trace-001",
        },
        requested_by="learning_application",
    )

    assert request.metadata["trace_id"] == "trace-001"

    assert (
        request.requested_by
        == "learning_application"
    )


def test_empty_request_id_is_rejected():

    try:
        CapabilityRequest(
            capability=CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE,
            request_id="",
            payload={},
        )
    except ValueError as exc:
        assert "request_id" in str(exc)
    else:
        raise AssertionError(
            "Expected empty request_id to be rejected."
        )


def test_empty_requester_is_rejected():

    try:
        CapabilityRequest(
            capability=CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE,
            request_id="req-003",
            payload={},
            requested_by="",
        )
    except ValueError as exc:
        assert "requested_by" in str(exc)
    else:
        raise AssertionError(
            "Expected empty requested_by to be rejected."
        )


def test_capability_result_reports_success():

    result = CapabilityResult(
        request_id="req-004",
        capability=CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE,
        status=CapabilityExecutionStatus.COMPLETED,
        output={
            "summary": "AI Act research",
        },
    )

    assert result.succeeded is True
    assert result.failed is False
    assert result.rejected is False


def test_capability_result_reports_failure():

    result = CapabilityResult(
        request_id="req-005",
        capability=CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE,
        status=CapabilityExecutionStatus.FAILED,
        error="Provider unavailable.",
    )

    assert result.succeeded is False
    assert result.failed is True
    assert result.rejected is False


def test_capability_result_reports_rejection():

    result = CapabilityResult(
        request_id="req-006",
        capability=CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE,
        status=CapabilityExecutionStatus.REJECTED,
        error="Governance policy blocked execution.",
    )

    assert result.succeeded is False
    assert result.failed is False
    assert result.rejected is True


def test_capability_result_preserves_metadata():

    result = CapabilityResult(
        request_id="req-007",
        capability=CapabilityType.EVALUATION,
        status=CapabilityExecutionStatus.ACCEPTED,
        metadata={
            "governance_decision": "allow",
        },
    )

    assert (
        result.metadata["governance_decision"]
        == "allow"
    )

TEST FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\tests\ai_runtime\capabilities\test_registry.py
============================================================
"""
Tests for the AI Runtime capability registry.
"""

import pytest

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.errors import (
    CapabilityAlreadyRegisteredError,
    CapabilityNotRegisteredError,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.executor import (
    CapabilityExecutor,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.models import (
    CapabilityExecutionStatus,
    CapabilityRequest,
    CapabilityResult,
    CapabilityType,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.registry import (
    CapabilityRegistry,
)


class FakeLiveIntelligenceExecutor(
    CapabilityExecutor,
):
    """
    Fake executor used to test dependency injection.
    """

    @property
    def capability(self) -> CapabilityType:
        return CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE

    async def execute(
        self,
        request: CapabilityRequest,
    ) -> CapabilityResult:

        return CapabilityResult(
            request_id=request.request_id,
            capability=request.capability,
            status=CapabilityExecutionStatus.COMPLETED,
            output={"ok": True},
        )


class FakeEvaluationExecutor(
    CapabilityExecutor,
):
    """
    Second fake executor used to verify multiple
    capability registrations.
    """

    @property
    def capability(self) -> CapabilityType:
        return CapabilityType.EVALUATION

    async def execute(
        self,
        request: CapabilityRequest,
    ) -> CapabilityResult:

        return CapabilityResult(
            request_id=request.request_id,
            capability=request.capability,
            status=CapabilityExecutionStatus.COMPLETED,
            output={"evaluated": True},
        )


def test_registry_registers_executor():

    executor = FakeLiveIntelligenceExecutor()

    registry = CapabilityRegistry()

    registry.register(executor)

    assert registry.contains(
        CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE
    )


def test_registry_resolves_registered_executor():

    executor = FakeLiveIntelligenceExecutor()

    registry = CapabilityRegistry(
        executors=[executor]
    )

    resolved = registry.resolve(
        CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE
    )

    assert resolved is executor


def test_registry_supports_multiple_capabilities():

    live_executor = FakeLiveIntelligenceExecutor()
    evaluation_executor = FakeEvaluationExecutor()

    registry = CapabilityRegistry(
        executors=[
            live_executor,
            evaluation_executor,
        ]
    )

    assert registry.resolve(
        CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE
    ) is live_executor

    assert registry.resolve(
        CapabilityType.EVALUATION
    ) is evaluation_executor


def test_duplicate_capability_registration_is_rejected():

    registry = CapabilityRegistry()

    registry.register(
        FakeLiveIntelligenceExecutor()
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        CapabilityAlreadyRegisteredError,
        match="already registered",
    ):
        registry.register(
            FakeLiveIntelligenceExecutor()
        )


def test_unknown_capability_resolution_is_rejected():

    registry = CapabilityRegistry()

    with pytest.raises(
        CapabilityNotRegisteredError,
        match="No executor registered",
    ):
        registry.resolve(
            CapabilityType.LEARNING_GENERATION
        )


def test_contains_returns_false_for_unknown_capability():

    registry = CapabilityRegistry()

    assert (
        registry.contains(
            CapabilityType.EVALUATION
        )
        is False
    )


def test_capabilities_returns_registered_capabilities():

    registry = CapabilityRegistry(
        executors=[
            FakeLiveIntelligenceExecutor(),
            FakeEvaluationExecutor(),
        ]
    )

    capabilities = registry.capabilities()

    assert (
        CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE
        in capabilities
    )

    assert (
        CapabilityType.EVALUATION
        in capabilities
    )

    assert (
        CapabilityType.LEARNING_GENERATION
        not in capabilities
    )

"""
Tests for Challenge Generator.
"""

import pytest

from src.ai_agents.live_intelligence_agent.handler import (
    LiveIntelligenceResponse,
)

from src.challenge_engine.generator import (
    ChallengeGenerator,
)

from src.challenge_engine.models import (
    DiscussionQuestion,
    VocabularyItem,
)


class FakeContentGenerator:

    async def generate_vocabulary(
        self,
        summary,
        language,
        learner_level,
    ):
        return [
            VocabularyItem(
                term="innovation",
                definition="A new idea or approach.",
            )
        ]

    async def generate_questions(
        self,
        summary,
        language,
        learner_level,
    ):
        return [
            DiscussionQuestion(
                question="Quel est votre avis ?"
            )
        ]


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_generate_learning_challenge():

    generator = ChallengeGenerator(
        content_generator=FakeContentGenerator()
    )

    intelligence = LiveIntelligenceResponse(
        topic="AI Regulation",
        summary="AI regulation is evolving.",
        sources=[],
        key_information=[
            "governance"
        ],
    )

    result = await generator.generate(
        intelligence
    )

    assert result.topic == "AI Regulation"
    assert result.language == "French"
    assert result.vocabulary[0].term == "innovation"
    assert len(result.discussion_questions) == 1


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_generator_rejects_empty_summary():

    generator = ChallengeGenerator(
        content_generator=FakeContentGenerator()
    )

    intelligence = LiveIntelligenceResponse(
        topic="AI",
        summary="",
        sources=[],
        key_information=[],
    )

    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        await generator.generate(
            intelligence
        )

"""
Integration test for Live Challenge vertical slice.

Flow tested:

Search Provider
        â†“
Live Intelligence Agent
        â†“
Challenge Generator
        â†“
Learning Challenge

This test validates the Sprint H1 integration boundaries.

Important:
- Uses test doubles only.
- Does not call the real Parallel API.
- Does not modify or depend on the Conversation Agent.
"""

import pytest

from src.ai_agents.live_intelligence_agent.handler import (
    LiveIntelligenceAgent,
    LiveIntelligenceRequest,
)

from src.challenge_engine.generator import (
    ChallengeGenerator,
)

from src.challenge_engine.models import (
    DiscussionQuestion,
    VocabularyItem,
)

from src.providers.search_provider.interface import (
    SearchResult,
)


class FakeSearchProvider:
    """
    Controlled search provider for integration testing.
    """

    async def search(
        self,
        query: str,
        max_results: int = 5,
    ) -> list[SearchResult]:
        return [
            SearchResult(
                title="AI Act Update",
                url="https://example.com/ai-act",
                content="European AI regulation update.",
                source="test-provider",
            )
        ]


class FakeIntelligenceProcessor:
    """
    Simulates intelligence synthesis without
    requiring an LLM provider.
    """

    async def summarise(
        self,
        results: list[SearchResult],
        language: str,
        learner_level: str,
    ) -> tuple[str, list[str]]:
        return (
            "The AI Act introduces new rules for "
            "artificial intelligence.",
            [
                "AI governance",
                "risk management",
            ],
        )


class FakeChallengeContentGenerator:
    """
    Simulates educational content generation.
    """

    async def generate_vocabulary(
        self,
        summary: str,
        language: str,
        learner_level: str,
    ) -> list[VocabularyItem]:
        return [
            VocabularyItem(
                term="gouvernance",
                definition=(
                    "Organisation and control of activities."
                ),
            )
        ]

    async def generate_questions(
        self,
        summary: str,
        language: str,
        learner_level: str,
    ) -> list[DiscussionQuestion]:
        return [
            DiscussionQuestion(
                question=(
                    "Pourquoi la gouvernance de l'IA "
                    "est-elle importante ?"
                ),
            )
        ]


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_live_challenge_vertical_slice():
    """
    Validate the complete Sprint H1 backend flow:

    Search Provider
        â†“
    Live Intelligence Agent
        â†“
    Challenge Generator
        â†“
    Learning Challenge
    """

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Step 1 â€” Create Live Intelligence Agent
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    intelligence_agent = LiveIntelligenceAgent(
        search_provider=FakeSearchProvider(),
        processor=FakeIntelligenceProcessor(),
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Step 2 â€” Execute Live Intelligence research
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    intelligence = await intelligence_agent.execute(
        request=LiveIntelligenceRequest(
            topic="AI Act",
        )
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Step 3 â€” Validate intelligence output
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    assert intelligence.topic == "AI Act"

    assert intelligence.summary == (
        "The AI Act introduces new rules for "
        "artificial intelligence."
    )

    assert intelligence.key_information == [
        "AI governance",
        "risk management",
    ]

    assert len(intelligence.sources) == 1
    assert intelligence.sources[0].title == "AI Act Update"
    assert intelligence.sources[0].source == "test-provider"

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Step 4 â€” Create Challenge Generator
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    challenge_generator = ChallengeGenerator(
        content_generator=FakeChallengeContentGenerator()
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Step 5 â€” Generate learning challenge
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    challenge = await challenge_generator.generate(
        intelligence=intelligence,
        language="French",
        learner_level="B2",
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Step 6 â€” Validate final learning output
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    assert challenge.title == (
        "AI Act Learning Challenge"
    )

    assert challenge.topic == "AI Act"
    assert challenge.language == "French"
    assert challenge.learner_level == "B2"

    assert challenge.summary == (
        "The AI Act introduces new rules for "
        "artificial intelligence."
    )

    assert len(challenge.vocabulary) == 1

    assert challenge.vocabulary[0].term == (
        "gouvernance"
    )

    assert (
        challenge.vocabulary[0].definition
        == "Organisation and control of activities."
    )

    assert len(challenge.discussion_questions) == 1

    assert (
        challenge.discussion_questions[0].question
        == "Pourquoi la gouvernance de l'IA est-elle importante ?"
    )

    assert (
        challenge.discussion_questions[0].difficulty
        == "B2"
    )

    assert (
        challenge.created_by_agent
        == "live_intelligence_agent"
    )

import pytest

from backend.intelligence.intelligence_context import (
    IntelligenceContext,
    IntelligenceSource,
)


def test_intelligence_context_creation():
    source = IntelligenceSource(
        title="European Commission",
        url="https://example.com",
        source="European Commission",
        content="AI regulation information.",
    )

    context = IntelligenceContext(
        query="latest European AI regulation",
        sources=(source,),
        summary="Current regulatory information.",
    )

    assert context.query == (
        "latest European AI regulation"
    )

    assert context.source_count == 1

    assert context.has_sources is True

    assert context.has_summary is True


def test_intelligence_context_is_immutable():
    context = IntelligenceContext(
        query="latest AI news",
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        AttributeError
    ):
        context.query = "changed"


def test_intelligence_context_rejects_empty_query():
    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="Intelligence query cannot be empty",
    ):
        IntelligenceContext(
            query="",
        )


def test_intelligence_source_requires_title():
    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="title cannot be empty",
    ):
        IntelligenceSource(
            title="",
            url="https://example.com",
            source="example",
        )


def test_intelligence_context_to_context_text():
    source = IntelligenceSource(
        title="AI Regulation",
        url="https://example.com/ai",
        source="Example",
        content="European AI regulation information.",
    )

    context = IntelligenceContext(
        query="latest European AI regulation",
        sources=(source,),
        summary="Regulatory developments.",
    )

    text = context.to_context_text()

    assert "LIVE INTELLIGENCE" in text

    assert (
        "latest European AI regulation"
        in text
    )

    assert "AI Regulation" in text

    assert "https://example.com/ai" in text

    assert "European AI regulation information." in text

import pytest

from backend.agents.agent_result import (
    AgentResult,
)

from backend.intelligence.intelligence_context import (
    IntelligenceContext,
)

from backend.intelligence.research_service import (
    ResearchService,
)


class FakeResearchAgent:

    async def execute(
        self,
        context,
    ):
        return AgentResult(
            success=True,
            agent_id="research_agent",
            output=IntelligenceContext(
                query=context.request,
            ),
        )


class FailingResearchAgent:

    async def execute(
        self,
        context,
    ):
        return AgentResult(
            success=False,
            agent_id="research_agent",
            output=None,
            error="Research unavailable",
        )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_research_service_returns_intelligence_context():

    service = ResearchService(
        agent=FakeResearchAgent()
    )

    result = await service.research(
        user_id="demo-user",
        query="Latest European AI regulation",
    )

    assert isinstance(
        result,
        IntelligenceContext,
    )

    assert result.query == (
        "Latest European AI regulation"
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_research_service_rejects_empty_query():

    service = ResearchService(
        agent=FakeResearchAgent()
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="Research query cannot be empty",
    ):
        await service.research(
            user_id="demo-user",
            query="",
        )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_research_service_propagates_failure():

    service = ResearchService(
        agent=FailingResearchAgent()
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        RuntimeError,
        match="Research unavailable",
    ):
        await service.research(
            user_id="demo-user",
            query="Latest European AI regulation",
        )

import pytest

from backend.agents.agent_result import (
    AgentResult,
)

from backend.characters.character_profile import (
    CharacterProfile,
)

from backend.intelligence.intelligence_context import (
    IntelligenceContext,
    IntelligenceSource,
)

from backend.memory.memory_service import (
    MemoryService,
)

from backend.memory.memory_store import (
    MemoryStore,
)

from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import (
    ExecutionContext,
)

from backend.services.companion_service import (
    CompanionService,
)


class FakeConversationAgent:

    def __init__(self):
        self.received_context = None

    async def execute(
        self,
        context,
    ):
        self.received_context = context

        return AgentResult(
            success=True,
            agent_id="conversation_agent",
            output={
                "character": "Yeti",
                "personality": (
                    "Curious and encouraging"
                ),
                "learning_style": (
                    "Conversation based learning"
                ),
                "intelligence_context": (
                    context.intelligence_context
                ),
            },
        )


class FakeResearchAgent:

    async def execute(
        self,
        context,
    ):
        return AgentResult(
            success=True,
            agent_id="research_agent",
            output=IntelligenceContext(
                query=context.request,
                sources=(
                    IntelligenceSource(
                        title=(
                            "European AI Regulation"
                        ),
                        url=(
                            "https://example.com/ai"
                        ),
                        source="test",
                        content=(
                            "Current regulatory "
                            "information."
                        ),
                    ),
                ),
                summary=(
                    "Recent European AI "
                    "regulatory developments."
                ),
            ),
        )


class FakeQualityAgent:

    async def execute(
        self,
        context,
    ):
        return AgentResult(
            success=True,
            agent_id="quality_agent",
            output={
                "quality_score": 1.0,
                "passed": True,
                "findings": [],
                "recommendations": [],
            },
        )


def create_character():
    return CharacterProfile(
        character_id="yeti",
        name="Yeti",
        description=(
            "Friendly AI learning companion"
        ),
        personality=(
            "Curious and encouraging"
        ),
        learning_style=(
            "Conversation based learning"
        ),
    )


def create_memory_service(
    tmp_path,
):
    return MemoryService(
        store=MemoryStore(
            database_path=(
                tmp_path / "memory.db"
            )
        )
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_companion_passes_research_to_conversation(
    tmp_path,
):

    conversation_agent = (
        FakeConversationAgent()
    )

    service = CompanionService(
        character=create_character(),
        conversation_agent=(
            conversation_agent
        ),
        research_agent=(
            FakeResearchAgent()
        ),
        quality_agent=(
            FakeQualityAgent()
        ),
        memory_service=(
            create_memory_service(
                tmp_path
            )
        ),
    )

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request=(
            "Explain the latest "
            "European AI regulation"
        ),
    )

    result = await service.interact(
        context,
        use_research=True,
    )

    assert result["character"] == (
        "Yeti"
    )

    assert (
        result["research"]
        is not None
    )

    assert isinstance(
        result["research"],
        IntelligenceContext,
    )

    received_context = (
        conversation_agent.received_context
    )

    assert (
        received_context is not None
    )

    assert (
        received_context
        is not context
    )

    assert (
        received_context.intelligence_context
        is not None
    )

    assert (
        received_context.intelligence_context.query
        == context.request
    )

    assert (
        received_context.intelligence_context.source_count
        == 1
    )

    assert (
        result["conversation"][
            "intelligence_context"
        ]
        is received_context.intelligence_context
    )

    assert (
        context.intelligence_context
        is None
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_companion_without_research_preserves_normal_conversation(
    tmp_path,
):

    conversation_agent = (
        FakeConversationAgent()

    service = CompanionService(

                tmp_path
            )
        ),
    )

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request=(
            "Explain artificial intelligence"
        ),
    )

    result = await service.interact(
        context,
        use_research=False,
    )

    assert result["research"] is None

    assert (
        conversation_agent.received_context
        is context
    )

    assert (
        conversation_agent.received_context
        .intelligence_context
        is None
    )

    assert (
        result["conversation"][
            "intelligence_context"
        ]
        is None
    )

import pytest

from backend.agents.conversation_agent import (
    ConversationAgent,
)

from backend.characters.character_profile import (
    CharacterProfile,
)

from backend.conversation.conversation_request import (
    ConversationRequest,
)

from backend.intelligence.intelligence_context import (
    IntelligenceContext,
    IntelligenceSource,
)

from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import (
    ExecutionContext,
)


def create_character():
    return CharacterProfile(
        character_id="yeti",
        name="Yeti",
        description=(
            "Friendly AI learning companion"
        ),
        personality=(
            "Curious and encouraging"
        ),
        learning_style=(
            "Conversation based learning"
        ),
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_conversation_agent():

    character = create_character()

    agent = ConversationAgent(
        character
    )

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request=(
            "Explain artificial intelligence"
        ),
    )

    result = await agent.execute(
        context
    )

    assert result.success is True

    assert result.agent_id == (
        "conversation_agent"
    )

    assert result.output["character"] == (
        "Yeti"
    )

    assert result.output["personality"] == (
        "Curious and encouraging"
    )

    assert result.output["learning_style"] == (
        "Conversation based learning"
    )

    assert isinstance(
        result.output["conversation_request"],
        ConversationRequest,
    )

    request = (
        result.output["conversation_request"]
    )

    assert request.character_id == "yeti"

    assert request.character_name == "Yeti"

    assert request.user_prompt == (
        "Explain artificial intelligence"
    )

    assert (
        request.has_conversation_context
        is False
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_conversation_agent_includes_live_intelligence():

    character = create_character()

    agent = ConversationAgent(
        character
    )

    intelligence = IntelligenceContext(
        query=(
            "Latest European AI regulation"
        ),
        sources=(
            IntelligenceSource(
                title="European AI Regulation",
                url="https://example.com/ai",
                source="parallel",
                content=(
                    "Current regulatory information."
                ),
            ),
        ),
        summary=(
            "Recent regulatory developments."
        ),
    )

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request=(
            "Explain the latest European "
            "AI regulation"
        ),
        intelligence_context=intelligence,
    )

    result = await agent.execute(
        context
    )

    assert result.success is True

    request = (
        result.output["conversation_request"]
    )

    assert isinstance(
        request,
        ConversationRequest,
    )

    assert (
        request.has_conversation_context
        is True
    )

    assert (
        "LIVE INTELLIGENCE"
        in request.conversation_context
    )

    assert (
        "European AI Regulation"
        in request.conversation_context
    )

    assert (
        "Current regulatory information."
        in request.conversation_context
    )

    assert (
        result.output["intelligence_context"]
        == request.conversation_context
    )

    agent = ConversationAgent(
        character
    )

    intelligence = IntelligenceContext(
        query="Latest AI regulation",
        sources=(
            IntelligenceSource(
                title="AI Act",
                url="https://example.com/ai-act",
                source="parallel",

        in request.conversation_context
    )

        request.character_name
        == "Yeti"
    )

TEST FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\tests\runtime\test_execution_context.py
============================================================
import pytest

from backend.intelligence.intelligence_context import (
    IntelligenceContext,
)

from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import (
    ExecutionContext,
)


def test_execution_context_creation():

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request="Hello",
    )

    assert context.user_id == (
        "demo-user"
    )

    assert context.request == (
        "Hello"
    )

    assert context.execution_id

    assert context.created_at

    assert context.metadata == {}

    assert context.intelligence_context is None

    assert context.has_intelligence_context is False


def test_execution_context_is_immutable():

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request="Hello",
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        AttributeError
    ):
        context.request = "Changed"


def test_execution_context_can_carry_intelligence():

    intelligence = IntelligenceContext(
        query="Latest European AI regulation",
    )

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request="Explain this to me",
        intelligence_context=intelligence,
    )

    assert context.intelligence_context is intelligence

    assert context.has_intelligence_context is True

    assert (
        context.intelligence_context.query
        == "Latest European AI regulation"
    )

============================================================
TEST FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\tests\runtime\test_research_agent.py
============================================================
import pytest

from backend.agents.research_agent import (
    ResearchAgent,
)

from backend.intelligence.intelligence_context import (
    IntelligenceContext,
    IntelligenceSource,
)

from backend.providers.provider_result import (
    ProviderResult,
)

from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import (
    ExecutionContext,
)


class FakeResearchProvider:
    """
    Fake research provider used for unit testing.

    This keeps the test independent from the real
    Parallel Search API.
    """

    async def execute(
        self,
        context,
    ):
        return ProviderResult(
            success=True,
            provider_id="fake_research",
            output={
                "query": context.request,
                "results": {
                    "results": [
                        {
                            "title": "European AI Regulation",
                            "url": "https://example.com/ai",
                            "excerpts": [
                                "Current regulatory information."
                            ],
                        }
                    ]
                },
            },
        )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_research_agent_returns_intelligence_context():

    agent = ResearchAgent(
        provider=FakeResearchProvider()
    )

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request="Latest European AI regulation",
    )

    result = await agent.execute(
        context
    )

    assert result.success is True

    assert result.agent_id == (
        "research_agent"
    )

    assert isinstance(
        result.output,
        IntelligenceContext,
    )

    assert result.output.query == (
        "Latest European AI regulation"
    )

    assert result.output.source_count == 1

    assert isinstance(
        result.output.sources[0],
        IntelligenceSource,
    )

    assert result.output.sources[0].title == (
        "European AI Regulation"
    )

    assert result.output.sources[0].url == (
        "https://example.com/ai"
    )

    assert (
        result.output.sources[0].content
        == "Current regulatory information."
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_research_agent_propagates_provider_failure():

    class FailingResearchProvider:
        """
        Fake provider representing a provider failure.
        """

        async def execute(
            self,
            context,
        ):
            return ProviderResult(
                success=False,
                provider_id="fake_research",
                output=None,
                error="Research provider unavailable",
            )

    agent = ResearchAgent(
        provider=FailingResearchProvider()
    )

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request="Latest European AI regulation",
    )

    result = await agent.execute(
        context
    )

    assert result.success is False

    assert result.agent_id == (
        "research_agent"
    )

    assert result.output is None

    assert result.error == (
        "Research provider unavailable"
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_research_agent_handles_empty_results():

    class EmptyResearchProvider:
        """
        Fake provider returning no research results.
        """

        async def execute(
            self,
            context,
        ):
            return ProviderResult(
                success=True,
                provider_id="fake_research",
                output={
                    "query": context.request,
                    "results": {
                        "results": []
                    },
                },
            )

    agent = ResearchAgent(
        provider=EmptyResearchProvider()
    )

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request="A very specific research question",
    )

    result = await agent.execute(
        context
    )

    assert result.success is True

    assert isinstance(
        result.output,
        IntelligenceContext,
    )

    assert result.output.source_count == 0

    assert result.output.has_sources is False

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\hackathon_demo_discovery_service.py

"""
EasyChatBot Hackathon â€” Unified Demonstration.

Demonstrates the four completed hackathon capabilities:

1. Governance
2. Live Intelligence
3. Learning Challenge
4. Quality Evaluation

Architecture:

User Request
     |
     v
Governance Check
     |
     +---- BLOCK -> Stop
     |
     v
Live Intelligence
     |
     v
Learning Challenge
     |
     v
Quality Evaluation
     |
     v
Final Demonstration Result

This file is an orchestration/demo layer only.

It does not modify:
- Conversation Agent
- Live Intelligence Agent
- Challenge Engine
- Evaluation Engine
- Governance Engine
- Parallel Provider
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import sys
from pathlib import Path


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Repository path bootstrap
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

REPOSITORY_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]

if str(REPOSITORY_ROOT) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(REPOSITORY_ROOT))


from src.ai_agents.live_intelligence_agent.handler import (
    LiveIntelligenceAgent,
    LiveIntelligenceRequest,
)

from src.challenge_engine.generator import (
    ChallengeGenerator,
)

from src.challenge_engine.models import (
    DiscussionQuestion,
    LearningChallenge,
    VocabularyItem,
)

from src.evaluation.criteria import (
    COMPLETENESS,
    EDUCATIONAL_VALUE,
    LANGUAGE_LEVEL,
    SOURCE_COVERAGE,
)

from src.evaluation.deterministic_evaluator import (
    DeterministicEvaluator,
)

from src.evaluation.engine import (
    EvaluationEngine,
)

from src.evaluation.report import (
    EvaluationReport,
)

from src.governance.engine import (
    GovernancePolicyEngine,
)

from src.governance.models import (
    GovernanceDecision,
    GovernancePolicy,
    RiskLevel,
)

from src.providers.search_provider.interface import (
    SearchResult,
)

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.flash_deal_demo import (
    build_demo_deal,
    build_demo_memory,
    render_demo_flash_deal_card,
)

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.service import (
    CharacterContext,
    DiscoveryRequest,
    DiscoveryService,
)

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.flash_deal_commerce import (
    FlashDealCommerce,
)

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.service import (
    CommerceService,
)

from src.ai_runtime.commerce.models import (
    CommerceActionStatus,
)


class DemoSearchProvider:
    """
    Controlled search provider for the unified demo.

    The real Parallel provider is already validated separately
    by H1 integration tests.

    This keeps the unified demo deterministic and suitable for
    repeatable hackathon demonstrations.
    """

    async def search(
        self,
        query: str,
        max_results: int = 5,
    ) -> list[SearchResult]:

        return [
            SearchResult(
                title="European AI Act â€” Demo Source",
                url="https://example.com/ai-act",
                content=(
                    "The European Union AI Act establishes "
                    "a risk-based framework for artificial "
                    "intelligence."
                ),
                source="demo-provider",
            )
        ]


class DemoIntelligenceProcessor:
    """
    Controlled intelligence processor.

    The real provider-backed intelligence flow is validated
    separately in H1.
    """

    async def summarise(
        self,
        results: list[SearchResult],
        language: str,
        learner_level: str,
    ) -> tuple[str, list[str]]:

        return (
            "The European Union AI Act establishes "
            "a risk-based framework for artificial "
            "intelligence.",
            [
                "AI governance",
                "risk management",
                "compliance",
            ],
        )


class DemoChallengeContentGenerator:
    """
    Controlled educational content generator.
    """

    async def generate_vocabulary(
        self,
        summary: str,
        language: str,
        learner_level: str,
    ) -> list[VocabularyItem]:

        return [
            VocabularyItem(
                term="gouvernance",
                definition=(
                    "La maniÃ¨re dont une organisation "
                    "est dirigÃ©e et contrÃ´lÃ©e."
                ),
                example=(
                    "La gouvernance de l'IA devient "
                    "essentielle."
                ),
            ),
            VocabularyItem(
                term="rÃ©glementation",
                definition=(
                    "Ensemble des rÃ¨gles qui encadrent "
                    "une activitÃ©."
                ),
                example=(
                    "La rÃ©glementation europÃ©enne Ã©volue."
                ),
            ),
        ]

    async def generate_questions(
        self,
        summary: str,
        language: str,
        learner_level: str,
    ) -> list[DiscussionQuestion]:

        return [
            DiscussionQuestion(
                question=(
                    "Pourquoi la rÃ©glementation de "
                    "l'IA est-elle importante ?"
                ),
                difficulty=learner_level,
            ),
            DiscussionQuestion(
                question=(
                    "Quels sont les avantages d'une "
                    "rÃ©glementation europÃ©enne de l'IA ?"
                ),
                difficulty=learner_level,
            ),
        ]


def create_governance_policy() -> GovernancePolicy:
    """
    Governance policy for the live intelligence demo.

    Live research may access external information but
    must never create external side effects.
    """

    return GovernancePolicy(
        capability="live_intelligence_research",
        risk_level=RiskLevel.MEDIUM,
        requires_human_review=False,
        allows_external_calls=True,
        allows_side_effects=False,
    )


async def create_learning_challenge() -> LearningChallenge:
    """
    Execute the H1 learning challenge flow.
    """

    intelligence_agent = LiveIntelligenceAgent(
        search_provider=DemoSearchProvider(),
        processor=DemoIntelligenceProcessor(),
    )

    intelligence = await intelligence_agent.execute(
        LiveIntelligenceRequest(
            topic="AI Act",
            language="French",
            learner_level="B2",
        )
    )

    challenge_generator = ChallengeGenerator(
        content_generator=DemoChallengeContentGenerator()
    )

    return await challenge_generator.generate(
        intelligence=intelligence,
        language="French",
        learner_level="B2",
    )


async def run_demo() -> None:
    """
    Execute the complete hackathon demonstration.
    """

    print()
    print("=" * 72)
    print("EasyChatBot â€” HACKATHON DEMONSTRATION")
    print("=" * 72)
    print()

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # STEP 1 â€” Governance
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    print("STEP 1 â€” GOVERNANCE")
    print("-" * 72)

    governance_engine = GovernancePolicyEngine()

    governance_result = governance_engine.evaluate(
        create_governance_policy()
    )

    print(
        f"Capability: {governance_result.capability}"
    )

    print(
        f"Risk level: "
        f"{governance_result.risk_level.value}"
    )

    print(
        f"Decision: "
        f"{governance_result.decision.value.upper()}"
    )

    if governance_result.decision != GovernanceDecision.ALLOW:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "Hackathon demo stopped because governance "
            "did not allow the capability."
        )

    print("Governance check: PASSED")
    print()

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # STEP 2 â€” Live Intelligence + Challenge
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    print("STEP 2 â€” LIVE INTELLIGENCE")
    print("-" * 72)

    challenge = await create_learning_challenge()

    print(f"Topic: {challenge.topic}")
    print(f"Language: {challenge.language}")
    print(f"Learner level: {challenge.learner_level}")
    print()

    print("Summary:")
    print(challenge.summary)
    print()

    print("Vocabulary:")

    for item in challenge.vocabulary:
        print(f"  â€¢ {item.term}: {item.definition}")

    print()

    print("Discussion questions:")

    for index, question in enumerate(
        challenge.discussion_questions,
        start=1,
    ):
        print(
            f"  {index}. {question.question}"
        )

    print()

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # STEP 3 â€” Quality Evaluation
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    print("STEP 3 â€” AI QUALITY EVALUATION")
    print("-" * 72)

    evaluator = DeterministicEvaluator()

    criterion_scores = evaluator.evaluate(
        challenge
    )

    evaluation_engine = EvaluationEngine(
        criteria=[
            COMPLETENESS,
            LANGUAGE_LEVEL,
            EDUCATIONAL_VALUE,
            SOURCE_COVERAGE,
        ],
        passing_score=0.70,
    )

    evaluation_result = evaluation_engine.evaluate(
        criterion_scores
    )

    report = EvaluationReport().generate(
        evaluation_result
    )

    print(report)

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # STEP 4 â€” PERSONALISED FLASH DEAL
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    print("STEP 4 â€” PERSONALISED DISCOVERY")
    print("-" * 72)

    # Build the same domain inputs that a real discovery
    # application flow would receive.
    demo_deal = build_demo_deal()
    demo_memory = build_demo_memory()

    discovery_request = DiscoveryRequest(
        user_id=demo_memory.user_id,
        deal=demo_deal,
        character=CharacterContext(
            character_id="yeti",
            character_name="Yeti",
            message=(
                "Look what I found! "
                "This could be perfect for you."
            ),
        ),
        product_image_url=(
            "https://example.com/demo/iphone17-pro.jpg"
        ),
        tagline="Pro. Beautiful. Powerful.",
        highlights=(
            "Pro camera system",
            "High-performance chip",
            "All-day battery life",
            "Premium display",
        ),
    )

    flash_deal_card = DiscoveryService().create_flash_deal(
        discovery_request,
        memories=[demo_memory],
    )

    print(
        render_demo_flash_deal_card(
            flash_deal_card
        )
    )

    assert flash_deal_card.character_id == "yeti"
    assert flash_deal_card.deal_id == demo_deal.deal_id
    assert flash_deal_card.product_name
    assert demo_memory.user_id == "demo-user"
    assert flash_deal_card.relevance_reason
    assert flash_deal_card.get_deal_url
    assert flash_deal_card.more_information_url

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # STEP 5 â€” COMMERCE ACTION
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    print("STEP 5 â€” COMMERCE ACTION")
    print("-" * 72)

    commerce_action = FlashDealCommerce().create_action(
        user_id="demo-user",
        card=flash_deal_card,
    )

    print(
        f"Product: {flash_deal_card.product_name}"
    )
    print(
        f"Vendor: {flash_deal_card.vendor_name}"
    )
    print(
        f"Commerce action: "
        f"{commerce_action.action_type.value.upper()}"
    )

    commerce_result = CommerceService().initiate(
        commerce_action
    )

    print(
        f"Status: "
        f"{commerce_result.status.value.upper()}"
    )

    print()
    print("GET DEAL")
    print("  -> Approved commerce boundary")
    print("  -> Vendor handoff")
    print()
    print(
        "Payment is not executed in the hackathon demo."
    )

    assert (
        commerce_result.status
        == CommerceActionStatus.REDIRECT
    )
    assert (
        commerce_result.deal_id
        == flash_deal_card.deal_id
    )
    assert (
        commerce_result.next_url
        == flash_deal_card.get_deal_url
    )

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # STEP 6 â€” Final result
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    print()
    print("=" * 72)

    if evaluation_result.passed:
        print("HACKATHON DEMO: SUCCESS")
        print()
        # ASCII-safe output keeps the executable reliable when
        # stdout is captured by Windows pytest/subprocess.
        # Preserve the stable hackathon readiness marker.
        print(
            "Governance -> Intelligence -> Learning -> Evaluation"
        )
        print(
            "Governance -> Intelligence -> Learning -> "
            "Evaluation -> Personalised Discovery"
        )
        print(
            "All demonstrated capabilities completed successfully."
        )
    else:
        print("HACKATHON DEMO: QUALITY CHECK FAILED")

    print("=" * 72)
    print()

    assert governance_result.decision == GovernanceDecision.ALLOW
    assert challenge.topic == "AI Act"
    assert evaluation_result.passed is True


def main() -> None:
    """
    Application entry point.
    """

    asyncio.run(run_demo())


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

    criterion_scores = evaluator.evaluate(
        challenge
    )

    evaluation_engine = EvaluationEngine(
        criteria=[
            COMPLETENESS,
            LANGUAGE_LEVEL,
            EDUCATIONAL_VALUE,
            SOURCE_COVERAGE,
        ],
        passing_score=0.70,
    )

    evaluation_result = evaluation_engine.evaluate(
        criterion_scores
    )

    report = EvaluationReport().generate(
        evaluation_result
    )

    print(report)

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # STEP 4 â€” PERSONALISED FLASH DEAL
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    print("STEP 4 â€” PERSONALISED DISCOVERY")
    print("-" * 72)

    # Build the same domain inputs that a real discovery
    # application flow would receive.
    demo_deal = build_demo_deal()
    demo_memory = build_demo_memory()

    discovery_request = DiscoveryRequest(
        user_id=demo_memory.user_id,
        deal=demo_deal,
        character=CharacterContext(
            character_id="yeti",
            character_name="Yeti",
            message=(
                "Look what I found! "
                "This could be perfect for you."
            ),
        ),
        product_image_url=(
            "https://example.com/demo/iphone17-pro.jpg"
        ),
        tagline="Pro. Beautiful. Powerful.",
        highlights=(
            "Pro camera system",
            "High-performance chip",
            "All-day battery life",
            "Premium display",
        ),
    )

    flash_deal_card = DiscoveryService().create_flash_deal(
        discovery_request,
        memories=[demo_memory],
    )

    print(
        render_demo_flash_deal_card(
            flash_deal_card
        )
    )

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # STEP 4A â€” CHARACTER DISCOVERY NOTIFICATION
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    discovery_notification = (
        DiscoveryNotification.from_flash_deal(
            notification_id="demo-notification-001",
            user_id=demo_memory.user_id,
            deal=flash_deal_card,
        )
    )

    print()
    print("CHARACTER NOTIFICATION")
    print("-" * 72)
    print(
        discovery_notification.render()
    )
    print()

    assert (
        discovery_notification.user_id
        == demo_memory.user_id
    )
    assert (
        discovery_notification.character_id
        == flash_deal_card.character_id
    )
    assert (
        discovery_notification.deal
        is flash_deal_card
    )

    assert flash_deal_card.character_id == "yeti"
    assert flash_deal_card.deal_id == demo_deal.deal_id
    assert flash_deal_card.product_name
    assert demo_memory.user_id == "demo-user"
    assert flash_deal_card.relevance_reason
    assert flash_deal_card.get_deal_url
    assert flash_deal_card.more_information_url

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # STEP 5 â€” COMMERCE ACTION
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    print("STEP 5 â€” COMMERCE ACTION")
    print("-" * 72)

    commerce_action = FlashDealCommerce().create_action(
        user_id="demo-user",
        card=flash_deal_card,
    )

    print(
        f"Product: {flash_deal_card.product_name}"
    )
    print(
        f"Vendor: {flash_deal_card.vendor_name}"
    )
    print(
        f"Commerce action: "
        f"{commerce_action.action_type.value.upper()}"
    )

    commerce_result = CommerceService().initiate(
        commerce_action
    )

    print(
        f"Status: "
        f"{commerce_result.status.value.upper()}"
    )

    print()
    print("GET DEAL")
    print("  -> Approved commerce boundary")
    print("  -> Vendor handoff")
    print()
    print(
        "Payment is not executed in the hackathon demo."
    )

    assert (
        commerce_result.status
        == CommerceActionStatus.REDIRECT
    )
    assert (
        commerce_result.deal_id
        == flash_deal_card.deal_id
    )
    assert (
        commerce_result.next_url
        == flash_deal_card.get_deal_url
    )

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # STEP 6 â€” Final result
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    print()
    print("=" * 72)

    if evaluation_result.passed:
        print("HACKATHON DEMO: SUCCESS")
        print()
        # ASCII-safe output keeps the executable reliable when
        # stdout is captured by Windows pytest/subprocess.
        # Preserve the stable hackathon readiness marker.
        print(
            "Governance -> Intelligence -> Learning -> Evaluation"
        )
        print(
            "Governance -> Intelligence -> Learning -> "
            "Evaluation -> Personalised Discovery"
        )
        print(
            "All demonstrated capabilities completed successfully."
        )
    else:
        print("HACKATHON DEMO: QUALITY CHECK FAILED")

    print("=" * 72)
    print()

    assert governance_result.decision == GovernanceDecision.ALLOW
    assert challenge.topic == "AI Act"
    assert evaluation_result.passed is True


def main() -> None:
    """
    Application entry point.
    """

    asyncio.run(run_demo())


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

"""

    repeatable hackathon demonstrations.

    async def search(
        self,

        return [

                    "a risk-based framework for artificial "

        ]

def create_governance_policy() -> GovernancePolicy:
    """
    Governance policy for the live intelligence demo.

    Live research may access external information but
    must never create external side effects.
    """

    return GovernancePolicy(
        capability="live_intelligence_research",
        risk_level=RiskLevel.MEDIUM,
        requires_human_review=False,
        allows_external_calls=True,
        allows_side_effects=False,
    )


async def create_learning_challenge() -> LearningChallenge:
    """
    Execute the H1 learning challenge flow.
    """

    intelligence_agent = LiveIntelligenceAgent(
        search_provider=DemoSearchProvider(),
        processor=DemoIntelligenceProcessor(),
    )

    intelligence = await intelligence_agent.execute(
        LiveIntelligenceRequest(
            topic="AI Act",
            language="French",
            learner_level="B2",
        )
    )

    challenge_generator = ChallengeGenerator(
        content_generator=DemoChallengeContentGenerator()
    )

    return await challenge_generator.generate(
        intelligence=intelligence,
        language="French",
        learner_level="B2",
    )


async def run_demo() -> None:
    """
    Execute the complete hackathon demonstration.
    """

    print()
    print("=" * 72)
    print("EasyChatBot â€” HACKATHON DEMONSTRATION")
    print("=" * 72)
    print()

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # STEP 1 â€” Governance
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    print("STEP 1 â€” GOVERNANCE")
    print("-" * 72)

    governance_engine = GovernancePolicyEngine()

    governance_result = governance_engine.evaluate(
        create_governance_policy()
    )

    print(
        f"Capability: {governance_result.capability}"
    )

    print(
        f"Risk level: "
        f"{governance_result.risk_level.value}"
    )

    print(
        f"Decision: "
        f"{governance_result.decision.value.upper()}"
    )

    if governance_result.decision != GovernanceDecision.ALLOW:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "Hackathon demo stopped because governance "
            "did not allow the capability."
        )

    print("Governance check: PASSED")
    print()

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # STEP 2 â€” Live Intelligence + Challenge
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    print("STEP 2 â€” LIVE INTELLIGENCE")
    print("-" * 72)

    challenge = await create_learning_challenge()

    print(f"Topic: {challenge.topic}")
    print(f"Language: {challenge.language}")
    print(f"Learner level: {challenge.learner_level}")
    print()

    print("Summary:")
    print(challenge.summary)
    print()

    print("Vocabulary:")

    for item in challenge.vocabulary:
        print(f"  â€¢ {item.term}: {item.definition}")

    print()

    print("Discussion questions:")

    for index, question in enumerate(
        challenge.discussion_questions,
        start=1,
    ):
        print(
            f"  {index}. {question.question}"
        )

    print()

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # STEP 3 â€” Quality Evaluation
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    print("STEP 3 â€” AI QUALITY EVALUATION")
    print("-" * 72)

    evaluator = DeterministicEvaluator()

    criterion_scores = evaluator.evaluate(
        challenge
    )

    evaluation_engine = EvaluationEngine(
        criteria=[
            COMPLETENESS,
            LANGUAGE_LEVEL,
            EDUCATIONAL_VALUE,
            SOURCE_COVERAGE,
        ],
        passing_score=0.70,
    )

    evaluation_result = evaluation_engine.evaluate(
        criterion_scores
    )

    report = EvaluationReport().generate(
        evaluation_result
    )

    print(report)

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # STEP 4 â€” PERSONALISED FLASH DEAL
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    print("STEP 4 â€” PERSONALISED DISCOVERY")
    print("-" * 72)
    print(
        "Yeti found something that matches "
        "what this learner likes."
    )
    print(
        "Here is the personalised discovery:"
    )
    print()

    # Build the same domain inputs that a real discovery
    # application flow would receive.
    demo_deal = build_demo_deal()
    demo_memory = build_demo_memory()

    discovery_request = DiscoveryRequest(
        user_id=demo_memory.user_id,
        deal=demo_deal,
        character=CharacterContext(
            character_id="yeti",
            character_name="Yeti",
            message=(
                "Look what I found! "
                "This could be perfect for you."
            ),
        ),
        product_image_url=(
            "https://example.com/demo/iphone17-pro.jpg"
        ),
        tagline="Pro. Beautiful. Powerful.",
        highlights=(
            "Pro camera system",
            "High-performance chip",
            "All-day battery life",
            "Premium display",
        ),
    )

    flash_deal_card = DiscoveryService().create_flash_deal(
        discovery_request,
        memories=[demo_memory],
    )

    print(
        FlashDealPresenter().render(
            flash_deal_card
        )
    )

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # STEP 4A â€” CHARACTER DISCOVERY NOTIFICATION
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    discovery_notification = (
        DiscoveryNotification.from_flash_deal(
            notification_id="demo-notification-001",
            user_id=demo_memory.user_id,
            deal=flash_deal_card,
        )
    )

    print()
    print("CHARACTER NOTIFICATION")
    print("-" * 72)
    print(
        discovery_notification.render()
    )
    print()

    assert (
        discovery_notification.user_id
        == demo_memory.user_id
    )
    assert (
        discovery_notification.character_id
        == flash_deal_card.character_id
    )
    assert (
        discovery_notification.deal
        is flash_deal_card
    )

    assert flash_deal_card.character_id == "yeti"
    assert flash_deal_card.deal_id == demo_deal.deal_id
    assert flash_deal_card.product_name
    assert demo_memory.user_id == "demo-user"
    assert flash_deal_card.relevance_reason
    assert flash_deal_card.get_deal_url
    assert flash_deal_card.more_information_url

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # STEP 5 â€” COMMERCE ACTION
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    print(
        "The learner can now inspect the vendor "
        "information or proceed through the deal link."
    )
    print()

    print("STEP 5 â€” COMMERCE ACTION")
    print("-" * 72)

    commerce_action = FlashDealCommerce().create_action(
        user_id="demo-user",
        card=flash_deal_card,
    )

    print(
        f"Product: {flash_deal_card.product_name}"
    )
    print(
        f"Vendor: {flash_deal_card.vendor_name}"
    )
    print(
        f"Commerce action: "
        f"{commerce_action.action_type.value.upper()}"
    )

    commerce_result = CommerceService().initiate(
        commerce_action
    )

    print(
        f"Status: "
        f"{commerce_result.status.value.upper()}"
    )

    print()
    print("GET DEAL")
    print("  -> Approved commerce boundary")
    print("  -> Vendor handoff")
    print()
    print(
        "Payment is not executed in the hackathon demo."
    )

    assert (
        commerce_result.status
        == CommerceActionStatus.REDIRECT
    )
    assert (
        commerce_result.deal_id
        == flash_deal_card.deal_id
    )
    assert (
        commerce_result.next_url
        == flash_deal_card.get_deal_url
    )

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # STEP 6 â€” Final result
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    print()
    print("=" * 72)

    if evaluation_result.passed:
        print("HACKATHON DEMO: SUCCESS")
        print()
        # ASCII-safe output keeps the executable reliable when
        # stdout is captured by Windows pytest/subprocess.
        # Preserve the stable hackathon readiness marker.
        print(
            "Governance -> Intelligence -> Learning -> Evaluation"
        )
        print(
            "Governance -> Intelligence -> Learning -> "
            "Evaluation -> Personalised Discovery"
        )
        print(
            "All demonstrated capabilities completed successfully."

    print("=" * 72)

    assert challenge.topic == "AI Act"
    assert evaluation_result.passed is True

"""

import asyncio
import sys
from pathlib import Path

async def run_demo() -> None:
    """
    Execute the complete H1 demonstration flow.
    """

    print()
    print("=" * 60)
    print("EasyChatBot â€” Sprint H1")
    print("Live Intelligence Learning Challenge")
    print("=" * 60)
    print()

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 1. Create Live Intelligence Agent
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    intelligence_agent = LiveIntelligenceAgent(
        search_provider=DemoSearchProvider(),
        processor=DemoIntelligenceProcessor(),
    )

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 2. Research topic
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    print("Researching topic...")
    print()

    intelligence = await intelligence_agent.execute(
        LiveIntelligenceRequest(
            topic="AI Act",
            language="French",
            learner_level="B2",
        )
    )

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 3. Display research result
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    print("RESEARCH")
    print("-" * 60)
    print(f"Topic: {intelligence.topic}")
    print(f"Summary: {intelligence.summary}")
    print()

    print("Key information:")

    for item in intelligence.key_information:
        print(f"  â€¢ {item}")

    print()

    print("Sources:")

    for source in intelligence.sources:
        print(f"  â€¢ {source.title}")
        print(f"    {source.url}")
        print(f"    Provider: {source.source}")

    print()

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 4. Create learning challenge
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    challenge_generator = ChallengeGenerator(
        content_generator=DemoChallengeContentGenerator()
    )

    challenge = await challenge_generator.generate(
        intelligence=intelligence,
        language="French",
        learner_level="B2",
    )

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 5. Display learning challenge
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    print("LEARNING CHALLENGE")
    print("-" * 60)

    print(f"Title: {challenge.title}")
    print(f"Language: {challenge.language}")
    print(f"Level: {challenge.learner_level}")
    print()

    print("Vocabulary:")

    for item in challenge.vocabulary:
        print(f"  â€¢ {item.term}")
        print(f"    {item.definition}")

        if item.example:
            print(f"    Example: {item.example}")

    print()

    print("Discussion Questions:")

    for index, question in enumerate(
        challenge.discussion_questions,
        start=1,
    ):
        print(f"  {index}. {question.question}")
        print(f"     Level: {question.difficulty}")

    print()
    print("=" * 60)
    print("H1 vertical slice completed successfully.")
    print("=" * 60)
    print()


def main() -> None:
    """
    Synchronous entry point.
    """

    asyncio.run(run_demo())


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

"""
Live Intelligence Agent Handler.

Responsible for executing the Live Intelligence Agent workflow.

Architecture:

AI Runtime
    |
    v
Live Intelligence Agent Handler
    |
    +--> Search Provider Interface
    |
    +--> Knowledge Processing
    |
    v
Structured Intelligence Output

Responsibilities:
- Validate agent input
- Call approved provider interfaces
- Normalise research output
- Return explicit structured results

Non-responsibilities:
- Provider SDK management
- Direct external API handling
- Autonomous actions
- Configuration changes
"""

from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import List, Protocol

from src.providers.search_provider.interface import (
    SearchProvider,
    SearchResult,
)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class LiveIntelligenceRequest:
    """
    Input contract for Live Intelligence Agent.
    """

    topic: str
    language: str = "French"
    learner_level: str = "B2"


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class LiveIntelligenceResponse:
    """
    Output contract from Live Intelligence Agent.
    """

    topic: str
    summary: str
    sources: List[SearchResult]
    key_information: List[str]


class IntelligenceProcessor(Protocol):
    """
    Contract for future LLM-based processing.

    Keeps the agent independent from
    any specific model/provider.
    """

    async def summarise(
        self,
        results: List[SearchResult],
        language: str,
        learner_level: str,
    ) -> tuple[str, List[str]]:
        ...


class LiveIntelligenceAgent:
    """
    Live Intelligence Agent implementation.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        search_provider: SearchProvider,
        processor: IntelligenceProcessor,
    ) -> None:
        self._search_provider = search_provider
        self._processor = processor

    async def execute(
        self,
        request: LiveIntelligenceRequest,
    ) -> LiveIntelligenceResponse:
        """
        Execute research workflow.

        Flow:

        Request
          |
          v
        Search Provider
          |
          v
        Intelligence Processor
          |
          v
        Structured Response
        """

        self._validate_request(request)

        search_results = await self._search_provider.search(
            query=request.topic,
            max_results=5,
        )

        summary, key_information = await self._processor.summarise(
            results=search_results,
            language=request.language,
            learner_level=request.learner_level,
        )

        return LiveIntelligenceResponse(
            topic=request.topic,
            summary=summary,
            sources=search_results,
            key_information=key_information,
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _validate_request(
        request: LiveIntelligenceRequest,
    ) -> None:
        """
        Validate required input.
        """

        if not request.topic.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Topic cannot be empty"
            )
