FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\src\ai_runtime\capabilities\executor.py
============================================================
"""
AI Runtime capability execution contract.

This module defines the interface between the AI Runtime
and concrete capability executors.

Architecture:

AI Runtime
    |
    v
Capability Executor Interface
    |
    +---- Live Intelligence Executor
    +---- Learning Executor
    +---- Evaluation Executor
    +---- Future Executors

The Runtime depends on this contract.

It does not depend directly on:
- agents
- providers
- SDKs
- external APIs

Concrete executors are responsible for translating the
provider-independent CapabilityRequest into the appropriate
capability implementation.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from abc import ABC, abstractmethod

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.models import (
    CapabilityRequest,
    CapabilityResult,
    CapabilityType,
)


class CapabilityExecutor(ABC):
    """
    Abstract executor for one Runtime capability.

    Each concrete executor must declare which capability
    it handles and implement asynchronous execution.
    """

    @property
    @abstractmethod
    def capability(self) -> CapabilityType:
        """
        Return the capability handled by this executor.
        """
        raise NotImplementedError

    @abstractmethod
    async def execute(
        self,
        request: CapabilityRequest,
    ) -> CapabilityResult:
        """
        Execute a capability request.

        Implementations must return a provider-independent
        CapabilityResult.

        They must not bypass the Runtime governance boundary.
        """
        raise NotImplementedError

============================================================
FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\src\ai_runtime\capabilities\gateway.py
============================================================
"""
AI Runtime Capability Gateway.

Controlled execution boundary between applications and
concrete AI capabilities.

Flow:

Application
    â†“
CapabilityRequest
    â†“
CapabilityGateway
    â†“
Governance
    â†“
CapabilityRegistry
    â†“
CapabilityExecutor
    â†“
CapabilityResult
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Protocol

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.errors import (
    CapabilityExecutionError,
    CapabilityGovernanceError,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.models import (
    CapabilityExecutionStatus,
    CapabilityRequest,
    CapabilityResult,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.registry import (
    CapabilityRegistry,
)


class GovernanceDecisionResult(Protocol):
    """
    Minimal governance result required by the Runtime.
    """

    @property
    def allowed(self) -> bool:
        """
        Whether execution is permitted.
        """
        ...


class CapabilityGovernance(Protocol):
    """
    Governance boundary required by the Runtime gateway.
    """

    def evaluate(
        self,
        request: CapabilityRequest,
    ) -> GovernanceDecisionResult:
        """
        Evaluate whether a capability may execute.
        """
        ...


class CapabilityGateway:
    """
    Controlled Runtime entry point for capability execution.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        registry: CapabilityRegistry,
        governance: CapabilityGovernance,
    ) -> None:
        self._registry = registry
        self._governance = governance

    async def execute(
        self,
        request: CapabilityRequest,
    ) -> CapabilityResult:
        """
        Execute an approved capability.

        Governance is evaluated before resolving and executing
        the capability.
        """

        try:
            governance_result = (
                self._governance.evaluate(request)
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            raise CapabilityGovernanceError(
                "Governance evaluation failed."
            ) from exc

        if not governance_result.allowed:
            return CapabilityResult(
                request_id=request.request_id,
                capability=request.capability,
                status=CapabilityExecutionStatus.REJECTED,
                error=(
                    "Capability execution was rejected "
                    "by governance."
                ),
                metadata={
                    "governance_decision": "rejected",
                },
            )

        executor = self._registry.resolve(
            request.capability
        )

        try:
            return await executor.execute(request)
        except Exception as exc:
            raise CapabilityExecutionError(
                "Capability executor failed: "
                f"{request.capability.value}"
            ) from exc

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\src\ai_runtime\capabilities\live_intelligence_executor.py
============================================================
"""
AI Runtime executor for Live Intelligence.

This adapter bridges the provider-independent AI Runtime
capability contract with the existing LiveIntelligenceAgent.

Architecture:

AI Runtime
    |
    v
CapabilityRequest
    |
    v
LiveIntelligenceExecutor
    |
    v
LiveIntelligenceAgent
    |
    v
LiveIntelligenceResponse

The adapter:

- validates the Runtime capability
- translates Runtime payloads into LiveIntelligenceRequest
- invokes the existing Live Intelligence Agent
- translates the agent response into CapabilityResult

It does not:

- implement intelligence logic
- call Parallel directly
- modify the LiveIntelligenceAgent
- perform governance decisions
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from src.ai_agents.live_intelligence_agent.handler import (
    LiveIntelligenceAgent,
    LiveIntelligenceRequest,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.executor import (
    CapabilityExecutor,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.models import (
    CapabilityExecutionStatus,
    CapabilityRequest,
    CapabilityResult,
    CapabilityType,
)


class LiveIntelligenceExecutor(
    CapabilityExecutor,
):
    """
    Runtime adapter for the existing LiveIntelligenceAgent.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        agent: LiveIntelligenceAgent,
    ) -> None:
        self._agent = agent

    @property
    def capability(self) -> CapabilityType:
        """
        Return the capability handled by this executor.
        """

        return CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE

    async def execute(
        self,
        request: CapabilityRequest,
    ) -> CapabilityResult:
        """
        Translate and execute a Live Intelligence request.
        """

        if (
            request.capability
            != CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "LiveIntelligenceExecutor received "
                "an unsupported capability: "
                f"{request.capability.value}"
            )

        agent_request = (
            self._build_agent_request(request)
        )

        response = await self._agent.execute(
            agent_request
        )

        return CapabilityResult(
            request_id=request.request_id,
            capability=request.capability,
            status=CapabilityExecutionStatus.COMPLETED,
            output=response,
            metadata={
                "executor": (
                    "live_intelligence_executor"
                ),
            },
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _build_agent_request(
        request: CapabilityRequest,
    ) -> LiveIntelligenceRequest:
        """
        Translate Runtime payload into the existing
        LiveIntelligenceRequest contract.
        """

        payload = request.payload

        topic = payload.get("topic")

        if not isinstance(topic, str):
            raise ValueError(
                "Live Intelligence capability requires "
                "a string 'topic'."
            )

        language = payload.get(
            "language",
            "French",
        )

        learner_level = payload.get(
            "learner_level",
            "B2",
        )

        if not isinstance(language, str):
            raise ValueError(
                "Live Intelligence 'language' "
                "must be a string."
            )

        if not isinstance(
            learner_level,
            str,
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "Live Intelligence 'learner_level' "
                "must be a string."
            )

        return LiveIntelligenceRequest(
            topic=topic,
            language=language,
            learner_level=learner_level,
        )

============================================================
FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\src\ai_runtime\capabilities\registry.py
============================================================
"""
AI Runtime capability registry.

The registry maps logical Runtime capabilities to their
concrete executors.

The registry:
- resolves executors by capability
- prevents duplicate registrations
- provides explicit dependency injection
- does not execute capabilities
- does not perform governance decisions
- does not know about providers
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.errors import (
    CapabilityAlreadyRegisteredError,
    CapabilityNotRegisteredError,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.executor import (
    CapabilityExecutor,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.models import (
    CapabilityType,
)


class CapabilityRegistry:
    """
    Dependency-injection registry for Runtime capabilities.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        executors: list[CapabilityExecutor] | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self._executors: dict[
            CapabilityType,
            CapabilityExecutor,
        ] = {}

        for executor in executors or []:
            self.register(executor)

    def register(
        self,
        executor: CapabilityExecutor,
    ) -> None:
        """
        Register an executor for its declared capability.
        """

        capability = executor.capability

        if capability in self._executors:
            raise CapabilityAlreadyRegisteredError(
                "Capability executor already registered: "
                f"{capability.value}"
            )

        self._executors[capability] = executor

    def resolve(
        self,
        capability: CapabilityType,
    ) -> CapabilityExecutor:
        """
        Resolve the executor responsible for a capability.
        """

        try:
            return self._executors[capability]
        except KeyError as exc:
            raise CapabilityNotRegisteredError(
                "No executor registered for capability: "
                f"{capability.value}"
            ) from exc

    def contains(
        self,
        capability: CapabilityType,
    ) -> bool:
        """
        Return whether an executor is registered.
        """

        return capability in self._executors

    def capabilities(
        self,
    ) -> tuple[CapabilityType, ...]:
        """
        Return all registered capabilities.
        """

        return tuple(self._executors.keys())

"""

import pytest

        return []

    async def summarise(
        self,
        results,
        language,

    ):
        return (
            "AI Act summary",
            ["regulation", "governance"],
        )

"""
Tests for the AI Runtime Capability Gateway.
"""

import pytest

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.errors import (
    CapabilityExecutionError,
    CapabilityGovernanceError,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.executor import (
    CapabilityExecutor,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.gateway import (
    CapabilityGateway,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.models import (
    CapabilityExecutionStatus,
    CapabilityRequest,
    CapabilityResult,
    CapabilityType,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.registry import (
    CapabilityRegistry,
)


class FakeGovernanceResult:
    """
    Minimal governance result for gateway tests.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        allowed: bool,
    ) -> None:
        self.allowed = allowed


class FakeGovernance:
    """
    Deterministic governance implementation for tests.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        allowed: bool,
    ) -> None:
        self.allowed = allowed
        self.calls = 0

    def evaluate(
        self,
        request: CapabilityRequest,
    ) -> FakeGovernanceResult:

        self.calls += 1

        return FakeGovernanceResult(
            allowed=self.allowed
        )


class FailingGovernance:
    """
    Governance implementation that fails unexpectedly.
    """

    def evaluate(
        self,
        request: CapabilityRequest,
    ) -> FakeGovernanceResult:

        raise RuntimeError(
            "governance infrastructure failure"
        )


class FakeExecutor(CapabilityExecutor):
    """
    Fake executor used to verify gateway orchestration.
    """

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.calls = 0
        self.last_request: CapabilityRequest | None = None

    @property
    def capability(self) -> CapabilityType:
        return CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE

    async def execute(
        self,
        request: CapabilityRequest,
    ) -> CapabilityResult:

        self.calls += 1
        self.last_request = request

        return CapabilityResult(
            request_id=request.request_id,
            capability=request.capability,
            status=CapabilityExecutionStatus.COMPLETED,
            output={
                "executed": True,
            },
        )


class FailingExecutor(CapabilityExecutor):
    """
    Executor that fails unexpectedly.
    """

    @property
    def capability(self) -> CapabilityType:
        return CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE

    async def execute(
        self,
        request: CapabilityRequest,
    ) -> CapabilityResult:

        raise RuntimeError(
            "provider failure"
        )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_gateway_executes_allowed_capability():

    executor = FakeExecutor()

    registry = CapabilityRegistry(
        executors=[executor]
    )

    governance = FakeGovernance(
        allowed=True
    )

    gateway = CapabilityGateway(
        registry=registry,
        governance=governance,
    )

    request = CapabilityRequest(
        capability=CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE,
        request_id="gateway-001",
        payload={
            "topic": "AI Act",
        },
    )

    result = await gateway.execute(
        request
    )

    assert (
        result.status
        == CapabilityExecutionStatus.COMPLETED
    )

    assert result.succeeded is True

    assert governance.calls == 1

    assert executor.calls == 1

    assert executor.last_request is request


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_gateway_rejects_blocked_capability():

    executor = FakeExecutor()

    registry = CapabilityRegistry(
        executors=[executor]
    )

    governance = FakeGovernance(
        allowed=False
    )

    gateway = CapabilityGateway(
        registry=registry,
        governance=governance,
    )

    request = CapabilityRequest(
        capability=CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE,
        request_id="gateway-002",
        payload={
            "topic": "AI Act",
        },
    )

    result = await gateway.execute(
        request
    )

    assert (
        result.status
        == CapabilityExecutionStatus.REJECTED
    )

    assert result.rejected is True

    assert executor.calls == 0

    assert governance.calls == 1

    assert (
        result.metadata["governance_decision"]
        == "rejected"
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_gateway_passes_original_request_to_executor():

    executor = FakeExecutor()

    registry = CapabilityRegistry(
        executors=[executor]
    )

    governance = FakeGovernance(
        allowed=True
    )

    gateway = CapabilityGateway(
        registry=registry,
        governance=governance,
    )

    request = CapabilityRequest(
        capability=CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE,
        request_id="gateway-003",
        payload={
            "topic": "European AI regulation",
            "language": "French",
        },
        metadata={
            "trace_id": "trace-003",
        },
        requested_by="learning_application",
    )

    await gateway.execute(
        request
    )

    assert executor.last_request is request

    assert (
        executor.last_request.payload["language"]
        == "French"
    )

    assert (
        executor.last_request.metadata["trace_id"]
        == "trace-003"
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_governance_is_evaluated_before_execution():

    executor = FakeExecutor()

    registry = CapabilityRegistry(
        executors=[executor]
    )

    governance = FakeGovernance(
        allowed=False
    )

    gateway = CapabilityGateway(
        registry=registry,
        governance=governance,
    )

    request = CapabilityRequest(
        capability=CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE,
        request_id="gateway-004",

        request

    assert result.rejected is True

    assert governance.calls == 1

    assert executor.calls == 0


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_gateway_wraps_governance_failure():

    executor = FakeExecutor()

    registry = CapabilityRegistry(
        executors=[executor]
    )

    gateway = CapabilityGateway(
        registry=registry,
        governance=FailingGovernance(),
    )

    request = CapabilityRequest(
        capability=CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE,
        request_id="gateway-error-001",
        payload={
            "topic": "AI Act",
        },
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        CapabilityGovernanceError,
        match="Governance evaluation failed",
    ):
        await gateway.execute(request)

    assert executor.calls == 0

async def test_gateway_wraps_executor_failure():

    executor = FailingExecutor()

    registry = CapabilityRegistry(
        executors=[executor]
    )

    gateway = CapabilityGateway(
        registry=registry,
        governance=FakeGovernance(
            allowed=True
        ),
    )

    request = CapabilityRequest(
        capability=CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE,
        request_id="gateway-error-002",
        payload={
            "topic": "AI Act",
        },
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        CapabilityExecutionError,
        match="Capability executor failed",
    ):
        await gateway.execute(request)

        request

    assert result.output == {
        "topic": "European AI regulation",
        "language": "French",
        "learner_level": "B2",
        "summary": "AI Act learning content",
    }

    assert (
        result.metadata["executor"]
        == "live_intelligence_executor"
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_executor_rejects_wrong_capability():

    agent = FakeLiveIntelligenceAgent()

    executor = LiveIntelligenceExecutor(
        agent=agent
    )

    request = CapabilityRequest(
        capability=CapabilityType.EVALUATION,
        request_id="live-runtime-004",
        payload={},
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="unsupported capability",
    ):
        await executor.execute(request)

    assert agent.calls == 0


def test_executor_requires_topic():

    request = CapabilityRequest(
        capability=CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE,
        request_id="live-runtime-005",
        payload={},
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="requires a string 'topic'",
    ):
        LiveIntelligenceExecutor._build_agent_request(
            request
        )


def test_executor_rejects_non_string_topic():

    request = CapabilityRequest(
        capability=CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE,
        request_id="live-runtime-006",
        payload={
            "topic": 123,
        },
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="requires a string 'topic'",
    ):
        LiveIntelligenceExecutor._build_agent_request(
            request
        )

"""
Tests for the AI Runtime capability registry.
"""

import pytest

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.errors import (
    CapabilityAlreadyRegisteredError,
    CapabilityNotRegisteredError,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.executor import (
    CapabilityExecutor,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.models import (
    CapabilityExecutionStatus,
    CapabilityRequest,
    CapabilityResult,
    CapabilityType,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.registry import (
    CapabilityRegistry,
)


class FakeLiveIntelligenceExecutor(
    CapabilityExecutor,
):
    """
    Fake executor used to test dependency injection.
    """

    @property
    def capability(self) -> CapabilityType:
        return CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE

    async def execute(
        self,
        request: CapabilityRequest,
    ) -> CapabilityResult:

        return CapabilityResult(
            request_id=request.request_id,
            capability=request.capability,
            status=CapabilityExecutionStatus.COMPLETED,
            output={"ok": True},
        )


class FakeEvaluationExecutor(
    CapabilityExecutor,
):
    """
    Second fake executor used to verify multiple
    capability registrations.
    """

    @property
    def capability(self) -> CapabilityType:
        return CapabilityType.EVALUATION

    async def execute(
        self,
        request: CapabilityRequest,
    ) -> CapabilityResult:

        return CapabilityResult(
            request_id=request.request_id,
            capability=request.capability,
            status=CapabilityExecutionStatus.COMPLETED,
            output={"evaluated": True},
        )


def test_registry_registers_executor():

    executor = FakeLiveIntelligenceExecutor()

    registry = CapabilityRegistry()

    registry.register(executor)

    assert registry.contains(
        CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE
    )


def test_registry_resolves_registered_executor():

    executor = FakeLiveIntelligenceExecutor()

    registry = CapabilityRegistry(
        executors=[executor]
    )

    resolved = registry.resolve(
        CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE
    )

    assert resolved is executor


def test_registry_supports_multiple_capabilities():

    live_executor = FakeLiveIntelligenceExecutor()
    evaluation_executor = FakeEvaluationExecutor()

    registry = CapabilityRegistry(
        executors=[
            live_executor,
            evaluation_executor,
        ]
    )

    assert registry.resolve(
        CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE
    ) is live_executor

    assert registry.resolve(
        CapabilityType.EVALUATION
    ) is evaluation_executor


def test_duplicate_capability_registration_is_rejected():

    registry = CapabilityRegistry()

    registry.register(
        FakeLiveIntelligenceExecutor()
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        CapabilityAlreadyRegisteredError,
        match="already registered",
    ):
        registry.register(
            FakeLiveIntelligenceExecutor()
        )


def test_unknown_capability_resolution_is_rejected():

    registry = CapabilityRegistry()

    with pytest.raises(
        CapabilityNotRegisteredError,
        match="No executor registered",
    ):
        registry.resolve(
            CapabilityType.LEARNING_GENERATION
        )


def test_contains_returns_false_for_unknown_capability():

    registry = CapabilityRegistry()

    assert (
        registry.contains(
            CapabilityType.EVALUATION
        )
        is False
    )


def test_capabilities_returns_registered_capabilities():

    registry = CapabilityRegistry(
        executors=[
            FakeLiveIntelligenceExecutor(),
            FakeEvaluationExecutor(),
        ]
    )

    capabilities = registry.capabilities()

    assert (
        CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE
        in capabilities
    )

    assert (
        CapabilityType.EVALUATION
        in capabilities
    )

    assert (
        CapabilityType.LEARNING_GENERATION
        not in capabilities
    )

"""
Integration test for Live Challenge vertical slice.

Flow tested:

Search Provider
        â†“
Live Intelligence Agent
        â†“
Challenge Generator
        â†“
Learning Challenge

This test validates the Sprint H1 integration boundaries.

Important:
- Uses test doubles only.
- Does not call the real Parallel API.
- Does not modify or depend on the Conversation Agent.
"""

import pytest

from src.ai_agents.live_intelligence_agent.handler import (
    LiveIntelligenceAgent,
    LiveIntelligenceRequest,
)

from src.challenge_engine.generator import (
    ChallengeGenerator,
)

from src.challenge_engine.models import (
    DiscussionQuestion,
    VocabularyItem,
)

from src.providers.search_provider.interface import (
    SearchResult,
)


class FakeSearchProvider:
    """
    Controlled search provider for integration testing.
    """

    async def search(
        self,
        query: str,
        max_results: int = 5,
    ) -> list[SearchResult]:
        return [
            SearchResult(
                title="AI Act Update",
                url="https://example.com/ai-act",
                content="European AI regulation update.",
                source="test-provider",
            )
        ]


class FakeIntelligenceProcessor:
    """
    Simulates intelligence synthesis without
    requiring an LLM provider.
    """

    async def summarise(
        self,
        results: list[SearchResult],
        language: str,
        learner_level: str,
    ) -> tuple[str, list[str]]:
        return (
            "The AI Act introduces new rules for "
            "artificial intelligence.",
            [
                "AI governance",
                "risk management",
            ],
        )


class FakeChallengeContentGenerator:
    """
    Simulates educational content generation.
    """

    async def generate_vocabulary(
        self,
        summary: str,
        language: str,
        learner_level: str,
    ) -> list[VocabularyItem]:
        return [
            VocabularyItem(
                term="gouvernance",
                definition=(
                    "Organisation and control of activities."
                ),
            )
        ]

    async def generate_questions(
        self,
        summary: str,
        language: str,
        learner_level: str,
    ) -> list[DiscussionQuestion]:
        return [
            DiscussionQuestion(
                question=(
                    "Pourquoi la gouvernance de l'IA "
                    "est-elle importante ?"
                ),
            )
        ]


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_live_challenge_vertical_slice():
    """
    Validate the complete Sprint H1 backend flow:

    Search Provider
        â†“
    Live Intelligence Agent
        â†“
    Challenge Generator
        â†“
    Learning Challenge
    """

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Step 1 â€” Create Live Intelligence Agent
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    intelligence_agent = LiveIntelligenceAgent(
        search_provider=FakeSearchProvider(),
        processor=FakeIntelligenceProcessor(),
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Step 2 â€” Execute Live Intelligence research
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    intelligence = await intelligence_agent.execute(
        request=LiveIntelligenceRequest(
            topic="AI Act",
        )
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Step 3 â€” Validate intelligence output
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    assert intelligence.topic == "AI Act"

    assert intelligence.summary == (
        "The AI Act introduces new rules for "
        "artificial intelligence."
    )

    assert intelligence.key_information == [
        "AI governance",
        "risk management",
    ]

    assert len(intelligence.sources) == 1
    assert intelligence.sources[0].title == "AI Act Update"
    assert intelligence.sources[0].source == "test-provider"

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Step 4 â€” Create Challenge Generator
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    challenge_generator = ChallengeGenerator(
        content_generator=FakeChallengeContentGenerator()
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Step 5 â€” Generate learning challenge
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    challenge = await challenge_generator.generate(
        intelligence=intelligence,
        language="French",
        learner_level="B2",
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Step 6 â€” Validate final learning output
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    assert challenge.title == (
        "AI Act Learning Challenge"
    )

    assert challenge.topic == "AI Act"
    assert challenge.language == "French"
    assert challenge.learner_level == "B2"

    assert challenge.summary == (
        "The AI Act introduces new rules for "
        "artificial intelligence."
    )

    assert len(challenge.vocabulary) == 1

    assert challenge.vocabulary[0].term == (
        "gouvernance"
    )

    assert (
        challenge.vocabulary[0].definition
        == "Organisation and control of activities."
    )

    assert len(challenge.discussion_questions) == 1

    assert (
        challenge.discussion_questions[0].question
        == "Pourquoi la gouvernance de l'IA est-elle importante ?"
    )

    assert (
        challenge.discussion_questions[0].difficulty
        == "B2"
    )

    assert (
        challenge.created_by_agent
        == "live_intelligence_agent"
    )

        return (

"""
Tests for the AI Runtime capability executor contract.
"""

import pytest

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.executor import (
    CapabilityExecutor,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.models import (
    CapabilityExecutionStatus,
    CapabilityRequest,
    CapabilityResult,
    CapabilityType,
)


class FakeCapabilityExecutor(CapabilityExecutor):
    """
    Minimal executor used only to verify the contract.
    """

    @property
    def capability(self) -> CapabilityType:
        return CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE

    async def execute(
        self,
        request: CapabilityRequest,
    ) -> CapabilityResult:

        return CapabilityResult(
            request_id=request.request_id,
            capability=request.capability,
            status=CapabilityExecutionStatus.COMPLETED,
            output={
                "executed": True,
            },
        )


def test_executor_declares_capability():

    executor = FakeCapabilityExecutor()

    assert (
        executor.capability
        == CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_executor_returns_provider_independent_result():

    executor = FakeCapabilityExecutor()

    request = CapabilityRequest(
        capability=CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE,
        request_id="req-executor-001",
        payload={
            "topic": "AI Act",
        },
    )

    result = await executor.execute(request)

    assert isinstance(
        result,
        CapabilityResult,
    )

    assert (
        result.request_id
        == "req-executor-001"
    )

    assert (
        result.capability
        == CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE
    )

    assert (
        result.status
        == CapabilityExecutionStatus.COMPLETED
    )

    assert result.output == {
        "executed": True,
    }

    with pytest.raises(TypeError):

        CapabilityExecutor()

TEST FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\tests\ai_runtime\capabilities\test_gateway.py

"""
Tests for the AI Runtime Capability Gateway.
"""

import pytest

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.errors import (
    CapabilityExecutionError,
    CapabilityGovernanceError,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.executor import (
    CapabilityExecutor,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.gateway import (
    CapabilityGateway,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.models import (
    CapabilityExecutionStatus,
    CapabilityRequest,
    CapabilityResult,
    CapabilityType,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.registry import (
    CapabilityRegistry,
)


class FakeGovernanceResult:
    """
    Minimal governance result for gateway tests.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        allowed: bool,
    ) -> None:
        self.allowed = allowed


class FakeGovernance:
    """
    Deterministic governance implementation for tests.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        allowed: bool,
    ) -> None:
        self.allowed = allowed
        self.calls = 0

    def evaluate(
        self,
        request: CapabilityRequest,
    ) -> FakeGovernanceResult:

        self.calls += 1

        return FakeGovernanceResult(
            allowed=self.allowed
        )


class FailingGovernance:
    """
    Governance implementation that fails unexpectedly.
    """

    def evaluate(
        self,
        request: CapabilityRequest,
    ) -> FakeGovernanceResult:

        raise RuntimeError(
            "governance infrastructure failure"
        )


class FakeExecutor(CapabilityExecutor):
    """
    Fake executor used to verify gateway orchestration.
    """

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.calls = 0
        self.last_request: CapabilityRequest | None = None

    @property
    def capability(self) -> CapabilityType:
        return CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE

    async def execute(
        self,
        request: CapabilityRequest,
    ) -> CapabilityResult:

        self.calls += 1
        self.last_request = request

        return CapabilityResult(
            request_id=request.request_id,
            capability=request.capability,
            status=CapabilityExecutionStatus.COMPLETED,
            output={
                "executed": True,
            },
        )


class FailingExecutor(CapabilityExecutor):
    """
    Executor that fails unexpectedly.
    """

    @property
    def capability(self) -> CapabilityType:
        return CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE

    async def execute(
        self,
        request: CapabilityRequest,
    ) -> CapabilityResult:

        raise RuntimeError(
            "provider failure"
        )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_gateway_executes_allowed_capability():

    executor = FakeExecutor()

    registry = CapabilityRegistry(
        executors=[executor]
    )

    governance = FakeGovernance(
        allowed=True
    )

    gateway = CapabilityGateway(
        registry=registry,
        governance=governance,
    )

    request = CapabilityRequest(
        capability=CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE,
        request_id="gateway-001",
        payload={
            "topic": "AI Act",
        },
    )

    result = await gateway.execute(
        request
    )

    assert (
        result.status
        == CapabilityExecutionStatus.COMPLETED
    )

    assert result.succeeded is True

    assert governance.calls == 1

    assert executor.calls == 1

    assert executor.last_request is request


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_gateway_rejects_blocked_capability():

    executor = FakeExecutor()

    registry = CapabilityRegistry(
        executors=[executor]
    )

    governance = FakeGovernance(
        allowed=False
    )

    gateway = CapabilityGateway(
        registry=registry,
        governance=governance,
    )

    request = CapabilityRequest(
        capability=CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE,
        request_id="gateway-002",
        payload={
            "topic": "AI Act",
        },
    )

    result = await gateway.execute(
        request
    )

    assert (
        result.status
        == CapabilityExecutionStatus.REJECTED
    )

    assert result.rejected is True

    assert executor.calls == 0

    assert governance.calls == 1

    assert (
        result.metadata["governance_decision"]
        == "rejected"
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_gateway_passes_original_request_to_executor():

    executor = FakeExecutor()

    registry = CapabilityRegistry(
        executors=[executor]
    )

    governance = FakeGovernance(
        allowed=True
    )

    gateway = CapabilityGateway(
        registry=registry,
        governance=governance,
    )

    request = CapabilityRequest(
        capability=CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE,
        request_id="gateway-003",
        payload={
            "topic": "European AI regulation",
            "language": "French",
        },
        metadata={
            "trace_id": "trace-003",
        },
        requested_by="learning_application",
    )

    await gateway.execute(
        request
    )

    assert executor.last_request is request

    assert (
        executor.last_request.payload["language"]
        == "French"
    )

    assert (
        executor.last_request.metadata["trace_id"]
        == "trace-003"
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_governance_is_evaluated_before_execution():

    executor = FakeExecutor()

    registry = CapabilityRegistry(
        executors=[executor]
    )

    governance = FakeGovernance(
        allowed=False
    )

    gateway = CapabilityGateway(
        registry=registry,
        governance=governance,
    )

    request = CapabilityRequest(
        capability=CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE,
        request_id="gateway-004",
        payload={},
    )

    result = await gateway.execute(
        request
    )

    assert result.rejected is True

    assert governance.calls == 1

    assert executor.calls == 0


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_gateway_wraps_governance_failure():

    executor = FakeExecutor()

    registry = CapabilityRegistry(
        executors=[executor]
    )

    gateway = CapabilityGateway(
        registry=registry,
        governance=FailingGovernance(),
    )

    request = CapabilityRequest(
        capability=CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE,
        request_id="gateway-error-001",
        payload={
            "topic": "AI Act",
        },
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        CapabilityGovernanceError,
        match="Governance evaluation failed",
    ):
        await gateway.execute(request)

    assert executor.calls == 0


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_gateway_wraps_executor_failure():

    executor = FailingExecutor()

    registry = CapabilityRegistry(
        executors=[executor]
    )

    gateway = CapabilityGateway(
        registry=registry,
        governance=FakeGovernance(
            allowed=True
        ),
    )

    request = CapabilityRequest(
        capability=CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE,
        request_id="gateway-error-002",
        payload={
            "topic": "AI Act",
        },
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        CapabilityExecutionError,
        match="Capability executor failed",
    ):
        await gateway.execute(request)

    )

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="requires a string 'topic'",
    ):
        LiveIntelligenceExecutor._build_agent_request(
            request
        )

TEST FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\tests\ai_runtime\capabilities\test_registry.py

"""
Tests for the AI Runtime capability registry.
"""

import pytest

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.errors import (
    CapabilityAlreadyRegisteredError,
    CapabilityNotRegisteredError,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.executor import (
    CapabilityExecutor,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.models import (
    CapabilityExecutionStatus,
    CapabilityRequest,
    CapabilityResult,
    CapabilityType,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.registry import (
    CapabilityRegistry,
)


class FakeLiveIntelligenceExecutor(
    CapabilityExecutor,
):
    """
    Fake executor used to test dependency injection.
    """

    @property
    def capability(self) -> CapabilityType:
        return CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE

    async def execute(
        self,
        request: CapabilityRequest,
    ) -> CapabilityResult:

        return CapabilityResult(
            request_id=request.request_id,
            capability=request.capability,
            status=CapabilityExecutionStatus.COMPLETED,
            output={"ok": True},
        )


class FakeEvaluationExecutor(
    CapabilityExecutor,
):
    """
    Second fake executor used to verify multiple
    capability registrations.
    """

    @property
    def capability(self) -> CapabilityType:
        return CapabilityType.EVALUATION

    async def execute(
        self,
        request: CapabilityRequest,
    ) -> CapabilityResult:

        return CapabilityResult(
            request_id=request.request_id,
            capability=request.capability,
            status=CapabilityExecutionStatus.COMPLETED,
            output={"evaluated": True},
        )


def test_registry_registers_executor():

    executor = FakeLiveIntelligenceExecutor()

    registry = CapabilityRegistry()

    registry.register(executor)

    assert registry.contains(
        CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE
    )


def test_registry_resolves_registered_executor():

    executor = FakeLiveIntelligenceExecutor()

    registry = CapabilityRegistry(
        executors=[executor]
    )

    resolved = registry.resolve(
        CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE
    )

    assert resolved is executor


def test_registry_supports_multiple_capabilities():

    live_executor = FakeLiveIntelligenceExecutor()
    evaluation_executor = FakeEvaluationExecutor()

    registry = CapabilityRegistry(
        executors=[
            live_executor,
            evaluation_executor,
        ]
    )

    assert registry.resolve(
        CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE
    ) is live_executor

    assert registry.resolve(
        CapabilityType.EVALUATION
    ) is evaluation_executor


def test_duplicate_capability_registration_is_rejected():

    registry = CapabilityRegistry()

    registry.register(
        FakeLiveIntelligenceExecutor()
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        CapabilityAlreadyRegisteredError,
        match="already registered",
    ):
        registry.register(
            FakeLiveIntelligenceExecutor()
        )


def test_unknown_capability_resolution_is_rejected():

    registry = CapabilityRegistry()

    with pytest.raises(
        CapabilityNotRegisteredError,
        match="No executor registered",
    ):
        registry.resolve(
            CapabilityType.LEARNING_GENERATION
        )


def test_contains_returns_false_for_unknown_capability():

    registry = CapabilityRegistry()

    assert (
        registry.contains(
            CapabilityType.EVALUATION
        )
        is False
    )


def test_capabilities_returns_registered_capabilities():

    registry = CapabilityRegistry(
        executors=[
            FakeLiveIntelligenceExecutor(),
            FakeEvaluationExecutor(),
        ]
    )

    capabilities = registry.capabilities()

    assert (
        CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE
        in capabilities
    )

    assert (
        CapabilityType.EVALUATION
        in capabilities
    )

    assert (
        CapabilityType.LEARNING_GENERATION
        not in capabilities
    )

"""

import pytest

from src.ai_agents.live_intelligence_agent.handler import (

)

    ChallengeGenerator,
)

    DiscussionQuestion,

)


class FakeContentGenerator:

    async def generate_vocabulary(
        self,
        summary,
        language,
        learner_level,
    ):
        return [
            VocabularyItem(
                term="innovation",
                definition="A new idea or approach.",
            )
        ]

    async def generate_questions(
        self,
        summary,
        language,
        learner_level,
    ):
        return [
            DiscussionQuestion(
                question="Quel est votre avis ?"
            )
        ]


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_generate_learning_challenge():

    generator = ChallengeGenerator(
        content_generator=FakeContentGenerator()
    )

    intelligence = LiveIntelligenceResponse(
        topic="AI Regulation",
        summary="AI regulation is evolving.",
        sources=[],
        key_information=[
            "governance"
        ],
    )

    result = await generator.generate(
        intelligence
    )

    assert result.topic == "AI Regulation"
    assert result.language == "French"
    assert result.vocabulary[0].term == "innovation"
    assert len(result.discussion_questions) == 1


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_generator_rejects_empty_summary():

    generator = ChallengeGenerator(
        content_generator=FakeContentGenerator()
    )

    intelligence = LiveIntelligenceResponse(
        topic="AI",
        summary="",
        sources=[],
        key_information=[],
    )

    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        await generator.generate(
            intelligence
        )

@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_live_challenge_vertical_slice():
    """
    Validate the complete Sprint H1 backend flow:

    Search Provider
        â†“
    Live Intelligence Agent
        â†“
    Challenge Generator
        â†“
    Learning Challenge
    """

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Step 1 â€” Create Live Intelligence Agent
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    intelligence_agent = LiveIntelligenceAgent(
        search_provider=FakeSearchProvider(),
        processor=FakeIntelligenceProcessor(),
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Step 2 â€” Execute Live Intelligence research
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    intelligence = await intelligence_agent.execute(
        request=LiveIntelligenceRequest(
            topic="AI Act",
        )
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Step 3 â€” Validate intelligence output
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    assert intelligence.topic == "AI Act"

    assert intelligence.summary == (
        "The AI Act introduces new rules for "
        "artificial intelligence."
    )

    assert intelligence.key_information == [
        "AI governance",
        "risk management",
    ]

    assert len(intelligence.sources) == 1
    assert intelligence.sources[0].title == "AI Act Update"
    assert intelligence.sources[0].source == "test-provider"

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Step 4 â€” Create Challenge Generator
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    challenge_generator = ChallengeGenerator(
        content_generator=FakeChallengeContentGenerator()
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Step 5 â€” Generate learning challenge
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    challenge = await challenge_generator.generate(
        intelligence=intelligence,
        language="French",
        learner_level="B2",
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Step 6 â€” Validate final learning output
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    assert challenge.title == (
        "AI Act Learning Challenge"
    )

    assert challenge.topic == "AI Act"
    assert challenge.language == "French"
    assert challenge.learner_level == "B2"

    assert challenge.summary == (
        "The AI Act introduces new rules for "
        "artificial intelligence."
    )

    assert len(challenge.vocabulary) == 1

    assert challenge.vocabulary[0].term == (
        "gouvernance"
    )

    assert (
        challenge.vocabulary[0].definition
        == "Organisation and control of activities."
    )

    assert len(challenge.discussion_questions) == 1

    assert (
        challenge.discussion_questions[0].question
        == "Pourquoi la gouvernance de l'IA est-elle importante ?"
    )

    assert (
        challenge.discussion_questions[0].difficulty
        == "B2"
    )

    assert (
        challenge.created_by_agent
        == "live_intelligence_agent"
    )

"""
EasyChatBot AI Companion Demo.

Demonstrates:

User
 |
Character
 |
Conversation Agent
 |
Research Agent
 |
Parallel Search
"""


import asyncio


from backend.services.companion_service import (
    CompanionService,
)

from backend.characters.character_profile import (
    CharacterProfile,
)

from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import (
    ExecutionContext,
)


async def main():

    yeti = CharacterProfile(
        character_id="yeti",
        name="Yeti",
        description=(
            "Friendly AI learning companion"
        ),
        personality=(
            "Curious and encouraging"
        ),
        learning_style=(
            "Conversation based learning"
        ),
    )


    companion = CompanionService(
        character=yeti
    )


    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request=(
            "Tell me about today's "
            "artificial intelligence news"
        ),
    )


    result = await companion.interact(
        context,
        use_research=True,
    )


    print("=" * 60)
    print("EasyChatBot Companion Demo")
    print("=" * 60)

    print()

    print(
        "Character:",
        result["character"],
    )

    print()

    print(
        "Conversation:"
    )

    print(
        result["conversation"]
    )

    print()

    print(
        "Research:"
    )

    print(
        result["research"]
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":

    asyncio.run(main())

"""

Demonstrates the four completed hackathon capabilities:

1. Governance
2. Live Intelligence

Architecture:

     |

     |

Live Intelligence
     |

Learning Challenge
     |

Quality Evaluation
     |
     v
Final Demonstration Result

This file is an orchestration/demo layer only.

It does not modify:
- Conversation Agent
- Live Intelligence Agent
- Challenge Engine
- Evaluation Engine
- Governance Engine
- Parallel Provider

def create_governance_policy() -> GovernancePolicy:
    """
    Governance policy for the live intelligence demo.

    Live research may access external information but
    must never create external side effects.
    """

    return GovernancePolicy(
        capability="live_intelligence_research",
        risk_level=RiskLevel.MEDIUM,
        requires_human_review=False,
        allows_external_calls=True,
        allows_side_effects=False,
    )


async def create_learning_challenge() -> LearningChallenge:
    """
    Execute the H1 learning challenge flow.
    """

    intelligence_agent = LiveIntelligenceAgent(
        search_provider=DemoSearchProvider(),
        processor=DemoIntelligenceProcessor(),
    )

    intelligence = await intelligence_agent.execute(
        LiveIntelligenceRequest(
            topic="AI Act",
            language="French",
            learner_level="B2",
        )
    )

    challenge_generator = ChallengeGenerator(
        content_generator=DemoChallengeContentGenerator()
    )

    return await challenge_generator.generate(
        intelligence=intelligence,
        language="French",
        learner_level="B2",
    )


async def run_demo() -> None:
    """
    Execute the complete hackathon demonstration.
    """

    print()
    print("=" * 72)
    print("EasyChatBot â€” HACKATHON DEMONSTRATION")
    print("=" * 72)
    print()

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # STEP 1 â€” Governance
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    print("STEP 1 â€” GOVERNANCE")
    print("-" * 72)

    governance_engine = GovernancePolicyEngine()

    governance_result = governance_engine.evaluate(
        create_governance_policy()
    )

    print(
        f"Capability: {governance_result.capability}"
    )

    print(
        f"Risk level: "
        f"{governance_result.risk_level.value}"
    )

    print(
        f"Decision: "
        f"{governance_result.decision.value.upper()}"
    )

    if governance_result.decision != GovernanceDecision.ALLOW:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "Hackathon demo stopped because governance "
            "did not allow the capability."
        )

    print("Governance check: PASSED")
    print()

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # STEP 2 â€” Live Intelligence + Challenge
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    print("STEP 2 â€” LIVE INTELLIGENCE")
    print("-" * 72)

    challenge = await create_learning_challenge()

    print(f"Topic: {challenge.topic}")
    print(f"Language: {challenge.language}")
    print(f"Learner level: {challenge.learner_level}")
    print()

    print("Summary:")
    print(challenge.summary)
    print()

    print("Vocabulary:")

    for item in challenge.vocabulary:
        print(f"  â€¢ {item.term}: {item.definition}")

    print()

    print("Discussion questions:")

    for index, question in enumerate(
        challenge.discussion_questions,
        start=1,
    ):
        print(
            f"  {index}. {question.question}"
        )

    print()

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # STEP 3 â€” Quality Evaluation
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    print("STEP 3 â€” AI QUALITY EVALUATION")
    print("-" * 72)

    evaluator = DeterministicEvaluator()

    criterion_scores = evaluator.evaluate(
        challenge
    )

    evaluation_engine = EvaluationEngine(
        criteria=[
            COMPLETENESS,
            LANGUAGE_LEVEL,
            EDUCATIONAL_VALUE,
            SOURCE_COVERAGE,
        ],
        passing_score=0.70,
    )

    evaluation_result = evaluation_engine.evaluate(
        criterion_scores
    )

    report = EvaluationReport().generate(
        evaluation_result
    )

    print(report)

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # STEP 4 â€” PERSONALISED FLASH DEAL
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    print("STEP 4 â€” PERSONALISED DISCOVERY")
    print("-" * 72)

    # Build the same domain inputs that a real discovery
    # application flow would receive.
    demo_deal = build_demo_deal()
    demo_memory = build_demo_memory()

    discovery_request = DiscoveryRequest(
        user_id=demo_memory.user_id,
        deal=demo_deal,
        character=CharacterContext(
            character_id="yeti",
            character_name="Yeti",
            message=(
                "Look what I found! "
                "This could be perfect for you."
            ),
        ),
        product_image_url=(
            "https://example.com/demo/iphone17-pro.jpg"
        ),
        tagline="Pro. Beautiful. Powerful.",
        highlights=(
            "Pro camera system",
            "High-performance chip",
            "All-day battery life",
            "Premium display",
        ),
    )

    flash_deal_card = DiscoveryService().create_flash_deal(
        discovery_request,
        memories=[demo_memory],
    )

    print(
        render_demo_flash_deal_card(
            flash_deal_card
        )
    )

    assert flash_deal_card.character_id == "yeti"
    assert flash_deal_card.deal_id == demo_deal.deal_id
    assert flash_deal_card.product_name
    assert demo_memory.user_id == "demo-user"
    assert flash_deal_card.relevance_reason
    assert flash_deal_card.get_deal_url
    assert flash_deal_card.more_information_url

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # STEP 5 â€” COMMERCE ACTION
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    print("STEP 5 â€” COMMERCE ACTION")
    print("-" * 72)

    commerce_action = FlashDealCommerce().create_action(
        user_id="demo-user",
        card=flash_deal_card,
    )

    print(
        f"Product: {flash_deal_card.product_name}"
    )
    print(
        f"Vendor: {flash_deal_card.vendor_name}"
    )
    print(
        f"Commerce action: "
        f"{commerce_action.action_type.value.upper()}"
    )

    commerce_result = CommerceService().initiate(
        commerce_action
    )

    print(
        f"Status: "
        f"{commerce_result.status.value.upper()}"
    )

    print()
    print("GET DEAL")
    print("  -> Approved commerce boundary")
    print("  -> Vendor handoff")
    print()
    print(
        "Payment is not executed in the hackathon demo."
    )

    assert (
        commerce_result.status
        == CommerceActionStatus.REDIRECT
    )
    assert (
        commerce_result.deal_id
        == flash_deal_card.deal_id
    )
    assert (
        commerce_result.next_url
        == flash_deal_card.get_deal_url
    )

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # STEP 6 â€” Final result
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    print()
    print("=" * 72)

    if evaluation_result.passed:
        print("HACKATHON DEMO: SUCCESS")
        print()
        # ASCII-safe output keeps the executable reliable when
        # stdout is captured by Windows pytest/subprocess.
        # Preserve the stable hackathon readiness marker.
        print(
            "Governance -> Intelligence -> Learning -> Evaluation"
        )
        print(
            "Governance -> Intelligence -> Learning -> "
            "Evaluation -> Personalised Discovery"
        )
        print(
            "All demonstrated capabilities completed successfully."
        )
    else:
        print("HACKATHON DEMO: QUALITY CHECK FAILED")

    print("=" * 72)
    print()

    assert governance_result.decision == GovernanceDecision.ALLOW
    assert challenge.topic == "AI Act"
    assert evaluation_result.passed is True

    """
    Application entry point.

    asyncio.run(run_demo())

def create_governance_policy() -> GovernancePolicy:
    """
    Governance policy for the live intelligence demo.

    Live research may access external information but
    must never create external side effects.
    """

    return GovernancePolicy(
        capability="live_intelligence_research",
        risk_level=RiskLevel.MEDIUM,
        requires_human_review=False,
        allows_external_calls=True,
        allows_side_effects=False,
    )


async def create_learning_challenge() -> LearningChallenge:
    """
    Execute the H1 learning challenge flow.
    """

    intelligence_agent = LiveIntelligenceAgent(
        search_provider=DemoSearchProvider(),
        processor=DemoIntelligenceProcessor(),
    )

    intelligence = await intelligence_agent.execute(
        LiveIntelligenceRequest(
            topic="AI Act",
            language="French",
            learner_level="B2",
        )
    )

    challenge_generator = ChallengeGenerator(
        content_generator=DemoChallengeContentGenerator()
    )

    return await challenge_generator.generate(
        intelligence=intelligence,
        language="French",
        learner_level="B2",
    )


async def run_demo() -> None:
    """
    Execute the complete hackathon demonstration.
    """

    print()
    print("=" * 72)
    print("EasyChatBot â€” HACKATHON DEMONSTRATION")
    print("=" * 72)
    print()

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # STEP 1 â€” Governance
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    print("STEP 1 â€” GOVERNANCE")
    print("-" * 72)

    governance_engine = GovernancePolicyEngine()

    governance_result = governance_engine.evaluate(
        create_governance_policy()
    )

    print(
        f"Capability: {governance_result.capability}"
    )

    print(
        f"Risk level: "
        f"{governance_result.risk_level.value}"
    )

    print(
        f"Decision: "
        f"{governance_result.decision.value.upper()}"
    )

    if governance_result.decision != GovernanceDecision.ALLOW:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "Hackathon demo stopped because governance "
            "did not allow the capability."
        )

    print("Governance check: PASSED")
    print()

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # STEP 2 â€” Live Intelligence + Challenge
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    print("STEP 2 â€” LIVE INTELLIGENCE")
    print("-" * 72)

    challenge = await create_learning_challenge()

    print(f"Topic: {challenge.topic}")
    print(f"Language: {challenge.language}")
    print(f"Learner level: {challenge.learner_level}")
    print()

    print("Summary:")
    print(challenge.summary)
    print()

    print("Vocabulary:")

    for item in challenge.vocabulary:
        print(f"  â€¢ {item.term}: {item.definition}")

    print()

    print("Discussion questions:")

    for index, question in enumerate(
        challenge.discussion_questions,
        start=1,
    ):
        print(
            f"  {index}. {question.question}"
        )

    print()

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # STEP 3 â€” Quality Evaluation
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    print("STEP 3 â€” AI QUALITY EVALUATION")
    print("-" * 72)

    evaluator = DeterministicEvaluator()

    criterion_scores = evaluator.evaluate(
        challenge
    )

    evaluation_engine = EvaluationEngine(
        criteria=[
            COMPLETENESS,
            LANGUAGE_LEVEL,
            EDUCATIONAL_VALUE,
            SOURCE_COVERAGE,
        ],
        passing_score=0.70,
    )

    evaluation_result = evaluation_engine.evaluate(
        criterion_scores
    )

    report = EvaluationReport().generate(
        evaluation_result
    )

    print(report)

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # STEP 4 â€” PERSONALISED FLASH DEAL
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    print("STEP 4 â€” PERSONALISED DISCOVERY")
    print("-" * 72)

    # Build the same domain inputs that a real discovery
    # application flow would receive.
    demo_deal = build_demo_deal()
    demo_memory = build_demo_memory()

    discovery_request = DiscoveryRequest(
        user_id=demo_memory.user_id,
        deal=demo_deal,
        character=CharacterContext(
            character_id="yeti",
            character_name="Yeti",
            message=(
                "Look what I found! "
                "This could be perfect for you."
            ),
        ),
        product_image_url=(
            "https://example.com/demo/iphone17-pro.jpg"
        ),
        tagline="Pro. Beautiful. Powerful.",
        highlights=(
            "Pro camera system",
            "High-performance chip",
            "All-day battery life",
            "Premium display",
        ),
    )

    flash_deal_card = DiscoveryService().create_flash_deal(
        discovery_request,
        memories=[demo_memory],
    )

    print(
        render_demo_flash_deal_card(
            flash_deal_card
        )
    )

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # STEP 4A â€” CHARACTER DISCOVERY NOTIFICATION
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    discovery_notification = (
        DiscoveryNotification.from_flash_deal(
            notification_id="demo-notification-001",
            user_id=demo_memory.user_id,
            deal=flash_deal_card,
        )
    )

    print()
    print("CHARACTER NOTIFICATION")
    print("-" * 72)
    print(
        discovery_notification.render()
    )
    print()

    assert (
        discovery_notification.user_id
        == demo_memory.user_id
    )
    assert (
        discovery_notification.character_id
        == flash_deal_card.character_id
    )
    assert (
        discovery_notification.deal
        is flash_deal_card
    )

    assert flash_deal_card.character_id == "yeti"
    assert flash_deal_card.deal_id == demo_deal.deal_id
    assert flash_deal_card.product_name
    assert demo_memory.user_id == "demo-user"
    assert flash_deal_card.relevance_reason
    assert flash_deal_card.get_deal_url
    assert flash_deal_card.more_information_url

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # STEP 5 â€” COMMERCE ACTION
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    print("STEP 5 â€” COMMERCE ACTION")
    print("-" * 72)

    commerce_action = FlashDealCommerce().create_action(
        user_id="demo-user",
        card=flash_deal_card,
    )

    print(
        f"Product: {flash_deal_card.product_name}"
    )
    print(
        f"Vendor: {flash_deal_card.vendor_name}"
    )
    print(
        f"Commerce action: "
        f"{commerce_action.action_type.value.upper()}"
    )

    commerce_result = CommerceService().initiate(
        commerce_action
    )

    print(
        f"Status: "
        f"{commerce_result.status.value.upper()}"
    )

    print()
    print("GET DEAL")
    print("  -> Approved commerce boundary")
    print("  -> Vendor handoff")
    print()
    print(
        "Payment is not executed in the hackathon demo."
    )

    assert (
        commerce_result.status
        == CommerceActionStatus.REDIRECT
    )
    assert (
        commerce_result.deal_id
        == flash_deal_card.deal_id
    )
    assert (
        commerce_result.next_url
        == flash_deal_card.get_deal_url
    )

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # STEP 6 â€” Final result
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    print()
    print("=" * 72)

    if evaluation_result.passed:
        print("HACKATHON DEMO: SUCCESS")
        print()
        # ASCII-safe output keeps the executable reliable when
        # stdout is captured by Windows pytest/subprocess.
        # Preserve the stable hackathon readiness marker.
        print(
            "Governance -> Intelligence -> Learning -> Evaluation"
        )
        print(
            "Governance -> Intelligence -> Learning -> "
            "Evaluation -> Personalised Discovery"
        )
        print(
            "All demonstrated capabilities completed successfully."
        )
    else:
        print("HACKATHON DEMO: QUALITY CHECK FAILED")

    print("=" * 72)
    print()

    assert governance_result.decision == GovernanceDecision.ALLOW
    assert challenge.topic == "AI Act"
    assert evaluation_result.passed is True


def main() -> None:
    """
    Application entry point.
    """

    asyncio.run(run_demo())


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
