FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\agents\conversation_agent.py
============================================================
"""
Conversation Agent.

Provides character-aware conversational responses
for the EasyChatBot hackathon runtime.

The agent remains provider-independent.

Architecture:

ExecutionContext
|
v
ConversationAgent
|
+--> CharacterProfile
|
+--> Optional IntelligenceContext
|
v
ConversationRequest
|
v
LLMService
|
v
LLMProvider
|
v
Concrete LLM Provider
|
v
ProviderResult
|
v
AgentResult

Responsibilities:

- apply character identity
- construct ConversationRequest
- include optional live intelligence
- execute an injected LLMService
- preserve deterministic demo behaviour when no
  LLM service is configured
- return a standard AgentResult

Does not:

- depend directly on OpenAI
- depend directly on Ollama
- call Parallel directly
- manage memory
- perform research
- manage voice
- execute external workflows
- select providers
- execute provider SDKs directly

Architecture boundary:

ConversationAgent
-> LLMService
-> LLMProvider
-> Concrete Provider
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from backend.agents.agent_manifest import (
    AgentManifest,
)

from backend.agents.agent_result import (
    AgentResult,
)

from backend.agents.base_agent import (
    BaseAgent,
)

from backend.characters.character_profile import (
    CharacterProfile,
)

from backend.conversation.conversation_request import (
    ConversationRequest,
)

from backend.providers.llm.llm_provider import (
    LLMProvider,
)

from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import (
    ExecutionContext,
)

from backend.services.llm_service import (
    LLMService,
)


class ConversationAgent(BaseAgent):
    """
    Character-aware conversation agent.

    The agent depends primarily on the application-level
    LLMService boundary.

    The LLMService is responsible for delegating
    execution to the configured LLMProvider.

    A legacy ``llm_provider`` argument remains accepted
    as a compatibility seam for existing tests and
    callers. When supplied, it is wrapped by LLMService.

    New composition code should inject ``llm_service``.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        character: CharacterProfile,
        llm_service: LLMService | None = None,
        llm_provider: LLMProvider | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        """
        Initialise the Conversation Agent.

        Parameters
        ----------
        character:
            Approved EasyChatBot character profile.

        llm_service:
            Application-level LLM execution service.

        llm_provider:
            Compatibility injection seam.

            When supplied and ``llm_service`` is not supplied,
            the provider is wrapped inside LLMService.

        Raises
        ------
        ValueError
            If both ``llm_service`` and ``llm_provider``
            are supplied simultaneously.
        """

        super().__init__(
            AgentManifest(
                agent_id="conversation_agent",
                name="Conversation Agent",
                description=(
                    "Provides character-aware "
                    "conversation responses."
                ),
            )
        )

        if (
            llm_service is not None
            and llm_provider is not None
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "Provide either llm_service or "
                "llm_provider, not both."
            )

        self.character = character

        if llm_service is not None:
            self.llm_service = llm_service

        elif llm_provider is not None:
            self.llm_service = LLMService(
                llm_provider
            )

        else:
            self.llm_service = None

    def build_request(
        self,
        context: ExecutionContext,
    ) -> ConversationRequest:
        """
        Build a provider-independent conversation request.

        Runtime state and character identity are translated
        into the ConversationRequest contract.

        No provider-specific implementation is introduced.
        """

        system_instruction = (
            f"You are {self.character.name}, "
            f"a character in EasyChatBot. "
            f"Your personality is: "
            f"{self.character.personality} "
            f"Your learning style is: "
            f"{self.character.learning_style} "
            f"Communicate in a way that is consistent "
            f"with this character."
        )

        conversation_context = None

        if context.has_intelligence_context:
            conversation_context = (
                context.intelligence_context.to_context_text()
            )

        return ConversationRequest(
            system_instruction=system_instruction,
            user_prompt=context.request,
            conversation_context=conversation_context,
            character_id=self.character.character_id,
            character_name=self.character.name,
        )

    async def execute(
        self,
        context: ExecutionContext,
    ) -> AgentResult:
        """
        Execute the conversation agent.

        Flow:

        ExecutionContext
            |
            v
        ConversationRequest
            |
            v
        LLMService
            |
            v
        LLMProvider
            |
            v
        ProviderResult
            |
            v
        AgentResult

        When no LLMService is configured, the existing
        deterministic hackathon behaviour is preserved.

        When an LLMService is configured, execution
        passes through the application service boundary.
        """

        conversation_request = self.build_request(
            context
        )

        provider_result = None

        if self.llm_service is not None:
            provider_result = (
                await self.llm_service.generate(
                    conversation_request
                )
            )

            if not provider_result.success:
                return AgentResult(
                    success=False,
                    agent_id=self.manifest.agent_id,
                    output={
                        "character": self.character.name,

                        "character_profile": {
                            "character_id": (
                                self.character.character_id
                            ),
                            "name": self.character.name,
                            "description": (
                                self.character.description
                            ),
                            "personality": (
                                self.character.personality
                            ),
                            "learning_style": (
                                self.character.learning_style
                            ),
                        },

                        "personality": (
                            self.character.personality
                        ),

                        "learning_style": (
                            self.character.learning_style
                        ),

                        "message": (
                            "The conversation provider "
                            "could not generate a response."
                        ),

                        "intelligence_context": (
                            conversation_request
                            .conversation_context
                        ),

                        "conversation_request": (
                            conversation_request
                        ),

                        "provider_id": (
                            provider_result.provider_id
                        ),

                        "provider_error": (
                            provider_result.error
                        ),
                    },
                )

        if provider_result is not None:
            message = provider_result.output

        else:
            message = (
                f"{self.character.name}: "
                f"Let's explore this together. "
                f"You asked: {context.request}"
            )

        output = {
            "character": self.character.name,

            "character_profile": {
                "character_id": (
                    self.character.character_id
                ),
                "name": self.character.name,
                "description": (
                    self.character.description
                ),
                "personality": (
                    self.character.personality
                ),
                "learning_style": (
                    self.character.learning_style
                ),
            },

            "personality": (
                self.character.personality
            ),

            "learning_style": (
                self.character.learning_style
            ),

            "message": message,

            "intelligence_context": (
                conversation_request
                .conversation_context
            ),

            "conversation_request": (
                conversation_request
            ),
        }

        if provider_result is not None:
            output["provider_id"] = (
                provider_result.provider_id
            )

        return AgentResult(
            success=True,
            agent_id=self.manifest.agent_id,
            output=output,
        )

============================================================
FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\agents\research_agent.py
============================================================
"""
Research Agent.

Retrieves external information through approved
knowledge providers and converts the result into
provider-independent IntelligenceContext data.

Architecture:

Research Agent
        |
        v
ParallelSearchProvider
        |
        v
ParallelClientWrapper
        |
        v
IntelligenceContext
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from backend.agents.agent_manifest import AgentManifest
from backend.agents.agent_result import AgentResult
from backend.agents.base_agent import BaseAgent

from backend.intelligence.intelligence_context import (
    IntelligenceContext,
    IntelligenceSource,
)

from backend.providers.parallel.parallel_provider import (
    ParallelSearchProvider,
)

from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import (
    ExecutionContext,
)


class ResearchAgent(BaseAgent):
    """
    Agent responsible for world information retrieval.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        provider=None,
    ):
        super().__init__(
            AgentManifest(
                agent_id="research_agent",
                name="Research Agent",
                description=(
                    "Retrieves live world information "
                    "for AI learning experiences."
                ),
            )
        )

        self.provider = (
            provider
            or ParallelSearchProvider()
        )

    async def execute(
        self,
        context: ExecutionContext,
    ) -> AgentResult:
        """
        Execute research and produce a normalized
        IntelligenceContext.
        """

        result = await self.provider.execute(
            context
        )

        if not result.success:
            return AgentResult(
                success=False,
                agent_id=self.manifest.agent_id,
                output=None,
                error=result.error,
            )

        intelligence = (
            self._build_intelligence_context(
                query=context.request,
                provider_output=result.output,
            )
        )

        return AgentResult(
            success=True,
            agent_id=self.manifest.agent_id,
            output=intelligence,
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _build_intelligence_context(
        query: str,
        provider_output,
    ) -> IntelligenceContext:
        """
        Convert provider output into the
        provider-independent IntelligenceContext.
        """

        if not isinstance(
            provider_output,
            dict,
        ):
            raise TypeError(
                "Research provider output must be a dictionary."
            )

        raw_results = provider_output.get(
            "results",
            {},
        )

        if isinstance(
            raw_results,
            dict,
        ):
            raw_results = raw_results.get(
                "results",
                [],
            )

        if not isinstance(
            raw_results,
            list,
        ):
            raw_results = []

        sources: list[IntelligenceSource] = []

        for raw_result in raw_results:

            if not isinstance(
                raw_result,
                dict,
            ):
                continue

            title = str(
                raw_result.get(
                    "title",
                    "",
                )
            ).strip()

            url = str(
                raw_result.get(
                    "url",
                    "",
                )
            ).strip()

            if not title or not url:
                continue

            content = ResearchAgent._extract_content(
                raw_result
            )

            sources.append(
                IntelligenceSource(
                    title=title,
                    url=url,
                    source="parallel",
                    content=content,
                )
            )

        return IntelligenceContext(
            query=query,
            sources=tuple(sources),
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _extract_content(
        raw_result: dict,
    ) -> str:
        """
        Extract available content from a normalized
        Parallel result.
        """

        excerpts = raw_result.get(
            "excerpts",
        )

        if excerpts is None:
            excerpts = raw_result.get(
                "snippet",
                raw_result.get(
                    "content",
                    "",
                ),
            )

        if isinstance(
            excerpts,
            list,
        ):
            return "\n\n".join(
                str(item).strip()
                for item in excerpts
                if str(item).strip()
            )

        return str(
            excerpts or ""
        ).strip()

============================================================
FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\conversation\conversation_request.py
============================================================
"""
Provider-independent conversation request.

Represents the information required to generate a
character-aware conversational response.

This contract belongs to the application/runtime boundary.

It deliberately does not know about:

- Gemini
- OpenAI
- Ollama
- Parallel
- voice
- HTTP
- persistence
- provider SDKs

Architecture:

CharacterProfile
        +
ExecutionContext
        +
Optional IntelligenceContext
        |
        v
ConversationRequest
        |
        v
Future / existing EasyChatBot LLM service

The production EasyChatBot conversation and voice
architecture remains outside this hackathon runtime.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class ConversationRequest:
    """
    Immutable provider-independent conversation request.

    Attributes
    ----------
    system_instruction:
        Stable instruction defining how the character
        should behave.

    user_prompt:
        User-facing request to be answered.

    conversation_context:
        Optional contextual information, such as live
        intelligence retrieved by the Research Agent.

    character_id:
        Identifier of the character producing the response.

    character_name:
        Display name of the character.
    """

    system_instruction: str

    user_prompt: str

    conversation_context: str | None = None

    character_id: str = ""

    character_name: str = ""

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        """
        Validate request invariants.
        """

        if not isinstance(
            self.system_instruction,
            str,
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "system_instruction must be a string."
            )

        if not isinstance(
            self.user_prompt,
            str,
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "user_prompt must be a string."
            )

        system_instruction = (
            self.system_instruction.strip()
        )

        user_prompt = (
            self.user_prompt.strip()
        )

        if not system_instruction:
            raise ValueError(
                "system_instruction cannot be empty."
            )

        if not user_prompt:
            raise ValueError(
                "user_prompt cannot be empty."
            )

        object.__setattr__(
            self,
            "system_instruction",
            system_instruction,
        )

        object.__setattr__(
            self,
            "user_prompt",
            user_prompt,
        )

        if self.conversation_context is not None:
            context = (
                self.conversation_context.strip()
            )

            if not context:
                raise ValueError(
                    "conversation_context cannot be blank."
                )

            object.__setattr__(
                self,
                "conversation_context",
                context,
            )

        if not isinstance(
            self.character_id,
            str,
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "character_id must be a string."
            )

        if not isinstance(
            self.character_name,
            str,
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "character_name must be a string."
            )

        object.__setattr__(
            self,
            "character_id",
            self.character_id.strip(),
        )

        object.__setattr__(
            self,
            "character_name",
            self.character_name.strip(),
        )

    @property
    def has_conversation_context(self) -> bool:
        """
        Return whether contextual information exists.
        """

        return bool(
            self.conversation_context
        )

    @property
    def context_length(self) -> int:
        """
        Return the length of the conversation context.

        Useful for diagnostics and future token estimation.
        """

        if not self.conversation_context:
            return 0

        return len(
            self.conversation_context
        )

    def summary(self) -> dict[str, str]:
        """
        Return a safe diagnostic summary.

        Prompt and context content are deliberately
        excluded from the summary.
        """

        return {
            "character_id": self.character_id,
            "character_name": self.character_name,
            "has_context": str(
                self.has_conversation_context
            ),
            "system_instruction_length": str(
                len(self.system_instruction)
            ),
            "user_prompt_length": str(
                len(self.user_prompt)
            ),
            "context_length": str(
                self.context_length
            ),
        }

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\intelligence\research_service.py

"""
Research Service.

Coordinates the Research Agent and exposes a
provider-independent intelligence capability.

Architecture:

Application / Runtime
        |
        v
ResearchService
        |
        v
ResearchAgent
        |
        v
Search Provider
        |
        v
IntelligenceContext

The service does not know about Parallel SDK details.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from backend.agents.research_agent import (
    ResearchAgent,
)

from backend.intelligence.intelligence_context import (
    IntelligenceContext,
)

from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import (
    ExecutionContext,
)


class ResearchService:
    """
    Application-facing service for live research.

    The service coordinates the Research Agent but does
    not implement provider-specific search behaviour.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        agent: ResearchAgent | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self.agent = (
            agent
            or ResearchAgent()
        )

    async def research(
        self,
        user_id: str,
        query: str,
    ) -> IntelligenceContext:
        """
        Execute research and return normalized intelligence.

        Args:
            user_id:
                Identifier for the current user/session.

            query:
                Research question.

        Returns:
            Provider-independent IntelligenceContext.

        Raises:
            ValueError:
                If the query is empty.

            RuntimeError:
                If the Research Agent fails.
        """

        if not query.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Research query cannot be empty."
            )

        context = ExecutionContext(
            user_id=user_id,
            request=query,
        )

        result = await self.agent.execute(
            context
        )

        if not result.success:
            raise RuntimeError(
                result.error
                or "Research execution failed."
            )

        if not isinstance(
            result.output,
            IntelligenceContext,
        ):
            raise TypeError(
                "Research Agent did not return "
                "an IntelligenceContext."
            )

        return result.output

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\providers\base_provider.py

"""
Base Provider Contract.

Defines the interface for external AI and knowledge providers.

Examples:
- Gemini
- Parallel Search
- Future LLM providers

The Runtime communicates with providers through this contract.

Providers are replaceable implementations.
The Agent layer must remain independent from provider details.
"""

from abc import ABC, abstractmethod

from backend.providers.provider_result import ProviderResult
from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import ExecutionContext


class BaseProvider(ABC):
    """
    Abstract base class for EasyChatBot providers.

    Providers are responsible for communicating
    with external services.

    They are NOT responsible for:
    - agent decisions
    - workflow orchestration
    - memory management
    - user interaction
    """

    provider_id: str

    def __init__(
        self,
        provider_id: str,
    ):
        """
        Initialise provider.

        Args:
            provider_id:
                Unique provider identifier.
        """

        self.provider_id = provider_id

    @abstractmethod
    async def execute(
        self,
        context: ExecutionContext,
    ) -> ProviderResult:
        """
        Execute provider operation.

        Args:
            context:
                Runtime execution context.

        Returns:
            ProviderResult containing provider output.
        """

        pass

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\providers\llm\openai_provider.py

"""
OpenAI LLM Provider.

Concrete LLM provider implementation for the EasyChatBot
hackathon runtime.

Architecture:

ConversationAgent
        |
        v
ConversationRequest
        |
        v
LLMProvider
        |
        v
OpenAIProvider
        |
        v
OpenAI API
        |
        v
ProviderResult

Responsibilities:

- execute ConversationRequest through OpenAI
- use the configured OpenAI model
- translate the OpenAI response into ProviderResult
- isolate the OpenAI SDK from the application layer

Does not:

- build prompts
- manage characters
- manage memory
- perform research
- perform routing
- modify application state
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
import os

from openai import AsyncOpenAI

from backend.conversation.conversation_request import (
    ConversationRequest,
)

from backend.providers.llm.llm_provider import (
    LLMProvider,
)

from backend.providers.provider_result import (
    ProviderResult,
)


logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class OpenAIProvider(LLMProvider):
    """
    OpenAI implementation of the LLMProvider contract.

    The OpenAI SDK is deliberately isolated inside this
    infrastructure adapter.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        model: str = "gpt-5-mini",
        client: AsyncOpenAI | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        """
        Initialise the OpenAI provider.

        Args:
            model:
                OpenAI model identifier.

            client:
                Optional injected AsyncOpenAI client.

                Dependency injection allows unit tests to
                provide a fake client without making a real
                API call.
        """

        super().__init__(
            provider_id="openai",
        )

        if not model.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "OpenAI model cannot be empty."
            )

        self.model = model.strip()

        self._client = (
            client
            if client is not None
            else AsyncOpenAI(
                api_key=[REDACTED]"OPENAI_API_KEY"
                )
            )
        )

    async def generate(
        self,
        request: ConversationRequest,
    ) -> ProviderResult:
        """
        Generate a response using OpenAI.

        The provider translates the provider-independent
        ConversationRequest into the OpenAI Responses API
        request.

        Args:
            request:
                Provider-independent conversation request.

        Returns:
            ProviderResult containing the generated text
            or an error description.
        """

        if request is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "Conversation request cannot be None."
            )

        messages = [
            {
                "role": "system",
                "content": request.system_instruction,
            },
        ]

        if request.conversation_context:
            messages.append(
                {
                    "role": "user",
                    "content": (
                        "Additional context:\n"
                        f"{request.conversation_context}"
                    ),
                }
            )

        messages.append(
            {
                "role": "user",
                "content": request.user_prompt,
            }
        )

        try:
            response = await self._client.responses.create(
                model=self.model,
                input=messages,
            )

        except Exception as exc:
            logger.exception(
                "OpenAI provider execution failed."
            )

            return ProviderResult(
                success=False,
                provider_id=self.provider_id,
                error=str(exc),
            )

        output_text = (
            getattr(
                response,
                "output_text",
                None,
            )
        )

        if not output_text:
            return ProviderResult(
                success=False,
                provider_id=self.provider_id,
                error=(
                    "OpenAI returned an empty response."
                ),
            )

        return ProviderResult(
            success=True,
            provider_id=self.provider_id,
            output=output_text,
        )

"""
Parallel Search API Client Wrapper.

This module isolates the official Parallel SDK.

The rest of EasyChatBot does not directly depend on
the Parallel SDK implementation.

Architecture:

Research Agent
        |
        v
Parallel Provider
        |
        v
Parallel Client Wrapper
        |
        v
Parallel API
"""

import asyncio
import os
from typing import Any

from dotenv import load_dotenv


load_dotenv()


class ParallelClientWrapper:
    """
    Wrapper around the official Parallel Search SDK.

    Responsibilities:
    - authenticate with Parallel
    - execute searches
    - normalize SDK responses

    Does not:
    - create learning content
    - manage agents
    - decide workflows
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        api_key: [REDACTED]"""
        Initialise the Parallel SDK client.
        """

        self.api_key = [REDACTED]"PARALLEL_API_KEY")
        )

        if not self.api_key:
            [REDACTED]"PARALLEL_API_KEY environment variable is required"
            )

        self._client = self._create_client()


    def _create_client(self):
        """
        Create the official Parallel SDK client.

        SDK:
            parallel-web 1.1.0
        """

        from parallel import Parallel

        return Parallel(
            api_key=[REDACTED]"""
        Convert the Parallel SDK response into
        application-safe data.

        Pydantic SDK models are converted to dictionaries.
        Other JSON-compatible values are returned unchanged.
        """

        if hasattr(response, "model_dump"):
            return response.model_dump()

        if hasattr(response, "dict"):
            return response.dict()

        return response


    async def search(
        self,
        query: str,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Execute a Parallel web search.

        Args:
            query:
                Natural-language research objective.

        Returns:
            Normalized application-level search result.
        """

        search_query = self._build_search_query(
            query
        )

        response = await asyncio.to_thread(
            self._client.search,
            search_queries=[
                search_query
            ],
            mode="advanced",
            objective=query,
        )

        return {
            "query": query,
            "search_queries": [
                search_query
            ],
            "results": self._normalize_response(
                response
            ),
        }


    @staticmethod
    def _build_search_query(
        query: str,
    ) -> str:
        """
        Build a concise search query from the
        user's research request.

        The full request remains the Parallel
        objective while the search query is kept
        concise.
        """

        words = query.strip().split()

        if len(words) <= 6:
            return query.strip()

        return " ".join(words[:6])

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\providers\parallel\parallel_provider.py

"""
Parallel Search Provider.

Provides live world information
through the Parallel Search API.
"""

from backend.providers.base_provider import (
    BaseProvider,
)

from backend.providers.provider_result import (
    ProviderResult,
)

from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import (
    ExecutionContext,
)


class ParallelSearchProvider(BaseProvider):
    """
    Provider implementation using Parallel Search.

    The SDK client is created only when required.
    This allows tests to run without API credentials.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        client=None,
    ):
        super().__init__(
            provider_id="parallel_search"
        )

        self.client = client


    def _get_client(self):
        """
        Lazy-load the real Parallel client.

        Avoids requiring API keys during unit tests.
        """

        if self.client is None:

            from backend.providers.parallel.parallel_client import (
                ParallelClientWrapper,
            )

            self.client = ParallelClientWrapper()

        return self.client


    async def execute(
        self,
        context: ExecutionContext,
    ) -> ProviderResult:
        """
        Execute a live Parallel search.
        """

        try:

            client = self._get_client()

            result = await client.search(
                query=context.request
            )

            return ProviderResult(
                success=True,
                provider_id=self.provider_id,
                output=result,
            )

        except Exception as exc:

            return ProviderResult(
                success=False,
                provider_id=self.provider_id,
                output=None,
                error=str(exc),
            )

def run_demo() -> None:
    """
    Execute the complete H2 evaluation demonstration.
    """

    print()
    print("=" * 64)
    print("EasyChatBot â€” Sprint H2")
    print("AI Quality Evaluation Demo")
    print("=" * 64)
    print()

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 1. Create representative H1 output
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    challenge = create_demo_challenge()

    print("INPUT")
    print("-" * 64)
    print(f"Title: {challenge.title}")
    print(f"Topic: {challenge.topic}")
    print(f"Language: {challenge.language}")
    print(f"Learner level: {challenge.learner_level}")
    print()

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 2. Evaluate challenge deterministically
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    evaluator = DeterministicEvaluator()

    criterion_scores = evaluator.evaluate(
        challenge
    )

    print("CRITERION SCORES")
    print("-" * 64)

    for score in criterion_scores:
        print(
            f"{score.criterion}: "
            f"{score.score:.2f}"
        )

    print()

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 3. Calculate weighted overall score
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    evaluation_engine = EvaluationEngine(
        criteria=[
            COMPLETENESS,
            LANGUAGE_LEVEL,
            EDUCATIONAL_VALUE,
            SOURCE_COVERAGE,
        ],
        passing_score=0.70,
    )

    evaluation_result = evaluation_engine.evaluate(
        criterion_scores
    )

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 4. Generate quality report
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    report_generator = EvaluationReport()

    report = report_generator.generate(
        evaluation_result
    )

    print(report)

    print()
    print("=" * 64)

    if evaluation_result.passed:
        print("H2 evaluation completed successfully.")
    else:
        print("H2 evaluation completed with a failing result.")

    print("=" * 64)
    print()


def main() -> None:
    """
    Application entry point.
    """

    run_demo()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

"""

from __future__ import annotations

import sys
from pathlib import Path


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Repository path bootstrap
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

REPOSITORY_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]

if str(REPOSITORY_ROOT) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(REPOSITORY_ROOT))


from src.governance.engine import (
    GovernancePolicyEngine,
)

from src.governance.models import (
    GovernanceDecision,
    GovernancePolicy,
    RiskLevel,
)


def run_demo() -> None:
    """
    Demonstrate the governance policy engine.
    """

    print()
    print("=" * 64)
    print("EasyChatBot â€” Sprint H3")
    print("AI Safety & Governance Demo")
    print("=" * 64)
    print()

    engine = GovernancePolicyEngine()

    scenarios = [
        GovernancePolicy(
            capability="content_formatting",
            risk_level=RiskLevel.LOW,
            requires_human_review=False,
            allows_external_calls=False,
            allows_side_effects=False,
        ),
        GovernancePolicy(
            capability="live_intelligence_research",
            risk_level=RiskLevel.MEDIUM,
            requires_human_review=False,
            allows_external_calls=True,
            allows_side_effects=False,
        ),
        GovernancePolicy(
            capability="high_risk_decision_support",
            risk_level=RiskLevel.HIGH,
            requires_human_review=False,
            allows_external_calls=True,
            allows_side_effects=False,
        ),
        GovernancePolicy(
            capability="critical_operation",
            risk_level=RiskLevel.CRITICAL,
            requires_human_review=False,
            allows_external_calls=True,
            allows_side_effects=False,
        ),
        GovernancePolicy(
            capability="external_system_modification",
            risk_level=RiskLevel.MEDIUM,
            requires_human_review=False,
            allows_external_calls=True,
            allows_side_effects=True,
        ),
    ]

    for index, policy in enumerate(
        scenarios,
        start=1,
    ):
        result = engine.evaluate(policy)

        print(f"SCENARIO {index}")
        print("-" * 64)
        print(f"Capability: {result.capability}")
        print(f"Risk level: {result.risk_level.value}")
        print(
            "External calls: "
            f"{policy.allows_external_calls}"
        )
        print(
            "Side effects: "
            f"{policy.allows_side_effects}"
        )
        print(
            "Human review: "
            f"{policy.requires_human_review}"
        )
        print(
            f"Decision: {result.decision.value.upper()}"
        )

        print("Reasons:")

        for reason in result.reasons:
            print(f"  â€¢ {reason}")

        print()

    print("=" * 64)
    print("Governance decision model demonstrated.")
    print("=" * 64)
    print()

    # Explicit assertions make the demo itself a useful
    # executable smoke test.

    decisions = [
        engine.evaluate(policy).decision
        for policy in scenarios
    ]

    assert decisions == [
        GovernanceDecision.ALLOW,
        GovernanceDecision.ALLOW,
        GovernanceDecision.REVIEW,
        GovernanceDecision.BLOCK,
        GovernanceDecision.BLOCK,
    ]


def main() -> None:
    """
    Application entry point.
    """

    run_demo()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

"""

Demonstrates the four completed hackathon capabilities:

class DemoSearchProvider:
    """
    Controlled search provider for the unified demo.

    The real Parallel provider is already validated separately
    by H1 integration tests.

    This keeps the unified demo deterministic and suitable for
    repeatable hackathon demonstrations.
    """

    async def search(
        self,
        query: str,
        max_results: int = 5,
    ) -> list[SearchResult]:

        return [
            SearchResult(
                title="European AI Act â€” Demo Source",
                url="https://example.com/ai-act",
                content=(
                    "The European Union AI Act establishes "
                    "a risk-based framework for artificial "
                    "intelligence."
                ),
                source="demo-provider",
            )
        ]


class DemoIntelligenceProcessor:
    """
    Controlled intelligence processor.

    The real provider-backed intelligence flow is validated
    separately in H1.
    """

    async def summarise(
        self,
        results: list[SearchResult],
        language: str,
        learner_level: str,
    ) -> tuple[str, list[str]]:

        return (
            "The European Union AI Act establishes "
            "a risk-based framework for artificial "
            "intelligence.",
            [
                "AI governance",
                "risk management",
                "compliance",
            ],
        )

def create_governance_policy() -> GovernancePolicy:
    """
    Governance policy for the live intelligence demo.

    Live research may access external information but
    must never create external side effects.
    """

    return GovernancePolicy(
        capability="live_intelligence_research",
        risk_level=RiskLevel.MEDIUM,
        requires_human_review=False,
        allows_external_calls=True,
        allows_side_effects=False,
    )


async def create_learning_challenge() -> LearningChallenge:
    """
    Execute the H1 learning challenge flow.
    """

    intelligence_agent = LiveIntelligenceAgent(
        search_provider=DemoSearchProvider(),
        processor=DemoIntelligenceProcessor(),
    )

    intelligence = await intelligence_agent.execute(
        LiveIntelligenceRequest(
            topic="AI Act",
            language="French",
            learner_level="B2",
        )
    )

    challenge_generator = ChallengeGenerator(
        content_generator=DemoChallengeContentGenerator()
    )

    return await challenge_generator.generate(
        intelligence=intelligence,
        language="French",
        learner_level="B2",
    )


async def run_demo() -> None:
    """
    Execute the complete hackathon demonstration.
    """

    print()
    print("=" * 72)
    print("EasyChatBot â€” HACKATHON DEMONSTRATION")
    print("=" * 72)
    print()

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # STEP 1 â€” Governance
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    print("STEP 1 â€” GOVERNANCE")
    print("-" * 72)

    governance_engine = GovernancePolicyEngine()

    governance_result = governance_engine.evaluate(
        create_governance_policy()
    )

    print(
        f"Capability: {governance_result.capability}"
    )

    print(
        f"Risk level: "
        f"{governance_result.risk_level.value}"
    )

    print(
        f"Decision: "
        f"{governance_result.decision.value.upper()}"
    )

    if governance_result.decision != GovernanceDecision.ALLOW:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "Hackathon demo stopped because governance "
            "did not allow the capability."
        )

    print("Governance check: PASSED")
    print()

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # STEP 2 â€” Live Intelligence + Challenge
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    print("STEP 2 â€” LIVE INTELLIGENCE")
    print("-" * 72)

    challenge = await create_learning_challenge()

    print(f"Topic: {challenge.topic}")
    print(f"Language: {challenge.language}")
    print(f"Learner level: {challenge.learner_level}")
    print()

    print("Summary:")
    print(challenge.summary)
    print()

    print("Vocabulary:")

    for item in challenge.vocabulary:
        print(f"  â€¢ {item.term}: {item.definition}")

    print()

    print("Discussion questions:")

    for index, question in enumerate(
        challenge.discussion_questions,
        start=1,
    ):
        print(
            f"  {index}. {question.question}"
        )

    print()

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # STEP 3 â€” Quality Evaluation
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    print("STEP 3 â€” AI QUALITY EVALUATION")
    print("-" * 72)

    evaluator = DeterministicEvaluator()

    criterion_scores = evaluator.evaluate(
        challenge
    )

    evaluation_engine = EvaluationEngine(
        criteria=[
            COMPLETENESS,
            LANGUAGE_LEVEL,
            EDUCATIONAL_VALUE,
            SOURCE_COVERAGE,
        ],
        passing_score=0.70,
    )

    evaluation_result = evaluation_engine.evaluate(
        criterion_scores
    )

    report = EvaluationReport().generate(
        evaluation_result
    )

    print(report)

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # STEP 4 â€” PERSONALISED FLASH DEAL
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    print("STEP 4 â€” PERSONALISED DISCOVERY")
    print("-" * 72)
    print(
        "Yeti found something that matches "
        "what this learner likes."
    )
    print(
        "Here is the personalised discovery:"
    )
    print()

    # Build the same domain inputs that a real discovery
    # application flow would receive.
    demo_deal = build_demo_deal()
    demo_memory = build_demo_memory()

    discovery_request = DiscoveryRequest(
        user_id=demo_memory.user_id,
        deal=demo_deal,
        character=CharacterContext(
            character_id="yeti",
            character_name="Yeti",
            message=(
                "Look what I found! "
                "This could be perfect for you."
            ),
        ),
        product_image_url=(
            "https://example.com/demo/iphone17-pro.jpg"
        ),
        tagline="Pro. Beautiful. Powerful.",
        highlights=(
            "Pro camera system",
            "High-performance chip",
            "All-day battery life",
            "Premium display",
        ),
    )

    flash_deal_card = DiscoveryService().create_flash_deal(
        discovery_request,
        memories=[demo_memory],
    )

    print(
        FlashDealPresenter().render(
            flash_deal_card
        )
    )

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # STEP 4A â€” CHARACTER DISCOVERY NOTIFICATION
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    discovery_notification = (
        DiscoveryNotification.from_flash_deal(
            notification_id="demo-notification-001",
            user_id=demo_memory.user_id,
            deal=flash_deal_card,
        )
    )

    print()
    print("CHARACTER NOTIFICATION")
    print("-" * 72)
    print(
        discovery_notification.render()
    )
    print()

    assert (
        discovery_notification.user_id
        == demo_memory.user_id
    )
    assert (
        discovery_notification.character_id
        == flash_deal_card.character_id
    )
    assert (
        discovery_notification.deal
        is flash_deal_card
    )

    assert flash_deal_card.character_id == "yeti"
    assert flash_deal_card.deal_id == demo_deal.deal_id
    assert flash_deal_card.product_name
    assert demo_memory.user_id == "demo-user"
    assert flash_deal_card.relevance_reason
    assert flash_deal_card.get_deal_url
    assert flash_deal_card.more_information_url

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # STEP 5 â€” COMMERCE ACTION
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    print(
        "The learner can now inspect the vendor "
        "information or proceed through the deal link."
    )
    print()

    print("STEP 5 â€” COMMERCE ACTION")
    print("-" * 72)

    commerce_action = FlashDealCommerce().create_action(
        user_id="demo-user",
        card=flash_deal_card,
    )

    print(
        f"Product: {flash_deal_card.product_name}"
    )
    print(
        f"Vendor: {flash_deal_card.vendor_name}"
    )
    print(
        f"Commerce action: "
        f"{commerce_action.action_type.value.upper()}"
    )

    commerce_result = CommerceService().initiate(
        commerce_action
    )

    print(
        f"Status: "
        f"{commerce_result.status.value.upper()}"
    )

    print()
    print("GET DEAL")
    print("  -> Approved commerce boundary")
    print("  -> Vendor handoff")
    print()
    print(
        "Payment is not executed in the hackathon demo."
    )

    assert (
        commerce_result.status
        == CommerceActionStatus.REDIRECT
    )
    assert (
        commerce_result.deal_id
        == flash_deal_card.deal_id
    )
    assert (
        commerce_result.next_url
        == flash_deal_card.get_deal_url
    )

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # STEP 6 â€” Final result
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    print()
    print("=" * 72)

    if evaluation_result.passed:
        print("HACKATHON DEMO: SUCCESS")
        print()
        # ASCII-safe output keeps the executable reliable when
        # stdout is captured by Windows pytest/subprocess.
        # Preserve the stable hackathon readiness marker.
        print(
            "Governance -> Intelligence -> Learning -> Evaluation"
        )
        print(
            "Governance -> Intelligence -> Learning -> "
            "Evaluation -> Personalised Discovery"
        )
        print(
            "All demonstrated capabilities completed successfully."
        )
    else:
        print("HACKATHON DEMO: QUALITY CHECK FAILED")

    print("=" * 72)
    print()

    assert governance_result.decision == GovernanceDecision.ALLOW
    assert challenge.topic == "AI Act"
    assert evaluation_result.passed is True


def main() -> None:
    """
    Application entry point.
    """

    asyncio.run(run_demo())


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

EasyChatBot Hackathon - Judge Demonstration.

Concise, deterministic, Windows-safe presentation of the completed

This module is an orchestration and presentation layer only.

It does not:

- call external providers
- execute payments
- create shopping infrastructure
- replace the AI Runtime
- replace application services

Demonstration story:

Governance
|
v
Live Intelligence

v
Learning

v
AI Quality Evaluation
|
v
Memory / Personalisation
|
v
Personalised Discovery
|----------------------+
|                      |
v                      v

v                      v

+----------+-----------+
           |
           v
    Commerce Boundary

v
Vendor Handoff

def _print_section(
    number: str,
    title: str,
) -> None:
    """Print a concise, Windows-safe judge-facing section heading."""

    print()
    print("=" * 72)
    print(f"{number} - {title}")
    print("=" * 72)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Existing Flash Deal flow
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def _build_flash_deal():
    """
    Build the existing deterministic Flash Deal using the
    existing discovery contracts.
    """

    deal = build_demo_deal()
    memory = build_demo_memory()

    request = DiscoveryRequest(
        user_id=memory.user_id,
        deal=deal,
        character=CharacterContext(
            character_id="yeti",
            character_name="Yeti",
            message=(
                "Look what I found! "
                "This could be perfect for you."
            ),
        ),
        product_image_url=(
            "https://example.com/demo/iphone17-pro.jpg"
        ),
        tagline="Pro. Beautiful. Powerful.",
        highlights=(
            "Pro camera system",
            "High-performance chip",
            "All-day battery life",
            "Premium display",
        ),
    )

    card = DiscoveryService().create_flash_deal(
        request,
        memories=[memory],
    )

    notification = DiscoveryNotification.from_flash_deal(
        notification_id="judge-demo-notification-001",
        user_id=memory.user_id,
        deal=card,
    )

    return memory, card, notification


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Deterministic podcast provider for the judge demo
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


class _JudgePodcastProvider:
    """
    Deterministic provider stand-in.

    The judge demo deliberately does not call Apple or Spotify live.

    The provider layer returns a normalised French podcast candidate.
    The Podcast Agent never knows which provider produced it.
    """

    def search(
        self,
        intent: PodcastSearchIntent,
    ) -> list[PodcastSearchResult]:
        return [
            PodcastSearchResult(
                provider=PodcastProviderName.APPLE,
                external_id="judge-podcast-fr-001",
                title="L'IA en Europe",
                description=(
                    "Artificial intelligence and European regulation "
                    "explained in French for learners and curious listeners."
                ),
                publisher="Demo Podcast Publisher",
                languages=("fr",),
                url=(
                    "https://podcasts.apple.com/demo/ai-europe"
                ),
                metadata={
                    "learner_level": "B2",
                },
            )
        ]


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Personalised Podcast Learning
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def _build_podcast_learning():
    """
    Build the complete deterministic podcast-learning vertical slice.

    Flow:

    Memory
        -> PodcastDiscoveryAgent
        -> provider-neutral intent
        -> normalised provider result
        -> language filtering
        -> preference ranking
        -> character recommendation
        -> learning challenge
    """

    memory = MemoryEntry(
        user_id="demo-user",
        memory_type=MemoryType.PREFERENCE,
        content=(
            "Prefers technology and current affairs."
        ),
        metadata={
            "topics": (
                "artificial intelligence, "
                "European regulation"
            ),
        },
    )

    learning_context = LearningContext(
        world_id="French World",
        target_language="fr",
        learner_level="B2",
        learning_goal=(
            "Learn French through current events."
        ),
        country_code="LU",
    )

    # The Agent only knows the learner's context and preferences.
    # It does not know Apple or Spotify.
    intent = PodcastDiscoveryAgent().build_search_intent(
        user_id=memory.user_id,
        learning_context=learning_context,
        memories=[memory],
    )

    recommendation = PodcastRecommendationService(
        [_JudgePodcastProvider()]
    ).recommend(
        intent=intent,
        character_id="yeti",
        character_name="Yeti",
        character_message=(
            "I found this podcast because you like "
            "AI and European technology."
        ),
    )

    challenge_service = PodcastLearningChallengeService()

    challenge = challenge_service.create_challenge(
        recommendation=recommendation,
    )

    return (
        memory,
        learning_context,
        recommendation,
        challenge,
    )

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

"""
Sprint H1 â€” Live Intelligence Challenge Demo.

Demonstrates the complete Live Intelligence vertical slice:

Search Provider
        â†“
Live Intelligence Agent
        â†“
Challenge Generator
        â†“
Learning Challenge

This demo intentionally uses controlled local implementations.
It does not require a real Parallel API key.

It is intended to prove that the H1 architecture works
end-to-end before connecting real external providers.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import sys
from pathlib import Path


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Repository path bootstrap
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
#
# When this file is executed directly:
#
#     python examples/live_challenge_demo.py
#
# Python places the "examples" directory on sys.path rather than the
# repository root. Add the repository root so the existing "src" package
# can be imported without modifying the application architecture.
#

REPOSITORY_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]

if str(REPOSITORY_ROOT) not in sys.path:

async def run_demo() -> None:
    """
    Execute the complete H1 demonstration flow.
    """

    print()
    print("=" * 60)
    print("EasyChatBot â€” Sprint H1")
    print("Live Intelligence Learning Challenge")
    print("=" * 60)
    print()

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 1. Create Live Intelligence Agent
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    intelligence_agent = LiveIntelligenceAgent(
        search_provider=DemoSearchProvider(),
        processor=DemoIntelligenceProcessor(),
    )

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 2. Research topic
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    print("Researching topic...")
    print()

    intelligence = await intelligence_agent.execute(
        LiveIntelligenceRequest(
            topic="AI Act",
            language="French",
            learner_level="B2",
        )
    )

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 3. Display research result
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    print("RESEARCH")
    print("-" * 60)
    print(f"Topic: {intelligence.topic}")
    print(f"Summary: {intelligence.summary}")
    print()

    print("Key information:")

    for item in intelligence.key_information:
        print(f"  â€¢ {item}")

    print()

    print("Sources:")

    for source in intelligence.sources:
        print(f"  â€¢ {source.title}")
        print(f"    {source.url}")
        print(f"    Provider: {source.source}")

    print()

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 4. Create learning challenge
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    challenge_generator = ChallengeGenerator(
        content_generator=DemoChallengeContentGenerator()
    )

    challenge = await challenge_generator.generate(
        intelligence=intelligence,
        language="French",
        learner_level="B2",
    )

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 5. Display learning challenge
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    print("LEARNING CHALLENGE")
    print("-" * 60)

    print(f"Title: {challenge.title}")
    print(f"Language: {challenge.language}")
    print(f"Level: {challenge.learner_level}")
    print()

    print("Vocabulary:")

    for item in challenge.vocabulary:
        print(f"  â€¢ {item.term}")
        print(f"    {item.definition}")

        if item.example:
            print(f"    Example: {item.example}")

    print()

    print("Discussion Questions:")

    for index, question in enumerate(
        challenge.discussion_questions,
        start=1,
    ):
        print(f"  {index}. {question.question}")
        print(f"     Level: {question.difficulty}")

    print()
    print("=" * 60)
    print("H1 vertical slice completed successfully.")
    print("=" * 60)
    print()


def main() -> None:
    """
    Synchronous entry point.
    """

    asyncio.run(run_demo())


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

"""
EasyChatBot Hackathon â€” Personalised Podcast Discovery Demo.

The demo proves the important product rule without network calls:

Memory + Learning World
        â†“
Podcast Agent
        â†“
Provider-neutral intent
        â†“
Apple + Spotify candidates
        â†“
Hard target-language filter
        â†“
Preference ranking
        â†“
Character recommendation

from __future__ import annotations

import sys

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Repository path bootstrap

if str(REPOSITORY_ROOT) not in sys.path:

from src.ai_runtime.memory.models import MemoryEntry, MemoryType
from src.ai_runtime.podcast.agent import PodcastDiscoveryAgent

    LearningContext,

class DemoAppleProvider:
    """
    Deterministic Apple Podcasts stand-in.

    This demo provider does not make network calls.
    It represents a normalised result returned by Apple Podcasts.
    """

    def search(
        self,
        intent: PodcastSearchIntent,
    ) -> list[PodcastSearchResult]:
        return [
            PodcastSearchResult(
                provider=PodcastProviderName.APPLE,
                external_id="apple-fr-001",
                title="L'IA en Europe",
                description=(
                    "Artificial intelligence and European regulation "
                    "in French."
                ),
                publisher="Demo Apple Publisher",
                languages=("fr",),
                url="https://podcasts.apple.com/demo/ai-europe",
            )
        ]


class DemoSpotifyProvider:
    """
    Deterministic Spotify stand-in.

    This deliberately returns an English candidate so the demo proves
    that EasyChatBot rejects a topic-relevant podcast when it does not
    satisfy the learner's target language.

    def search(
        self,

    ) -> list[PodcastSearchResult]:
        return [

            )
        ]

def main() -> None:
    """
    Run the deterministic personalised podcast discovery demonstration.
    """

    memory = MemoryEntry(
        user_id="demo-user",
        memory_type=MemoryType.PREFERENCE,
        content=(
            "Prefers technology and current affairs."
        ),
        metadata={
            "topics": (
                "artificial intelligence, "
                "European regulation"
            ),
        },
    )

    learning_context = LearningContext(
        world_id="French World",
        target_language="fr",
        learner_level="B2",
        learning_goal=(
            "Learn French through current events."
        ),
        country_code="LU",
    )

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 1. Agent creates provider-neutral intent
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    intent = PodcastDiscoveryAgent().build_search_intent(
        user_id="demo-user",
        learning_context=learning_context,
        memories=[memory],
    )

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 2. Providers return normalised candidates
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    recommendation = PodcastRecommendationService(
        [
            DemoAppleProvider(),
            DemoSpotifyProvider(),
        ]
    ).recommend(
        intent=intent,
        character_id="yeti",
        character_name="Yeti",
        character_message=(
            "I found a podcast I think you'll enjoy!"
        ),
    )

    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 3. Present deterministic result
    # -----------------------------------------------------------------------

    print()
    print("=" * 72)
    print("EASYCHATBOT â€” PERSONALISED PODCAST DISCOVERY")
    print("=" * 72)
    print()

    print(
        f"Learning world: "
        f"{learning_context.world_id}"
    )

    print(
        f"Target language: "
        f"{learning_context.target_language}"
    )

    print(
        f"Learner level: "
        f"{learning_context.learner_level}"
    )

    print()

    print(
        "[PASS] Preferences converted into "
        "provider-neutral intent"
    )

    print(
        "[PASS] Apple + Spotify candidates "
        "normalised"
    )

    print(
        "[PASS] Wrong-language candidate rejected"
    )

    print(
        "[PASS] Target-language candidate selected"
    )

    print()

    print(
        f"{recommendation.character_name}:"
    )

    print(
        f'"{recommendation.character_message}"'
    )

    print()

    print(
        f"Podcast: "
        f"{recommendation.result.title}"
    )

    print(
        f"Provider: "
        f"{recommendation.result.provider.value}"
    )

    print(
        f"Language: "
        f"{recommendation.result.languages[0]}"
    )

    print(
        f"Why: "
        f"{recommendation.relevance_reason}"
    )

    print(
        f"Match score: "
        f"{recommendation.match_score:.0f}/100"
    )

    print()

    print("[ LISTEN ]")

    print(
        f"  {recommendation.result.url}"
    )

    print()

    print("=" * 72)
    print("PODCAST DISCOVERY DEMO: SUCCESS")
    print("=" * 72)
    print()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\src\ai_agents\live_intelligence_agent\handler.py
============================================================
"""
Live Intelligence Agent Handler.

Responsible for executing the Live Intelligence Agent workflow.

Architecture:

AI Runtime
    |
    v
Live Intelligence Agent Handler
    |
    +--> Search Provider Interface
    |
    +--> Knowledge Processing
    |
    v
Structured Intelligence Output

Responsibilities:
- Validate agent input
- Call approved provider interfaces
- Normalise research output
- Return explicit structured results

Non-responsibilities:
- Provider SDK management
- Direct external API handling
- Autonomous actions
- Configuration changes
"""

from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import List, Protocol

from src.providers.search_provider.interface import (
    SearchProvider,
    SearchResult,
)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class LiveIntelligenceRequest:
    """
    Input contract for Live Intelligence Agent.
    """

    topic: str
    language: str = "French"
    learner_level: str = "B2"


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class LiveIntelligenceResponse:
    """
    Output contract from Live Intelligence Agent.
    """

    topic: str
    summary: str
    sources: List[SearchResult]
    key_information: List[str]


class IntelligenceProcessor(Protocol):
    """
    Contract for future LLM-based processing.

    Keeps the agent independent from
    any specific model/provider.
    """

    async def summarise(
        self,
        results: List[SearchResult],
        language: str,
        learner_level: str,
    ) -> tuple[str, List[str]]:
        ...


class LiveIntelligenceAgent:
    """
    Live Intelligence Agent implementation.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        search_provider: SearchProvider,
        processor: IntelligenceProcessor,
    ) -> None:
        self._search_provider = search_provider
        self._processor = processor

    async def execute(
        self,
        request: LiveIntelligenceRequest,
    ) -> LiveIntelligenceResponse:
        """
        Execute research workflow.

        Flow:

        Request
          |
          v
        Search Provider
          |
          v
        Intelligence Processor
          |
          v
        Structured Response
        """

        self._validate_request(request)

        search_results = await self._search_provider.search(
            query=request.topic,
            max_results=5,
        )

        summary, key_information = await self._processor.summarise(
            results=search_results,
            language=request.language,
            learner_level=request.learner_level,
        )

        return LiveIntelligenceResponse(
            topic=request.topic,
            summary=summary,
            sources=search_results,
            key_information=key_information,
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _validate_request(
        request: LiveIntelligenceRequest,
    ) -> None:
        """
        Validate required input.
        """

        if not request.topic.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Topic cannot be empty"
            )

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\src\ai_runtime\capabilities\live_intelligence_executor.py
============================================================
"""
AI Runtime executor for Live Intelligence.

This adapter bridges the provider-independent AI Runtime
capability contract with the existing LiveIntelligenceAgent.

Architecture:

AI Runtime
    |
    v
CapabilityRequest
    |
    v
LiveIntelligenceExecutor
    |
    v
LiveIntelligenceAgent
    |
    v
LiveIntelligenceResponse

The adapter:

- validates the Runtime capability
- translates Runtime payloads into LiveIntelligenceRequest
- invokes the existing Live Intelligence Agent
- translates the agent response into CapabilityResult

It does not:

- implement intelligence logic
- call Parallel directly
- modify the LiveIntelligenceAgent
- perform governance decisions
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from src.ai_agents.live_intelligence_agent.handler import (
    LiveIntelligenceAgent,
    LiveIntelligenceRequest,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.executor import (
    CapabilityExecutor,
)

from src.ai_runtime.capabilities.models import (
    CapabilityExecutionStatus,
    CapabilityRequest,
    CapabilityResult,
    CapabilityType,
)


class LiveIntelligenceExecutor(
    CapabilityExecutor,
):
    """
    Runtime adapter for the existing LiveIntelligenceAgent.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        agent: LiveIntelligenceAgent,
    ) -> None:
        self._agent = agent

    @property
    def capability(self) -> CapabilityType:
        """
        Return the capability handled by this executor.
        """

        return CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE

    async def execute(
        self,
        request: CapabilityRequest,
    ) -> CapabilityResult:
        """
        Translate and execute a Live Intelligence request.
        """

        if (
            request.capability
            != CapabilityType.LIVE_INTELLIGENCE
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "LiveIntelligenceExecutor received "
                "an unsupported capability: "
                f"{request.capability.value}"
            )

        agent_request = (
            self._build_agent_request(request)
        )

        response = await self._agent.execute(
            agent_request
        )

        return CapabilityResult(
            request_id=request.request_id,
            capability=request.capability,
            status=CapabilityExecutionStatus.COMPLETED,
            output=response,
            metadata={
                "executor": (
                    "live_intelligence_executor"
                ),
            },
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _build_agent_request(
        request: CapabilityRequest,
    ) -> LiveIntelligenceRequest:
        """
        Translate Runtime payload into the existing
        LiveIntelligenceRequest contract.
        """

        payload = request.payload

        topic = payload.get("topic")

        if not isinstance(topic, str):
            raise ValueError(
                "Live Intelligence capability requires "
                "a string 'topic'."
            )

        language = payload.get(
            "language",
            "French",
        )

        learner_level = payload.get(
            "learner_level",
            "B2",
        )

        if not isinstance(language, str):
            raise ValueError(
                "Live Intelligence 'language' "
                "must be a string."
            )

        if not isinstance(
            learner_level,
            str,
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "Live Intelligence 'learner_level' "
                "must be a string."
            )

        return LiveIntelligenceRequest(
            topic=topic,
            language=language,
            learner_level=learner_level,
        )

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\src\ai_runtime\discovery\flash_deal_card.py

"""
Hackathon Flash Deal Card presentation contract.

This module defines the user-facing data contract for the
EasyChatBot Flash Deal Card shown by a character.

Architecture:

Parallel / Discovery
        |
        v
     DealCard
        |
        +---- Memory Context
        |
        +---- Character
        |
        v
  FlashDealCard
        |
        +---- GET DEAL
        |
        +---- MORE INFORMATION
        |
        v
 External Vendor / Future Commerce Flow

The model is presentation-oriented.

It does not:
- call providers
- access SQLite
- perform payments
- execute agents
- scrape vendor pages
- invent product information
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.models import DealCard


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class FlashDealCard:
    """
    User-facing Flash Deal Card.

    This contract maps directly to the hackathon UX:

    - character discovery
    - product information
    - pricing
    - discount
    - personal relevance
    - vendor
    - GET DEAL action
    - MORE INFORMATION action

    The card deliberately contains no payment implementation.
    """

    deal_id: str
    character_id: str
    character_name: str
    character_message: str

    product_name: str
    product_image_url: str
    tagline: str
    highlights: tuple[str, ...]

    current_price: float
    currency: str
    previous_price: float | None
    discount_percentage: float | None
    savings: float | None

    relevance_reason: str

    vendor_name: str
    more_information_url: str
    get_deal_url: str

    badge: str = "NEW FIND!"
    card_id: str | None = None
    metadata: dict[str, str] = field(
        default_factory=dict
    )

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        """
        Validate the presentation contract.
        """

        required_text_fields = {
            "deal_id": self.deal_id,
            "character_id": self.character_id,
            "character_name": self.character_name,
            "character_message": self.character_message,
            "product_name": self.product_name,
            "product_image_url": self.product_image_url,
            "tagline": self.tagline,
            "currency": self.currency,
            "relevance_reason": self.relevance_reason,
            "vendor_name": self.vendor_name,
            "more_information_url": self.more_information_url,
            "get_deal_url": self.get_deal_url,
        }

        for field_name, value in required_text_fields.items():
            if not value.strip():
                raise ValueError(
                    f"FlashDealCard {field_name} cannot be empty."
                )

        if not self.highlights:
            raise ValueError(
                "FlashDealCard must contain at least one highlight."
            )

        if any(
            not highlight.strip()
            for highlight in self.highlights
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "FlashDealCard highlights cannot contain empty values."
            )

        if self.current_price < 0:
            raise ValueError(
                "FlashDealCard current_price cannot be negative."
            )

        if (
            self.previous_price is not None
            and self.previous_price < 0
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "FlashDealCard previous_price cannot be negative."
            )

        if (
            self.discount_percentage is not None
            and not 0 <= self.discount_percentage <= 100
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "FlashDealCard discount_percentage must be "
                "between 0 and 100."
            )

        if self.savings is not None and self.savings < 0:
            raise ValueError(
                "FlashDealCard savings cannot be negative."
            )

        self._validate_url(
            self.product_image_url,
            "product_image_url",
        )
        self._validate_url(
            self.more_information_url,
            "more_information_url",
        )
        self._validate_url(
            self.get_deal_url,
            "get_deal_url",
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _validate_url(
        url: str,
        field_name: str,
    ) -> None:
        """
        Validate a user-facing URL.
        """

        if not (
            url.startswith("https://")
            or url.startswith("http://")
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                f"FlashDealCard {field_name} must use "
                "HTTP or HTTPS."
            )

    @classmethod
    def from_deal(
        cls,
        *,
        deal: DealCard,
        character_id: str,
        character_name: str,
        character_message: str,
        relevance_reason: str,
        product_image_url: str,
        tagline: str,
        highlights: list[str] | tuple[str, ...],
        badge: str = "NEW FIND!",
        metadata: dict[str, str] | None = None,
    ) -> "FlashDealCard":
        """
        Build a presentation card from a domain DealCard.

        Product, pricing and vendor information comes from the
        DealCard. Character and personalisation information is
        supplied explicitly by the application layer.

        No product or pricing information is invented here.
        """

        savings: float | None = None

        if (
            deal.previous_price is not None
            and deal.previous_price > deal.price
        ):
            savings = round(
                deal.previous_price - deal.price,
                2,
            )

        return cls(
            deal_id=deal.deal_id,
            character_id=character_id,
            character_name=character_name,
            character_message=character_message,
            product_name=deal.product_name,
            product_image_url=product_image_url,
            tagline=tagline,
            highlights=tuple(highlights),
            current_price=deal.price,
            currency=deal.currency,
            previous_price=deal.previous_price,
            discount_percentage=deal.discount_percentage,
            savings=savings,
            relevance_reason=relevance_reason,
            vendor_name=deal.vendor,
            more_information_url=(
                deal.commerce_link.destination_url
            ),
            get_deal_url=(
                deal.commerce_link.click_url
            ),
            badge=badge,
            metadata=(
                dict(metadata)
                if metadata is not None
                else {}
            ),
        )

    @property
    def has_discount(self) -> bool:
        """Return whether a verified discount exists."""

        return (
            self.discount_percentage is not None
            and self.discount_percentage > 0
        )

    @property
    def has_previous_price(self) -> bool:
        """Return whether a previous price is available."""

        return self.previous_price is not None

    @property
    def display_discount(self) -> str | None:
        """Return a UI-friendly discount label."""

        if self.discount_percentage is None:
            return None

        return f"{self.discount_percentage:.0f}% OFF"

    @property
    def display_savings(self) -> str | None:
        """Return a UI-friendly savings label."""

        if self.savings is None:
            return None

        return f"Save {self.currency} {self.savings:.2f}"
