"""
Hackathon Flash Deal Card demo builder.

This module provides a deterministic demonstration flow for the
user-facing Flash Deal Card.

Flow:

Memory
    |
    v
DealCard
    |
    v
Personal relevance
    |
    v
Character
    |
    v
FlashDealCard

The module is intentionally deterministic for the hackathon.

It does not:
- call external APIs
- call Parallel
- process payments
- access SQLite directly
- execute agents
- create real orders
- pretend that demo pricing is live vendor data

Production discovery should provide the real DealCard and
verified source information.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.flash_deal_card import (
    FlashDealCard,
)

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.models import (
    CommerceLink,
    DealCard,
)

from src.ai_runtime.memory.models import (
    MemoryEntry,
    MemoryType,
)


DEMO_PRODUCT_IMAGE_URL = (
    "https://example.com/demo/iphone17-pro.jpg"

) -> FlashDealCard:

def render_demo_flash_deal_card(
    card: FlashDealCard | None = None,
) -> str:
    """
    Render a terminal-friendly representation of the Flash
    Deal Card.

    This is useful for the hackathon demonstration before a
    graphical frontend is connected.
    """

    if card is None:
        card = build_demo_flash_deal_card()

    lines = [
        "",
        "=" * 72,
        "EASYCHATBOT â€” FLASH DEAL",
        "=" * 72,
        "",
        f"[ {card.badge} ]",
        "",
        f"{card.character_name}",
        f'"{card.character_message}"',
        "",
        f"{card.product_name}",
        card.tagline,
        "",
        "HIGHLIGHTS",
        "-" * 72,
    ]

    for highlight in card.highlights:
        lines.append(f"  â€¢ {highlight}")

    lines.extend(
        [
            "",
            "PRICE",
            "-" * 72,
            f"  Previous price: "
            f"{_format_price(card.previous_price, card.currency)}",
            f"  Discount: "
            f"{card.display_discount or 'No verified discount'}",
            f"  Current price: "
            f"{_format_price(card.current_price, card.currency)}",
            f"  Savings: "
            f"{card.display_savings or 'Not available'}",
            "",
            "WHY THIS IS RELEVANT TO YOU",
            "-" * 72,
            f"  {card.relevance_reason}",
            "",
            f"AVAILABLE FROM: {card.vendor_name}",
            "",
            "[ GET DEAL ]",
            f"  {card.get_deal_url}",
            "",
            "[ MORE INFORMATION ]",
            f"  {card.more_information_url}",
            "",
            "NOTE: This is deterministic hackathon demo data.",
            "=" * 72,
        ]
    )

    return "\n".join(lines)


def _format_price(
    value: float | None,
    currency: str,
) -> str:
    """
    Format an optional price for terminal output.
    """

    if value is None:
        return "N/A"

    return f"{currency} {value:,.2f}"


if __name__ == "__main__":
    print(
        render_demo_flash_deal_card()
    )

"""
EasyChatBot Hackathon â€” Apple Podcasts catalogue provider.

Uses Apple's iTunes Search API for read-only podcast catalogue discovery.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from urllib.parse import urlencode
from urllib.request import Request, urlopen

from src.ai_runtime.podcast.models import (
    PodcastProviderName,
    PodcastSearchIntent,
    PodcastSearchResult,
    normalise_language,
)


class ApplePodcastProvider:
    """Read-only Apple podcast catalogue provider."""

    BASE_URL = "https://itunes.apple.com/search"

    def __init__(self, *, timeout_seconds: float = 10.0) -> None:
        self._timeout_seconds = timeout_seconds

    def search(
        self,
        intent: PodcastSearchIntent,
    ) -> list[PodcastSearchResult]:
        query_terms = list(intent.topics) + list(intent.keywords)
        params = {
            "term": " ".join(query_terms[:6]),
            "country": intent.learning_context.country_code.upper(),
            "media": "podcast",
            "entity": "podcast",
            "limit": str(intent.max_results),
            "explicit": "No",
        }

        request = Request(
            f"{self.BASE_URL}?{urlencode(params)}",
            headers={
                "Accept": "application/json",
                "User-Agent": "EasyChatBot-Hackathon/1.0",
            },
            method="GET",
        )

        with urlopen(
            request,
            timeout=self._timeout_seconds,
        ) as response:
            payload = json.loads(
                response.read().decode("utf-8")
            )

        return [
            self._normalise(item)
            for item in payload.get("results", [])
            if self._is_podcast_result(item)
        ]

    @staticmethod
    def _is_podcast_result(item: dict[str, object]) -> bool:
        return (
            str(item.get("kind", "")).lower() == "podcast"
            or str(item.get("wrapperType", "")).lower() == "track"
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _normalise(
        item: dict[str, object],
    ) -> PodcastSearchResult:
        language = normalise_language(
            str(item.get("language") or "unknown")
        )

        return PodcastSearchResult(
            provider=PodcastProviderName.APPLE,
            external_id=str(
                item.get("collectionId")
                or item.get("trackId")
                or ""
            ),
            title=str(
                item.get("collectionName")
                or item.get("trackName")
                or ""
            ),
            description=str(
                item.get("description")
                or item.get("longDescription")
                or ""
            ),
            publisher=str(
                item.get("artistName")
                or "Unknown publisher"
            ),
            languages=(language,),
            url=str(
                item.get("collectionViewUrl")
                or item.get("trackViewUrl")
                or ""
            ),
            image_url=str(
                item.get("artworkUrl600")
                or item.get("artworkUrl100")
                or ""
            ) or None,
            published_at=str(
                item.get("releaseDate") or ""
            ) or None,
            explicit=(
                str(
                    item.get("trackExplicitness", "")
                ).lower() == "explicit"
            ),
            metadata={"provider": "apple_podcasts"},
        )

def main() -> None:
    memory = MemoryEntry(
        user_id="demo-user",
        memory_type=MemoryType.PREFERENCE,
        content="Prefers technology and current affairs.",
        metadata={
            "topics": (
                "artificial intelligence, European regulation"
            ),
        },
    )

    learning_context = LearningContext(
        world_id="French World",
        target_language="fr",
        learner_level="B2",
        learning_goal="Learn French through current events.",
        country_code="LU",
    )

    intent = PodcastDiscoveryAgent().build_search_intent(
        user_id="demo-user",
        learning_context=learning_context,
        memories=[memory],
    )

    recommendation = PodcastRecommendationService(
        [DemoProvider()]
    ).recommend(
        intent=intent,
        character_id="yeti",
        character_name="Yeti",
        character_message=(
            "I found a podcast I think you'll enjoy. "
            "Listen to it, then let's turn it into a French challenge!"
        ),
    )

    challenge = PodcastLearningChallengeService().create_challenge(
        recommendation=recommendation,
    )

    result = PodcastLearningChallengeService().evaluate(
        challenge=challenge,
        answers=(0, 0),
    )

    presenter = PodcastLearningChallengePresenter()

    print()
    print("=" * 72)
    print("EASYCHATBOT â€” PODCAST â†’ LEARNING CHALLENGE")
    print("=" * 72)
    print()
    print("PERSONALISED RECOMMENDATION")
    print("-" * 72)
    print(f"{recommendation.character_name}:")
    print(f'"{recommendation.character_message}"')
    print()
    print(
        f"[PASS] Podcast selected: "
        f"{recommendation.result.title}"
    )
    print(
        f"[PASS] Language: "
        f"{recommendation.result.languages[0]}"
    )
    print(
        f"[PASS] Preference relevance: "
        f"{recommendation.relevance_reason}"
    )

    print(
        presenter.render(challenge)
    )

    print(
        presenter.render_result(result)
    )

    print()
    print("=" * 72)
    print("PODCAST LEARNING CHALLENGE: SUCCESS")
    print("=" * 72)
    print()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\src\ai_runtime\podcast\service.py
============================================================
"""
EasyChatBot Hackathon â€” personalised podcast recommendation service.

This is the application-level selection boundary.

Language is a HARD filter because EasyChatBot is a language-learning
platform. Preference/topic relevance is evaluated only after a candidate
is language-eligible.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
from uuid import uuid4

from src.ai_runtime.podcast.models import (
    PodcastRecommendation,
    PodcastSearchIntent,
    PodcastSearchResult,
    normalise_language,
)
from src.ai_runtime.podcast.providers import PodcastProvider


class PodcastRecommendationService:
    """Search providers and select a personalised candidate."""

    def __init__(self, providers: list[PodcastProvider]) -> None:
        if not providers:
            raise ValueError(
                "PodcastRecommendationService requires at least one provider."
            )
        self._providers = list(providers)

    def recommend(
        self,
        *,
        intent: PodcastSearchIntent,
        character_id: str,
        character_name: str,
        character_message: str,
    ) -> PodcastRecommendation:
        candidates: list[PodcastSearchResult] = []

        for provider in self._providers:
            candidates.extend(provider.search(intent))

        ranked = self.rank_candidates(
            candidates=candidates,
            intent=intent,
        )

        if not ranked:
            raise LookupError(
                "No podcast matched the learner's language and preferences."
            )

        result, score, reason = ranked[0]

        return PodcastRecommendation(
            recommendation_id=str(uuid4()),
            user_id=intent.user_id,
            character_id=character_id,
            character_name=character_name,
            character_message=character_message,
            result=result,
            relevance_reason=reason,
            match_score=score,
        )

    @classmethod
    def rank_candidates(
        cls,
        *,
        candidates: list[PodcastSearchResult],
        intent: PodcastSearchIntent,
    ) -> list[tuple[PodcastSearchResult, float, str]]:
        ranked: list[tuple[PodcastSearchResult, float, str]] = []

        target_language = normalise_language(
            intent.learning_context.target_language
        )

        preference_terms = [
            term.lower()
            for term in list(intent.topics) + list(intent.keywords)
            if term.strip()
        ]

        for candidate in candidates:
            candidate_languages = {
                normalise_language(language)
                for language in candidate.languages
            }

            # Critical learning-platform rule:
            # unknown/wrong-language content is not eligible.
            if (
                intent.language_required
                and target_language not in candidate_languages
            ):
                continue

            haystack = " ".join(
                [
                    candidate.title,
                    candidate.description,
                    candidate.publisher,
                ]
            ).lower()

            matched_terms = [
                term
                for term in preference_terms
                if cls._term_matches(term, haystack)
            ]

            score = 50.0

            if matched_terms:
                score += min(30.0, len(matched_terms) * 10.0)

            if intent.learning_context.world_id.lower() in haystack:
                score += 10.0

            level = intent.learning_context.learner_level.lower()
            if level and level in haystack:
                score += 10.0

            reason = cls._build_reason(
                language=intent.learning_context.target_language,
                matched_terms=matched_terms,
                world_id=intent.learning_context.world_id,
            )

            ranked.append(
                (candidate, min(score, 100.0), reason)
            )

        ranked.sort(
            key=lambda item: (
                item[1],
                item[0].published_at or "",
            ),
            reverse=True,
        )

        return ranked[: intent.max_results]

    @staticmethod
    def _term_matches(term: str, haystack: str) -> bool:
        term = term.strip().lower()
        if not term:
            return False
        if term in haystack:
            return True

        words = [
            word
            for word in re.findall(r"[a-z0-9Ã€-Ã¿'-]+", term)
            if len(word) > 2
        ]
        return bool(words) and any(
            word in haystack
            for word in words
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _build_reason(
        *,
        language: str,
        matched_terms: list[str],
        world_id: str,
    ) -> str:
        reasons = [f"It is available in {language}."]

        if matched_terms:
            reasons.append(
                "It matches your interests in "
                + ", ".join(matched_terms[:3])
                + "."
            )

        reasons.append(
            f"It fits your {world_id} learning world."
        )

        return " ".join(reasons)

"""
EasyChatBot Hackathon â€” Spotify podcast catalogue provider.

The MVP searches episodes, because episode metadata exposes a `languages`
field. This allows EasyChatBot's language-learning rule to remain strict.

Credentials are injected as an access token; this module does not manage
OAuth state or user accounts.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from urllib.parse import urlencode
from urllib.request import Request, urlopen

from src.ai_runtime.podcast.models import (
    PodcastProviderName,
    PodcastSearchIntent,
    PodcastSearchResult,
    normalise_language,
)


class SpotifyPodcastProvider:
    """Read-only Spotify catalogue provider."""

    BASE_URL = "https://api.spotify.com/v1/search"

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        access_token: [REDACTED]"SpotifyPodcastProvider access_token cannot be empty."
            )
        self._access_token = [REDACTED]"q": " ".join(query_terms[:6]),
            "type": "episode",
            "limit": str(intent.max_results),
        }

        market = self._market or intent.learning_context.country_code
        if market:
            params["market"] = market.upper()

        request = Request(
            f"{self.BASE_URL}?{urlencode(params)}",
            headers={
                "Accept": "application/json",
                "Authorization": f"Bearer {self._access_token}",
                "User-Agent": "EasyChatBot-Hackathon/1.0",
            },
            method="GET",
        )

        with urlopen(
            request,
            timeout=self._timeout_seconds,
        ) as response:
            payload = json.loads(
                response.read().decode("utf-8")
            )

        episodes = payload.get("episodes", {})
        if not isinstance(episodes, dict):
            return []

        items = episodes.get("items", [])
        if not isinstance(items, list):
            return []

        results: list[PodcastSearchResult] = []

        for item in items:
            if not isinstance(item, dict):
                continue

            result = self._normalise_episode(item)
            if result is not None:
                results.append(result)

        return results

    @staticmethod
    def _normalise_episode(
        item: dict[str, object],
    ) -> PodcastSearchResult | None:
        external_id = str(item.get("id") or "")
        title = str(item.get("name") or "")

        external_urls = item.get("external_urls")
        url = ""
        if isinstance(external_urls, dict):
            url = str(
                external_urls.get("spotify") or ""
            )

        if not external_id or not title or not url:
            return None

        raw_languages = item.get("languages", [])
        if not isinstance(raw_languages, list):
            return None

        languages = tuple(
            normalise_language(str(language))
            for language in raw_languages
            if str(language).strip()
        )

        # Unknown language is not invented. The recommendation service
        # will reject a candidate if language_required=True.
        if not languages:
            return None

        images = item.get("images", [])
        image_url = None
        if isinstance(images, list) and images:
            first = images[0]
            if isinstance(first, dict):
                image_url = str(first.get("url") or "") or None

        duration_ms = item.get("duration_ms")
        duration_seconds = (
            int(duration_ms) // 1000
            if isinstance(duration_ms, (int, float))
            else None
        )

        return PodcastSearchResult(
            provider=PodcastProviderName.SPOTIFY,
            external_id=external_id,
            title=title,
            description=str(item.get("description") or ""),
            publisher="Spotify Podcast",
            languages=languages,
            url=url,
            image_url=image_url,
            published_at=str(
                item.get("release_date") or ""
            ) or None,
            duration_seconds=duration_seconds,
            explicit=bool(item.get("explicit", False)),
            metadata={
                "provider": "spotify",
                "content_type": "episode",
            },
        )

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\src\providers\search_provider\interface.py

"""
Search Provider Interface

Defines the provider-independent contract for external search capabilities.

Architecture:
Agent -> Provider Interface -> Provider Adapter

The agent must never depend directly on a vendor SDK.
"""

from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import List


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SearchResult:
    """
    Normalised search result returned by any provider.
    """

    title: str
    url: str
    content: str
    source: str


class SearchProvider(ABC):
    """
    Abstract search provider contract.

    Implementations:
    - ParallelSearchProvider
    - Future providers
    """

    @abstractmethod
    async def search(
        self,
        query: str,
        max_results: int = 5,
    ) -> List[SearchResult]:
        """
        Execute a search query.

        Args:
            query:
                Search query.

            max_results:
                Maximum number of results.

        Returns:
            Normalised search results.
        """
        raise NotImplementedError

============================================================
FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\src\providers\search_provider\parallel_adapter.py
============================================================
"""
Parallel Search Provider Adapter.

Bridges the provider-independent SearchProvider contract
to the existing EasyChatBot ParallelClientWrapper.

Architecture:

Live Intelligence Agent
        |
        v
SearchProvider Interface
        |
        v
ParallelSearchProvider
        |
        v
Existing ParallelClientWrapper
        |
        v
Parallel Search API

The official Parallel SDK remains isolated inside:

    backend/providers/parallel/parallel_client.py

This adapter must not import or instantiate the Parallel SDK
directly.
"""

from typing import Any

from backend.providers.parallel.parallel_client import (
    ParallelClientWrapper,
)

from src.providers.search_provider.interface import (
    SearchProvider,
    SearchResult,
)


class ParallelSearchProvider(SearchProvider):
    """
    SearchProvider implementation backed by the existing
    ParallelClientWrapper.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        api_key: [REDACTED]"""
        Initialise the provider.

        Args:
            api_key:
                [REDACTED]"""

        self._client = client or ParallelClientWrapper(
            api_key=[REDACTED]"""
        Execute a Parallel search and normalise its results.

        Args:
            query:
                Natural-language research objective.

            max_results:
                Maximum number of results returned.

        Returns:
            Provider-independent SearchResult objects.
        """

        if not query.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Search query cannot be empty"
            )

        if max_results <= 0:
            raise ValueError(
                "max_results must be greater than zero"
            )

        response = await self._client.search(
            query=query
        )

        raw_results = self._extract_results(
            response
        )

        normalised_results: list[SearchResult] = []

        for raw_result in raw_results[:max_results]:
            result = self._normalise_result(
                raw_result
            )

            if result is not None:
                normalised_results.append(result)

        return normalised_results

    @staticmethod
    def _extract_results(
        response: Any,
    ) -> list[Any]:
        """
        Extract the actual result collection from the
        ParallelClientWrapper response.

        The existing wrapper returns:

            {
                "query": "...",
                "search_queries": [...],
                "results": <normalised SDK response>
            }

        The SDK response may itself contain:

            {
                "results": [...]
            }

        Therefore this method handles both levels of wrapping.
        """

        if not isinstance(response, dict):
            raise TypeError(
                "Parallel client response must be a dictionary"
            )

        raw_results = response.get(
            "results",
            []
        )

        # The SDK response itself may contain a nested
        # "results" collection.
        if isinstance(raw_results, dict):
            nested_results = raw_results.get(
                "results"
            )

            if isinstance(nested_results, list):
                return nested_results

            # Some SDK versions may expose the result
            # collection under another common field.
            nested_data = raw_results.get(
                "data"
            )

            if isinstance(nested_data, list):
                return nested_data

        if isinstance(raw_results, list):
            return raw_results

        # If the SDK returned an object that was not converted
        # to a dictionary, try to inspect its result attribute.
        nested_results = getattr(
            raw_results,
            "results",
            None,
        )

        if isinstance(nested_results, list):
            return nested_results

        raise TypeError(
            "Parallel search response does not contain "
            "a supported results collection"
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _normalise_result(
        raw_result: Any,
    ) -> SearchResult | None:
        """
        Convert one Parallel result into SearchResult.

        Parallel results may arrive either as dictionaries
        or SDK/Pydantic objects.
        """

        if isinstance(raw_result, dict):
            title = str(
                raw_result.get(
                    "title",
                    ""
                )
            ).strip()

            url = str(
                raw_result.get(
                    "url",
                    ""
                )
            ).strip()

            excerpts = raw_result.get(
                "excerpts",
                []
            )

            # Support providers/SDK versions that use
            # "snippet" or "content" instead.
            if not excerpts:
                excerpts = raw_result.get(
                    "snippet",
                    raw_result.get(
                        "content",
                        []
                    ),
                )

        else:
            title = str(
                getattr(
                    raw_result,
                    "title",
                    ""
                )
            ).strip()

            url = str(
                getattr(
                    raw_result,
                    "url",
                    ""
                )
            ).strip()

            excerpts = getattr(
                raw_result,
                "excerpts",
                None,
            )

            if not excerpts:
                excerpts = getattr(
                    raw_result,
                    "snippet",
                    getattr(
                        raw_result,
                        "content",
                        [],
                    ),
                )

        if not title or not url:
            return None

        content = ParallelSearchProvider._normalise_excerpts(
            excerpts
        )

        return SearchResult(
            title=title,
            url=url,
            content=content,
            source="parallel",
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _normalise_excerpts(
        excerpts: Any,
    ) -> str:
        """
        Convert Parallel excerpts/content into a single
        application-level content string.
        """

        if excerpts is None:
            return ""

        if isinstance(excerpts, str):
            return excerpts.strip()

        if isinstance(excerpts, list):
            return "\n\n".join(
                str(excerpt).strip()
                for excerpt in excerpts
                if str(excerpt).strip()
            )

        return str(excerpts).strip()

"""
Tests for Live Intelligence Agent Handler.
"""

import pytest

from src.ai_agents.live_intelligence_agent.handler import (
    LiveIntelligenceAgent,
    LiveIntelligenceRequest,
)


class FakeSearchProvider:
    async def search(
        self,
        query: str,
        max_results: int = 5,
    ):
        return []


class FakeProcessor:
    async def summarise(
        self,
        results,
        language,
        learner_level,
    ):
        return (
            "AI Act summary",
            ["regulation", "governance"],
        )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_live_intelligence_agent_execution():

    agent = LiveIntelligenceAgent(
        search_provider=FakeSearchProvider(),
        processor=FakeProcessor(),
    )

    result = await agent.execute(
        LiveIntelligenceRequest(
            topic="AI Act updates"
        )
    )

    assert result.topic == "AI Act updates"
    assert result.summary == "AI Act summary"
    assert "governance" in result.key_information


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_live_intelligence_agent_rejects_empty_topic():

    agent = LiveIntelligenceAgent(
        search_provider=FakeSearchProvider(),
        processor=FakeProcessor(),
    )

    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        await agent.execute(
            LiveIntelligenceRequest(
                topic=""
            )
        )

"""
Tests for the hackathon Discovery domain models.
"""

import pytest

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.models import (
    CommerceLink,
    CommerceLinkType,
    DealCard,
    FlashIdea,
)


def test_commerce_link_uses_vendor_destination():

    link = CommerceLink(
        vendor="Example Vendor",
        destination_url=(
            "https://example.com/product/123"
        ),
    )

    assert link.vendor == "Example Vendor"

    assert (
        link.destination_url
        == "https://example.com/product/123"
    )

    assert (
        link.link_type
        == CommerceLinkType.DIRECT
    )

    assert (
        link.click_url
        == link.destination_url
    )


def test_commerce_link_supports_affiliate_url():

    link = CommerceLink(
        vendor="Example Vendor",
        destination_url=(
            "https://example.com/product/123"
        ),
        link_type=CommerceLinkType.AFFILIATE,
        tracking_url=(
            "https://example.com/track/123"
        ),
    )

    assert (
        link.link_type
        == CommerceLinkType.AFFILIATE
    )

    assert (
        link.click_url
        == "https://example.com/track/123"
    )


def test_commerce_link_rejects_invalid_url():

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="HTTP or HTTPS",
    ):
        CommerceLink(
            vendor="Example Vendor",
            destination_url="not-a-url",
        )


def test_deal_card_contains_external_vendor_link():

    link = CommerceLink(
        vendor="Example Vendor",
        destination_url=(
            "https://example.com/product/123"
        ),
    )

    deal = DealCard(
        product_name="Travel Backpack",
        vendor="Example Vendor",
        price=89.0,
        currency="EUR",
        commerce_link=link,
        previous_price=129.0,
        discount_percentage=31.0,
        source="parallel",
    )

    assert deal.product_name == "Travel Backpack"

    assert deal.vendor == "Example Vendor"

    assert deal.price == 89.0

    assert deal.currency == "EUR"

    assert deal.commerce_link.click_url == (
        "https://example.com/product/123"
    )

    assert deal.deal_id


def test_deal_card_rejects_negative_price():

    link = CommerceLink(
        vendor="Example Vendor",
        destination_url=(
            "https://example.com/product/123"
        ),
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="price cannot be negative",
    ):
        DealCard(
            product_name="Travel Backpack",
            vendor="Example Vendor",
            price=-1,
            currency="EUR",
            commerce_link=link,
        )


def test_deal_card_rejects_vendor_mismatch():

    link = CommerceLink(
        vendor="Vendor A",
        destination_url=(
            "https://example.com/product/123"
        ),
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="vendor must match",
    ):
        DealCard(
            product_name="Travel Backpack",
            vendor="Vendor B",
            price=89.0,
            currency="EUR",
            commerce_link=link,
        )


def test_flash_idea_connects_character_to_deal():

    idea = FlashIdea(
        character_id="yeti",
        deal_id="deal-001",
        title="Yeti found this",
        message=(
            "I found a travel backpack "
            "that matches your style."
        ),
    )

    assert idea.character_id == "yeti"

    assert idea.deal_id == "deal-001"

    assert (
        idea.title
        == "Yeti found this"
    )

    assert (
        "travel backpack"
        in idea.message
    )

    assert idea.idea_id

"""
Tests for the hackathon Flash Deal Card presentation contract.

The tests validate the user-facing card described by the
hackathon UX:

Character
    â†“
Product
    â†“
Pricing / Deal
    â†“
Why relevant to you
    â†“
Vendor
    â†“
GET DEAL / MORE INFORMATION
"""

import pytest

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.flash_deal_card import (
    FlashDealCard,
)

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.models import (
    CommerceLink,
    CommerceLinkType,
    DealCard,
)


def make_deal(
    *,
    affiliate: bool = False,
) -> DealCard:
    link = CommerceLink(
        vendor="eBay",
        destination_url=(
            "https://www.ebay.com/itm/example"
        ),
        link_type=(
            CommerceLinkType.AFFILIATE
            if affiliate
            else CommerceLinkType.DIRECT
        ),
        tracking_url=(
            "https://tracking.example.com/deal/001"
            if affiliate
            else None
        ),
    )

    return DealCard(
        product_name="iPhone 17 Pro",
        vendor="eBay",
        price=999.99,
        currency="EUR",
        previous_price=1199.00,
        discount_percentage=17.0,
        commerce_link=link,
        source="parallel",
        description="Pro smartphone with advanced camera system.",
    )


def test_flash_deal_card_is_created_from_deal():

    deal = make_deal()

    card = FlashDealCard.from_deal(
        deal=deal,
        character_id="yeti",
        character_name="Yeti",
        character_message="Look what I found!",
        relevance_reason=(
            "This matches your interest in powerful "
            "mobile photography."
        ),
        product_image_url=(
            "https://example.com/images/iphone17.jpg"
        ),
        tagline="Pro. Beautiful. Powerful.",
        highlights=[
            "Pro camera system",
            "A19 Bionic chip",
            "All-day battery life",
            "6.3-inch Super Retina XDR display",
        ],
    )

    assert card.deal_id == deal.deal_id
    assert card.character_id == "yeti"
    assert card.character_name == "Yeti"

    assert card.product_name == "iPhone 17 Pro"
    assert card.tagline == "Pro. Beautiful. Powerful."

    assert len(card.highlights) == 4

    assert card.current_price == 999.99
    assert card.previous_price == 1199.00
    assert card.discount_percentage == 17.0

    assert card.savings == 199.01

    assert card.vendor_name == "eBay"

    assert (
        card.more_information_url
        == "https://www.ebay.com/itm/example"
    )

    assert (
        card.get_deal_url
        == "https://www.ebay.com/itm/example"
    )


def test_flash_deal_card_contains_personal_relevance():

    deal = make_deal()

    card = FlashDealCard.from_deal(
        deal=deal,
        character_id="yeti",
        character_name="Yeti",
        character_message="Look what I found!",
        relevance_reason=(
            "You often look for strong mobile "
            "photography performance."
        ),
        product_image_url=(
            "https://example.com/images/iphone17.jpg"
        ),
        tagline="Pro. Beautiful. Powerful.",
        highlights=[
            "Pro camera system",
        ],
    )

    assert (
        card.relevance_reason
        == "You often look for strong mobile "
        "photography performance."
    )


def test_flash_deal_card_exposes_discount():

    deal = make_deal()

    card = FlashDealCard.from_deal(
        deal=deal,
        character_id="yeti",
        character_name="Yeti",
        character_message="Look what I found!",
        relevance_reason="Relevant to you.",
        product_image_url=(
            "https://example.com/images/iphone17.jpg"
        ),
        tagline="Pro. Beautiful. Powerful.",
        highlights=["Pro camera system"],
    )

    assert card.has_discount is True
    assert card.has_previous_price is True

    assert card.display_discount == "17% OFF"
    assert card.display_savings == "Save EUR 199.01"

    deal = DealCard(
        product_name="Travel Backpack",
        vendor="Example Vendor",
        price=89.00,
        currency="EUR",
        commerce_link=CommerceLink(
            vendor="Example Vendor",
            destination_url=(
                "https://example.com/backpack"
            ),
        ),
    )

    card = FlashDealCard.from_deal(
        deal=deal,
        character_id="yeti",
        character_name="Yeti",
        character_message="I found something for you.",
        relevance_reason="This matches your travel interests.",
        product_image_url=(
            "https://example.com/images/backpack.jpg"
        ),
        tagline="Lightweight and practical.",
        highlights=[
            "Lightweight",
            "Water resistant",
        ],
    )

    assert card.has_discount is False
    assert card.has_previous_price is False
    assert card.display_discount is None
    assert card.display_savings is None
    assert card.savings is None

"""
Tests for personalised Flash Ideas.
"""

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.flash_idea import (
    FlashIdeaBuilder,
)

from src.ai_runtime.discovery.models import (
    CommerceLink,
    DealCard,
)

from src.ai_runtime.memory.models import (
    MemoryEntry,
    MemoryType,
)


def make_deal() -> DealCard:
    link = CommerceLink(
        vendor="Example Vendor",
        destination_url=(
            "https://example.com/product/backpack"
        ),
    )

    return DealCard(
        product_name="Minimalist Travel Backpack",
        vendor="Example Vendor",
        price=89.0,
        currency="EUR",
        previous_price=129.0,
        discount_percentage=31.0,
        commerce_link=link,
        source="parallel",
    )


def test_flash_idea_uses_user_preference():

    memory = MemoryEntry(
        user_id="user-001",
        memory_type=MemoryType.PREFERENCE,
        content="prefers minimalist travel products",
    )

    idea = FlashIdeaBuilder().build(
        character_id="yeti",
        deal=make_deal(),
        memories=[memory],
    )

    assert idea.character_id == "yeti"

    assert (
        idea.deal_id
        == make_deal().deal_id
        or idea.deal_id
    )

    assert (
        "minimalist travel products"
        in idea.message
    )

    assert (
        "Minimalist Travel Backpack"
        in idea.message
    )

    assert (
        "31% off"
        in idea.message
    )


def test_flash_idea_works_without_memory():

    idea = FlashIdeaBuilder().build(
        character_id="yeti",
        deal=make_deal(),
        memories=[],
    )

    assert (
        "I found something you might like"
        in idea.message
    )

    assert (
        "Minimalist Travel Backpack"
        in idea.message
    )


def test_flash_idea_contains_commerce_url():

    idea = FlashIdeaBuilder().build(
        character_id="yeti",
        deal=make_deal(),
        memories=[],
    )

    assert (
        idea.metadata["commerce_url"]
        == "https://example.com/product/backpack"
    )


def test_non_preference_memory_does_not_become_preference():

    memory = MemoryEntry(
        user_id="user-001",
        memory_type=MemoryType.DISCOVERY_CONTEXT,
        content="Looking for travel gear",
    )

    idea = FlashIdeaBuilder().build(
        character_id="yeti",
        deal=make_deal(),
        memories=[memory],
    )

    assert (
        "I found something you might like"
        in idea.message
    )

import json

from src.ai_runtime.podcast.apple import ApplePodcastProvider
from src.ai_runtime.podcast.models import (
    LearningContext,
    PodcastProviderName,
    PodcastSearchIntent,
)
from src.ai_runtime.podcast.spotify import SpotifyPodcastProvider


class FakeResponse:
    def __init__(self, payload: dict):
        self.payload = payload

    def __enter__(self):
        return self

    def __exit__(self, exc_type, exc, tb):
        return False

    def read(self):
        return json.dumps(self.payload).encode("utf-8")


def intent() -> PodcastSearchIntent:
    return PodcastSearchIntent(
        user_id="user-001",
        learning_context=LearningContext(
            world_id="French World",
            target_language="fr",
            learner_level="B2",
        ),
        topics=("artificial intelligence",),
    )


def test_apple_normalises_language(monkeypatch) -> None:
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        "src.ai_runtime.podcast.apple.urlopen",
        lambda *args, **kwargs: FakeResponse(
            {
                "results": [
                    {
                        "kind": "podcast",
                        "collectionId": 123,
                        "collectionName": "IA en France",
                        "artistName": "Demo Publisher",
                        "language": "French",
                        "collectionViewUrl": (
                            "https://podcasts.apple.com/example"
                        ),
                    }
                ]
            }
        ),
    )

    results = ApplePodcastProvider().search(intent())

    assert len(results) == 1
    assert results[0].provider == PodcastProviderName.APPLE
    assert results[0].languages == ("fr",)


def test_spotify_normalises_episode_language(monkeypatch) -> None:
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        "src.ai_runtime.podcast.spotify.urlopen",
        lambda *args, **kwargs: FakeResponse(
            {
                "episodes": {
                    "items": [
                        {
                            "id": "episode-001",
                            "name": "IA en Europe",
                            "description": "European AI regulation",
                            "languages": ["fr"],
                            "duration_ms": 1800000,
                            "release_date": "2026-08-10",
                            "external_urls": {
                                "spotify": (
                                    "https://open.spotify.com/episode/episode-001"
                                )
                            },
                            "images": [],
                        }
                    ]
                }
            }
        ),
    )

    results = SpotifyPodcastProvider(
        access_token="[REDACTED]"
    ).search(intent())

    assert len(results) == 1
    assert results[0].provider == PodcastProviderName.SPOTIFY
    assert results[0].languages == ("fr",)
    assert results[0].duration_seconds == 1800

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\tests\api\test_chat.py
============================================================
import pytest

from httpx import ASGITransport, AsyncClient

from backend.api.main import app
from backend.api import routes


class FakeCompanionService:
    """
    Test double for the application service.

    This test deliberately does not invoke Parallel.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        character,
    ):
        self.character = character
        self.received_context = None
        self.received_use_research = None

    async def interact(
        self,
        context,
        use_research=False,
    ):
        self.received_context = context
        self.received_use_research = (
            use_research
        )

        return {
            "character": self.character.name,
            "conversation": {
                "character": self.character.name,
                "message": (
                    f"{self.character.name}: "
                    f"Test response"
                ),
                "intelligence_context": (
                    "TEST INTELLIGENCE"
                    if use_research
                    else None
                ),
            },
            "research": (
                {
                    "query": context.request,
                    "test": True,
                }
                if use_research
                else None
            ),
            "quality": {
                "quality_score": 1.0,
                "passed": True,
                "findings": [],
                "recommendations": [],
            },
            "memory_count": 0,
        }


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_chat_passes_research_false(
    monkeypatch,
):
    """
    Verify /chat passes research=False
    to CompanionService.
    """

    captured = {}

    def fake_factory(
        character,
    ):
        service = FakeCompanionService(
            character
        )

        captured["service"] = service

        return service

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        routes,
        "create_companion_service",
        fake_factory,
    )

    transport = ASGITransport(
        app=app
    )

    async with AsyncClient(
        transport=transport,
        base_url="http://test",
    ) as client:

        response = await client.post(
            "/chat",
            json={
                "user_id": "api-test-user",
                "character": "yeti",
                "message": (
                    "Explain artificial intelligence"
                ),
                "research": False,
            },
        )

    assert response.status_code == 200

    data = response.json()

    service = captured["service"]

    assert (
        service.received_use_research
        is False
    )

    assert (
        service.received_context.user_id
        == "api-test-user"
    )

    assert (
        service.received_context.request
        == "Explain artificial intelligence"
    )

    assert data["character"] == "Yeti"

    assert data["research"] is None

    assert (
        data["conversation"][
            "intelligence_context"
        ]
        is None
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_chat_passes_research_true(
    monkeypatch,
):
    """
    Verify /chat passes research=True
    to CompanionService.

    This is an API contract test and therefore
    intentionally does not call Parallel.
    """

    captured = {}

    def fake_factory(
        character,
    ):
        service = FakeCompanionService(
            character
        )

        captured["service"] = service

        return service

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        routes,
        "create_companion_service",
        fake_factory,
    )

    transport = ASGITransport(
        app=app
    )

    async with AsyncClient(
        transport=transport,
        base_url="http://test",
    ) as client:

        response = await client.post(
            "/chat",
            json={
                "user_id": "api-test-user",
                "character": "yeti",
                "message": (
                    "What are the latest "
                    "European AI regulation "
                    "developments?"
                ),
                "research": True,
            },
        )

    assert response.status_code == 200

    data = response.json()

    service = captured["service"]

    assert (
        service.received_use_research
        is True
    )

    assert (
        service.received_context.user_id
        == "api-test-user"
    )

    assert (
        service.received_context.request
        == (
            "What are the latest "
            "European AI regulation "
            "developments?"
        )
    )

    assert data["character"] == "Yeti"

    assert data["research"] is not None

    assert (
        data["conversation"][
            "intelligence_context"
        ]
        == "TEST INTELLIGENCE"
    )

"""
Reliability tests for the unified EasyChatBot hackathon demo.

H4.3 â€” Demo Reliability

These tests verify that the hackathon demonstration:

- starts successfully
- completes within a reasonable time
- does not require external API credentials
- produces the expected final success marker

The test intentionally executes the demo as a separate process,
matching how the hackathon presentation will actually run it.

It does not modify or bypass H1, H2, or H3 validation.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path


REPOSITORY_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]

DEMO_PATH = (
    REPOSITORY_ROOT
    / "examples"
    / "hackathon_demo.py"
)


def _run_demo() -> subprocess.CompletedProcess[str]:
    """
    Execute the unified demo with a bounded runtime.

    A timeout protects CI and the hackathon environment from
    a demo process hanging indefinitely.
    """

    return subprocess.run(
        [
            sys.executable,
            str(DEMO_PATH),
        ],
        cwd=REPOSITORY_ROOT,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
        check=False,
        timeout=30,
    )


def test_hackathon_demo_completes_within_timeout():
    """
    Verify that the demo completes within 30 seconds.
    """

    result = _run_demo()

    assert result.returncode == 0, (
        "Hackathon demo did not complete successfully.\n\n"
        f"STDOUT:\n{result.stdout}\n\n"
        f"STDERR:\n{result.stderr}"
    )


def test_hackathon_demo_is_self_contained():
    """
    Verify that the deterministic hackathon demonstration
    reaches its expected final state.

    The demo uses controlled providers and therefore should
    not require Parallel, LLM, or other external credentials.
    """

    result = _run_demo()

    assert result.returncode == 0

    assert (
        "Governance check: PASSED"
        in result.stdout
    )

    assert (
        "Overall Score: 1.00"
        in result.stdout
    )

    assert (
        "Status: PASS"
        in result.stdout
    )

    assert (
        "HACKATHON DEMO: SUCCESS"
        in result.stdout
    )

    assert (
        "Governance -> Intelligence -> Learning -> Evaluation"
        in result.stdout
    )


def test_hackathon_demo_script_exists():

    assert DEMO_PATH.exists()
    assert DEMO_PATH.is_file()

"""
Integration test for H2 evaluation of the H1 learning challenge.

Flow:

H1 Live Intelligence / Challenge Engine
        â†“
LearningChallenge
        â†“
H2 DeterministicEvaluator
        â†“
CriterionScore[]
        â†“
EvaluationEngine
        â†“
EvaluationResult

This test intentionally uses deterministic test doubles.

It does not:
- call the Parallel API
- call an LLM
- modify H1 components
- use the Conversation Agent
"""

from src.challenge_engine.models import (
    DiscussionQuestion,
    LearningChallenge,
    VocabularyItem,
)

from src.evaluation.criteria import (
    COMPLETENESS,
    EDUCATIONAL_VALUE,
    LANGUAGE_LEVEL,
    SOURCE_COVERAGE,
)

from src.evaluation.deterministic_evaluator import (
    DeterministicEvaluator,
)

from src.evaluation.engine import (
    EvaluationEngine,
)


def _create_h1_learning_challenge() -> LearningChallenge:
    """
    Create a representative H1 learning challenge.

    This mirrors the structure produced by the H1
    Challenge Engine without calling external services.
    """

    return LearningChallenge(
        title="AI Act Learning Challenge",
        topic="AI Act",
        language="French",
        learner_level="B2",
        summary=(
            "The European Union AI Act establishes "
            "a risk-based framework for artificial "
            "intelligence."
        ),
        vocabulary=[
            VocabularyItem(
                term="gouvernance",
                definition=(
                    "La maniÃ¨re dont une organisation "
                    "est dirigÃ©e et contrÃ´lÃ©e."
                ),
                example=(
                    "La gouvernance de l'IA devient "
                    "essentielle."
                ),
            ),
            VocabularyItem(
                term="rÃ©glementation",
                definition=(
                    "Ensemble des rÃ¨gles qui encadrent "
                    "une activitÃ©."
                ),
                example=(
                    "La rÃ©glementation europÃ©enne Ã©volue."
                ),
            ),
        ],
        discussion_questions=[
            DiscussionQuestion(
                question=(
                    "Pourquoi la rÃ©glementation de "
                    "l'IA est-elle importante ?"
                ),
                difficulty="B2",
            ),
            DiscussionQuestion(
                question=(
                    "Quels sont les avantages d'une "
                    "rÃ©glementation europÃ©enne de l'IA ?"
                ),
                difficulty="B2",
            ),
        ],
    )


def test_h1_learning_challenge_can_be_evaluated():
    """
    Validate the complete H1 â†’ H2 evaluation boundary.
    """

    challenge = _create_h1_learning_challenge()

    evaluator = DeterministicEvaluator()

    scores = evaluator.evaluate(
        challenge
    )

    assert len(scores) == 4

    score_names = {
        score.criterion
        for score in scores
    }

    assert score_names == {
        "completeness",
        "language_level",
        "educational_value",
        "source_coverage",
    }


def test_h1_learning_challenge_produces_quality_result():
    """
    Evaluate the H1 challenge through the H2 scoring engine.
    """

    challenge = _create_h1_learning_challenge()

    evaluator = DeterministicEvaluator()

    scores = evaluator.evaluate(
        challenge
    )

    engine = EvaluationEngine(
        criteria=[
            COMPLETENESS,
            LANGUAGE_LEVEL,
            EDUCATIONAL_VALUE,
            SOURCE_COVERAGE,
        ],
        passing_score=0.70,
    )

    result = engine.evaluate(
        scores
    )

    assert result.overall_score == 1.0

    assert result.passed is True

    assert len(result.scores) == 4

    assert result.evaluator == "deterministic"


def test_h1_learning_challenge_contains_high_quality_components():
    """
    Verify the H1 challenge satisfies the basic quality
    dimensions currently supported by deterministic evaluation.
    """

    challenge = _create_h1_learning_challenge()

    evaluator = DeterministicEvaluator()

    scores = evaluator.evaluate(
        challenge
    )

    for score in scores:
        assert 0.0 <= score.score <= 1.0
        assert score.reasoning.strip()

    assert all(
        score.score == 1.0
        for score in scores
    )

"""
Integration test for Live Challenge vertical slice.

Flow tested:

Search Provider
        â†“
Live Intelligence Agent
        â†“
Challenge Generator
        â†“
Learning Challenge

This test validates the Sprint H1 integration boundaries.

Important:
- Uses test doubles only.
- Does not call the real Parallel API.
- Does not modify or depend on the Conversation Agent.
"""

import pytest

from src.ai_agents.live_intelligence_agent.handler import (
    LiveIntelligenceAgent,
    LiveIntelligenceRequest,
)

from src.challenge_engine.generator import (
    ChallengeGenerator,
)

from src.challenge_engine.models import (
    DiscussionQuestion,
    VocabularyItem,
)

from src.providers.search_provider.interface import (
    SearchResult,
)


class FakeSearchProvider:
    """
    Controlled search provider for integration testing.
    """

    async def search(
        self,
        query: str,
        max_results: int = 5,
    ) -> list[SearchResult]:
        return [
            SearchResult(
                title="AI Act Update",
                url="https://example.com/ai-act",
                content="European AI regulation update.",
                source="test-provider",
            )
        ]


class FakeIntelligenceProcessor:
    """
    Simulates intelligence synthesis without
    requiring an LLM provider.
    """

    async def summarise(
        self,
        results: list[SearchResult],
        language: str,
        learner_level: str,
    ) -> tuple[str, list[str]]:
        return (
            "The AI Act introduces new rules for "
            "artificial intelligence.",
            [
                "AI governance",
                "risk management",
            ],
        )


class FakeChallengeContentGenerator:
    """
    Simulates educational content generation.
    """

    async def generate_vocabulary(
        self,
        summary: str,
        language: str,
        learner_level: str,
    ) -> list[VocabularyItem]:
        return [
            VocabularyItem(
                term="gouvernance",
                definition=(
                    "Organisation and control of activities."
                ),
            )
        ]

    async def generate_questions(
        self,
        summary: str,
        language: str,
        learner_level: str,
    ) -> list[DiscussionQuestion]:
        return [
            DiscussionQuestion(
                question=(
                    "Pourquoi la gouvernance de l'IA "
                    "est-elle importante ?"
                ),
            )
        ]


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_live_challenge_vertical_slice():
    """
    Validate the complete Sprint H1 backend flow:

    Search Provider
        â†“
    Live Intelligence Agent
        â†“
    Challenge Generator
        â†“
    Learning Challenge
    """

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Step 1 â€” Create Live Intelligence Agent
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    intelligence_agent = LiveIntelligenceAgent(
        search_provider=FakeSearchProvider(),
        processor=FakeIntelligenceProcessor(),
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Step 2 â€” Execute Live Intelligence research
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    intelligence = await intelligence_agent.execute(
        request=LiveIntelligenceRequest(
            topic="AI Act",
        )
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Step 3 â€” Validate intelligence output
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    assert intelligence.topic == "AI Act"

    assert intelligence.summary == (
        "The AI Act introduces new rules for "
        "artificial intelligence."
    )

    assert intelligence.key_information == [
        "AI governance",
        "risk management",
    ]

    assert len(intelligence.sources) == 1
    assert intelligence.sources[0].title == "AI Act Update"
    assert intelligence.sources[0].source == "test-provider"

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Step 4 â€” Create Challenge Generator
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    challenge_generator = ChallengeGenerator(
        content_generator=FakeChallengeContentGenerator()
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Step 5 â€” Generate learning challenge
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    challenge = await challenge_generator.generate(
        intelligence=intelligence,
        language="French",
        learner_level="B2",
    )

    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Step 6 â€” Validate final learning output
    # ---------------------------------------------------------

    assert challenge.title == (
        "AI Act Learning Challenge"
    )

    assert challenge.topic == "AI Act"
    assert challenge.language == "French"
    assert challenge.learner_level == "B2"

    assert challenge.summary == (
        "The AI Act introduces new rules for "
        "artificial intelligence."
    )

    assert len(challenge.vocabulary) == 1

    assert challenge.vocabulary[0].term == (
        "gouvernance"
    )

    assert (
        challenge.vocabulary[0].definition
        == "Organisation and control of activities."
    )

    assert len(challenge.discussion_questions) == 1

    assert (
        challenge.discussion_questions[0].question
        == "Pourquoi la gouvernance de l'IA est-elle importante ?"
    )

    assert (
        challenge.discussion_questions[0].difficulty
        == "B2"
    )

    assert (
        challenge.created_by_agent
        == "live_intelligence_agent"
    )

"""
Integration test for H3 governance of the H1 Live Intelligence capability.

Flow:

H1 Live Intelligence Capability
        |
        v
Governance Policy
        |
        v
Governance Policy Engine
        |
        v
Governance Decision

This test validates the governance boundary only.

It does not:
- execute the Conversation Agent
- execute the Parallel API
- modify H1 implementation
- modify H2 evaluation
- perform external side effects
"""

from src.governance.engine import (
    GovernancePolicyEngine,
)

from src.governance.models import (
    GovernanceDecision,
    GovernancePolicy,
    RiskLevel,
)


def create_live_intelligence_policy() -> GovernancePolicy:
    """
    Define the governance policy for H1 Live Intelligence.

    Live research:
    - may access external information
    - must not create external side effects
    - is considered medium risk
    - does not require human approval for passive research
    """

    return GovernancePolicy(
        capability="live_intelligence_research",
        risk_level=RiskLevel.MEDIUM,
        requires_human_review=False,
        allows_external_calls=True,
        allows_side_effects=False,
    )


def test_live_intelligence_capability_is_governed():

    engine = GovernancePolicyEngine()

    policy = create_live_intelligence_policy()

    result = engine.evaluate(
        policy
    )

    assert (
        result.capability
        == "live_intelligence_research"
    )

    assert (
        result.risk_level
        == RiskLevel.MEDIUM
    )

    assert (
        result.decision
        == GovernanceDecision.ALLOW
    )


def test_live_intelligence_allows_external_research():

    engine = GovernancePolicyEngine()

    result = engine.evaluate(
        create_live_intelligence_policy()
    )

    assert any(
        "External calls are explicitly permitted."
        in reason
        for reason in result.reasons
    )


def test_live_intelligence_does_not_allow_side_effects():

    policy = create_live_intelligence_policy()

    assert policy.allows_side_effects is False


def test_live_intelligence_with_side_effects_is_blocked():

    engine = GovernancePolicyEngine()

    unsafe_policy = GovernancePolicy(
        capability="live_intelligence_research",
        risk_level=RiskLevel.MEDIUM,
        requires_human_review=False,
        allows_external_calls=True,
        allows_side_effects=True,
    )

    result = engine.evaluate(
        unsafe_policy
    )

    assert (
        result.decision
        == GovernanceDecision.BLOCK
    )


def test_live_intelligence_high_risk_requires_review():

    engine = GovernancePolicyEngine()

    high_risk_policy = GovernancePolicy(
        capability="live_intelligence_research",
        risk_level=RiskLevel.HIGH,
        requires_human_review=False,
        allows_external_calls=True,
        allows_side_effects=False,
    )

    result = engine.evaluate(
        high_risk_policy
    )

    assert (
        result.decision
        == GovernanceDecision.REVIEW
    )

import pytest

from backend.agents.agent_result import (
    AgentResult,
)

from backend.intelligence.intelligence_context import (
    IntelligenceContext,
)

from backend.intelligence.research_service import (
    ResearchService,
)


class FakeResearchAgent:

    async def execute(
        self,
        context,
    ):
        return AgentResult(
            success=True,
            agent_id="research_agent",
            output=IntelligenceContext(
                query=context.request,
            ),
        )


class FailingResearchAgent:

    async def execute(
        self,
        context,
    ):
        return AgentResult(
            success=False,
            agent_id="research_agent",
            output=None,
            error="Research unavailable",
        )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_research_service_returns_intelligence_context():

    service = ResearchService(
        agent=FakeResearchAgent()
    )

    result = await service.research(
        user_id="demo-user",
        query="Latest European AI regulation",
    )

    assert isinstance(
        result,
        IntelligenceContext,
    )

    assert result.query == (
        "Latest European AI regulation"
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_research_service_rejects_empty_query():

    service = ResearchService(
        agent=FakeResearchAgent()
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="Research query cannot be empty",
    ):
        await service.research(
            user_id="demo-user",
            query="",
        )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_research_service_propagates_failure():

    service = ResearchService(
        agent=FailingResearchAgent()
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        RuntimeError,
        match="Research unavailable",
    ):
        await service.research(
            user_id="demo-user",
            query="Latest European AI regulation",
        )

import pytest

from backend.providers.parallel.parallel_client import (
    ParallelClientWrapper,
)


class FakeSearchResult:

    def model_dump(self):
        return {
            "results": [
                {
                    "title": "Example",
                    "url": "https://example.com",
                }
            ]
        }


class FakeParallelClient:

    def search(
        self,
        *,
        search_queries,
        mode,
        objective,
    ):
        assert len(search_queries) == 1
        assert mode == "advanced"
        assert objective == (
            "Latest artificial intelligence news"
        )

        return FakeSearchResult()


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_parallel_client_normalizes_response():

    wrapper = object.__new__(
        ParallelClientWrapper
    )

    wrapper.api_key = "[REDACTED]"

    wrapper._client = FakeParallelClient()

    result = await wrapper.search(
        "Latest artificial intelligence news"
    )

    assert result["query"] == (
        "Latest artificial intelligence news"
    )

    assert result["results"] == {
        "results": [
            {
                "title": "Example",
                "url": "https://example.com",
            }
        ]
    }

"""
Parallel Provider Tests.

These tests validate the provider layer
without calling the external Parallel API.

The real API integration is demonstrated
through the hackathon demo flow.
"""

import pytest

from backend.providers.parallel.parallel_provider import (
    ParallelSearchProvider,
)

from backend.providers.provider_result import (
    ProviderResult,
)

from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import (
    ExecutionContext,
)


class MockParallelClient:
    """
    Fake Parallel client for automated tests.
    """

    async def search(
        self,
        query: str,
    ):

        return {
            "query": query,
            "results": [
                {
                    "title": "Example source",
                    "url": "https://example.com",
                }
            ],
        }


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_parallel_provider_returns_results(
    monkeypatch,
):

    provider = ParallelSearchProvider(
    client=MockParallelClient()
)

    provider.client = MockParallelClient()

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request="Latest AI news",
    )

    result = await provider.execute(
        context
    )

    assert isinstance(
        result,
        ProviderResult,
    )

    assert result.success is True

    assert result.provider_id == (
        "parallel_search"
    )

    assert result.output["query"] == (
        "Latest AI news"
    )

    assert len(
        result.output["results"]
    ) == 1

"""
Tests for Search Provider Interface.

Validates:
- SearchResult data model behaviour
- SearchProvider abstract contract
"""

import pytest

from src.providers.search_provider.interface import (
    SearchProvider,
    SearchResult,
)


def test_search_result_creation():
    """
    SearchResult should store normalised provider output.
    """

    result = SearchResult(
        title="AI Act Update",
        url="https://example.com/article",
        content="Latest AI regulation news.",
        source="example",
    )

    assert result.title == "AI Act Update"
    assert result.url == "https://example.com/article"
    assert result.content == "Latest AI regulation news."
    assert result.source == "example"


def test_search_result_is_immutable():
    """
    SearchResult is frozen and should not allow mutation.
    """

    result = SearchResult(
        title="Test",
        url="https://example.com",
        content="Content",
        source="test",
    )

    with pytest.raises(AttributeError):
        result.title = "Changed"


def test_search_provider_cannot_be_instantiated():
    """
    SearchProvider is an abstract interface.
    """

    with pytest.raises(TypeError):
        SearchProvider()

"""
Integration test for the real Parallel Search Provider.

Sprint H1.8 â€” Real Provider Validation

Flow:

SearchProvider Interface
        â†“
ParallelSearchProvider
        â†“
Existing ParallelClientWrapper
        â†“
Parallel SDK
        â†“
Parallel Search API

The API key is loaded by the existing ParallelClientWrapper
from the project's .env configuration.
"""

import pytest

from src.providers.search_provider.interface import (
    SearchResult,
)

from src.providers.search_provider.parallel_adapter import (
    ParallelSearchProvider,
)


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_parallel_search_provider_returns_normalised_results():
    """
    Validate the real Parallel provider.
    """

    try:
        provider = ParallelSearchProvider()

    except ValueError as exc:
        pytest.skip(
            f"Parallel API credentials are not configured: {exc}"
        )

    results = await provider.search(
        query="latest European AI regulation",
        max_results=3,
    )

    assert isinstance(results, list)

    assert len(results) > 0

    for result in results:
        assert isinstance(
            result,
            SearchResult,
        )

        assert result.title.strip()
        assert result.url.strip()
        assert result.source == "parallel"

import pytest

from backend.agents.conversation_agent import (
    ConversationAgent,
)

from backend.characters.character_profile import (
    CharacterProfile,
)

from backend.conversation.conversation_request import (
    ConversationRequest,
)

from backend.intelligence.intelligence_context import (
    IntelligenceContext,
    IntelligenceSource,
)

from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import (
    ExecutionContext,
)


def create_character():
    return CharacterProfile(
        character_id="yeti",
        name="Yeti",
        description=(
            "Friendly AI learning companion"
        ),
        personality=(
            "Curious and encouraging"
        ),
        learning_style=(
            "Conversation based learning"
        ),
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_conversation_agent():

    character = create_character()

    agent = ConversationAgent(
        character
    )

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request=(
            "Explain artificial intelligence"
        ),
    )

    result = await agent.execute(
        context
    )

    assert result.success is True

    assert result.agent_id == (
        "conversation_agent"
    )

    assert result.output["character"] == (
        "Yeti"
    )

    assert result.output["personality"] == (
        "Curious and encouraging"
    )

    assert result.output["learning_style"] == (
        "Conversation based learning"
    )

    assert isinstance(
        result.output["conversation_request"],
        ConversationRequest,
    )

    request = (
        result.output["conversation_request"]
    )

    assert request.character_id == "yeti"

    assert request.character_name == "Yeti"

    assert request.user_prompt == (
        "Explain artificial intelligence"
    )

    assert (
        request.has_conversation_context
        is False
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_conversation_agent_includes_live_intelligence():

    character = create_character()

    agent = ConversationAgent(
        character
    )

    intelligence = IntelligenceContext(
        query=(
            "Latest European AI regulation"
        ),
        sources=(
            IntelligenceSource(
                title="European AI Regulation",
                url="https://example.com/ai",
                source="parallel",
                content=(
                    "Current regulatory information."
                ),
            ),
        ),
        summary=(
            "Recent regulatory developments."
        ),
    )

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request=(
            "Explain the latest European "
            "AI regulation"
        ),
        intelligence_context=intelligence,
    )

    result = await agent.execute(
        context
    )

    assert result.success is True

    request = (
        result.output["conversation_request"]
    )

    assert isinstance(
        request,
        ConversationRequest,
    )

    assert (
        request.has_conversation_context
        is True
    )

    assert (
        "LIVE INTELLIGENCE"
        in request.conversation_context
    )

    assert (
        "European AI Regulation"
        in request.conversation_context
    )

    assert (
        "Current regulatory information."
        in request.conversation_context
    )

    assert (
        result.output["intelligence_context"]
        == request.conversation_context
    )


def test_conversation_agent_build_request_without_intelligence():

    character = create_character()

    agent = ConversationAgent(
        character
    )

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request="Explain AI",
    )

        context
    )

    assert isinstance(
        request,
        ConversationRequest,
    )

    assert request.character_id == "yeti"

    assert request.character_name == "Yeti"

    assert request.user_prompt == (
        "Explain AI"
    )

    assert (
        request.conversation_context
        is None
    )


def test_conversation_agent_build_request_with_intelligence():

    character = create_character()

    agent = ConversationAgent(
        character
    )

    intelligence = IntelligenceContext(
        query="Latest AI regulation",
        sources=(
            IntelligenceSource(
                title="AI Act",
                url="https://example.com/ai-act",
                source="parallel",
                content="AI regulation information.",
            ),
        ),
    )

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request="Explain the AI Act",
        intelligence_context=intelligence,
    )

        context
    )

    assert request.has_conversation_context

    assert (
        "LIVE INTELLIGENCE"
        in request.conversation_context
    )

    assert (
        request.character_name
        == "Yeti"
    )

import pytest

from backend.agents.research_agent import (
    ResearchAgent,
)

from backend.intelligence.intelligence_context import (
    IntelligenceContext,
    IntelligenceSource,
)

from backend.providers.provider_result import (
    ProviderResult,
)

from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import (
    ExecutionContext,
)


class FakeResearchProvider:
    """
    Fake research provider used for unit testing.

    This keeps the test independent from the real
    Parallel Search API.
    """

    async def execute(
        self,
        context,
    ):
        return ProviderResult(
            success=True,
            provider_id="fake_research",
            output={
                "query": context.request,
                "results": {
                    "results": [
                        {
                            "title": "European AI Regulation",
                            "url": "https://example.com/ai",
                            "excerpts": [
                                "Current regulatory information."
                            ],
                        }
                    ]
                },
            },
        )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_research_agent_returns_intelligence_context():

    agent = ResearchAgent(
        provider=FakeResearchProvider()
    )

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request="Latest European AI regulation",
    )

    result = await agent.execute(
        context
    )

    assert result.success is True

    assert result.agent_id == (
        "research_agent"
    )

    assert isinstance(
        result.output,
        IntelligenceContext,
    )

    assert result.output.query == (
        "Latest European AI regulation"
    )

    assert result.output.source_count == 1

    assert isinstance(
        result.output.sources[0],
        IntelligenceSource,
    )

    assert result.output.sources[0].title == (
        "European AI Regulation"
    )

    assert result.output.sources[0].url == (
        "https://example.com/ai"
    )

    assert (
        result.output.sources[0].content
        == "Current regulatory information."
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_research_agent_propagates_provider_failure():

    class FailingResearchProvider:
        """
        Fake provider representing a provider failure.
        """

        async def execute(
            self,
            context,
        ):
            return ProviderResult(
                success=False,
                provider_id="fake_research",
                output=None,
                error="Research provider unavailable",
            )

    agent = ResearchAgent(
        provider=FailingResearchProvider()
    )

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request="Latest European AI regulation",
    )

    result = await agent.execute(
        context
    )

    assert result.success is False

    assert result.agent_id == (
        "research_agent"
    )

    assert result.output is None

    assert result.error == (
        "Research provider unavailable"
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_research_agent_handles_empty_results():

    class EmptyResearchProvider:
        """
        Fake provider returning no research results.
        """

        async def execute(
            self,
            context,
        ):
            return ProviderResult(
                success=True,
                provider_id="fake_research",
                output={
                    "query": context.request,
                    "results": {
                        "results": []
                    },
                },
            )

    agent = ResearchAgent(
        provider=EmptyResearchProvider()
    )

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request="A very specific research question",
    )

    result = await agent.execute(
        context
    )

    assert result.success is True

    assert isinstance(
        result.output,
        IntelligenceContext,
    )

    assert result.output.source_count == 0

    assert result.output.has_sources is False
