"""
Unit tests for CompanionService.

These tests verify the application orchestration boundary:

ExecutionContext
|
v
CompanionService
|
+--> MemoryService
|
+--> ResearchAgent
|       |
|       v
|   IntelligenceContext
|
+--> ConversationAgent
|
+--> QualityAgent
|
v
Interaction Result

No real LLM provider, OpenAI API, Parallel API,
or external infrastructure is called.
"""

from dataclasses import dataclass

import pytest

from backend.agents.agent_result import (
    AgentResult,
)

from backend.characters.character_profile import (
    CharacterProfile,
)

from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import (
    ExecutionContext,
)

from backend.services.companion_service import (
    CompanionService,
)


@dataclass
class FakeMemory:
    """
    Deterministic memory record for tests.
    """

    user_id: str
    category: str
    content: str


class FakeMemoryService:
    """
    Deterministic MemoryService test double.
    """

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.memories = [
            FakeMemory(
                user_id="demo-user",
                category="conversation",
                content="Previous conversation",
            ),
        ]

        self.recall_calls = []
        self.remember_calls = []

    def recall(
        self,
        user_id: str,
    ):
        self.recall_calls.append(
            user_id
        )

        return [
            memory
            for memory in self.memories
            if memory.user_id == user_id
        ]

    def remember(
        self,
        user_id: str,
        category: str,
        content: str,
    ) -> None:
        self.remember_calls.append(
            {
                "user_id": user_id,
                "category": category,
                "content": content,
            }
        )


class FakeResearchAgent:
    """
    Deterministic ResearchAgent test double.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        result: AgentResult,
    ) -> None:
        self.result = result
        self.received_contexts = []

    async def execute(
        self,
        context: ExecutionContext,
    ) -> AgentResult:
        self.received_contexts.append(
            context
        )

        return self.result


class FakeConversationAgent:
    """
    Deterministic ConversationAgent test double.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        result: AgentResult,
    ) -> None:
        self.result = result
        self.received_contexts = []

    async def execute(
        self,
        context: ExecutionContext,
    ) -> AgentResult:
        self.received_contexts.append(
            context
        )

        return self.result


class FakeQualityAgent:
    """
    Deterministic QualityAgent test double.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        result: AgentResult,
    ) -> None:
        self.result = result
        self.received_contexts = []

    async def execute(
        self,
        context: ExecutionContext,
    ) -> AgentResult:
        self.received_contexts.append(
            context
        )

        return self.result


def create_character() -> CharacterProfile:
    """
    Create a deterministic test character.
    """

    return CharacterProfile(
        character_id="yeti",
        name="Yeti",
        description=(
            "Friendly AI learning companion"
        ),
        personality=(
            "Curious and encouraging"
        ),
        learning_style=(
            "Conversation based learning"
        ),
    )


def create_context(
    request: str = "Explain artificial intelligence",
) -> ExecutionContext:
    """
    Create a deterministic execution context.
    """

    return ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request=request,
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_companion_service_orchestrates_conversation():
    """
    CompanionService should execute the ConversationAgent
    and QualityAgent for a normal interaction.

    Research must not execute when use_research=False.
    """

    conversation_agent = FakeConversationAgent(
        AgentResult(
            success=True,
            agent_id="conversation_agent",
            output={
                "message": "Fake conversation response",
            },
        )
    )

    research_agent = FakeResearchAgent(
        AgentResult(
            success=True,
            agent_id="research_agent",
            output="Should not execute",
        )
    )

    quality_agent = FakeQualityAgent(
        AgentResult(
            success=True,
            agent_id="quality_agent",
            output={
                "quality_score": 1.0,
                "passed": True,
            },
        )
    )

    memory_service = FakeMemoryService()

    service = CompanionService(
        character=create_character(),
        conversation_agent=conversation_agent,
        research_agent=research_agent,
        quality_agent=quality_agent,
        memory_service=memory_service,
    )

    context = create_context()

    result = await service.interact(
        context,
        use_research=False,
    )

    assert result["character"] == "Yeti"

    assert result["conversation"] == {
        "message": "Fake conversation response",
    }

    assert result["research"] is None

    assert result["quality"] == {
        "quality_score": 1.0,
        "passed": True,
    }

    assert result["memory_count"] == 1

    assert (
        len(
            conversation_agent.received_contexts
        )
        == 1
    )

    assert (
        conversation_agent.received_contexts[0]
        is context
    )

    assert (
        len(
            research_agent.received_contexts
        )
        == 0
    )

    assert (
        len(
            quality_agent.received_contexts
        )
        == 1
    )

    assert (
        quality_agent.received_contexts[0]
        is context
    )

    assert memory_service.recall_calls == [
        "demo-user",
    ]

    assert memory_service.remember_calls == [
        {
            "user_id": "demo-user",
            "category": "conversation",
            "content": (
                "Explain artificial intelligence"
            ),
        }
    ]


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_companion_service_executes_research_when_requested():
    """
    When research is requested, CompanionService should:

    1. execute ResearchAgent
    2. attach its IntelligenceContext to a new
       ExecutionContext
    3. execute ConversationAgent with the enriched context
    4. execute QualityAgent with the enriched context
    """

    intelligence_context = object()

    research_agent = FakeResearchAgent(
        AgentResult(
            success=True,
            agent_id="research_agent",
            output=intelligence_context,
        )
    )

    conversation_agent = FakeConversationAgent(
        AgentResult(
            success=True,
            agent_id="conversation_agent",
            output={
                "message": "Research-aware response",
            },
        )
    )

    quality_agent = FakeQualityAgent(
        AgentResult(
            success=True,
            agent_id="quality_agent",
            output={
                "quality_score": 0.95,
                "passed": True,
            },
        )
    )

    memory_service = FakeMemoryService()

    service = CompanionService(
        character=create_character(),
        conversation_agent=conversation_agent,
        research_agent=research_agent,
        quality_agent=quality_agent,
        memory_service=memory_service,
    )

    context = create_context(
        "What are the latest AI developments?"
    )

    result = await service.interact(
        context,
        use_research=True,
    )

    assert result["character"] == "Yeti"

    assert result["conversation"] == {
        "message": "Research-aware response",
    }

    assert (
        result["research"]
        is intelligence_context
    )

    assert result["quality"] == {
        "quality_score": 0.95,
        "passed": True,
    }

    assert (
        len(
            research_agent.received_contexts
        )
        == 1
    )

    assert (
        research_agent.received_contexts[0]
        is context
    )

    assert (
        len(
            conversation_agent.received_contexts
        )
        == 1
    )

    enriched_context = (
        conversation_agent.received_contexts[0]
    )

    assert enriched_context is not context

    assert (
        enriched_context.request
        == context.request
    )

    assert (
        enriched_context.user_id
        == context.user_id
    )

    assert (
        enriched_context.intelligence_context
        is intelligence_context
    )

    assert (
        quality_agent.received_contexts[0]
        is enriched_context
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_companion_service_does_not_mutate_original_context():
    """
    Research enrichment must create a new
    ExecutionContext rather than mutating the original.
    """

    intelligence_context = object()

    research_agent = FakeResearchAgent(
        AgentResult(
            success=True,
            agent_id="research_agent",
            output=intelligence_context,
        )
    )

    conversation_agent = FakeConversationAgent(
        AgentResult(
            success=True,
            agent_id="conversation_agent",
            output={
                "message": "Response",
            },
        )
    )

    quality_agent = FakeQualityAgent(
        AgentResult(
            success=True,
            agent_id="quality_agent",
            output={
                "quality_score": 1.0,
                "passed": True,
            },
        )
    )

    memory_service = FakeMemoryService()

    service = CompanionService(
        character=create_character(),
        conversation_agent=conversation_agent,
        research_agent=research_agent,
        quality_agent=quality_agent,
        memory_service=memory_service,
    )

    context = create_context()

    assert (
        context.intelligence_context
        is None
    )

    await service.interact(
        context,
        use_research=True,
    )

    assert (
        context.intelligence_context
        is None
    )

    enriched_context = (
        conversation_agent.received_contexts[0]
    )

    assert enriched_context is not context

    assert (
        enriched_context.intelligence_context
        is intelligence_context
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_companion_service_handles_research_failure():
    """
    If research fails, ConversationAgent should still
    execute using the original context.

    The service should not attach failed research output
    to the conversation context.
    """

    research_agent = FakeResearchAgent(
        AgentResult(
            success=False,
            agent_id="research_agent",
            output=None,
        )
    )

    conversation_agent = FakeConversationAgent(
        AgentResult(
            success=True,
            agent_id="conversation_agent",
            output={
                "message": "Fallback response",
            },
        )
    )

    quality_agent = FakeQualityAgent(
        AgentResult(
            success=True,
            agent_id="quality_agent",
            output={
                "quality_score": 0.9,
                "passed": True,
            },
        )
    )

    memory_service = FakeMemoryService()

    service = CompanionService(
        character=create_character(),
        conversation_agent=conversation_agent,
        research_agent=research_agent,
        quality_agent=quality_agent,
        memory_service=memory_service,
    )

    context = create_context()

    result = await service.interact(
        context,
        use_research=True,
    )

    assert result["research"] is None

    assert result["conversation"] == {
        "message": "Fallback response",
    }

    assert (
        conversation_agent.received_contexts[0]
        is context
    )

    assert (
        conversation_agent.received_contexts[0]
        .intelligence_context
        is None
    )


def test_companion_service_uses_injected_dependencies():
    """
    CompanionService should preserve explicitly injected
    dependencies rather than replacing them with defaults.
    """

    conversation_agent = FakeConversationAgent(
        AgentResult(
            success=True,
            agent_id="conversation_agent",
            output={},
        )
    )

    research_agent = FakeResearchAgent(
        AgentResult(
            success=True,
            agent_id="research_agent",
            output=None,
        )
    )

    quality_agent = FakeQualityAgent(
        AgentResult(
            success=True,
            agent_id="quality_agent",
            output={},
        )
    )

    memory_service = FakeMemoryService()

    service = CompanionService(
        character=create_character(),
        conversation_agent=conversation_agent,
        research_agent=research_agent,
        quality_agent=quality_agent,
        memory_service=memory_service,
    )

    assert (
        service.conversation_agent
        is conversation_agent
    )

    assert (
        service.research_agent
        is research_agent
    )

    assert (
        service.quality_agent
        is quality_agent
    )

    assert (
        service.memory_service
        is memory_service
    )

===== backend/providers/base_provider.py =====
"""
Base Provider Contract.

Defines the interface for external AI and knowledge providers.

Examples:
- Gemini
- Parallel Search
- Future LLM providers

The Runtime communicates with providers through this contract.

Providers are replaceable implementations.
The Agent layer must remain independent from provider details.
"""

from abc import ABC, abstractmethod

from backend.providers.provider_result import ProviderResult
from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import ExecutionContext


class BaseProvider(ABC):
    """
    Abstract base class for EasyChatBot providers.

    Providers are responsible for communicating
    with external services.

    They are NOT responsible for:
    - agent decisions
    - workflow orchestration
    - memory management
    - user interaction
    """

    provider_id: str

    def __init__(
        self,
        provider_id: str,
    ):
        """
        Initialise provider.

        Args:
            provider_id:
                Unique provider identifier.
        """

        self.provider_id = provider_id

    @abstractmethod
    async def execute(
        self,
        context: ExecutionContext,
    ) -> ProviderResult:
        """
        Execute provider operation.

        Args:
            context:
                Runtime execution context.

        Returns:
            ProviderResult containing provider output.
        """

        pass
===== backend/providers/provider_result.py =====
"""
Provider Result Contract.

Defines the standard response returned
by external providers.

All providers return this structure so
the Runtime can process results consistently.
"""

from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ProviderResult:
    """
    Immutable provider execution result.

    Attributes:

        success:
            Indicates whether provider execution succeeded.

        provider_id:
            Identifier of the provider.

        output:
            Provider response payload.

        error:
            Error details if execution failed.
    """

    success: bool
    provider_id: str
    output: Any = None
    error: str | None = None
===== backend/providers/parallel/parallel_provider.py =====
"""
Parallel Search Provider.

Provides live world information
through the Parallel Search API.
"""

from backend.providers.base_provider import (
    BaseProvider,
)

from backend.providers.provider_result import (
    ProviderResult,
)

from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import (
    ExecutionContext,
)


class ParallelSearchProvider(BaseProvider):
    """
    Provider implementation using Parallel Search.

    The SDK client is created only when required.
    This allows tests to run without API credentials.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        client=None,
    ):
        super().__init__(
            provider_id="parallel_search"
        )

        self.client = client


    def _get_client(self):
        """
        Lazy-load the real Parallel client.

        Avoids requiring API keys during unit tests.
        """

        if self.client is None:

            from backend.providers.parallel.parallel_client import (
                ParallelClientWrapper,
            )

            self.client = ParallelClientWrapper()

        return self.client


    async def execute(
        self,
        context: ExecutionContext,
    ) -> ProviderResult:
        """
        Execute a live Parallel search.
        """

        try:

            client = self._get_client()

            result = await client.search(
                query=context.request
            )

            return ProviderResult(
                success=True,
                provider_id=self.provider_id,
                output=result,
            )

        except Exception as exc:

            return ProviderResult(
                success=False,
                provider_id=self.provider_id,
                output=None,
                error=str(exc),
            )
===== backend/agents/research_agent.py =====
"""
Research Agent.

Retrieves external information through approved
knowledge providers and converts the result into
provider-independent IntelligenceContext data.

Architecture:

Research Agent
        |
        v
ParallelSearchProvider
        |
        v
ParallelClientWrapper
        |
        v
IntelligenceContext
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from backend.agents.agent_manifest import AgentManifest
from backend.agents.agent_result import AgentResult
from backend.agents.base_agent import BaseAgent

from backend.intelligence.intelligence_context import (
    IntelligenceContext,
    IntelligenceSource,
)

from backend.providers.parallel.parallel_provider import (
    ParallelSearchProvider,
)

from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import (
    ExecutionContext,
)


class ResearchAgent(BaseAgent):
    """
    Agent responsible for world information retrieval.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        provider=None,
    ):
        super().__init__(
            AgentManifest(
                agent_id="research_agent",
                name="Research Agent",
                description=(
                    "Retrieves live world information "
                    "for AI learning experiences."
                ),
            )
        )

        self.provider = (
            provider
            or ParallelSearchProvider()
        )

    async def execute(
        self,
        context: ExecutionContext,
    ) -> AgentResult:
        """
        Execute research and produce a normalized
        IntelligenceContext.
        """

        result = await self.provider.execute(
            context
        )

        if not result.success:
            return AgentResult(
                success=False,
                agent_id=self.manifest.agent_id,
                output=None,
                error=result.error,
            )

        intelligence = (
            self._build_intelligence_context(
                query=context.request,
                provider_output=result.output,
            )
        )

        return AgentResult(
            success=True,
            agent_id=self.manifest.agent_id,
            output=intelligence,
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _build_intelligence_context(
        query: str,
        provider_output,
    ) -> IntelligenceContext:
        """
        Convert provider output into the
        provider-independent IntelligenceContext.
        """

        if not isinstance(
            provider_output,
            dict,
        ):
            raise TypeError(
                "Research provider output must be a dictionary."
            )

        raw_results = provider_output.get(
            "results",
            {},
        )

        if isinstance(
            raw_results,
            dict,
        ):
            raw_results = raw_results.get(
                "results",
                [],
            )

        if not isinstance(
            raw_results,
            list,
        ):
            raw_results = []

        sources: list[IntelligenceSource] = []

        for raw_result in raw_results:

            if not isinstance(
                raw_result,
                dict,
            ):
                continue

            title = str(
                raw_result.get(
                    "title",
                    "",
                )
            ).strip()

            url = str(
                raw_result.get(
                    "url",
                    "",
                )
            ).strip()

            if not title or not url:
                continue

            content = ResearchAgent._extract_content(
                raw_result
            )

            sources.append(
                IntelligenceSource(
                    title=title,
                    url=url,
                    source="parallel",
                    content=content,
                )
            )

        return IntelligenceContext(
            query=query,
            sources=tuple(sources),
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _extract_content(
        raw_result: dict,
    ) -> str:
        """
        Extract available content from a normalized
        Parallel result.
        """

        excerpts = raw_result.get(
            "excerpts",
        )

        if excerpts is None:
            excerpts = raw_result.get(
                "snippet",
                raw_result.get(
                    "content",
                    "",
                ),
            )

        if isinstance(
            excerpts,
            list,
        ):
            return "\n\n".join(
                str(item).strip()
                for item in excerpts
                if str(item).strip()
            )

        return str(
            excerpts or ""
        ).strip()

===== backend/agents/discovery/discovery_agent.py =====

"""
Discovery Agent.

Owns the complete Beta 1 personalised discovery workflow.

The Discovery Agent orchestrates nine bounded Discovery Skills:

1. Preference Understanding
2. Discovery Query Generation
3. Content/Product Discovery
4. Relevance Matching
5. Recommendation Ranking
6. Recommendation Explanation
7. Personalised Content Adaptation
8. Discovery Quality Verification
9. User Choice & Controlled Handoff

The Discovery Agent:

- receives Runtime ExecutionContext
- obtains approved preferences through PreferenceSource
- executes Skills explicitly and sequentially
- passes provider-independent outputs between Skills
- preserves the Runtime boundary
- stops at the explicit learner-choice boundary
- creates a controlled handoff only after explicit selection
- returns the standard AgentResult contract

The Discovery Agent does not:

- call provider SDKs directly
- bypass the Runtime
- persist memory
- modify learner preferences
- perform uncontrolled external actions
- purchase products
- automatically select recommendations

Preference architecture:

ExecutionContext
|
v
PreferenceSource
|
v
PreferenceContext
|
v
PreferenceUnderstandingSkill
|
v
DiscoveryProfile
|
v
QueryGenerationSkill
|
v
DiscoveryQuery[]
|
v
ContentProductDiscoverySkill
|
v
DiscoveryCandidate[]
|
v
RelevanceMatchingSkill
|
v
RelevanceMatch[]
|
v
RecommendationRankingSkill
|
v
RankedRecommendation[]
|
v
RecommendationExplanationSkill
|
v
RecommendationExplanation[]
|
v
PersonalisedContentAdaptationSkill
|
v
PersonalisedRecommendation[]
|
v
DiscoveryQualityVerificationSkill
|
v
VerifiedRecommendation[]
|
v
UserChoiceControlledHandoffSkill
|
+--> awaiting_user_choice
|
v
Explicit learner selection
|
v
Controlled handoff
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from backend.agents.agent_manifest import AgentManifest
from backend.agents.agent_result import AgentResult
from backend.agents.base_agent import BaseAgent
from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import ExecutionContext

from backend.skills.discovery.hackathon_preference_source import (
    HackathonPreferenceSource,
)
from backend.skills.discovery.preference_source import (
    PreferenceContext,
    PreferenceSource,
)
from backend.skills.discovery.preference_understanding import (
    DiscoveryProfile,
    PreferenceUnderstandingSkill,
)
from backend.skills.discovery.query_generation import (
    DiscoveryQueryGenerationSkill,
)
from backend.skills.discovery.content_product_discovery import (
    ContentProductDiscoverySkill,
)
from backend.skills.discovery.relevance_matching import (
    RelevanceMatchingSkill,
)
from backend.skills.discovery.recommendation_ranking import (
    RecommendationRankingSkill,
)
from backend.skills.discovery.recommendation_explanation import (
    RecommendationExplanationSkill,
)
from backend.skills.discovery.personalised_content_adaptation import (
    PersonalisedContentAdaptationSkill,
)
from backend.skills.discovery.discovery_quality_verification import (
    DiscoveryQualityVerificationSkill,
)
from backend.skills.discovery.user_choice_handoff import (
    UserChoiceControlledHandoffSkill,
)
from backend.skills.skill import SkillInput


class DiscoveryAgent(BaseAgent):
    """
    Beta 1 Discovery Agent.

    Owns orchestration of the nine Discovery Skills.

    Preferences remain behind PreferenceSource.

    External discovery remains behind
    ContentProductDiscoverySkill and its DiscoveryGateway.

    The Agent itself never calls an external provider SDK.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        preference_skill: (
            PreferenceUnderstandingSkill | None
        ) = None,
        preference_source: (
            PreferenceSource | None
        ) = None,
        query_generation_skill: (
            DiscoveryQueryGenerationSkill | None
        ) = None,
        discovery_skill: (
            ContentProductDiscoverySkill | None
        ) = None,
        relevance_matching_skill: (
            RelevanceMatchingSkill | None
        ) = None,
        recommendation_ranking_skill: (
            RecommendationRankingSkill | None
        ) = None,
        recommendation_explanation_skill: (
            RecommendationExplanationSkill | None
        ) = None,
        personalised_content_adaptation_skill: (
            PersonalisedContentAdaptationSkill | None
        ) = None,
        quality_verification_skill: (
            DiscoveryQualityVerificationSkill | None
        ) = None,
        user_choice_handoff_skill: (
            UserChoiceControlledHandoffSkill | None
        ) = None,
    ) -> None:
        """
        Initialise the Discovery Agent.

        All Skills are injectable for deterministic testing and
        controlled Runtime composition.

        ContentProductDiscoverySkill remains optional for backward
        compatibility with the existing preference-only workflow.

        When it is not supplied, the Agent stops after producing
        the DiscoveryProfile.

        When it is supplied, the complete Beta 1 discovery pipeline
        is executed.
        """

        super().__init__(
            AgentManifest(
                agent_id="discovery_agent",
                name="Discovery Agent",
                description=(
                    "Discovers and personalises podcasts, "
                    "news, products and related content "
                    "according to approved learner "
                    "preferences."
                ),
                version="1.0.0",
            )
        )

        self.preference_skill = (
            preference_skill
            if preference_skill is not None
            else PreferenceUnderstandingSkill()
        )

        self.preference_source = (
            preference_source
            if preference_source is not None
            else HackathonPreferenceSource()
        )

        self.query_generation_skill = (
            query_generation_skill
            if query_generation_skill is not None
            else DiscoveryQueryGenerationSkill()
        )

        self.discovery_skill = (
            discovery_skill
            if discovery_skill is not None
            else None
        )

        self.relevance_matching_skill = (
            relevance_matching_skill
            if relevance_matching_skill is not None
            else RelevanceMatchingSkill()
        )

        self.recommendation_ranking_skill = (
            recommendation_ranking_skill
            if recommendation_ranking_skill is not None
            else RecommendationRankingSkill()
        )

        self.recommendation_explanation_skill = (
            recommendation_explanation_skill
            if recommendation_explanation_skill is not None
            else RecommendationExplanationSkill()
        )

        self.personalised_content_adaptation_skill = (
            personalised_content_adaptation_skill
            if personalised_content_adaptation_skill is not None
            else PersonalisedContentAdaptationSkill()
        )

        self.quality_verification_skill = (
            quality_verification_skill
            if quality_verification_skill is not None
            else DiscoveryQualityVerificationSkill()
        )

        self.user_choice_handoff_skill = (
            user_choice_handoff_skill
            if user_choice_handoff_skill is not None
            else UserChoiceControlledHandoffSkill()
        )

    async def execute(
        self,
        context: ExecutionContext,
    ) -> AgentResult:
        """
        Execute the Discovery workflow.

        Backward-compatible mode:

            PreferenceSource
                â†“
            PreferenceUnderstandingSkill
                â†“
            DiscoveryProfile

        Full Beta 1 mode:

            Preference Understanding
                â†“
            Query Generation
                â†“
            Content/Product Discovery
                â†“
            Relevance Matching
                â†“
            Recommendation Ranking
                â†“
            Recommendation Explanation
                â†“
            Personalised Content Adaptation
                â†“
            Quality Verification
                â†“
            User Choice / Controlled Handoff

        The full pipeline is activated when a
        ContentProductDiscoverySkill is injected.

        No recommendation is automatically selected.
        """

        validation_error = self._validate_context(
            context
        )

        if validation_error is not None:
            return validation_error

        request = context.request.strip()

        # ---------------------------------------------------------
        # Step 1 â€” Obtain approved learner preferences
        # ---------------------------------------------------------

        try:
            preference_context = (
                await self.preference_source.get_preferences(
                    context.user_id
                )
            )

        except (ValueError, TypeError) as exc:
            return self._failure(
                "Preference source failed: "
                f"{exc}"
            )

        if not isinstance(
            preference_context,
            PreferenceContext,
        ):
            return self._failure(
                "Preference source returned an invalid "
                "PreferenceContext."
            )

        # ---------------------------------------------------------
        # Step 2 â€” Preference Understanding
        # ---------------------------------------------------------

        preference_result = (
            await self.preference_skill.execute(
                SkillInput(
                    request=request,
                    user_id=context.user_id,
                    memory_context=None,
                    metadata={
                        "execution_id": context.execution_id,
                        "preference_context": (
                            preference_context
                        ),
                    },
                )
            )
        )

        if not preference_result.success:
            return self._skill_failure(
                preference_result
            )

        profile = preference_result.output

        if not isinstance(
            profile,
            DiscoveryProfile,
        ):
            return self._failure(
                "Preference Understanding returned an "
                "invalid DiscoveryProfile."
            )

        # ---------------------------------------------------------
        # Backward compatibility
        #
        # Existing Discovery Agent consumers and tests use the
        # Agent with only PreferenceSource injected.
        #
        # Do not force external discovery infrastructure onto
        # those callers.
        # ---------------------------------------------------------

        if self.discovery_skill is None:
            return AgentResult(
                success=True,
                agent_id=self.manifest.agent_id,
                output={
                    "discovery_profile": (
                        profile.as_dict()
                    ),
                    "preference_context": (
                        self._preference_context_dict(
                            preference_context
                        )
                    ),
                    "skills_executed": [
                        preference_result.skill_id,
                    ],
                    "status": "profile_ready",
                },
            )

        # ---------------------------------------------------------
        # Step 3 â€” Discovery Query Generation
        # ---------------------------------------------------------

        query_result = (
            await self.query_generation_skill.execute(
                SkillInput(
                    request=request,
                    user_id=context.user_id,
                    metadata={
                        "execution_id": context.execution_id,
                        "discovery_profile": profile,
                    },
                )
            )
        )

        if not query_result.success:
            return self._skill_failure(
                query_result
            )

        discovery_queries = query_result.output

        # ---------------------------------------------------------
        # Step 4 â€” Content/Product Discovery
        #
        # External discovery remains behind the injected
        # DiscoveryGateway-backed Skill.
        # ---------------------------------------------------------

        discovery_result = (
            await self.discovery_skill.execute(
                SkillInput(
                    request=request,
                    user_id=context.user_id,
                    metadata={
                        "execution_id": context.execution_id,
                        "discovery_queries": (
                            discovery_queries
                        ),
                    },
                )
            )
        )

        if not discovery_result.success:
            return self._skill_failure(
                discovery_result
            )

        candidates = discovery_result.output

        # ---------------------------------------------------------
        # Step 5 â€” Relevance Matching
        # ---------------------------------------------------------

        relevance_result = (
            await self.relevance_matching_skill.execute(
                SkillInput(
                    request=request,
                    user_id=context.user_id,
                    metadata={
                        "execution_id": context.execution_id,
                        "discovery_profile": profile,
                        "discovery_candidates": candidates,
                    },
                )
            )
        )

        if not relevance_result.success:
            return self._skill_failure(
                relevance_result
            )

        relevance_matches = relevance_result.output

        # ---------------------------------------------------------
        # Step 6 â€” Recommendation Ranking
        # ---------------------------------------------------------

        ranking_result = (
            await self.recommendation_ranking_skill.execute(
                SkillInput(
                    request=request,
                    user_id=context.user_id,
                    metadata={
                        "execution_id": context.execution_id,
                        "relevance_matches": (
                            relevance_matches
                        ),
                    },
                )
            )
        )

        if not ranking_result.success:
            return self._skill_failure(
                ranking_result
            )

        ranked_recommendations = (
            ranking_result.output
        )

        # ---------------------------------------------------------
        # Step 7 â€” Recommendation Explanation
        # ---------------------------------------------------------

        explanation_result = (
            await self.recommendation_explanation_skill.execute(
                SkillInput(
                    request=request,
                    user_id=context.user_id,
                    metadata={
                        "execution_id": context.execution_id,
                        "ranked_recommendations": (
                            ranked_recommendations
                        ),
                    },
                )
            )
        )

        if not explanation_result.success:
            return self._skill_failure(
                explanation_result
            )

        recommendation_explanations = (
            explanation_result.output
        )

        # ---------------------------------------------------------
        # Step 8 â€” Personalised Content Adaptation
        # ---------------------------------------------------------

        adaptation_result = (
            await self.personalised_content_adaptation_skill.execute(
                SkillInput(
                    request=request,
                    user_id=context.user_id,
                    metadata={
                        "execution_id": context.execution_id,
                        "discovery_profile": profile,
                        "recommendation_explanations": (
                            recommendation_explanations
                        ),
                    },
                )
            )
        )

        if not adaptation_result.success:
            return self._skill_failure(
                adaptation_result
            )

        personalised_recommendations = (
            adaptation_result.output
        )

        # ---------------------------------------------------------
        # Step 9 â€” Discovery Quality Verification
        # ---------------------------------------------------------

        quality_result = (
            await self.quality_verification_skill.execute(
                SkillInput(
                    request=request,
                    user_id=context.user_id,
                    metadata={
                        "execution_id": context.execution_id,
                        "personalised_recommendations": (
                            personalised_recommendations
                        ),
                    },
                )
            )
        )

        if not quality_result.success:
            return self._skill_failure(
                quality_result
            )

        quality_output = quality_result.output

        if not isinstance(
            quality_output,
            dict,
        ):
            return self._failure(
                "Discovery Quality Verification returned "
                "an invalid output."
            )

        verified_recommendations = (
            quality_output.get(
                "verified",
                [],
            )
        )

        # ---------------------------------------------------------
        # Step 10 â€” User Choice & Controlled Handoff
        # ---------------------------------------------------------

        selected_option_id = (
            context.metadata.get(
                "selected_option_id"
            )
        )

        handoff_result = (
            await self.user_choice_handoff_skill.execute(
                SkillInput(
                    request=request,
                    user_id=context.user_id,
                    metadata={
                        "execution_id": context.execution_id,
                        "verified_recommendations": (
                            verified_recommendations
                        ),
                        "selected_option_id": (
                            selected_option_id
                        ),
                    },
                )
            )
        )

        if not handoff_result.success:
            return self._skill_failure(
                handoff_result
            )

        handoff_output = handoff_result.output

        if not isinstance(
            handoff_output,
            dict,
        ):
            return self._failure(
                "User Choice Controlled Handoff returned "
                "an invalid output."
            )

        # ---------------------------------------------------------
        # Final AgentResult
        # ---------------------------------------------------------

        return AgentResult(
            success=True,
            agent_id=self.manifest.agent_id,
            output={
                "status": handoff_output.get(
                    "status"
                ),
                "discovery_profile": (
                    profile.as_dict()
                ),
                "preference_context": (
                    self._preference_context_dict(
                        preference_context
                    )
                ),
                "discovery_queries": (
                    self._serialise_items(
                        discovery_queries
                    )
                ),
                "candidates": (
                    self._serialise_items(
                        candidates
                    )
                ),
                "relevance_matches": (
                    self._serialise_items(
                        relevance_matches
                    )
                ),
                "ranked_recommendations": (
                    self._serialise_items(
                        ranked_recommendations
                    )
                ),
                "recommendation_explanations": (
                    self._serialise_items(
                        recommendation_explanations
                    )
                ),
                "personalised_recommendations": (
                    self._serialise_items(
                        personalised_recommendations
                    )
                ),
                "quality": (
                    self._serialise_value(
                        quality_output
                    )
                ),
                "handoff": (
                    self._serialise_value(
                        handoff_output
                    )
                    if handoff_output.get("status")
                    == "handoff_approved"
                    else None
                ),
                "user_choice": (
                    self._serialise_value(
                        handoff_output
                    )
                ),
                "skills_executed": [
                    preference_result.skill_id,
                    query_result.skill_id,
                    discovery_result.skill_id,
                    relevance_result.skill_id,
                    ranking_result.skill_id,
                    explanation_result.skill_id,
                    adaptation_result.skill_id,
                    quality_result.skill_id,
                    handoff_result.skill_id,
                ],
            },
        )

    def _validate_context(
        self,
        context: ExecutionContext | None,
    ) -> AgentResult | None:
        """
        Validate the Runtime execution context.
        """

        if context is None:
            return self._failure(
                "Execution context cannot be None."
            )

        if not isinstance(
            context.request,
            str,
        ):
            return self._failure(
                "Execution context request must be a string."
            )

        if not context.request.strip():
            return self._failure(
                "Execution context request cannot be empty."
            )

        return None

    def _failure(
        self,
        error: str,
        *,
        output: Any = None,
    ) -> AgentResult:
        """
        Create a standard failed AgentResult.
        """

        return AgentResult(
            success=False,
            agent_id=self.manifest.agent_id,
            output=output,
            error=error,
        )

    def _skill_failure(
        self,
        skill_result: Any,
    ) -> AgentResult:
        """
        Convert a failed SkillResult into AgentResult.
        """

        return self._failure(
            (
                f"Skill '{skill_result.skill_id}' failed: "
                f"{skill_result.error}"
            ),
            output={
                "skill_id": skill_result.skill_id,
                "error": skill_result.error,
            },
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _preference_context_dict(
        preference_context: PreferenceContext,
    ) -> dict[str, object]:
        """
        Convert PreferenceContext into a serialisable payload.
        """

        return {
            "topics": list(
                preference_context.topics
            ),
            "content_types": list(
                preference_context.content_types
            ),
            "languages": list(
                preference_context.languages
            ),
            "learning_level": (
                preference_context.learning_level
            ),
            "source": preference_context.source,
        }

    @staticmethod
    def _serialise_value(
        value: Any,
    ) -> Any:
        """
        Convert Skill output objects into transport-safe data.

        Objects exposing as_dict() use that contract.

        Lists, tuples and dictionaries are recursively serialised.
        """

        if hasattr(
            value,
            "as_dict",
        ):
            return value.as_dict()

        if isinstance(
            value,
            (list, tuple),
        ):
            return [
                DiscoveryAgent._serialise_value(
                    item
                )
                for item in value
            ]

        if isinstance(
            value,
            dict,
        ):
            return {
                key: DiscoveryAgent._serialise_value(
                    item
                )
                for key, item in value.items()
            }

        return value

    @staticmethod
    def _serialise_items(
        values: Any,
    ) -> list[Any]:
        """
        Serialise a sequence of Skill outputs.
        """

        if not isinstance(
            values,
            (list, tuple),
        ):
            return []

        return [
            DiscoveryAgent._serialise_value(
                value
            )
            for value in values
        ]


===== backend/skills/discovery/user_choice_handoff.py =====

"""
Discovery User Choice & Controlled Handoff Skill.

Presents verified recommendations and converts an explicit
learner choice into a controlled handoff request.

The Skill establishes the human decision boundary.

It does not:

- perform discovery
- call external providers
- call LLMs
- automatically select a recommendation for the learner
- execute external actions
- persist memory
- modify learner preferences
- bypass Runtime controls

Architecture:

Verified Recommendations
        |
        v
User Choice & Controlled Handoff
        |
        +--> Awaiting learner choice
        |
        v
Explicit learner selection
        |
        v
Controlled Handoff
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass

from backend.skills.discovery.personalised_content_adaptation import (
    PersonalisedRecommendation,
)
from backend.skills.discovery.discovery_quality_verification import (
    VerifiedRecommendation,
)
from backend.skills.skill import (
    Skill,
    SkillInput,
)
from backend.skills.skill_result import (
    SkillResult,
)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class HandoffOption:
    """
    Recommendation option presented to the learner.
    """

    option_id: str
    rank: int
    recommendation: PersonalisedRecommendation

    def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, object]:
        """Return a serialisable representation."""

        return {
            "option_id": self.option_id,
            "rank": self.rank,
            "recommendation": (
                self.recommendation.as_dict()
            ),
        }


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ControlledHandoff:
    """
    Explicit learner-approved handoff.

    A handoff is created only after the learner provides
    a valid option identifier.
    """

    option_id: str
    rank: int
    recommendation: PersonalisedRecommendation

    def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, object]:
        """Return a serialisable representation."""

        return {
            "option_id": self.option_id,
            "rank": self.rank,
            "recommendation": (
                self.recommendation.as_dict()
            ),
        }


class UserChoiceControlledHandoffSkill(Skill):
    """
    Present verified recommendations and process explicit
    learner selection.

    Expected metadata:

        {
            "verified_recommendations": [
                VerifiedRecommendation(...),
                ...
            ],

            "selected_option_id": "recommendation-1"
        }

    If selected_option_id is absent, the Skill returns the
    available options and an awaiting-choice state.

    The Skill never automatically selects an option.
    """

    skill_id = "discovery.user_choice_handoff"
    name = "User Choice and Controlled Handoff"
    version = "1.0.0"

    @staticmethod
    def _option_id(rank: int) -> str:
        """Create a stable option identifier."""

        return f"recommendation-{rank}"

    async def execute(
        self,
        skill_input: SkillInput,
    ) -> SkillResult:
        """
        Present verified recommendations or process an
        explicit learner selection.
        """

        if skill_input is None:
            return SkillResult.failed(
                skill_id=self.skill_id,
                error="Skill input cannot be None.",
            )

        raw_recommendations = (
            skill_input.metadata.get(
                "verified_recommendations"
            )
        )

        if raw_recommendations is None:
            return SkillResult.failed(
                skill_id=self.skill_id,
                error=(
                    "verified_recommendations are required."
                ),
            )

        if not isinstance(
            raw_recommendations,
            (list, tuple),
        ):
            return SkillResult.failed(
                skill_id=self.skill_id,
                error=(
                    "verified_recommendations must be "
                    "a list or tuple."
                ),
            )

        options: list[HandoffOption] = []

        for item in raw_recommendations:
            if not isinstance(
                item,
                VerifiedRecommendation,
            ):
                continue

            recommendation = item.recommendation

            options.append(
                HandoffOption(
                    option_id=self._option_id(
                        recommendation
                        .recommendation
                        .recommendation
                        .rank
                    ),
                    rank=(
                        recommendation
                        .recommendation
                        .recommendation
                        .rank
                    ),
                    recommendation=recommendation,
                )
            )

        selected_option_id = (
            skill_input.metadata.get(
                "selected_option_id"
            )
        )

        # -------------------------------------------------
        # Human decision boundary
        #
        # No selection means the learner must choose.
        # -------------------------------------------------

        if selected_option_id is None:
            return SkillResult.succeeded(
                skill_id=self.skill_id,
                output={
                    "status": "awaiting_user_choice",
                    "options": options,
                    "handoff": None,
                },
            )

        if not isinstance(
            selected_option_id,
            str,
        ):
            return SkillResult.failed(
                skill_id=self.skill_id,
                error=(
                    "selected_option_id must be a string."
                ),
            )

        selected_option_id = (
            selected_option_id.strip()
        )

        if not selected_option_id:
            return SkillResult.failed(
                skill_id=self.skill_id,
                error=(
                    "selected_option_id cannot be empty."
                ),
            )

        selected = next(
            (
                option
                for option in options
                if option.option_id
                == selected_option_id
            ),
            None,
        )

        if selected is None:
            return SkillResult.failed(
                skill_id=self.skill_id,
                error=(
                    "Selected recommendation does not "
                    "exist in the verified options."
                ),
            )

        handoff = ControlledHandoff(
            option_id=selected.option_id,
            rank=selected.rank,
            recommendation=selected.recommendation,
        )

        return SkillResult.succeeded(
            skill_id=self.skill_id,
            output={
                "status": "handoff_approved",
                "options": options,
                "handoff": handoff,
            },
        )

==================== RESULT ====================
EasyChatBot Runtime Architecture

1. Purpose

This document defines the current architecture and responsibility boundaries of theEasyChatBot Runtime.

The Runtime is the provider-independent execution layer between an externalapplication/integration boundary and the EasyChatBot Agent framework.

The Runtime must remain independently usable by different application andhackathon workstreams.

2. Architectural Boundary

Current validated flow:

External Application / Integration
              |
              v
     ExternalRuntimeService
              |
              v
        RuntimeEngine
              |
              v
           Agent
              |
              v
           Skills
              |
              v
     Provider Abstraction
              |
              v
       Provider Adapter
              |
              v
       External Provider

The external integration boundary is implemented by:

backend/services/external_runtime_service.py

The Runtime execution engine is implemented by:

backend/runtime/runtime_engine.py

The execution context is defined by:

backend/runtime/context/execution_context.py

3. Validated Runtime Components

Runtime Engine

RuntimeEngine is responsible for controlled execution of an Agent using anExecutionContext.

It returns the standard AgentResult contract.

The Runtime does not own application-specific user experience logic.

Execution Context

ExecutionContext carries the explicit information required for an execution.

Agents receive the context rather than obtaining execution state throughuncontrolled global access.

Agent Contract

Agents implement the standard Agent execution contract and return AgentResult.

The Runtime invokes Agents rather than allowing an external application todirectly orchestrate individual Agent internals.

Skill Contract

Skills are bounded capabilities executed by Agents.

Skills receive explicit SkillInput and return SkillResult.

Skills do not become autonomous execution runtimes.

Provider Contract

Providers implement the provider abstraction.

The Runtime and Agent architecture should depend on provider contracts ratherthan provider SDK implementations.

4. External Runtime Service

The existing external boundary is:

backend/services/external_runtime_service.py

Its architectural role is to provide a controlled application-facing entry pointinto the Runtime.

The validated test coverage includes:

tests/application/test_external_runtime_composition.py
tests/application/test_external_runtime_service.py

Current validation:

test_external_runtime_composition.py  -> 2 passed
test_external_runtime_service.py      -> 3 passed

Therefore the external composition boundary is already an implemented andtested capability. A second Runtime/Application architecture should not becreated for this purpose.

5. Agent Layer

Agents remain responsible for bounded domain execution.

Examples currently present include:

backend/agents/base_agent.py
backend/agents/discovery/discovery_agent.py
backend/agents/research_agent.py
backend/agents/conversation_agent.py
backend/agents/quality_agent.py

The Runtime owns execution orchestration.

Agents own their bounded domain workflow.

This separation must be preserved.

6. Provider Independence

The intended dependency direction is:

Runtime
   |
   v
Agent
   |
   v
Provider Contract
   |
   v
Provider Implementation
   |
   v
External Provider

The Runtime must not directly import or call provider SDKs.

The Parallel integration belongs behind the provider/gateway boundaries and mustnot become Runtime orchestration logic.

7. Current Provider Independence Check

The repository search was performed against:

backend/runtime/**/*.py

The search found one occurrence:

backend/runtime/models/model_id.py
line 19
gemini-2.5-flash

This occurrence is a model identifier in the Runtime model-ID/configuration area.It is not, by itself, evidence that the Runtime imports or calls the Gemini SDK.

Therefore it should NOT be removed merely to satisfy a textual provider search.

The architectural rule is:

Runtime may represent approved model identifiers/configuration.

Runtime must not directly depend on provider SDK implementations.

A future implementation review should distinguish:

model identifiers/configuration;

provider SDK imports;

provider execution calls.

Only the latter two represent direct provider coupling.

8. Parallel Challenge Workstream Boundary

The Parallel challenge workstream and the EasyChatBot Runtime workstream areseparate but compatible.

Runtime Platform Workstream

Owns:

RuntimeEngine
ExecutionContext
Agent contracts
Skill contracts
Provider contracts
Runtime governance
Runtime testing

Parallel / Hackathon Workstream

Owns the challenge-specific integration and demonstration experience.

The workstreams connect through stable application/runtime contracts rather thanby embedding challenge-specific provider logic into the Runtime.

Target relationship:

Parallel / Hackathon Application
              |
              v
     ExternalRuntimeService
              |
              v
       EasyChatBot Runtime

The two workstreams may therefore progress independently.

9. Google Cloud / Gemini Boundary

Google Cloud and Gemini integrations must not be embedded directly into thecore Runtime merely to satisfy an external execution requirement.

The preferred integration boundary is:

Google Cloud / Gemini Integration
              |
              v
     External Runtime Boundary
              |
              v
        EasyChatBot Runtime

This preserves provider independence and allows the Runtime to remain reusable.

Any future Gemini-specific adapter must be reviewed separately for:

authentication;

configuration;

dependency direction;

error handling;

observability;

security;

testability;

provider replacement.

10. Security and Governance Principles

The Runtime architecture must preserve:

explicit execution boundaries;

least-privilege provider access;

controlled external side effects;

explicit Agent and Skill contracts;

no uncontrolled Agent actions;

provider abstraction;

auditable execution;

configuration-driven behaviour where appropriate.

Agents and Skills must not bypass the Runtime to perform uncontrolled productionchanges.

11. Testing Strategy

Runtime changes should follow:

Unit Tests
    |
    v
Contract Tests
    |
    v
Integration Tests
    |
    v
External Composition Tests
    |
    v
Smoke / Runtime Validation

The existing external composition and external runtime service tests are part ofthe Runtime boundary validation.

No new duplicate Runtime composition layer should be introduced while theseexisting boundaries remain valid.

12. Architecture Freeze Decision

For the current Beta/Hackathon integration phase:

Keep

ExternalRuntimeService
RuntimeEngine
ExecutionContext
AgentResult
BaseAgent
Skill
SkillResult
BaseProvider
Parallel Provider/Gateway boundaries

Do not introduce

A second Runtime engine
A second external application/runtime boundary
Direct Parallel SDK usage inside Runtime
Direct Gemini SDK usage inside Runtime
Direct provider SDK usage inside Agents

Integration principle

External Integration
        |
        v
ExternalRuntimeService
        |
        v
RuntimeEngine
        |
        v
Agents
        |
        v
Provider Contracts
        |
        v
Provider Implementations

This is the architectural baseline for subsequent work.

13. Definition of Done for Runtime Architecture Freeze

The Runtime architecture is considered frozen when:

the existing external composition tests pass;

the external runtime service tests pass;

Runtime-to-Agent execution remains contract-based;

Runtime does not directly call provider SDKs;

Parallel remains behind its provider/gateway boundary;

Google/Gemini integration has a defined external integration boundary;

the two development workstreams can evolve independently;

architectural changes are documented before implementation.
"""
EasyChatBot Runtime Engine.

The Runtime is responsible for orchestrating
agent execution.

Responsibilities:
- receive execution requests
- provide execution context
- call approved agents
- return structured results

The Runtime does not:
- implement business logic
- call providers directly
- manage user interfaces
"""

from backend.agents.base_agent import BaseAgent
from backend.agents.agent_result import AgentResult
from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import ExecutionContext


class RuntimeEngine:
    """
    Central execution orchestrator.

    Agents execute through the Runtime.
    """

    async def execute(
        self,
        agent: BaseAgent,
        context: ExecutionContext,
    ) -> AgentResult:
        """
        Execute an agent using runtime control.

        Args:
            agent:
                Agent implementation.

            context:
                Runtime execution context.

        Returns:
            AgentResult.
        """

        return await agent.execute(context)

"""
Acceptance tests for the Windows-safe judge-facing EasyChatBot demo.

The judge demo now includes the Payment Pass contract. The commerce
assertions therefore validate the current presentation contract:

GET DEAL
    ->
Payment Pass
    ->
READY / DEMO
    ->
No payment executed
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path


REPOSITORY_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]

DEMO_PATH = (
    REPOSITORY_ROOT
    / "examples"
    / "hackathon_judge_demo.py"
)


def run_judge_demo() -> subprocess.CompletedProcess[str]:
    """Execute the real judge-facing demonstration."""

    return subprocess.run(
        [
            sys.executable,
            str(DEMO_PATH),
        ],
        cwd=REPOSITORY_ROOT,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
        check=False,
    )


def test_judge_demo_succeeds():

    result = run_judge_demo()

    assert result.returncode == 0, (
        "Judge demo failed.\n\n"
        f"STDOUT:\n{result.stdout}\n\n"
        f"STDERR:\n{result.stderr}"
    )

    output = result.stdout

    assert (
        "EasyChatBot - JUDGE DEMONSTRATION"
        in output
    )

    assert (
        "Discover -> Understand -> Personalise -> "
        "Recommend -> Act"
        in output
    )

    assert "JUDGE DEMO: SUCCESS" in output


def test_judge_demo_contains_all_capabilities():

    result = run_judge_demo()

    assert result.returncode == 0

    output = result.stdout

    assert "1 - GOVERNANCE" in output
    assert "2 - LIVE INTELLIGENCE" in output
    assert "3 - LEARNING" in output
    assert "4 - AI QUALITY EVALUATION" in output
    assert "5 - PERSONALISED DISCOVERY" in output
    assert "6 - CHARACTER DISCOVERY" in output
    assert "7 - FLASH DEAL" in output
    assert "8 - COMMERCE BOUNDARY" in output


def test_judge_demo_shows_personalised_discovery():

    result = run_judge_demo()

    assert result.returncode == 0

    output = result.stdout

    assert "Yeti" in output
    assert "Look what I found!" in output
    assert "iPhone 17 Pro" in output
    assert "Pro. Beautiful. Powerful." in output
    assert "WHY THIS IS RELEVANT TO YOU" in output
    assert "Demo Vendor" in output


def test_judge_demo_shows_commerce_boundary():

    result = run_judge_demo()

    assert result.returncode == 0

    output = result.stdout

    assert "GET DEAL -> vendor handoff" in output

    assert "PAYMENT PASS" in output

    assert "Payment status: READY" in output

    assert "Execution mode: DEMO" in output

    assert "[ PROCEED TO CHECKOUT ]" in output

    assert "No payment has been executed." in output

    assert (
        "Vendor checkout remains outside "
        "the hackathon demo."
        in output
    )

    assert "[PASS] Payment execution: NO" in output

    assert "[PASS] Execution mode: DEMO" in output

    assert "EasyChatBot is not a shop." in output


def test_judge_demo_preserves_execution_order():

    result = run_judge_demo()

    assert result.returncode == 0

    output = result.stdout

    governance = output.index(
        "1 - GOVERNANCE"
    )

    intelligence = output.index(
        "2 - LIVE INTELLIGENCE"
    )

    learning = output.index(
        "3 - LEARNING"
    )

    evaluation = output.index(
        "4 - AI QUALITY EVALUATION"
    )

    discovery = output.index(
        "5 - PERSONALISED DISCOVERY"
    )

    character = output.index(
        "6 - CHARACTER DISCOVERY"
    )

    deal = output.index(
        "7 - FLASH DEAL"
    )

    commerce = output.index(
        "8 - COMMERCE BOUNDARY"
    )

    payment = output.index(
        "PAYMENT PASS"
    )

    success = output.index(
        "JUDGE DEMO: SUCCESS"
    )

    assert governance < intelligence
    assert intelligence < learning
    assert learning < evaluation
    assert evaluation < discovery
    assert discovery < character
    assert character < deal
    assert deal < commerce
    assert commerce < payment
    assert payment < success
